### [1] [When Should a Language Model Trust Itself? Same-Model Self-Verification as a Conditional Confidence Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02915)
*Aditya Ajay Phalod*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Self-verification as a confidence signal for selective prediction works well on some tasks (ARC-Challenge) and models (Phi-2, Qwen), but fails on others (TruthfulQA-MC). It is not a general-purpose uncertainty estimator and should be understood as a conditional signal that depends on task, model, and prompt configuration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While same-model self-verification (prompting a model to audit its own answers) is intuitively plausible as a confidence signal for selective prediction, its practical value remains unclear when compared against strong likelihood-based baselines.

Method: Evaluated self-verification against two likelihood-based baselines (LL-AVG and LL-SUM) on ARC-Challenge and TruthfulQA-MC using multiple model families, scales, and prompt variants. Measured both correctness ranking and abstention quality through AURC and operating-point analyses.

Result: Results are sharply task- and model-dependent. On ARC-Challenge, self-verification substantially improves over LL-AVG for Phi-2 and Qwen models (largest gains in Qwen-7B). On TruthfulQA-MC, the signal is less reliable: smaller models become prompt-sensitive, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-8B degrades relative to LL-AVG, and LL-SUM often remains the stronger baseline.

Conclusion: Self-verification should be understood as a conditional confidence signal rather than a general-purpose uncertainty estimator, as its effectiveness depends heavily on task type, model family, prompt formulation, and the specific baseline it must outperform.

Abstract: Same-model self-verification, prompting a model to audit its own predicted answer, is a plausible confidence signal for selective prediction, but its practical value remains unclear once strong likelihood-based baselines are taken seriously. We evaluate self-verification against two such baselines, LL-AVG and LL-SUM, on ARC-Challenge and TruthfulQA-MC across multiple model families, scales, and prompt variants. We measure not only correctness ranking, but also abstention quality through AURC and operating-point analyses. The results are sharply task- and model-dependent. On ARC-Challenge, self-verification substantially improves over LL-AVG for Phi-2 and the Qwen models, with the largest gains appearing in Qwen-7B. On TruthfulQA-MC, however, the signal is less reliable: smaller models can become prompt-sensitive, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-8B degrades relative to LL-AVG, and LL-SUM often remains the stronger practical baseline. We therefore do not treat self-verification as a general-purpose uncertainty estimator. In this setting, it is better understood as a conditional confidence signal whose value depends on task type, model family, prompt formulation, and, crucially, the baseline it must beat.

</details>


### [2] [Evaluating Reasoning Models for Queries with Presuppositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03050)
*Rose Sathyanathan,Kinshuk Vasisht,Danish Pruthi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估大型推理模型（LRM）识别和纠正用户查询中错误预设的能力，发现推理模型虽有小幅提升，但仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户频繁向AI模型提出包含错误预设的问题，此前研究发现LLM难以挑战这些错误假设。随着推理模型的兴起，需重新评估其在假设检验方面的能力。

Method: 构建涵盖健康、科学和常识领域、预设强度不同的查询集，对多个主流模型进行评估对比。

Result: 推理模型相比非推理模型准确率仅提升2-11%，仍无法纠正26-42%的错误预设，且对预设表达强度的敏感度较高。

Conclusion: 尽管推理能力有所提升，大型推理模型仍难以可靠地纠正错误假设，对表达强度的敏感性表明其在处理预设问题上的脆弱性。

Abstract: Millions of users turn to AI models for their information needs. It is conceivable that a large number of user queries contain assumptions that may be factually inaccurate. Prior work notes that large language models (LLMs) often fail to challenge such erroneous assumptions, and can reinforce users' misinformed opinions. However, given the recent advances, especially in model's reasoning capabilities, we revisit whether large reasoning models (LRMs) can reason about the underlying assumptions and respond to user queries appropriately. We construct queries with varying degrees of presuppositions spanning health, science, and general knowledge, and use it to evaluate several widely-deployed models When compared to non-reasoning models, we find that reasoning models achieve a slightly higher accuracy (2-11%), but they still fail to challenge a large fraction (26-42%) of false presuppositions. Further, reasoning models remain susceptible to how strongly the presupposition is expressed.

</details>


### [3] [How Language Models Process Negation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03052)
*Zhejian Zhou,Tianyi Zhou,Robin Jia,Jonathan May*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）如何从机制层面处理否定词。研究发现：1）开源模型内部确实存在正确处理否定的组件，但晚期注意力层的"捷径"行为导致错误输出；2）模型通过两种机制处理否定——抑制机制（注意力头抑制被否定概念）和构建机制（直接构建否定短语表示），其中构建机制更为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理否定相关问题时经常给出错误答案，但其内部是否存在正确处理否定的组件尚不清楚。理解这一机制有助于改进模型的推理能力。

Method: 在Mistral-7B和Llama-3.1-8B上应用观测可解释性和因果可解释性技术，通过消融实验移除特定注意力模块，结合对注意力头的分析来验证假设。

Result: 开源模型内部包含正确处理否定的组件；晚期注意力层的"捷径"行为是导致错误的主要原因；消融这些注意力模块可显著提升否定相关问题的准确率。

Conclusion: LLM同时使用两种否定处理机制（抑制机制和构建机制），且构建机制占主导地位；模型内部存在竞争性机制共存的现象。

Abstract: We study how Large Language Models (LLMs) process negation mechanistically. First, we establish that even though open-weight models often provide wrong answers to questions involving negation, they do possess internal components that process negation correctly. Their poor accuracy is due to late-layer attention behavior that promotes simple shortcuts; ablating those attention modules greatly improves accuracy on negation-related questions. Second, we uncover how models process negation. We consider two hypotheses: models could use attention heads that attend to the phrase being negated and suppress related concepts, or they could directly construct a representation of the entire negative phrase (e.g., representing "not gas" as a vector that promotes liquids and solids). We apply a range of observational and causal interpretability techniques on Mistral-7B and Llama-3.1-8B to show that models implement both mechanisms, with the "constructive" mechanism being more prominent. Combined, our work deepens the understanding of LLMs' internals, highlighting construction-dominant computations and the coexistence of competing mechanisms within LLMs.

</details>


### [4] [The TTS-STT Flywheel: Synthetic Entity-Dense Audio Closes the Indic ASR Gap Where Commercial and Open-Source Systems Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03073)
*Venkata Pushpak Teja Menta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个 TTS<->STT 飞轮方法，通过开源印地语 TTS 合成实体密集的代码混合训练数据，并结合 LoRA 微调来提升小众领域印地语语音识别的实体命中率。该方法在泰卢固语、印地语和泰米尔语上均取得了显著提升（最高达 22 倍），同时发现 LoRA 微调可解决泰卢固语特有的脚本坍缩问题，但该方案不适用于印地语和泰米尔语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源 SOTA 模型和商业系统在处理小众领域的印地语语音识别时表现欠佳，具体包括数字串、货币金额、地址、品牌名称以及英语/印地语代码混合等实体密集场景。例如，在合成的实体密集泰卢固语测试集上，开源 SOTA 模型 vasista22/whisper-telugu-large-v2 的 Entity-Hit-Rate（EHR）仅为 0.027，商用系统 Deepgram Nova-3 也仅达到 0.16，存在巨大的改进空间。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个自包含的 TTS<->STT 飞轮系统：首先使用开源印地语 TTS 流程合成约 22,000 条实体密集的印地语-英语代码混合语句（边际成本低于 50 美元），然后在此合成语料库上对 vasista22/whisper-telugu-large-v2 进行 LoRA 微调。消融实验（EDSA 隔离）通过仅在 FLEURS-Te 上进行 LoRA 训练来验证合成语料库的贡献。此外，针对不同语言进行了脚本坍缩（SFR）分析，以确定 LoRA 微调在不同语言上的适用性。

Result: 泰卢固语测试集上，LoRA 微调模型的 EHR 达到 0.473，相比开源 SOTA 提升 17 倍，相比 Deepgram 提升 3 倍；在印地语测试集上达到 0.337（相比基线提升 7 倍）；泰米尔语测试集上达到 0.543（相比基线和 Deepgram 均提升 22 倍）。在干净语音回归测试中，WER 仅增加 6.6 个百分点。本地人类录音验证（n=20）确认了模型能够有效迁移到真实语音场景（EHR 0.516）。消融实验表明，EDSA 语料库贡献了几乎 100% 的性能提升。此外，发现标准 Whisper-large-v3 在泰卢固语上存在脚本坍缩问题（SFR 0.46-0.71），LoRA 微调可有效修复（SFR 0.81-0.97），但该方法不适用于印地语和泰米尔语，因为这些语言的标准模型 SFR 已 ≥ 0.98。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个自包含的 TTS<->STT 飞轮系统，在印地语和泰米尔语的小众领域代码混合语音识别上取得了显著改进（分别达到 7 倍和 22 倍的提升）。尽管未达到预设的 EHR 目标（Te: 0.75, Hi/Ta: 0.65），但本地人类录音验证了合成数据训练出的模型能够有效迁移到真实语音场景。此外，研究发现了一个重要的语言条件特性：标准 Whisper-large-v3 在泰卢固语上存在脚本坍缩问题（SFR 0.46-0.71），而 LoRA 微调可解决此问题，但对印地语和泰米尔语则不适用，因为这些语言的标准模型 SFR 已 ≥ 0.98。所有代码、测试集、预测结果和 EDSA 语料库均已开源。

Abstract: Niche-domain Indic ASR -- digit strings, currency amounts, addresses, brand names, English/Indic codemix -- is under-served by both open-source SOTA and commercial systems. On a synthesised entity-dense Telugu test set (held-out by synthesis system), vasista22/whisper-telugu-large-v2 (open SOTA) achieves Entity-Hit-Rate (EHR) 0.027 and Deepgram Nova-3 (commercial) 0.16. We close this gap with a self-contained TTS<->STT flywheel: an open-source Indic TTS pipeline synthesises ~22,000 entity-dense Indic-English code-mix utterances at <$50 marginal cost, and a LoRA fine-tune on top of vasista22 achieves EHR 0.473 on the held-out test (17x over open SOTA, 3x over commercial), with read-prose regression bounded to +6.6 pp WER on FLEURS-Te. Cross-language: beta-Hi 0.337 (7x vs vasista22) and beta-Ta 0.543 (22x vs vasista22, 22x vs Deepgram); on Hindi where Deepgram has substantial entity coverage, the flywheel underperforms commercial. All three beta models fall below pre-registered EHR targets (0.75 for Te, 0.65 for Hi/Ta); we report honestly. A native-human-recorded sanity check (n=20 Telugu) confirms transfer to real speech (beta-Te EHR 0.516 on native vs 0.473 on synth). An EDSA-isolation ablation (LoRA on FLEURS-Te alone) yields EHR 0.020 on the same held-out, attributing ~100% of the gain to the EDSA corpus. We additionally report a language-conditional finding: vanilla Whisper-large-v3 has Telugu-specific Script Collapse (SFR 0.46-0.71) that a per-language LoRA corrects (SFR 0.81-0.97), but the recipe is contraindicated on Hindi and Tamil where vanilla SFR >= 0.98. Code, holdouts, predictions, EDSA corpus, and entity dictionaries are released open-source.

</details>


### [5] [Semantically Enriching Investor Micro-blogs for Opinion-Aware Emotion Analysis: A Practical Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03092)
*Gaurav Negi,Paul Buitelaar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过在StockEmotions数据集中加入语义结构化的意见图，并利用GNN验证，发现意见语义能显著提升金融文本情感分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融NLP中的情感分析缺乏理解情感"背后原因"的粒度，现有方法无法识别情绪的目标对象。

Method: 扩展StockEmotions数据集，引入语义结构化的意见图；使用声明式LLM管道为每句话生成意见图（10,000条StockTwits评论）；采用GNN作为基线分类器研究效果。

Result: 融入意见语义后，在不同情绪谱上的分类性能均有所提升。

Conclusion: 意见图提供超越传统情感和情绪标签的细粒度语义深度，有效增强金融文本的情感分类能力。

Abstract: While sentiment analysis is the staple of financial NLP, capturing the nuances of 'why' behind that sentiment remains a challenge. There have been attempts to address this by analysing investor emotions alongside sentiment; however, this does not provide the additional granularity required to understand the target of the emotion/sentiment. We address this by augmenting the StockEmotions dataset with semantically structured opinion graphs, which provide granular semantic depth to the existing sentiment and emotion labels. Using a declarative LLM pipeline, we augment the StockEmotions dataset with opinion graphs for each sentence, derived from 10,000 comments collected from StockTwits. In addition, we study the effect of introducing opinion semantics on baseline classifiers using Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Our analysis demonstrates that incorporating opinion semantics improves classification performance across different emotional spectrums

</details>


### [6] [SURE-RAG: Sufficiency and Uncertainty-Aware Evidence Verification for Selective Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03534)
*Jingxi Qiu,Zeyu Han,Cheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SURE-RAG是一种用于检索增强生成（RAG）中证据充分性验证的透明聚合协议，通过分析证据的集合级属性（覆盖度、关系强度、分歧、冲突和检索不确定性）来判断检索内容是否充分支持答案，输出可审计的三路决策和选择性评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统中，检索结果可能与问题相关但无法真正支撑答案（检索≠验证），现有方法独立评分各段落无法检测缺失推理步骤和未解决冲突，需要新的证据充分性验证框架。

Method: SURE-RAG采用共享的断言-证据验证器生成局部分布，再聚合为可解释的信号：覆盖度（coverage）、关系强度（relation strength）、分歧度（disagreement）、冲突（conflict）和检索不确定性（retrieval uncertainty），最终输出三路决策（支持/反驳/不充分）并附带可审计的选择性评分。

Result: 在HotpotQA-RAG v3基准上，标定后的SURE-RAG达到0.9075 Macro-F1（0.8951±0.0069），显著优于DeBERTa平均池化（0.6516）和GPT-4o判断（0.7284），与不透明的双列交叉编码器性能相当（0.8888±0.0109）但具有完整可审计性。在30%覆盖率下，风险从0.2588降至0.1642，不安全回答减少37%。

Conclusion: SURE-RAG在证据充分性验证任务上表现优异且完全可审计；在HaluBench不安全检测上GPT-4o反超（0.3343 vs 0.7389），表明受控的充分性验证与自然幻觉检测是本质不同的问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds answers in retrieved passages, but retrieval is not verification: a passage can be topical and still fail to justify the answer. We frame this gap as evidence sufficiency verification for selective RAG answering: given a question, a candidate answer, and retrieved evidence, predict whether the evidence supports, refutes, or is insufficient, and abstain unless support is established. We present SURE-RAG, a transparent aggregation protocol built on the observation that evidence sufficiency is a set-level property: missing hops and unresolved conflicts cannot be detected by independent passage scoring. A shared pair-level claim-evidence verifier produces local relation distributions, which SURE-RAG aggregates into interpretable answer-level signals -- coverage, relation strength, disagreement, conflict, and retrieval uncertainty -- yielding a three-way decision and an auditable selective score. We evaluate on HotpotQA-RAG v3, a controlled multi-hop benchmark, under an artifact-aware protocol (shortcut baselines, counterfactual swaps, no-oracle checks, GPT-4o audits). Calibrated SURE-RAG reaches 0.9075 Macro-F1 (0.8951 +/- 0.0069), substantially above DeBERTa mean-pooling (0.6516) and a GPT-4o judge (0.7284), while matching a strong but opaque concat cross-encoder (0.8888 +/- 0.0109) with full auditability. Risk at 30% coverage drops from 0.2588 to 0.1642, a 37% reduction in unsafe answers. To deliberately probe the task boundary, we further contrast SURE-RAG with GPT-4o on HaluBench unsafe detection: the ranking reverses (0.3343 vs 0.7389 unsafe-F1), establishing that controlled sufficiency verification and natural hallucination detection are distinct problems.

</details>


### [7] [MedStruct-S: A Benchmark for Key Discovery, Key-Conditioned QA and Semi-Structured Extraction from OCR Clinical Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03103)
*Yingyun Li,Yu Wang,Haiyang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedStruct-S是一个针对OCR临床报告的半结构化信息抽取基准，解决了现有评估忽略异质键表示和OCR噪声的问题，涵盖9个模型（0.11B-103B参数），为不同场景下的模型选择提供可靠依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估低估了两个关键因素：异质且未知的键表示、OCR引入的噪声，难以评估模型在真实场景中的鲁棒性

Method: 构建MedStruct-S基准数据集，包含3,582页真实临床报告；测试两种范式：encoder-only序列标注+后处理 和 decoder-only结构化生成，涵盖4个encoder-only和5个decoder-only模型（0.11B-103B参数）

Result: Encoder-only模型在非空值键条件QA中表现最佳，即使规模更小；同等规模下encoder-only模型整体更优；不考虑规模时，微调decoder-only模型整体最强

Conclusion: MedStruct-S provides可靠且实用 basis for模型选择和对比。Encoder-only模型在非空值键条件QA中表现最佳；类似规模下encoder-only模型整体更优；不考虑模型规模时微调的decoder-only模型整体最强。

Abstract: Semi-structured information extraction (IE) from OCR-derived clinical reports is crucial for efficiently reconstructing patients' longitudinal medical histories. In practice, this scenario commonly involves three tasks: (i) field-header (key) discovery, (ii) key-conditioned question answering (QA), and (iii) end-to-end key-value pair extraction. However, existing evaluations often under-model two factors: heterogeneous and incompletely known key representations, and OCR-induced noise. This makes it difficult to assess model robustness in real-world settings.
  We present MedStruct-S, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate these tasks under unknown keys and OCR noise. MedStruct-S contains 3,582 annotated real-world clinical report pages. Using MedStruct-S, we benchmark two representative paradigms: encoder-only sequence labeling with post-processing and decoder-only structured generation, covering four encoder-only and five decoder-only models spanning 0.11B to 103B parameters. Our results show that encoder-only models achieve the best performance for non-null-value key-conditioned QA despite being substantially smaller than decoder-only models. When comparing models of similar order of magnitude, encoder-only models still perform better overall. Without controlling for model scale, fine-tuned decoder-only models deliver the strongest overall results. These findings show that the benchmark provides a reliable and practical basis for selecting and comparing models across different semi-structured IE settings.

</details>


### [8] [Effective Performance Measurement: Challenges and Opportunities in KPI Extraction from Earnings Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03147)
*Rasmus T. Aavang,Rasmus Tjalk-Bøggild,Alexandre Iolov,Giovanni Rizzi,Mike Zhang,Johannes Bjerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对财报电话会议信息提取困难的问题，构建了SECB、ECB等新基准，验证了SEC训练模型存在领域迁移问题，并提出LLM方案达到79.7%精确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 财报电话会议是获取上市公司财务信息的关键来源，但不同于SEC的结构化 filings，这些会议记录缺乏标注、结构松散、语言口语化，难以直接应用SEC训练的模型进行信息提取，存在显著的领域迁移挑战。

Method: (1) 在SECB数据集上评估SEC预训练模型的泛化能力作为基线；(2) 构建ECB和ECB-A两个新基准用于评估；(3) 设计基于LLM的开放域信息提取系统，并通过专家标注验证结果。

Result: 基于编码器的模型在跨领域应用时表现不佳，存在明显的领域偏移问题；LLM系统从非结构化电话会议记录中提取KPI的精确度达79.7%，为该领域建立了可行的基线。

Conclusion: LLM系统结合人工验证可有效解决领域迁移问题，为从非结构化财报电话会议中提取关键财务信息提供了可行的技术路径，精确度达79.7%。

Abstract: Earnings calls are a key source of financial information about public companies. However, extracting information from these calls is difficult. Unlike the templatic filings required by the U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (SEC) to report a company's financial situation, earnings conference calls have no built-in labels, are unstructured, and feature conversational language. We explore this challenging domain by assessing the information captured by models trained on SEC filings and in-context learning methods. To establish a baseline, we first evaluate the generalization capabilities of SEC-trained models across established SEC datasets. To support our investigation, we introduce three novel benchmarks: (1) SEC Filings Benchmark (SECB), (2) Earnings Calls Benchmark (ECB), and ECB-A, a subset with 2,460 expert annotation groups to support our qualitative analysis. We find that encoder-based models struggle with the domain shift. Finally, we propose a system utilizing LLMs to perform open-ended extraction from unstructured call transcripts, verified by human evaluation (79.7% precision), providing a baseline for this valuable domain through the consistent tracking of emergent KPIs.

</details>


### [9] [Rethinking Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval: Evaluating and Advancing Retrievers in Agentic Search Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04018)
*Yilun Zhao,Jinbiao Wei,Tingyu Song,Siyue Zhang,Chen Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BRIGHT-Pro 专家标注基准（为 BRIGHT StackExchange 子集的每个查询补充多「推理维度（reasoning aspect）」金证据与权重），并配套静态检索与 agentic 检索（含固定轮次/自适应轮次）两套评测协议；同时构建面向「互补证据组合」的合成语料 RTriever-Synth（参考回答→非重叠推理维度分解→互补正例 + 正例条件化难负例），LoRA 微调 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 得到 RTriever-4B。结论是：维度感知的 α-nDCG 与 agentic 协议能暴露单段落指标掩盖的行为，静态检索质量并不稳定预测其在 agent 循环中的效用；RTriever-4B 显著优于其基座并超过多数 7–8B 通用嵌入模型，但整体仍不及 BGE-Reasoner-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理密集型检索的目标不是「话题相似」，而是为下游推理提供可支撑结论的证据组合（evidence portfolio）。随着 Deep-Research 类 agentic 搜索系统通过「规划—检索—阅读—综合」的多轮迭代弥补单轮检索不足，算力与延迟成本很高，因此需要能在单次检索中给出「推理就绪」证据的检索器。然而现有评测与训练均与之错位：(1) 评测侧，主力基准 BRIGHT 每查询仅有一两个网页来源拆出的少量金段落，且孤立地评测检索器，不放入动态研究循环，无法衡量证据覆盖度、迭代效率与最终答案质量；(2) 训练侧，现有合成语料（DIVER、ReasonIR 等）为每个查询仅配一个正例加难负例，训练的是「把一条相关段落排到最前」，而非「检索互补的多方面证据」，导致模型在单段落指标上表现尚可，却无法覆盖 agent 所需的完整推理面。

Method: 1) BRIGHT-Pro：以 BRIGHT 的 StackExchange 子集（7 个专家领域：Biology、Earth Science、Economics、Psychology、Robotics、Stack Overflow、Sustainable Living）为基础，共 739 个查询、526,319 篇文档，平均每查询 7.13 个正例、3.74 个推理维度。标注流程：专家先独立分解信息需求为紧凑的「推理维度」（每个维度附 1–2 句依据说明），并用 1–5 Likert 量表给出重要度，归一化为权重 w_a（Σw_a=1）；随后重新审计 BRIGHT 原金段落（剔除弱相关者、合并同源连续/重叠段落以保留上下文），并借助常规搜索与 Perplexity/ChatGPT Web Search 等 AI 辅助搜索补充新正例；基于 FireCrawl 框架定制界面抓取 HTML、去除广告导航等模板噪声，人工精修后按维度切分为多个 aspect-specific 正例；标注过程中迭代修正维度体系（澄清定义、细分过宽维度、合并高度相关维度）。质量控制：同领域第二位标注者独立复核维度粒度/均衡/权重与证据有效性；抽样 50 个查询重打分，权重加权 Cohen's κ = 0.742。2) 评测协议：静态设定主指标为带新颖性惩罚的 α-nDCG@k（α=0.5，对已覆盖维度重复检索打折），辅以加权维度召回 A-Recall@k、NDCG@k、Recall@k；agentic 设定将检索器作为唯一变量接入单一 search 工具的 LLM agent（GPT-5-mini-08-07 与自托管 Qwen3.5-122B-A10B），采用固定轮次（R∈{1,2,3}，每轮 top-5）与自适应轮次（agent 自行判断停止）两种协议，用 GPT-5 作 LLM-as-Judge 逐维度给 {0,0.5,1} 覆盖分并给 1–5 总体质量分，报告推理完备度（加权 aspect 覆盖映射到 1–5）、总体质量、检索轮数与效率-质量奖励 AER = OQ·e^{-0.05(R-1)}；固定 175 个查询（每任务 25）评测。参考回答由 GPT-5 基于人工维度与正例生成，抽样 40 例盲评验证其完备度 4.80/质量 4.85 与人工 4.75/4.90 相当。3) RTriever-Synth：从 100 万 MS MARCO 查询随机采样 140K，用 PersonaHub 人设做格式示例改写为 DeepResearch 风格长查询，并分类 factual/analytical；analytical 查询先由强 LLM 生成自包含参考回答，再分解为 2–3 个非重叠维度，为每个维度生成「蓝图」（依据、段落类型、来源、标题、三句 TL;DR）并实例化为正例，从而保证每条正例都是推理链上的「承重件」且彼此互补；随后在与正例等量条件下生成难负例：保留话题/词汇线索、但显式规避某一维度的证据，并在文本实现阶段加入「避免某维度」的约束。4) 训练：从 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 出发用 ms-swift 做 LoRA 微调（r=16、α=32、冻结原嵌入参数），InfoNCE 温度 0.02，每个 query 配 1 正 1 负并以批内文档作 in-batch 负例，5 epoch、峰值 lr 1e-5、5% 线性 warmup、bf16 + DeepSpeed ZeRO-2、每卡 batch 384（有效 768）、2×B200、序列截断 2048，得到 RTriever-4B。5) 对比 12 个公开检索器（BM25；GritLM-7B、InstructOR-XL、GTE-Qwen2-7B、Qwen3-Embedding-8B、EmbeddingGemma-300M、OpenAI text-embedding-3-Large；ReasonIR-8B、DIVER-4B、DIVER-4B-1020、BGE-Reasoner-8B、INF-Retriever-Pro-7B）。

Result: 静态：α-nDCG@25 上 BGE-Reasoner-8B 68.0 最佳，DIVER-4B-1020 63.7、DIVER-4B 59.9 次之，RTriever-4B 55.3 位列上中部，超过全部 7–8B 通用嵌入模型（含 Qwen3-Embedding-8B 的 49.5、OpenAI-3L 的 45.8）；ReasonIR-8B 因单正例训练目标跌至第 11（41.0），说明训练目标比参数规模更关键。维度感知指标清晰区分出 BRIGHT NDCG@10 所掩盖的差距。固定轮次 agentic（GPT-5-mini）：BGE-Reasoner-8B 领先（Round-3 α-nDCG@15 = 63.0，总体质量 4.31），DIVER-4B（4.29）反超其更新版 DIVER-4B-1020（4.16），RTriever-4B 第三（4.25）；BM25 从静态最差（40.3）升至 Round-3 51.5，因 LLM 后续查询带来具体关键词缓解词汇错配。自适应轮次：BGE-Reasoner-8B 最优（AER 3.65、5.10 轮），RTriever-4B 按两后端平均 AER 居第二（GPT-5-mini 下 AER 3.51、6.01 轮）；GTE-7B 总体质量最高（4.51）但轮数 6.67 使 AER 降至 3.44，正体现 AER 惩罚「最终好但收敛慢」的失败模式；换用 Qwen3.5-122B 后 DIVER-4B-1020 从第 2 掉至第 7（AER 3.56→3.11），RTriever-4B 升至第 2（3.38），说明低梯队排序更多反映检索器—agent 兼容性而非检索器本身质量。案例分析归纳出五类失效模式：早期检索高效（早停、推理完备）、证据缺失导致臆测（Gazebo 案例 13 轮 0 金标、结论错误）、重复偏置与自我强化（60 槽位仅 28 唯一文档、53% 重复）、维度隧道视野（7 轮全押单一维度，加权覆盖仅 0.50）、早成功后假设跳跃（第 2 轮已获金标却继续 4 轮试探替代概念）。

Conclusion: BRIGHT-Pro 通过多维度金证据与「检索器在环」的 agentic 协议，证明传统的单段落、单次检索评测无法刻画推理密集型检索的真实价值：静态排序与 agent 循环内的答案质量、迭代效率之间只存在松散对应，且低梯队排序高度依赖检索器与 agent 的匹配。训练侧，互补正例 + 正例条件化难负例的监督信号显著优于基座（RTriever-4B 大幅超过 Qwen3-Embedding-4B），说明「面向证据组合」而非「面向单一高分段落」的目标是有效的。总体倡议：应围绕完整证据组合而非单段落来构建与评测检索器。局限：仅覆盖 BRIGHT StackExchange 的 7 个专家领域；受专家标注成本限制规模有限（739 查询）；训练仍为简单的一正一负三元组，尚未探索多正例目标、维度感知采样、负例课程等。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive retrieval aims to surface evidence that supports downstream reasoning rather than merely matching topical similarity. This capability is increasingly important for agentic search systems, where retrievers must provide complementary evidence across iterative search and synthesis. However, existing work remains limited on both evaluation and training: benchmarks such as BRIGHT provide narrow gold sets and evaluate retrievers in isolation, while synthetic training corpora often optimize single-passage relevance rather than evidence portfolio construction. We introduce BRIGHT-Pro, an expert-annotated benchmark that expands each query with multi-aspect gold evidence and evaluates retrievers under both static and agentic search protocols. We further construct RTriever-Synth, an aspect-decomposed synthetic corpus that generates complementary positives and positive-conditioned hard negatives, and use it to LoRA fine-tune RTriever-4B from Qwen3-Embedding-4B. Experiments across lexical, general-purpose, and reasoning-intensive retrievers show that aspect-aware and agentic evaluation expose behaviors hidden by standard metrics, while RTriever-4B substantially improves over its base model.

</details>


### [10] [Geometric Deviation as an Unsupervised Pre-Generation Reliability Signal: Probing LLM Representations for Answerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03196)
*Yucheng Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标注失败数据、无需模型输出的“生成前”可靠性信号：把每个提示的隐藏状态与“可回答参考集”的质心做余弦距离比较（geometric deviation），在数学类提示上ROC-AUC达0.78–0.84，优于拒答关键词基线与5次采样的自一致性基线；而在事实类提示上无可靠信号，代码类效应量大但显著性不稳定，说明该信号是“形式条件性”而非普适性的，并定位于早期层（2–5层）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉（自信但错误的生成）是部署LLM的核心可靠性问题。若能在生成前判断某查询超出模型知识边界，就可触发弃答或人工复核，且不增加解码延迟。既有可靠性估计方法（校准/自洽性、内部探针分类器、表示操控、监督式内部状态分析）要么依赖带标注的失败数据，要么需要访问模型输出，要么对模型特性敏感；而“仅用无监督的表示几何”能否作为实用的生成前可靠性信号仍未被充分探索。本文的核心动机是检验这种最简方案：与“可回答”参考集的质心比较余弦距离，不需要标注、不需要微调、不需要采样。

Method: 1) 模型：三个架构/对齐不同的7–8B指令模型（Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3），固定规模以隔离架构与对齐差异。2) 表示提取：取最后层隐状态，对全部输入token做均值池化，减去全局均值（去各向异性），距离为余弦距离1−cosθ；own_dist = 到本形式“A类（可回答）质心”的距离，质心仅由A类提示构成，故不含任何U标签信息；Math与Fact联合均值中心化共享参考系，Code单独中心化。3) 数据：三种提示形式的匹配对（matched pairs），每对仅改动一处使其不可回答而保持领域、长度、句法结构一致——Math（n=50，数学上未定义运算、极值不可能性、未知量替换）、Fact（n=10，未来事件/不存在实体/反事实前提）、Code（n=30，类型错误、非终止计算、运行时异常）。4) 统计：单侧置换检验（5000次重算质心以避免零假设违规）检验Δ=dist̄U−dist̄A，报告Cohen’s d；分类性能用均值中点阈值给出ROC-AUC与F1。5) 基线：拒答关键词分类器（需一次完整生成）与自一致性（k=5，温度0.7，答案token多数投票或ROUGE-1分歧度）。6) 层级分析：提取每层均值池化表示计算逐层间隙δl；并人工标注Math-U输出（拒答/部分/幻觉）以考察几何—行为关系。代码与提示已开源。

Result: 几何主要编码“任务形式”而非答案性：Math、Fact、Code形成彼此分离的簇（Fact–Math质心余弦约−0.84~−0.85，近正交；Fact–Unknown为正且接近，说明数学式不可回答提示被拉向Math质心，属纯形式效应）。受控配对结果：Math三模型全部高度显著（p<0.0001，d=+1.12~+1.41，Δ≈+0.37~+0.39）；Fact三模型均不显著（p=0.36~0.57，d=+0.44~+0.76，A/U分布完全重叠，且n=10时Math效应已p<0.01，非功效不足）；Code效应量大但显著性不一（d=+1.01/+1.31/+1.14，仅Qwen p=0.008，Llama 0.11、Mistral 0.16，因部分A类本身偏离大导致方差升高）。预测性能：Math几何AUC 0.782–0.841，显著高于拒答基线（0.63–0.73）与SC（0.30–0.62，因模型倾向“一致地幻觉”，5次采样分歧近零）；Fact几何仅0.66–0.71，Qwen拒答基线0.75更优；Code几何0.77–0.82优于SC（0.37–0.50），Qwen拒答基线0.85略高（会显式说出TypeError）。层级剖面：三模型均在第2–5层达峰（δ≈0.98–1.09）后向输出层衰减（末层≈0.44–0.48），衰减源于Math-A深层向生成就绪流形漂移而非U信号衰减。行为标注：Llama/Qwen各有10/20幻觉，Qwen拒答更多（7/20 vs 5/20）；n=50时三模型一致将19条提示漂移到Fact质心（极值/无穷对象、无界聚合、未知量），跨模型几何共识强；同一几何离群提示上Llama幻觉而Qwen拒答，说明对齐影响“如何行动”而非“信息本身”。

Conclusion: 几何偏离可在形式具有形式化答案性约束的结构化领域（数学、代码）中充当轻量、无监督、生成前的可靠性信号（Math AUC 0.78–0.84，跨三种架构一致），并能捕获模型未显式拒答的失败案例；而对开放域事实不可回答性无可靠信号，故不可当作通用幻觉检测器（Fact AUC仅0.66–0.71且置换检验不显著）。信号源于早期层（2–5）并向末层衰减，提示可用中间层激活做更高效的在线监控；未来可结合几何与输出级信号（语义熵、校准置信度）。局限：样本量小（Math 50、Fact 10、Code 30，Code需n≳80–100才达常规显著）、基线较弱（未测完整语义熵与PRISM/MHAD监督探针）、层选择未系统化、行为标注单人完成、无法排除词汇罕见/类内方差等混淆（建议做困惑度控制）、未测基座与RLHF变体及更大规模、负结果仅基于单一余弦质心探针（未尝试PCA方向/学习超平面/早层池化）。

Abstract: A reliable language model should be able to signal, prior to generation, when a query falls outside its knowledge. We investigate whether representation geometry can provide such a pre-generation signal by measuring the deviation of hidden states from an answerable reference set, requiring no labeled failure data and no access to model outputs.
  Across three instruction-tuned models (Llama 3.1-8B, Qwen 2.5-7B, and Mistral-7B-Instruct) and three prompt forms (Math, Fact, Code), we find that geometry primarily encodes task form. Within mathematical prompts, unanswerable inputs consistently deviate from the answerable centroid, yielding strong separation (ROC-AUC 0.78-0.84). This single-pass pre-generation signal outperforms a simple refusal baseline and compares favorably to self-consistency. It also captures cases where models do not explicitly refuse.
  In contrast, no reliable geometric signal emerges for factual prompts, indicating that the effect is form-conditional rather than universal. Code prompts show large effect sizes with higher variance, suggesting partial generalization beyond mathematical form.
  A layer-wise analysis reveals that the signal arises in early layers and gradually attenuates toward the output. These results suggest that answerability-related geometry is established before the final stages of generation. Together, these findings indicate that geometric deviation can serve as a lightweight pre-generation signal that is reliable in structured domains with formal answerability constraints, with clear boundaries on where it generalizes.

</details>


### [11] [EQUITRIAGE: A Fairness Audit of Gender Bias in LLM-Based Emergency Department Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03998)
*Richard J. Young,Alice M. Matthews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM急诊分诊系统普遍存在性别偏见，5个模型flip rate为9.9%-43.8%，其中两个模型存在系统性女性undertriage。公平属性相互独立，干预效果因模型而异，需要逐模型进行反事实审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院正在试点使用LLM作为急诊分诊决策支持工具，而临床证据显示人工分诊存在持续的性别偏见。需要评估这些模型是否复制或减轻了已知偏见。

Method: 使用374,275次评估，涵盖18,714个MIMIC-IV-ED病例 vignettes，对比5个LLM模型（Gemini-3-Flash、Nemotron-3-Super、DeepSeek-V3.1、Mistral-Small-3.2、GPT-4.1-Nano）在四种prompt策略下的表现。通过9,346对性别交换的反事实对（counterfactual pairs）评估公平性，测量flip rate（F/M分类不一致率）、Chouldechova风格的校准分析，以及人口统计学盲法干预实验。

Result: 所有5个模型的flip rate均超过5%阈值（范围9.9%-43.8%）。DeepSeek和Gemini出现女性undertriage方向性偏见（F/M比率分别为2.15:1和1.34:1），两个模型接近均等，一个模型高敏感性但存在弱男性方向不对称。DeepSeek的低校准差距（0.013）与组内校准和组间反事实不变性的分离现象共存。人口统计学盲法使Gemini的flip rate降至0.5%；保留年龄的盲法变体仍使DeepSeek保留F/M 1.25比率，提示年龄是残余偏见通道。消融实验揭示同一方向性表型背后机制不同：Gemini中信号来自姓名+性别交换的组合，而DeepSeek中仅性别token即携带信号。

Conclusion: LLM-based急诊分诊系统存在显著的性别偏见，且不同模型表现差异巨大。公平属性（组间均等、反事实不变性、性别校准）相互独立，需要针对每个模型进行独立的反事实审计后才能进行临床部署。干预措施的有效性具有模型依赖性，Chain-of-thought prompting会降低准确性。

Abstract: Emergency department triage assigns patients an acuity score that determines treatment priority, and clinical evidence documents persistent gender disparities in human acuity assessment. As hospitals pilot large language models (LLMs) as triage decision support, a critical question is whether these models reproduce or mitigate known biases. We present EQUITRIAGE, a fairness audit of LLM-based ESI assignment evaluating five models (Gemini-3-Flash, Nemotron-3-Super, DeepSeek-V3.1, Mistral-Small-3.2, GPT-4.1-Nano) across 374,275 evaluations on 18,714 MIMIC-IV-ED vignettes under four prompt strategies. Of 9,368 originals, 9,346 are paired with a gender-swapped counterfactual. All five models produced flip rates above a pre-registered 5% threshold (9.9% to 43.8%). Two showed directional female undertriage (DeepSeek F/M 2.15:1, Gemini 1.34:1); two were near-parity; one had high sensitivity with weak male-direction asymmetry. DeepSeek's directional bias coexisted with a low outcome-linked calibration gap (0.013 against MIMIC-IV admission), a Chouldechova-style dissociation between within-group calibration and between-pair counterfactual invariance. Demographic blinding reduced Gemini's flip rate to 0.5%; an age-preserving blind variant left DeepSeek with residual F/M 1.25, implicating age as a residual channel. Chain-of-thought prompting degraded accuracy for all five models. A two-model ablation reveals opposite underlying mechanisms for the same directional phenotype: in Gemini the signal is emergent in the combined name+gender swap, while in DeepSeek the gender token alone carries it. EQUITRIAGE shows that group parity, counterfactual invariance, and gender calibration are distinct fairness properties, that intervention effectiveness is model-dependent, and that per-model counterfactual auditing should precede clinical deployment.

</details>


### [12] [Sparse Memory Finetuning as a Low-Forgetting Alternative to LoRA and Full Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03229)
*Prakhar Gupta,Garv Shah,Satyam Goyal,Anirudh Kanchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在 Qwen-2.5-0.5B-Instruct 上复现稀疏记忆微调（SMF），与 LoRA 和全量微调做受控对比。方法上在 Qwen 的第 6/12/18 层插入乘积键记忆（PKM）层，两种接入方式（替换 MLP 与加性 α·mem 分支，α 可选可训练），并在稀疏任务训练阶段每步仅用 top-512 记忆值行更新（占该层值行的 3.125%），提出新的 KL 散度行选择规则与已有 TF-IDF 规则对比。结果：加性稀疏记忆 + KL 在 MedMCQA 上提升 2.5 个百分点（0.344→0.369），而 WikiText 困惑度（12.72，略优于基线）与 TriviaQA 准确率（0.256→0.252）基本不漂移；LoRA（+4.6pp，PPL +17%，TriviaQA −6pp）与全量微调（+5.4pp，PPL +44%，TriviaQA −9.3pp）学得更多但遗忘明显；替换式记忆在两条 Pareto 前沿上均被支配。结论：保留预训练 MLP 通路与"每步更新局部化"（1.38M 参数/步 vs 9M/494M）是低遗忘的来源；KL 与 TF-IDF 在困惑度与事实记忆之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型适配到窄领域任务时，LoRA 与全量微调都会修改影响所有输入的权重，导致灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）。稀疏记忆微调（SMF, Lin et al. 2025）通过在模型中插入 key–value 记忆层、每步只更新当前 batch 高频读取的少量记忆行，试图以少量可塑性换取更小的能力漂移。作者希望在一个受控、可复现的设置下检验这一假设：这种"局部化更新"是否真能在保持预训练通用能力的同时带来可观的任务增益，并与 LoRA、全量微调在同一目标任务、同一遗忘探针上做公平对比。此前 SMF 的工作未系统比较行选择规则，也未在现代小型 instruct 模型上与 LoRA/全量微调并排评估。

Method: 模型与记忆层：基座 Qwen-2.5-0.5B-Instruct，采用 Lample et al. (2019) 的 PKM 及 Berges et al. (2024) 的扩展形式，在第 {6,12,18} 层各插入一个独立记忆模块，n_k=128（M=16,384 槽/层，d=896），4 个查询头，top-k=16 邻居，key 维度 256；查询对半拆分后与子键表匹配，top-k 值行做 softmax 加权求和，再经 SiLU 门控的 SwiGLU 式输出投影 mem(h)=W_o(r(h)⊙silu(W_g h))。接入方式两种：Replacement（MLP_ℓ ← mem_ℓ，丢弃原 MLP）与 Additive（MLP_ℓ(h)+α·mem_ℓ(h)，α 初始化 0.01）；另设 Additive +S 变体在稀疏任务阶段把 α 变为可训练标量。稀疏更新：统计当前 batch 各值行读取次数 c(i)（跨头、token、top-k 求和），每层选 top-T=512 槽，在值矩阵上挂反向钩子用布尔掩码乘梯度，其余行该步冻结；基座参数与记忆键始终冻结。行选择规则两种：TF-IDF（Lin et al. 2025，s=c(i)/C·log((N+1)/(df(i)+1))，df 为背景 batch 中被读过的次数）与本文新提出的 KL 规则（s=p_batch(i)·log((p_batch(i)+ε)/(p_bg(i)+ε))，p_bg 由背景语料 token 级累计读取计数 b(i) 平滑归一化得到）。两阶段训练：Stage 1 稠密改造（仅训记忆参数，冻结其他参数，在 50,000 条 OpenAssistant 回复上训 2 epoch）；Stage 2 稀疏任务训练（在 60,000 条 MedMCQA 上训 3 epoch，仅 top-T 行接收梯度）。基线：LoRA（r=16, α=32, dropout 0.05，作用于所有注意力与 MLP 线性投影）与全量微调，同样 60k 样本 3 epoch。优化：AdamW、cosine 调度、100 步 warmup、最大序列长度 1024、梯度裁剪 1.0、全局 batch 16；稀疏/LoRA 学习率 5e-4/2e-4，全量微调 5e-5。评测：MedMCQA 按"{label}. {option}"续写的平均逐 token 对数似然打分（避免长度偏置）；WikiText-103 测试集困惑度（窗口 1024、步长 512，token 只计一次）；TriviaQA rc.nocontext 贪心解码 32 token 后按别名子串匹配判对；各用 1,000 条切片，多随机种子报告均值±标准差，并以散点图给出塑性–稳定性 Pareto 前沿。附录区分了"存储的适配参数""推理期净体积增量"和"Stage 2 每步实际更新的参数"三类量（1.38M vs ~9M vs ~494M）。

Result: 主结果（MedMCQA acc / WikiText PPL / TriviaQA acc，基线 0.344 / 13.146 / 0.256）：Replacement sparse (KL) 0.354 / 16.577 / 0.179；Replacement sparse (TF-IDF) 0.355 / 16.501 / 0.207；Additive sparse (KL) 0.369 / 12.723 / 0.252；Additive sparse (TF-IDF) 0.346 / 12.773 / 0.255；Additive sparse +S (KL) 0.378 / 13.139 / 0.223；Additive sparse +S (TF-IDF) 0.369 / 13.456 / 0.245；LoRA 0.390 / 15.470 / 0.193；Full finetune 0.398 / 18.907 / 0.163。结论性观察：(1) 替换式记忆被支配——仅 +1.0~1.1pp 任务增益，困惑度上升约 26%，TriviaQA 掉 5~8pp，在两条 Pareto 前沿之外；(2) Additive sparse (KL) 是最清晰的低遗忘点——+2.5pp MedMCQA，困惑度甚至略低于基线，TriviaQA 仅 −0.4pp（落在种子波动内）；Additive +S (TF-IDF) 可比（+2.5pp、−1.1pp TriviaQA、+0.31 PPL 漂移）；(3) LoRA 与全量微调位于前沿的高遗忘端：全量微调 PPL +44%、TriviaQA −9.3pp；LoRA PPL +17%、TriviaQA −6pp；(4) KL 与 TF-IDF 呈互补权衡：KL 维持更紧的困惑度，TF-IDF 保留更多 TriviaQA 准确率，最清晰的对照在 Additive +S 中——由 TF-IDF 换成 KL 换来 +0.9pp MedMCQA 但损失 2.2pp TriviaQA，作者推测 KL 反复更新被高频读取的槽，若这些槽承载事实知识则更易被破坏；(5) 每步仅 1.38M 值参数（占插入值行的 3.125%）获得梯度，远小于 LoRA 的 ~9M 与全量微调的 ~494M。

Conclusion: 在 Qwen-2.5-0.5B 上，加性稀疏记忆微调构成低遗忘的替代方案：以牺牲约 1~2pp MedMCQA 精度换取显著更小的通用能力漂移（两个遗忘探针都保持在基线 1pp/1% 量级内），而 LoRA 与全量微调虽学得更多却出现明显漂移；替换式记忆（丢弃原 MLP）在该规模下过于破坏性，应保留预训练 MLP 通路、让缩放记忆分支专门化。稀疏方法更低遗忘的可能机制是"更新局部性"——LoRA 适配器每步影响所有输入，而稀疏记忆仅更新检索选中的少量值行（每步约 1.38M 参数）；但作者承认尚未把更新局部性与稠密改造阶段的影响分离。行选择规则的取舍取决于部署更看重语言建模困惑度还是事实记忆，且依赖背景语料（本文用 OpenAssistant）。局限：仅一个模型、一个领域、1,000 条评测切片，遗忘仅用 WikiText 与 TriviaQA 两个探针（指令跟随、代码、多步推理等未覆盖），Pareto 顺序在更大模型/更长训练/其他领域可能改变；未来可探索用更对齐语料（如 PubMed）对记忆层做领域特定初始化。作者声明本文为进行中的工作，代码开源于 github.com/prakharg55/SMF-ICML-FG。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained language model to a new task often hurts the general capabilities it already had, a problem known as catastrophic forgetting. Sparse Memory Finetuning (SMF) tries to avoid this by adding key-value memory layers to the model and, on each training step, updating only the small set of memory rows that the current batch reads most heavily. We re-implement SMF on Qwen-2.5-0.5B-Instruct and compare it with LoRA and full finetuning on MedMCQA, a 4-choice medical exam task, using WikiText perplexity and TriviaQA accuracy as forgetting probes. SMF improves MedMCQA by 2.5 percentage points while keeping both forgetting probes within roughly 1 point of the base model, whereas LoRA and full finetuning achieve larger gains but with clear drift on both. We also compare two row-selection rules (KL-divergence and TF-IDF), which balance the two forgetting metrics differently.

</details>


### [13] [S^2tory: Story Spine Distillation for Movie Script Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03244)
*Mingzhe Lu,Yanbing Liu,Qihao Wang,Jiarui Zhang,Jiayue Wu,Yue Hu,Yunpeng Li,Yangyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: S^2tory是一个基于叙事学的框架,通过角色发展轨迹识别驱动叙事的关键事件(情节核),同时过滤掉边缘卫星事件。NEAgent进行理论约束推理,其提炼的知识用于微调小型模型识别情节核,另一个模型据此生成摘要。实验表明该方法在MovieSum上实现了3.5倍压缩率下的语义保真度最优,并在BookSum上展现出良好的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Movie scripts present a unique summarization challenge due to their non-linear, cross-cut narrative structure. Surface-level saliency methods are ineffective at preserving core story progression, necessitating a narratology-grounded approach.

Method: The authors propose S^2tory (Story Spine Distillation), which leverages character development trajectories to identify plot nuclei while filtering peripheral satellite events. A Narrative Expert Agent (NEAgent) performs theory-constrained reasoning to distill knowledge that conditions a small model for plot nucleus identification. Another model then generates summaries based on these nuclei.

Result: Experiments on MovieSum demonstrate state-of-the-art semantic fidelity at approximately 3.5x compression. Zero-shot evaluation on BookSum confirms strong out-of-domain generalization.

Conclusion: Narratological theory provides an indispensable foundation for modeling complex, non-linear narratives. The framework successfully bridges narrative theory with practical summarization, achieving both high fidelity and strong generalization.

Abstract: Movie scripts pose a fundamental challenge for automatic summarization due to their non-linear, cross-cut narrative structure, which makes surface-level saliency methods ineffective at preserving core story progression. To address this, we introduce S^2tory (Story Spine Distillation), a narratology-grounded framework that leverages character development trajectories to identify plot nuclei, the essential events that drive the narrative forward, while filtering out peripheral satellite events that merely enrich atmosphere or emotion. Our Narrative Expert Agent (NEAgent) performs theory-constrained reasoning, whose distilled knowledge conditions a small model to identify plot nuclei. Another model then uses these plot nuclei to generate the summary. Experiments on the MovieSum dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art semantic fidelity at approximately 3.5x compression, and zero-shot evaluation on BookSum confirms strong out-of-domain generalization. Human evaluation further validates that narratological theory provides an indispensable foundation for modeling complex, non-linear narratives.

</details>


### [14] [LLM-XTM: Enhancing Cross-Lingual Topic Models with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03299)
*Minh Chu Xuan,Tien-Phat Nguyen,Linh Ngo Van,Dinh Viet Sang,Nguyen Thi Ngoc Diep,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM-XTM提出了一种结合LLM引导的主题细化和自洽不确定性量化的框架，用于改进跨语言主题建模。该方法实现了更好的主题一致性和对齐性，同时减少了对双语词典和高成本LLM调用的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cross-lingual topic modeling lacks coherent and well-aligned topics due to reliance on sparse bilingual resources; existing LLM-based methods are costly, document-level limited, and prone to hallucination, while white-box approaches require inaccessible token probabilities.

Method: The framework integrates LLM-guided topic refinement with self-consistency uncertainty quantification, enabling efficient enhancement without requiring token probabilities or extensive bilingual resources.

Result: Experiments on multilingual corpora demonstrate that LLM-XTM achieves superior topic coherence and alignment while reducing reliance on bilingual dictionaries and expensive LLM calls.

Conclusion: LLM-XTM provides a black-box, stable, and scalable solution for cross-lingual topic modeling that achieves superior performance while reducing resource dependencies.

Abstract: Cross-lingual topic modeling aims to discover shared semantic structures across languages, yet existing models depend on sparse bilingual resources and often yield incoherent or weakly aligned topics. Recent LLM-based refinements improve interpretability but are costly, document-level, and prone to hallucination, with prior white-box approaches requiring inaccessible token probabilities. We propose LLM-XTM, a framework that integrates LLM-guided topic refinement with self-consistency uncertainty quantification, enabling black-box, stable, and scalable enhancement of cross-lingual topic models. Experiments on multilingual corpora show that LLM-XTM achieves superior topic coherence and alignment while reducing reliance on bilingual dictionaries and expensive LLM calls.

</details>


### [15] [SHIELD: A Diverse Clinical Note Dataset and Distilled Small Language Models for Enterprise-Scale De-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03301)
*Jose D. Posada,David Love,Somalee Datta,Priya Desai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SHIELD数据集（1,394条笔记，10,505个PHI spans，9个类别）以解决过时基准问题；通过评估4种LLM并蒸馏到本地可部署的SLM，最佳模型在标准硬件上达到P=0.88、R=0.86的性能；分布分析证明SHIELD的独特性，跨数据集评估表明通用与专用模型结合是最佳部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准（如i2b2 2006/2014）已超过十年，缺乏现代叙事的语义和人口统计学多样性；LLM虽达到最先进的零样本提取性能，但企业部署受限于计算成本和云API PHI治理限制。

Method: 采用集覆盖多样性采样与人工参与裁决的方法构建SHIELD数据集；评估了4种LLM（2种专有、2种开源）建立性能上限；使用Frechet Text Distance和Jensen-Shannon Divergence进行分布分析；将LLM能力蒸馏到可本地部署的小型语言模型（SLM）中。

Result: SHIELD包含1,394条笔记和10,505个跨9类别的金标准PHI spans；最佳蒸馏模型在结构化PHI类别（DATE、DOCTOR、ID、PATIENT、PHONE）上匹配教师模型性能；分布分析确认SHIELD在生物医学嵌入和词汇空间中占据与遗留基准不同的独特区域；跨数据集评估显示多样性训练的模型对通用结构化PHI泛化良好。

Conclusion: 最佳蒸馏模型在结构化PHI类别上匹配教师模型，在标准工作站硬件上达到微平均span级精确率0.88、召回率0.86；跨数据集评估表明多样性训练模型对通用结构化PHI泛化良好，但机构特定实体难以迁移，建议结合覆盖广泛的模型与针对高频笔记的专业模型进行最优部署；SHIELD数据集和蒸馏的DeBERTa v3模型已公开发布。

Abstract: De-identification of clinical text remains essential for secondary use of electronic health records (EHRs), yet public benchmarks such as i2b2 2006/2014 are over a decade old and lack the semantic and demographic diversity of modern narratives. While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art zero-shot extraction, enterprise deployment is hindered by compute costs and governance restricting Protected Health Information (PHI) from cloud APIs. We introduce SHIELD (Synthetic Human-annotated Identifier-replaced Entries for Learning and De-identification), a diverse dataset of 1,394 notes with 10,505 gold-standard PHI spans across 9 categories, built via set-cover diversity sampling with human-in-the-loop adjudication. We evaluate four LLMs (two proprietary, two open-weight) to establish a performance ceiling, then distill these capabilities into locally deployable Small Language Models (SLMs). Distributional analysis using Frechet Text Distance and Jensen-Shannon Divergence confirms SHIELD occupies a distinct region of biomedical embedding and vocabulary space versus legacy benchmarks. Our best distilled model matches its teacher on structured PHI categories (DATE, DOCTOR, ID, PATIENT, PHONE) and achieves micro-averaged span-level precision of 0.88 and recall of 0.86 on standard workstation hardware. Cross-dataset evaluation shows diversity-trained models generalize well on universal structured PHI, while institution-specific entities remain hard to transfer, suggesting optimal deployment combines broad-coverage models with specialized models for high-volume notes. We publicly release the SHIELD dataset and the distilled DeBERTa v3 model.

</details>


### [16] [When to Think, When to Speak: Learning Disclosure Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03314)
*Jiaqi Wei,Xuehang Guo,Pengfei Yu,Xiang Zhang,Wanli Ouyang,Siqi Sun,Qingyun Wang,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Side-by-Side (SxS) Interleaved Reasoning：在标准单流自回归生成中引入 think/speak 双动作标签，把"何时向用户披露内容"变成可学习的决策变量。方法由两部分组成：(1) 用 entailment 对齐的交错轨迹做 SFT，使早期披露必须被当前推理前缀所支撑；(2) 用 GRPO 强化学习恢复交错格式下的推理精度。无需第二模型、隐藏状态或专用推理机制。在 Qwen3-30B-A3B(MoE) 与 Qwen3-4B(dense) 上，覆盖 AIME25(域内)、GPQA-Diamond(域外)、LiveCodeBench、KOR-Bench，SxS 整体改善了"准确率–内容延迟"的 Pareto 权衡，收益主要体现在缩短可见更新之间的等待(AIRW)，而非单纯前移全部回答 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单流自回归接口中，同一批 token 既更新模型内部状态，又构成不可撤销的公开承诺，二者被强制耦合。由此产生两个结构性问题：(1)"沉默税(silence tax)"——更多内部推演会推迟首个任务相关内容的出现，用户面对长串无信息的前导文本；(2) 朴素提前流式输出虽缩短延迟，却造成过早承诺(premature commitment)，既会偏置后续生成，又容易退化为低信息填充(filler)以骗取延迟指标。已有做法要么用固定模板/启发式决定披露时机，要么直接奖励"更早输出"从而被 filler 和无依据承诺利用；系统层的加速方案（推测解码等）只降低墙钟时间，无法回答"在推理尚未结束时，哪些任务相关内容可以被安全承诺"这一问题。作者的核心诉求是 justified disclosure（有依据的披露）：早期可见文本必须由截至当前已产生的推理所蕴含，而非低信息填充，从而把"响应节奏"从格式选择转变为可学习的承诺策略(pacing / commitment policy)。

Method: 【问题形式化】定义耦合承诺：h_{t+1}=f(x,Γ_t)、Γ_{t+1}=Γ_t⊕y_{t+1}，公开前缀一旦披露即被固定，定义"解码可行集" Y_dec(x,Γ_t) 来刻画过早承诺的代价。引入可见性受控的流：轨迹 τ=(c_1,z_1,…,c_K,z_K)，通道动作 c_k∈{R(私有推理), A(公开披露)}，私有上下文 Σ_k 与公开转录 Γ_k 分离，披露单调递增；标准接口是 c≡A 的特例。理论分解 p_{θ,φ}(τ|x)=∏π_φ(c_k|Σ_{k-1})·p_θ(z_k|Σ_{k-1},c_k)，实际中 c_k 由同一模型用轻量标签预测（无独立策略网络、无第二通道、speak 文本是最终回答的前缀）。优化目标 min_φ E[L_task(a_1:T,y*)+λ·L_lat(g(τ))]，其中 g(τ) 为内容型延迟统计（首个含任务相关内容的 speak 块的出现位置，排除通用寒暄词表），避免直接优化"首次公开时刻 k*"导致奖励 filler。

【阶段一：SFT 的蕴含对齐交错监督】由标准 (x,r,a) 三元组构造交错序列 S=(x, r⁽¹⁾,a⁽¹⁾,…,r⁽ᴺ⁾,a⁽ᴺ⁾,[r⁽ᴺ⁺¹⁾])：(1) 用固定分隔符 "\n\n" 确定性切分推理与回答（曾尝试 LLM 学习式切分，因开销与级联错误被弃用），并规范化空白；(2) 用蕴含判定器（GPT-OSS-120B 作为 "Response Coverage Decider"，含 No-New-Derivation 约束以防"幻觉式蕴含"）为每个推理前缀 R_{1:k} 计算对齐边界 ℓ_k = 被该推理前缀支持的最大回答前缀索引 A_{1:ℓ_k}，并施加单调性修正 ℓ_k←max(ℓ_{k-1},ℓ̃_k)，末位强制 ℓ_{K_R}=K_A 作终局保险；(3) 仅当 ℓ_k 增大时释放新增回答增量 ΔA_k，答案未解锁时合并相邻推理块以减少碎片化；若答案提前被完全支持，则把剩余推理作为尾部推理块 r_trail 附加，保留"解出后自我检查"行为。工程优化：异步乐观并行地对所有推理前缀做蕴含检查、后处理强制单调性修复、返回全覆盖时激进取消剩余任务以省算力。

【阶段二：RL 恢复推理性能】SFT 造成分布迁移（推理不再是单一连续跨度），带来"对齐税"：30B-A3B 从 76.9% 掉到 50.8%。因此用 GRPO 训练：后 SFT 模型作参考策略，按 G=16 采样带标签的 rollout，剥离 R 标签得到可见回答，用精确答案匹配得到二值正确性标签；默认奖励仅用结果正确性（无结构性激励），并过滤全对/全错的退化组以稳定训练；KL 未启用。另研究可选的"保正确性"粒度塑形：以最大 think 块长度 ℓ_i 归一化得结构分 S_i，再解一个凸二次规划 min Σ(R_i−S_i)²，约束正确样本 R_i≥R̄+ε、错误样本 R_i≤R̄−ε（ε=0.5），在保持正确性符号的前提下按粒度排序。

Result: 【延迟指标】定义 ARI（所有 speak token 平均位置）、ABO（每个 speak 块首 token 位置均值）、AIRW（相邻 speak 块之间 think 跨度的平均 token 数），均基于完整生成序列计算，是用户感知等待的 token 级代理。

【AIME25 / GPQA-Diamond 主结果】Qwen3-4B：Standard CoT RL Final 73.8%/AIRW 21,316，GPQA 仅 19.0%；SxS Interleaved RL Final 达 80.0%/AIRW 8,519，GPQA 49.3%。Qwen3-30B-A3B：CoT RL Final AIME25 80.6%/AIRW 16,709、GPQA 51.4%；SxS RL Final 79.2%/AIRW 13,829、GPQA 57.1%。SxS-SFT 阶段延迟最优（如 4B AIRW 8,002/ARI）但精度塌陷（38.3%），RL 呈"先降后升"的恢复曲线（交错模型额外训练 120 步），恢复后仍保留交错结构、未坍缩回一次性 CoT。

【OOD 与灾难性遗忘】数学域偏置的 RL 使 Standard CoT 在 GPQA 上严重遗忘（4B 55.9%→19.0%），SxS 保留 49.3%，作者推测双通道监督把中间推理与可见承诺耦合、抑制了与最终答案脱节的"奖励黑客式"理由，但明确表示未直接测量该机制。

【LCB / KOR-Bench】SxS RL Final 全面优于 Standard CoT RL Final：4B LCB 39.62 vs 39.34 且 AIRW 12,579→9,631；4B KOR-Bench 32.96 vs 19.52 且 AIRW 1,681→1,321；30B LCB 54.60 vs 54.79、AIRW 10,401→9,270，KOR 31.76 vs 32.08、AIRW 1,429→1,304。

【粒度可控性消融】仅用结果奖励的 RL 使平均推理块数从 6.0 降至 3.7，但未坍缩为单一整块；加入 QP 塑形后块数升至 7.5，证明交错粒度可控，代价是精度收敛变慢。

【总体结论】SxS 的收益集中在"更新间隔(AIRW)"而非均匀前移全部 token（ARI/ABO 变化较小），即它主要改变披露的节奏而非内容分布；总 token 仅有小幅开销（标签与切换）。绝对精度上 base/post-trained 模型仍最强，核心主张是精度–延迟 Pareto 权衡的改善。

Conclusion: 本文把单流 CoT 的"何时说话"问题形式化为标准自回归解码内的序贯控制问题，交付了 SxS Interleaved Reasoning：以 think/speak 标签实现可见性可控，双通道同上下文、无需架构改动或额外模块；用蕴含对齐的交错轨迹把"更早"与"有支撑"绑定，用 SFT 学格式、GRPO 恢复精度。实验显示在两种 Qwen3 架构/规模、四类基准上一致改善准确率–内容延迟 Pareto 权衡，尤其显著压缩可见更新之间的等待，同时对域外保持更好的鲁棒性（较少灾难性遗忘），说明响应节奏可以作为稳定、可学习的策略维度而非靠启发式模板。作者指出结论仍属经验性：核心机制（为何降低遗忘、避免 filler）未被直接度量，延迟仅为 token 级代理而非真实墙钟/用户感知延迟，且 RL 阶段默认奖励中并未显式包含延迟项，λ·L_lat(g(τ)) 的完整实例化被留作后续工作。此外存在"回答先于推理细节出现"导致交错退化为常规 reasoning–response 结构的情形（作者未加约束，留作未来方向）。

Abstract: In single-stream autoregressive interfaces, the same tokens both update the model state and constitute an irreversible public commitment. This coupling creates a \emph{silence tax}: additional deliberation postpones the first \emph{task-relevant} content, while naive early streaming risks premature commitments that bias subsequent generations. We introduce \textbf{\emph{Side-by-Side (SxS)}} Interleaved Reasoning, which makes \emph{disclosure timing} a controllable decision within standard autoregressive generation. SxS interleaves partial disclosures with continued private reasoning in the same context, but releases content only when it is \emph{supported} by the reasoning so far. To learn such pacing without incentivizing filler, we construct entailment-aligned interleaved trajectories by matching answer prefixes to supporting reasoning prefixes, then train with SFT to acquire the dual-action semantics and RL to recover reasoning performance under the new format. Across two Qwen3 architectures/scales (MoE \textbf{Qwen3-30B-A3B}, dense \textbf{Qwen3-4B}) and both in-domain (AIME25) and out-of-domain (GPQA-Diamond) benchmarks, SxS improves accuracy--\emph{content-latency} Pareto trade-offs under token-level proxies (e.g., inter-update waiting).

</details>


### [17] [From prompting to evidence-based translation: A RAG+prompt system for Japanese-Chinese translation and its pedagogical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03387)
*Wenshi Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种检索增强生成(RAG)+Prompt的日汉翻译系统，针对包含名词修饰语从句(NMCCs)的句子进行优化。系统通过整合语言学分析、嵌入检索、提示构建和LLM生成，在不修改基础模型的情况下提升翻译质量。实验表明，当知识库规模从0扩展至2,000条时，BLEU分数从24.28提升至29.96，提升幅度达23.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在资源丰富的语言对翻译上表现良好，但对日汉语句子（特别是包含名词修饰语从句NMCCs的句子）翻译可靠性较差。NMCCs涉及复杂的嵌套结构、词汇选择、语序和语体等问题，给机器翻译带来挑战。

Method: 研究构建了一个RAG+Prompt翻译系统，包含分析模块（输出A1内外NMCC分类和A2风险预测）和检索模块（基于L2距离检索top-5相似日汉例句）。使用GPT-4o作为基础模型，在66句测试集上比较了六种知识库规模（0/100/200/500/1,000/2,000）的翻译效果。评估指标为字符级宏平均句子BLEU（1-4-gram含简短惩罚）。

Result: 知识库规模与翻译质量呈正相关。禁用RAG（0条）时BLEU为24.28，使用2,000条知识库时提升至29.96，绝对增幅+5.68，相对提升23.4%。所有规模下趋势一致，规模越大分数越高。

Conclusion: RAG+Prompt系统能够以可解释、可审计的方式改善日汉NMCC句子的翻译质量。研究局限性包括仅使用单一基础模型和单一评估指标，未来工作将扩展到更多语体、语言对和评估维度。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on high-resource pairs but are less reliable for Japanese-Chinese sentences containing noun-modifying clause constructions (NMCCs). This study evaluates a retrieval-augmented generation RAG+Prompt translation system that integrates linguistic analysis, embedding-based retrieval, prompt construction, and LLM generation without modifying the base model. The analysis module outputs A1 (inner vs. outer NMCC) and A2 (risk predictions: lexical choice/NMCC handling/word order/style/register); top-k = 5 similar Ja-Zh examples (L2 distance) and A1/A2 are inserted into an enhanced prompt. Using GPT-4o and a 66-sentence test set, we compare six knowledge-base sizes (0/100/200/500/1,000/2,000). Macro-averaged sentence-level BLEU (1-4-gram with brevity penalty; cased; Chinese at the character level) is the sole metric. Mean BLEU increases from 24.28 at 0 (RAG disabled) to 29.96 at 2,000 (+5.68; +23.4%). The upward trend holds across sizes, with larger knowledge bases yielding higher scores. We conclude that the RAG+Prompt translation system improves Ja-Zh translation of sentences containing NMCCs in an interpretable and auditable manner. Limitations include one base model, one metric, and reliance on published texts and commercial APIs; future work will broaden genres, language pairs, and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [18] [Geolocating News about Extreme Climate Events: A Comparative Analysis of Off-the-Shelf Tools for Toponym Identification in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03414)
*Brielen Madureira,Mariana Madruga de Brito,Andreas Niekler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对比了三种NER工具（Flair、Spacy、Stanza）在德语新闻文章中识别极端气候事件地点的表现，评估其对下游地理定位任务的影响。研究表明不同NER工具的选择会显著影响事件国家判断，进而影响媒体显著性分析结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端气候事件和灾害的地理位置确定是气候影响与适应研究中的常见问题，NER工具用于识别地名作为候选事件位置，但不同工具的表现差异尚不明确。

Method: 对三种现成NER工具（Flair、Spacy、Stanza）进行对比分析，基于德语新闻文章语料，通过三种方法来评估工具识别事件发生国家的能力，量化其输出差异。

Result: 三个NER工具的输出存在显著差异，这些差异会传播到下游任务，导致对文档地理焦点的不同判断，最终影响各国在德国媒体中显著性的结论。

Conclusion: NER工具的选择会影响地理焦点判断，进而影响对各国在德国媒体中显著性的结论。不同NER工具的输出差异会传播到下游任务，导致不同的地理定位决策。

Abstract: Determining the geolocation of extreme climate events and disasters in texts is a common problem in climate impact and adaptation research. Named-entity recognition (NER) tools are typically used to identify a pool of toponyms that serve as candidate event locations. In this study, we conduct a comparative analysis of three off-the-shelf NER tools, namely Flair, Spacy and Stanza. We describe and quantify differences between their outputs for German news articles and evaluate them extrinsically based on three methods to determine the country where events took place. We show how their contrasts are propagated into downstream tasks and can yield distinct decisions about a document's geographical focus, which, in turn, can impact conclusions about countries' prominence in German media.

</details>


### [19] [Benchmarking Logistic Regression, SVM, Naive Bayes, and IndoBERT Fine-Tuning for Sentiment Analysis on Indonesian Product Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03439)
*Nabila Zakiyah Zahra,Salwa Farhanatussaidah,Nasywa Nur Afifah,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在Tokopedia产品评论数据集上进行三分类情感分析(正/中/负)，对比传统ML方法(TF-IDF+LogisticRegression/SVM/NaiveBayes)与IndoBERT模型。结果显示Linear SVM以97.60%准确率显著优于IndoBERT(88.70%)，但性能差距主要源于实验数据采样不一致。该pipeline已部署为Gradio交互式Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印尼电商平台的快速增长产生了大量用户产品评论，需要大规模分析用户情感以衡量客户满意度并识别产品问题。现有研究在处理多语言(印尼语)电商评论的三分类情感分析任务上仍存在挑战。

Method: 传统机器学习方法采用TF-IDF特征提取结合三种分类算法(Logistic Regression、Linear SVM、Multinomial Naive Bayes)；Transformer方法使用IndoBERT-base-p1进行微调序列分类。针对数据不平衡问题，传统模型采用平衡类权重策略，Transformer模型则设计自定义加权交叉熵损失函数。评估指标包括Accuracy、Macro F1-score和Weighted F1-score。

Result: Linear SVM模型获得最佳性能，准确率达97.60%，Macro F1为0.5510，Weighted F1为0.9504。IndoBERT模型准确率为88.70%，Macro F1为0.5088。传统机器学习方法在此场景下优于深度学习Transformer模型。

Conclusion: Linear SVM结合TF-IDF特征在准确率(97.60%)和Macro F1(0.5510)上均优于IndoBERT模型(88.70%, 0.5088)。性能差距主要源于实验设置中的数据采样不一致问题。研究还通过Gradio部署了交互式Web应用，展示了该技术在电商平台实际应用的可行性。

Abstract: The exponential growth of e-commerce platforms in Indonesia has generated a massive volume of user-generated product reviews. Analyzing the sentiment of these reviews is critical for measuring customer satisfaction and identifying product issues at scale. This paper benchmarks traditional Machine Learning (ML) approaches against a Transformer-based Deep Learning model for a three-class sentiment analysis task (positive, neutral, negative) on the Tokopedia Product Reviews 2025 dataset. We implemented Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) feature extraction coupled with three algorithms: Logistic Regression, Linear Support Vector Machine (SVM), and Multinomial Naive Bayes as robust baselines. Subsequently, we fine-tuned the IndoBERT model (indobenchmark/indobert-base-p1) for contextual sequence classification. To computationally address the severe class imbalance inherent in e-commerce feedback, we applied balanced class weights for the baseline models and engineered a custom weighted cross-entropy loss function within the IndoBERT training loop, following the broader motivation of imbalanced-learning research. Our comprehensive evaluation using Accuracy, Macro F1-score, and Weighted F1-score revealed that the traditional Linear SVC model significantly outperformed the IndoBERT model in our experimental setup, achieving an Accuracy of 97.60% and a Macro F1-score of 0.5510, compared to IndoBERT's 88.70% and 0.5088. Detailed analysis indicates that this performance gap was primarily driven by discrepancies in the data sampling regimes, where baselines utilized the full corpus while the Transformer was constrained to a sampled subset. Finally, we demonstrate the practical viability of our pipeline by deploying the final sentiment classification model as an interactive Gradio web application.

</details>


### [20] [A Comparison of Traditional Machine Learning Algorithms and LSTM-Based Deep Learning Models for Email Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03440)
*Virdio Samuel Saragih,Baruna Abirawa,Kartini Lovian Simbolon,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Traditional ML models (SVM, Logistic Regression, Naive Bayes) and LSTM were compared for email classification using Word2Vec embeddings. SVM with linear kernel achieved 98.74% accuracy and best efficiency-accuracy trade-off, while LSTM excelled in recall but demanded more computational resources.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rapid growth of electronic communication necessitates more robust systems for email classification and sentiment detection.

Method: Word2Vec embeddings for feature representation; comparative evaluation using confusion matrices

Result: SVM achieves highest efficiency and accuracy (98.74%); LSTM has exceptional recall for spam-related sentiments but requires significantly more computational time; traditional classifiers remain highly robust for dense vector spaces.

Conclusion: SVM with linear kernel offers the most optimal balance between predictive precision and processing speed for email classification tasks, providing critical insights for developing high-performance automated email filtering systems.

Abstract: The rapid growth of electronic communication has necessitated more robust systems for email classification and sentiment detection. This study presents a comparative performance analysis between traditional machine learning algorithms and deep learning architectures, specifically focusing on Support Vector Machines (SVMs), Logistic Regression, Naive Bayes, and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM). Utilizing Word2Vec embeddings for feature representation, our experimental results indicate that the SVM model with a linear kernel achieves the highest efficiency and accuracy, reaching a peak performance of 98.74%. While the LSTM model demonstrates exceptional recall capabilities in detecting spam-related sentiments, it requires significantly more computational time compared to discriminative statistical models. Detailed evaluations via confusion matrices further reveal that traditional classifiers remain highly robust for dense vector spaces. This research concludes that for email detection tasks, SVM offers the most optimal balance between predictive precision and processing speed. These findings provide critical insights for developing high-performance automated email filtering systems in professional and academic environments.

</details>


### [21] [Sentiment Analysis of Indonesian Spotify Reviews Using Machine Learning and BiLSTM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03443)
*Uliano Wilyam Purba,Andre Hadiman Rotua Parhusip,Sahid Maulana,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对印度尼西亚语 Spotify 应用商店评论的三分类情感分析（正向/中性/负向），在同一套预处理流程下对比三种传统机器学习模型（SVM、Multinomial Naive Bayes、Decision Tree，均基于 TF-IDF + SMOTE）与一个两层 BiLSTM（PyTorch）。结果表明：传统模型中决策树最优（加权 F1 = 0.7269），BiLSTM 在加权指标上整体更优（加权 F1 = 0.8069、准确率 0.8314），但对占 5.9% 的少数类"中性"完全失效（F1 = 0.000）；因此结论是"整体极性判别用 BiLSTM，三类均衡识别用 ML + SMOTE"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用商店评论能规模化反映用户满意度、投诉与功能诉求，但印尼语评论高度非正式、俚语丰富、拼写不一致，使情感分类更具挑战。作者指出，尚未见在共享预处理下对大规模印尼语 Spotify 评论做 Scikit-learn 传统模型与 PyTorch BiLSTM 的直接基准对比，因此希望提供：（1）统一的印尼语文本预处理管线；（2）三种传统分类器 + TF-IDF + SMOTE 的 5 折基准；（3）参数量仅约 109.6 万的轻量 BiLSTM 评估；（4）整体与分类别性能都透明呈现的对比，并通过 Hugging Face Spaces 公开部署模型以便复现与直接检验。

Method: 数据：使用 google-play-scraper 抓取 100,000 条印尼语 Spotify 评论（仅保留 Ulasan 与 Rating 列），按评分映射标签（1–2→Negatif，3→Netral，4–5→Positif），去重与去空后得 70,155 条（正 47,991 / 68.4%，负 18,026 / 25.7%，中 4,138 / 5.9%）。预处理（两条轨道共用）：小写化→正则去 URL/@提及/标点/数字→124 条俚语映射→809 个（含领域词）停用词→Sastrawi 词干还原；处理后中位长度 4 token、95 分位 17 token，故 max_len=17，清理后为空的评论被移除。机器学习轨道：TF-IDF（max_features=3000），每折训练集内施加 SMOTE，SVM（linear, C=1.0）、MNB（alpha=1.0）、DT（gini）在 70,155 条上做分层 5 折交叉验证，PyCaret 编排并以 macro F1 调参（n_iter=50）后 finalize_model()。深度学习轨道：词级分词，词表仅由训练划分构建（min_freq=2，3,285 词），样本按分层抽取 20,000 条（train/val/test = 14,219/2,510/2,953）；两层双向 LSTM（嵌入 128、每方向隐藏 128、dropout 0.5）+ 全连接头，参数量 1,096,579；Adam、lr=1e-3、batch=64、max_epochs=10、unweighted CrossEntropy、早停 patience=3（monitor val_loss），最佳检查点为 epoch 3。环境为纯 CPU（PyTorch 2.2.2+cpu），seed=42。评估指标包括准确率、加权/宏平均 P/R/F1、Cohen's Kappa，ML 另报 AUC。

Result: ML 5 折交叉验证：Decision Tree 最优（Acc 0.7286、加权 F1 0.7269、Kappa 0.5928、AUC 0.8055；分类别 F1：正 0.802、中 0.717、负 0.707，负/中混淆最严重），SVM 次之（Acc 0.6991、加权 F1 0.6958、Kappa 0.5487），MNB 最差（Acc 0.4948、加权 F1 0.4619、Kappa 0.2422），说明其特征独立假设对口语化印尼语过于受限。BiLSTM 在 2,953 条测试集上：Acc 0.8314、加权 P 0.7841、加权 R 0.8314、加权 F1 0.8069、Macro F1 仅 0.5478、测试损失 0.4735；分类别 F1 为正 0.9028、负 0.7406、中 0.0000（176 条中性样本无一被正确分类）。训练动态显示训练损失单调下降（0.5747→0.3046，6 轮）而验证损失在第 3 轮触底（0.4491，val_acc 0.8434）后回升，呈典型过拟合；验证准确率自第 2 轮起稳定在 0.83–0.84，说明 14,219 条样本已饱和模型表示能力。中性类崩塌归因于类别失衡且 DL 管线未做类别加权/过采样，加之中性语义本身与正/负模糊。

Conclusion: 加权指标上 BiLSTM 优于最佳传统模型（加权 F1 0.8069 vs 0.7269，准确率 0.8314 vs 0.7286），表明序列上下文对短印尼语评论确有增益；但宏平均 F1 上决策树（~0.72）远优于 BiLSTM（0.5478）。核心权衡在于类别均衡：BiLSTM 适合以正/负极性判别为主的整体情感检测，决策树 + SMOTE 更适合需要三类均衡识别的场景。作者明确承认局限：训练数据规模不对称（70,155 vs 14,219，约 4.9 倍）、DL 侧缺失类别平衡、ML 用交叉验证而 DL 用留出测试（评测设定不等价）、未使用预训练印尼语嵌入。因此该对比应解读为计算受限的实用基准，而非严格受控的架构消融。未来工作：在全量语料上训练 BiLSTM、在 DL 管线引入类别平衡、测试预训练印尼语嵌入或基于 Transformer 的模型（如 IndoBERT/IndoNLU 资源）。

Abstract: This paper benchmarks classical machine learning and deep learning approaches for three-class sentiment classification of Indonesian Spotify reviews. Using 100,000 scraped reviews and 70,155 cleaned samples, the study compares Support Vector Machine, Multinomial Naive Bayes, and Decision Tree models with a two-layer BiLSTM. Both approaches use the same preprocessing pipeline, including slang normalization, stopword removal, and stemming. Decision Tree achieves the best performance among the classical models, while BiLSTM attains the highest weighted F1-score overall but fails on the minority neutral class. The paper concludes that BiLSTM is stronger for overall sentiment detection, whereas machine learning with SMOTE provides more balanced three-class performance.

</details>


### [22] [An ERP Study of Recursive Possessive Parsing in ASD Children and Its Cognitive Neuro Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03447)
*Fu Chenxi,Wang Xiaoyi,Zhuang Ziman,Yang Caimei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该ERP研究探讨了自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）儿童处理普通话递归所有格结构的在线加工机制。与典型发育（TD）儿童相比，ASD儿童在P200（早期知觉加工）、N400（语义加工）和P600（句法再分析）上均表现出异常模式。结果表明，ASD儿童的词汇-语义加工相对保留，但递归结构所需的在线句法计算能力严重受损，支持语言模块化分离理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归结构是人类语言的核心属性，但关于ASD儿童如何处理复杂递归的研究甚少。该研究旨在揭示ASD儿童在递归结构加工中的认知神经机制，以区分其语言障碍中的保留与缺陷成分。

Method: 采用ERP技术，以汉语普通话两层级递归所有格结构为材料，通过句子-图片匹配范式，对比ASD儿童（n=12）与TD儿童（n=12）的在线加工过程。分析P200（150-250ms）、N400（300-500ms）和P600（500-1000ms）三个成分。

Result: ASD儿童P200振幅显著降低，且缺乏典型的后部语法效应，提示早期知觉加工异常；N400在两组均无显著违规效应，但ASD儿童呈反转的前部效应和减弱的后部效应；P600振幅显著降低、缺乏后部语法效应且潜伏期有延迟趋势，反映句法再分析的核心缺陷。

Conclusion: ASD儿童的词汇-语义加工相对保留，但递归结构所需的在线句法计算能力严重受损，支持语言模块化分离理论。该研究为理解ASD语言障碍的认知神经基础提供了重要证据。

Abstract: Recursive structures are a core property of human language, yet little is known about how children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD) process complex recursion. This ERP study investigated the online processing of two-level recursive possessive structures in Mandarin-speaking children with ASD (n = 12) compared to typically developing (TD) peers (n = 12) using a sentence-picture matching paradigm. ERPs were analyzed for P200 (150-250 ms), N400 (300-500 ms), and P600 (500-1000 ms). Results showed that ASD children exhibited significantly reduced P200 amplitudes and failed to show the typical posterior grammaticality effect, indicating atypical early perceptual processing. No robust N400 violation effect was observed in either group, confirming the mismatch was not a semantic anomaly; however, ASD children showed a reversed anterior effect and an attenuated posterior effect. For the P600, ASD children had significantly reduced amplitudes, no posterior grammaticality effect, and a trend toward delayed latency, reflecting a core deficit in syntactic reanalysis. These findings demonstrate that while lexical-semantic processing is relatively preserved in ASD, the online syntactic computation required for recursion is severely impaired, supporting modular dissociation accounts of language in autism.

</details>


### [23] [Retrieving Floods without Floodlights: Topic Models as Binary Classifiers for Extreme Climate Events in German News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03450)
*Brielen Madureira,Mariana Madruga de Brito,Andreas Niekler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究将主题模型作为二分类器用于极端气候事件新闻检索，发现可通过后验分布概率筛选相关文档，性能与微调嵌入模型相当但无需标注数据；LLM精确率最低；不同气候灾害需单独建模，不可统一处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法训练需要大量标注数据，而极端气候事件的新闻语料库往往缺乏足够的标注样本；主题模型虽具有无监督和可解释的优势，但通常仅用于探索性分析或数据表征，未被充分利用为分类器。

Method: 利用主题模型估计的后验分布概率进行文档筛选，无需修改模型的训练流程；同时使用关键词查询时的概率信息辅助选择相关主题。通过有标注样本引导评估，与微调文本嵌入分类器和开源LLM进行对比。

Result: 主题模型作为二分类器可有效筛选德国媒体中七类极端气候事件的相关新闻；使用关键词概率信息可改善样本精确率；结果表现出明显的灾害依赖性，LLM精确率最低。

Conclusion: 通过将主题模型从仅用于探索性分析提升为二分类器，在无需额外标注数据的情况下实现了可比较的检索性能；LLM虽然便捷但精确率最低；研究结果表明不同气候灾害需区别对待，不应将其视为统一类别。

Abstract: In studies of media coverage of extreme climate events, NLP methods have become indispensable for identifying relevant texts in large news databases. Still, enough annotated data to train accurate deep learning-based classifiers from scratch is often not available. Topic Models have the advantage of being both unsupervised and interpretable, but are typically used only for exploratory analysis or data characterisation. In this study, we investigate how to employ Topic Models as binary classifiers for refining the retrieval of relevant news about seven types of extreme climate events in the German media. Our method relies on the posterior distributions estimated by Topic Models to select relevant documents, without modifying their training procedure. Using an annotated sample to guide the evaluation, we show that the probabilities assigned to keywords used to query news databases can also be informative for selecting relevant topics and improve sample precision. We compare our results to a fine-tuned text embedding classifier and an open-weight LLM, discussing observed trade-offs, e.g. the LLM's lowest precision. Moreover, we show that results are hazard-dependent, which speaks against considering climate events as a single category in NLP tasks.

</details>


### [24] [Detecting Stealth Sycophancy in Mental-Health Dialogue with Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03472)
*Tianze Han,Beining Xu,Hanbo Zhang,Yongming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DESG（动态情感签名图谱），一种模型无关的对话质量评估器，通过解耦临床状态表示和非对称临床几何评分机制，在心理支持、咨询对话和危机干预三类场景中实现了显著优于现有方法的评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式AI治疗师在心理支持场景中的广泛应用，如何可靠地进行离线评估成为关键挑战。现有直接LLM评判器和对称文本相似度指标与治疗质量对齐度较差，因为目标标签取决于临床方向（调节/重构/维持/恶化）。

Method: DESG将对话窗口表示为解耦的临床状态，通过非对称临床几何进行评分。模型采用集成架构，在600窗口保留测试集上验证。

Result: DESG-Ensemble在600窗口保留测试集上达到0.9353宏观F1分数，分别超过ConcatANN 1.51个百分点、BERTScore 19.63个百分点、TRACT 33.81个百分点。

Conclusion: DESG通过临床状态流形作为主要判别基质，结合图谱轨迹分量提供非对称评分和可解释诊断，在保持模型无关性的同时实现了精确的对话质量评估。

Abstract: As conversational AI therapists are increasingly used in psychological support settings, reliable offline evaluation of therapeutic response quality remains an open problem. This paper studies multi-domain support-dialogue evaluation without relying on large language models as final judges. We use a direct LLM judge as a baseline that reads raw dialogue text and predicts whether the target response is harmful, productive, or neutral. We find that direct LLM judges and symmetric text-similarity metrics are poorly aligned with therapeutic quality because the target label depends on clinical direction: whether the response moves the user state toward regulation or reframing, leaves it broadly unchanged, or reinforces deterioration through higher risk affect or cognitive-distortion mass. To address this issue, we propose Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs (DESG), a model-agnostic evaluator that represents dialogue windows with decoupled clinical states and scores them using asymmetric clinical geometry. We evaluate DESG on a constructed diagnostic stress-test benchmark of 3{,}000 dialogue windows from EmpatheticDialogues, ESConv, and CRADLE-Dialogue, covering peer support, counseling dialogue, and crisis-oriented interaction. On the 600-window held-out test aggregate, DESG-Ensemble achieves 0.9353 macro-F1, exceeding ConcatANN by 1.51 percentage points, BERTScore by 19.63 points, and TRACT by 33.81 points. Feature ablations, artifact controls, a 100-window blinded adjudicator audit, and qualitative disagreement cases indicate that the clinical state manifold is the main discriminative substrate, while graph-based trajectory components provide asymmetric scoring and interpretable diagnostics rather than serving as the sole source of performance.

</details>


### [25] [CuraView: A Multi-Agent Framework for Medical Hallucination Detection with GraphRAG-Enhanced Knowledge Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03476)
*Severin Ye,Xiao Kong,Xiaopeng He,Guangsu Yan,Dongsuk Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CuraView通过GraphRAG知识图谱+多智能体闭环检测框架，有效解决LLM生成放电摘要中的忠实度幻觉问题，在250例患者数据集上验证安全关键E4指标F1达0.831，召回率90.9%，相比基线相对提升50%，同时生成可复用的标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放电摘要需从冗长电子健康记录中提取关键信息，人工处理耗时且LLM生成虽高效但易产生忠实度幻觉（与源记录矛盾的陈述），直接威胁患者安全。

Method: 提出CuraView多智能体框架，基于GraphRAG从患者EHR构建知识图谱，实现闭环生成-检测流程。框架包含句子级证据检索和分类模块，将证据划分为E1-E4四个等级（从强支持到直接矛盾），输出结构化可解释的证据链用于解释检测结果。

Result: 在250例患者（50例测试）的Discharge-Me基准上，微调Qwen3-14B模型在E4指标达F1=0.831（召回率90.9%，精确率76.5%），E3+E4的F1为0.823，相比基线模型相对提升50%，优于RAGTruth和QAGS方法。

Conclusion: 证据链图检索验证显著提升了临床文档的事实可靠性，同时生成了可用于下游模型训练和蒸馏的可复用标注数据集。实验证明基于GraphRAG的知识图谱结合多智能体框架能够有效检测放电摘要中的忠实度幻觉问题，Qwen3-14B微调模型在安全关键的E4指标上F1达0.831，相对基线提升50%。

Abstract: Discharge summaries require extracting critical information from lengthy electronic health records (EHRs), a process that is labor-intensive when performed manually. Large language models (LLMs) can improve generation efficiency; however, they are prone to producing faithfulness hallucinations, statements that contradict source records, posing direct risks to patient safety. To address this, we present CuraView, a multi-agent framework for sentence-level detection and evidence-grounded explanation of faithfulness hallucinations in discharge summaries. CuraView constructs a GraphRAG-based knowledge graph from patient-level EHRs and implements a closed-loop generation-detection pipeline with sentence-level evidence retrieval and classification spanning four evidence grades from strong support to direct contradiction (E1-E4), yielding structured and interpretable evidence chains.
  We evaluate CuraView on a subset of 250 patients from the Discharge-Me benchmark, with 50 patients held out for testing. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-14B detection model achieves an F1 of 0.831 on the safety-critical E4 metric (90.9% recall, 76.5% precision) and an F1 of 0.823 on E3+E4, representing a 50.0% relative improvement over the base model and outperforming RAGTruth-style and QAGS-style baselines. These results demonstrate that evidence-chain-based graph retrieval verification substantially improves the factual reliability of clinical documentation, while simultaneously producing reusable annotated datasets for downstream model training and distillation.

</details>


### [26] [Rational Communication Shapes Morphological Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03510)
*Fengyuan Yang,Yongqian Peng,Yuxi Ma,Chenheng Xu,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文用 RSA（RSA）框架把形态构词建模为"说话者在历史某一时刻从当时可用的语素组合中做选择"的理性沟通博弈：在 4323 个英语复合词与派生词（1820–2019，源自 WordNet + COHA/COCA）上，训练"语义可恢复性 × 生产成本"的排序模型，结果显示有实证的语素序列系统性排在同期生成但未实证的替代候选之上；结合语义与成本的模型在 MRR 与 Acc@k 上优于纯语义和纯成本基线，且 S1 相对纯语义的优势随候选集扩大而增大（成本 alone MRR=0.031，语义 0.047，S1 0.050–0.053，非线性 S1 Acc@10=12.08% vs 语义 11.10%；无约束的判别式模型最强 MRR=0.096）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究（形态生产力、类比模型、信息论效率模型如 Xu et al. 2024）解释了哪些构词模式可用或被偏好、哪些引入形式被采纳，但都没有回答"当多个同样可行的语素序列能为同一概念命名时，语言最终为何选择了其中一个"这一候选层面（candidate-level）的问题。RSA 提供显式的候选比较结构：S1 说话者在多个形式间权衡听者可恢复性与自身生产成本，产生候选排序分布。作者因此提出：形态组合可能是"表达性—经济性"沟通权衡的产物，即词汇化反映可恢复性与生产成本的系统性取舍。

Method: （1）数据：WordNet 中 4323 个英语复合词与派生词（1820–2019 首现），MorphSeg 切分金标准语素序列，词元/音节数取自 CMU Pronouncing Dictionary；语素可得性由 COHA 十年累计型/词频与 COCA 年度统计量化；时间索引词库 L_t 记录词义、历史频率、音系与形式特征。（2）候选集 C(c,t)：由 WordNet 同义词集词元、关系邻居与分时代训练的 word2vec（COHA 按十年、COCA 按年）近邻组合，最多枚举 3 个语素，并用语义门控 g(u,c) 限制在目标释义 kNN 邻域内以控制搜索空间。（3）语义模型：用 Qwen3-Embedding-8B 嵌入释义与语素，对每个候选用"语素—概念余弦相似度分布"的五个统计量（均值、最大值、标准差、熵、整体相似度）输入三层 MLP f_θ。（4）成本模型：每个语素取八个时间索引特征（字符长度、累计型频/词频、30 年词频、独立词频及 30 年独立词频、音素数、音节数），经两层 MLP h_ϕ 得标量成本并跨语素可加求和。（5）S1 模型：二阶段建模，冻结独立训练的 Cost 与 Semantic 基线打分（批归一化后由线性 ψ 或两层 MLP 融合），对应 U = α·f̃_θ − β·Cost + b 与非线性交互版本。（6）训练：pairwise softplus 排序损失 + 课程式难负例挖掘（语义相近、与金标准重叠者）、Adam、梯度裁剪、早停；按年份分层留出（每年 20% 测试，5 折轮转，864 条测试项），在最多 1024 个候选中排序，报告 MRR 与 Acc@k；另做累积时间窗（截止 1830–1910）的时间稳健性检验。

Result: 有实证形式系统性地排在同时期可用的未实证替代之上。四类模型中：纯成本最弱（MRR=0.031），纯语义较强（0.047），S1 两变体进一步提升（0.050–0.053；非线性 S1 Acc@10=12.08%、Acc@20=20.94%，优于纯语义的 11.10%/19.19%），判别式（13 维特征拼接 MLP）最佳（MRR=0.096），说明存在超出"两标量"RSA 分解的额外结构。S1 相对纯语义的优势随 k 增大而扩大，正好对应"语义不足以定夺、需成本在大量意义邻近的候选中筛选"的区域；纯语义预测长度最长（过度生成冗长/冗余形式，如 cynic+cynic、person+fiance），纯成本偏好短高频但语义空洞的片段（ed+ing 作 saucepan），S1 更平衡（laundry 排第 1，affiliated、fiancée 排第 2）。线性 S1 中语义权重与成本系数均为正（α=0.38，β_score=0.48，比值≈1.28），确认两种压力同时起作用。各时间窗下模型排序稳定。

Conclusion: 形态组合可被建模为历史词汇库约束下的理性沟通优化：语言倾向于选择既对听者可恢复、又对说话者经济的语素组合，将理性沟通研究从话语层面推进到词的内部结构。作者给出保守版本的结论——效果显著但幅度适中（历史数据噪声大、候选集巨大），且判别式模型的优势表明形态选择依赖"两项效用"之外的额外结构。局限与展望：候选被视为无序集合（未建模形态序位、词头结构、语素顺序约束）；语义打分依赖当代 Qwen3 嵌入且以首次出现近似概念涌现（词汇化滞后）；仅覆盖英语，需扩展到形态类型多样的语言；未来可联合微调语义与成本分量、引入形态音位约束、让成本依赖语境信息量，并用行为实验检验个体说话者是否呈现同一权衡。

Abstract: Human languages expand vocabularies by combining existing morphemes rather than inventing arbitrary forms. Communicative efficiency shapes lexical systems at multiple levels (Gibson et al., 2019), yet morphological composition -- combining morphemes through compounding or affixation -- has rarely been modeled as a historically situated speaker choice among competing morpheme sequences, leaving unanswered why a language settles on one morpheme combination over other plausible alternatives. We ask whether a trade-off between listener recoverability and speaker production cost can predict attested compositions over contemporaneously available alternatives. Here we show, within the Rational Speech Act (RSA) framework (Frank & Goodman, 2012; Goodman & Frank, 2016) using a time-indexed lexicon constructed from Corpus of Historical American English (COHA) and Corpus of Contemporary American English (COCA), that across 4323 naturally occurring English compounds and derivations spanning 1820--2019, attested compositions are systematically ranked above unattested alternatives generated from contemporaneously available morphemes. Models integrating semantic informativeness with production cost outperform semantic-only and cost-only baselines on Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) and top-k accuracy (Acc@k), with the advantage of the Pragmatic Speaker model ($S_1$) over the semantic-only baseline growing as the candidate set expands, where meaning alone leaves morphological choice underdetermined. These findings suggest that lexicalization reflects a communicative trade-off between expressiveness and efficiency, extending rational accounts of communication from utterance-level choice to the internal structure of words.

</details>


### [27] [Revisiting Graph-Tokenizing Large Language Models: A Systematic Evaluation of Graph Token Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03514)
*Zhongjian Zhang,Yue Yu,Mengmei Zhang,Junping Du,Xiao Wang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑GTokenLLMs是否真正理解图tokens，提出GTEval评估框架进行验证，发现现有方法在图token理解上存在显著缺陷，指令微调无法完全解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用于各类图任务，其中GTokenLLMs范式将图数据压缩为图tokens并作为前缀查询LLM。然而作者质疑这种范式是否真正让LLM有效理解图tokens。

Method: 提出GTEval评估框架，通过格式级和内容级的指令转换来评估GTokenLLMs对图tokens的理解能力，涵盖6个代表性模型的广泛实验。

Result: 现有GTokenLLMs无法完全理解图tokens，表现出对指令变化的过度敏感或过度不敏感，强烈依赖文本推理；图tokens虽保留任务相关信息并被各层关注，但利用程度因模型和指令变体而异。

Conclusion: GTokenLLMs在图-token理解方面存在显著不足，现有的指令微调方法无法完全解决这一问题，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has motivated researchers to adapt them as universal predictors for various graph tasks. As a widely recognized paradigm, Graph-Tokenizing LLMs (GTokenLLMs) compress complex graph data into graph tokens and treat them as prefix tokens for querying LLMs, leading many to believe that LLMs can understand graphs more effectively and efficiently. In this paper, we challenge this belief: \textit{Do GTokenLLMs fully understand graph tokens in the natural-language embedding space?} Motivated by this question, we formalize a unified framework for GTokenLLMs and propose an evaluation pipeline, \textbf{GTEval}, to assess graph-token understanding via instruction transformations at the format and content levels. We conduct extensive experiments on 6 representative GTokenLLMs with GTEval. The primary findings are as follows: (1) Existing GTokenLLMs do not fully understand graph tokens. They exhibit over-sensitivity or over-insensitivity to instruction changes, and rely heavily on text for reasoning; (2) Although graph tokens preserve task-relevant graph information and receive attention across LLM layers, their utilization varies across models and instruction variants; (3) Additional instruction tuning can improve performance on the original and seen instructions, but it does not fully address the challenge of graph-token understanding, calling for further improvement.

</details>


### [28] [PatRe: A Full-Stage Office Action and Rebuttal Generation Benchmark for Patent Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03571)
*Qiyao Wang,Xinyi Chen,Longze Chen,Hongbo Wang,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Yuan Lin,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PatRe是首个建模完整专利审查生命周期的基准，包含Office Action生成和申请人反驳，采用480个真实案例，支持多轮动态交互评估。该工作揭示了LLM在专利审查场景中的潜力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将专利审查仅视为判别分类或静态提取任务，未能捕捉其内在的交互性和迭代性特征，这与学术出版中的同行评审和 rebuttal 过程类似。

Method: 收集480个真实案例，支持神谕评估和检索模拟两种评估设置，将专利审查重新定义为动态多轮交互过程，包含审查员分析生成和申请人反驳两个核心任务。

Result: 实验揭示了关键洞察：闭源与开源模型表现存在差异，审查员分析与申请人反驳任务间存在不对称性。

Conclusion: 该基准推动了专利审查建模研究，为评估LLM在复杂法律推理和技术新颖性判断中的能力提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: Patent examination is a complex, multi-stage process requiring both technical expertise and legal reasoning, increasingly challenged by rising application volumes. Prior benchmarks predominantly view patent examination as discriminative classification or static extraction, failing to capture its inherently interactive and iterative nature, similar to the peer review and rebuttal process in academic publishing. In this paper, we introduce PatRe, the first benchmark that models the full patent examination lifecycle, including Office Action generation and applicant rebuttal. PatRe comprises 480 real-world cases and supports both oracle and retrieval-simulated evaluation settings. Our benchmark reframes patent examination as a dynamic, multi-turn process of justification and response. Extensive experiments across various LLMs reveal critical insights into model performance, including differences between proprietary and open-source models, as well as task asymmetries between examiner analysis and applicant-side rebuttal. These findings highlight both the potential and current limitations of LLMs in modeling complex, real-world legal reasoning and technical novelty judgment in patent examination. We release our code and dataset to facilitate future research on patent examination modeling.

</details>


### [29] [AfriVox-v2: A Domain-Verticalized Benchmark for In-the-Wild African Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03590)
*Busayo Awobade,Gabrial Zencha Ashungafac,Tobi Olatunji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AfriVox-v2 是一个面向非洲语言的"领域垂直化"语音识别基准：在 AfriVox-v1（朗读语音）基础上，新增 7 语种、10 小时的 Intron-YT 野外即兴语音语料，整合 AFN、Waxal 等对话语料，定义 10 个应用领域（含数字、专有名实体子集），并在统一条件下评测 Sahara-v2、Gemini-3 Flash 与 Omni-CTC（300M/1B/7B）。结果显示：地区化训练的 Sahara-v2 在 AfriVox-v2 上以 23.78% 平均 WER 领先，Omni-CTC 呈现清晰规模效应（39.20→33.91→32.20），Gemini-3 Flash 落后（32.13%）；电信、体育等垂直领域及数字、实体转写仍是主要失效点，暴露了现有模型在真实非洲部署场景中的泛化缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言在全球语音识别基准中长期严重欠代表：尽管 Whisper 等多语模型在英语标准测试集上 WER 低于 5%，但在非洲口音上性能下降 5–10 倍，在本土语言上输出甚至近乎不可懂（WER>100%）。作者指出既有基准存在三大缺陷：①"朗读语音偏置"——主流数据集为脚本化朗读语音，无法反映真实环境中的自发语音、背景噪声与重叠语音；②领域覆盖表面化——农业、金融、电信等垂直行业涉及大量专业术语、数字与命名实体，通用模型失效严重；③模型谱系过时——Omni-ASR 系列与 Gemini 3 等新一代模型未纳入评测，既有排名已不代表技术前沿。因此需要一个生态效度更高（in-the-wild）、领域粒度更细、模型画像更新的基准，以指导本地化语音 AI 的工程选型与部署。
