Method: 语料构建：(1) 新建 Intron-YT 语料库——从播客、访谈、公共录音等开放许可多媒体中，由人工标注员在非洲众包平台按主导语言挑选内容，用 VAD 切分并拼接为最长 30 秒话段，由母语者逐字转写（保留口语现象），标注员为 18–35 岁大学及以上双语者，按任务复杂度付酬 10–50 美元/小时；质检采用两级流程（母语者初标 + 研究生级标注者对每位贡献者 10–20% 样本独立复审，准确率低于 80% 者被剔除），覆盖 7 语种约 10 小时。(2) 聚合既有对话语料：AFN（盖茨基金资助，9k+ 小时，15 语种；无官方划分的子集按说话人分层做 80/10/10 训练/开发/测试切分以防说话人泄漏）、Waxal（Google 资助，6 语种约 69.5 小时），并保留 Common Voice、FLEURS、NCHLT 等朗读语料以维持与旧基准可比性；质量过滤采用"用表现最好的模型筛出 WER 异常高的样本 + 人工听音与转写校验"策略，保留约 90% 样本，全部音频统一重采样至 16 kHz。领域垂直化：建立 10 域分类体系（农业、文化与社会、金融、综合、健康、体育与爱好、电信、交通运输、命名实体、数字），因源数据标签不全，采用 Gemini-3 进行多标签自动标注，并对每语种每标签抽取约 50 条做人工校验（重点覆盖句数最多的 6 语种，各 500–800 条），得到 70% 召回、42% 精确率。评测对象与协议：Gemini-3-Flash（多模态语音 LLM）、Sahara-v2（区域优化 ASR，Intron Health 出品）、Omni-ASR v2 CTC 300M/1B/7B（推理速度更快）；全部使用各维护方默认预处理与超参、支持时传入语言提示以减少跨语言混淆；指标为 WER，并新增实体错误率 EWER（限制在含命名实体样本上的 WER）与数字错误率 NWER（限制在含数字样本上的 WER），同时报告域标签分布、总体 WER、域条件 WER 及语言×域细粒度结果。

Result: 1) AfriVox-v2（自发野外语音）普遍比 AfriVox-v1（朗读语音）更难，但差异非均匀：Kinyarwanda、Swahili 等语言部分模型在新基准上 WER 反而更低（提示训练数据重叠/域相似性），而 Yoruba、Amharic 等声学变异大或训练覆盖不足的语言退化更明显，说明基准成绩可能反映训练数据暴露而非泛化能力。2) 模型排名（AfriVox-v2 平均 WER）：Sahara-v2 最优 23.78，Gemini-3 Flash 32.13，Omni-CTC 7B 32.20、1B 33.91、300M 39.20——CTC 系列呈明确规模效应，但多模态 LLM 落后于语音原生 ASR 模型，反映语义优化架构在精确声学转写上的短板；区域化训练可胜过更大规模的通用多语模型。3) 域条件性能差异显著：电信与体育领域错误率最高（多数系统 >30–35%），因专业术语、缩写与命名实体在通用语料中代表性不足；即使最强模型 Sahara-v2，数字（20.32%）与命名实体（23.11）仍是主要失效模式。4) 语言资源水平与域敏感性叠加：高资源语言 Kinyarwanda、Swahili 跨域稳定在 8–14% WER，低资源语言 Fulani 波动达 34–59%；Ga、French 等 0.0% WER 单元格反映数据稀疏而非模型能力；Pedi 在电信域异常高达 50.0%。5) 部署启示：总体 WER 会掩盖域级差异，实体与数字错误可引发金额、标识符、专名等下游严重错误。

Conclusion: AfriVox-v2 通过引入野外即兴语音、整合 20+ 语种对话语料与严格的领域垂直化评测，揭示了被传统"朗读语音 + 平均 WER"基准掩盖的现代 ASR 真实局限：模型在垂直领域术语、自发对话及语言多样性上仍显薄弱；三项核心洞见是——聚合指标掩盖域级差异、实体与数字是主要错误源、区域化数据训练的模型优于更大的全球通用模型。该基准为构建者提供选型与优化的可靠蓝图，并推动更具包容性的非洲语音 AI 研究。局限：仅覆盖非洲语言多样性的一小部分，部分语种的对话语音量小、细粒度分析统计效力有限；域标注部分依赖 LLM 辅助（42% 精确率、70% 召回率），域级结论应视为趋势性指示而非精确性能估计；未来需构建全人工标注的领域语料并把校验扩展到所有支持语言。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) show strong speech recognition and translation capabilities for high-resource languages. However, African languages remain dramatically underrepresented in benchmarks, limiting their practical use in low-resource settings. While early benchmarks tested African languages and accents, they lacked exhaustive real-world noise and granular domain evaluations. We present AfriVox-v2, a comprehensive benchmark designed to test speech models under realistic African deployment conditions. AfriVox-v2 introduces "in the wild" unscripted audio for all supported languages. We also introduce strict domain verticalization, evaluating model accuracy across ten sectors including government, finance, health, and agriculture and conducting targeted tests on numbers and named entities. Finally, we benchmark a new generation of speech models, including Sahara-v2, Gemini 3 Flash, and the Omnilingual CTC models. Our results expose the true generalization gap of modern speech models in specialized, noisy African contexts and provide a reliable blueprint for developers building localized voice AI.

</details>


### [30] [BIT.UA-AAUBS at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Evaluating Open-Source and Proprietary LLMs via Prompting in Low-Resource QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03618)
*Richard A. A. Jonker,Alexander Christiansen,Alexandros Maniatis,Rúben Garrido,Rogério Braunschweiger de Freitas Lima,Roman Jurowetzki,Sérgio Matos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper presents a prompt-based approach using LLMs without fine-tuning for clinical question answering under data privacy constraints, achieving 1st place in evidence citation alignment and 3rd place in patient-friendly answer generation tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clinical question answering in a low-resource setting with no training data available and strict data privacy constraints (GDPR) in the healthcare domain.

Method: Evaluated state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source LLMs (without weight updates) using various prompt engineering strategies including task decomposition, Chain-of-Thought, and in-context learning. Explored majority voting and LLM-as-a-judge ensembling techniques.

Result: Proprietary models showed strong resilience to prompt variations, while domain-adapted open-source models (MedGemma 3 27B) achieved competitive performance with appropriate prompting.

Conclusion: The prompt-based approach proved highly effective, securing 1st place in Subtask 4 (evidence citation alignment) and 3rd place in Subtask 3 (patient-friendly answer generation).

Abstract: This paper presents the joint participation of the BIT.UA and AAUBS groups in the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task, which focuses on clinical question answering and evidence grounding in a low-resource setting. Due to the absence of training data and the strict data privacy constraints inherent to the healthcare domain (e.g. GDPR), we investigate the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) without weight updates. We evaluate several state-of-the-art proprietary models and locally deployable open-source alternatives using various prompt engineering strategies, including task decomposition, Chain-of-Thought, and in-context learning. Furthermore, we explore majority voting and LLM-as-a-judge ensembling techniques to maximize predictive robustness. Our results demonstrate that while proprietary models exhibit strong resilience to prompt variations, domain-adapted open-source models (such as MedGemma 3 27B) achieve highly competitive performance when paired with the right prompt. Overall, our prompt-based approach proved highly effective, securing 1st place in Subtask 4 (evidence citation alignment) and 3rd place in Subtask 3 (patient-friendly answer generation). All code, results, and prompts are available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/bioinformatics-ua/ArchEHR-QA-2026.

</details>


### [31] [Annotation Quality in Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis: A Case Study Comparing Experts, Students, Crowdworkers, and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03624)
*Niklas Donhauser,Jakob Fehle,Nils Constantin Hellwig,Markus Weinberger,Udo Kruschwitz,Christian Wolff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper investigates how different annotation sources (experts, students, crowdworkers, LLMs) affect German Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis dataset quality and downstream model performance, providing practical guidance for dataset construction in under-resourced NLP scenarios.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABSA research for non-English languages like German is limited due to lack of high-quality annotated datasets, necessitating investigation into how different annotation sources affect dataset quality and downstream model performance.

Method: Re-annotates an existing German ABSA dataset using expert annotators as ground truth, then evaluates annotations from students, crowdworkers, and LLMs. Compares annotation quality via Inter-Annotator Agreement (IAA) and downstream model performance using BERT-, T5-, and LLaMA-based approaches across fine-tuning and in-context learning settings.

Result: The study evaluates annotation quality from multiple sources and their impact on ABSA subtasks (ACSA and TASD), revealing trade-offs between reliability and efficiency across different annotation methods.

Conclusion: Provides practical guidance on trade-offs between annotation reliability and efficiency for dataset construction, particularly beneficial for under-resourced NLP scenarios.

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) enables fine-grained opinion analysis by identifying sentiments toward specific aspects or targets within a text. While ABSA has been widely studied for English, research on other languages such as German remains limited, largely due to the lack of high-quality annotated datasets. This paper examines how different annotation sources influence the development of German ABSA. To this end, an existing dataset is re-annotated by experts to establish a ground truth, which serves as a reference for evaluating annotations produced by students, crowdworkers, Large Language Models (LLMs), and experts. Annotation quality is compared using Inter-Annotator Agreement (IAA) and its impact on downstream model performance for different ABSA subtasks. The evaluation focuses on Aspect Category Sentiment Analysis (ACSA) and Target Aspect Sentiment Detection (TASD). We apply State-of-the-Art (SOTA) methods for ABSA, including BERT-, T5-, and LLaMA-based approaches to assess performance differences, spanning fine-tuning and in-context learning with instruction prompts. The findings provide practical insights into trade-offs between annotation reliability and efficiency, offering guidance for dataset construction in under-resourced Natural Language Processing (NLP) scenarios.

</details>


### [32] [A Paradigm for Interpreting Metrics and Identifying Critical Errors in Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03671)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对语音转录评估指标（WER/CER）与人类感知相关性差、难以解释的问题，提出将度量指标与最小编辑距离（minED）相结合的评估范式。该方法在保持误差率可解释性的同时，提升了与人类感知的相关性，并支持对转录错误严重性的深入研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最常用的自动语音转录评估指标（WER和CER）与人类感知相关性差，且无法捕捉语言和语义信息。虽然基于度量的嵌入方法可以近似人类感知，但其评分难以解释，不像WER和CER那样直观。

Method: 提出一种将度量指标融入其中的范式，以获得误差率的等价物——最小编辑距离（minED）。该方法将转录错误与人类感知进行关联对照。

Result: 实现了既保留可解释性（类似WER/CER的误差率形式），又提升与人类感知相关性的评估框架，并可对语音转录错误的严重程度进行原创性研究。

Conclusion: 提出了一种结合指标嵌入与最小编辑距离的新范式，能够在不牺牲可解释性的前提下提升评估指标与人类感知的相关性，并可应用于语音转录错误严重性的原创性研究。

Abstract: The most commonly used metrics for evaluating automatic speech transcriptions, namely Word Error Rate (WER) and Character Error Rate (CER), have been heavily criticized for their poor correlation to human perception and their inability to take into account linguistic and semantic information. While metric-based embeddings, seeking to approximate human perception, have been proposed, their scores remain difficult to interpret, unlike WER and CER. In this article, we overcome this problem by proposing a paradigm that consists in incorporating a chosen metric into it in order to obtain an equivalent of the error rate: a Minimum Edit Distance (minED). This approach parallels transcription errors with their human perception, also allowing an original study of the severity of these errors from a human perspective.

</details>


### [33] [A Comprehensive Analysis of Tokenization and Self-Supervised Learning in End-to-End Automatic Speech Recognition applied on French Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03696)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以法语为对象，实验性地研究子词分词算法（BPE、SentencePiece、Unigram、字符、grapheme初始化）与自监督学习（SSL）模型（wav2vec 2.0 法语1k/3k/7k、英文53k、XLSR多语）对端到端ASR系统性能的影响，并提出一套涵盖词汇、语义、声学三个层面的综合评估指标（WER/CER、SemDist、UWER、PhonER）。基于Speechbrain搭建28个端到端CTC系统，在约356小时法语广播语料（ESTER1/2、EPAC、ETAPE、REPERE）上训练，并在REPERE 10小时测试集上评测。主要结论：法语SSL预训练优于英文与多语模型，SSL训练数据量越大性能越好；子词分词整体优于字符分词，其中150词表的Unigram在所有指标上最佳；小词表提升OOV泛化能力；grapheme（音素化）分词并未带来增益；不同评估指标给出的系统排序相互冲突（Spearman相关性差异大，WER与CER仅0.55），说明单一WER不足以评判系统优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端ASR系统的性能高度依赖分词方式与自监督预训练模型的选择，但以往研究通常仅以WER/CER作为唯一评判依据；已有工作表明这些指标并不足以反映下游转写文本的实际质量，且针对端到端架构下分词与SSL影响的系统性研究仍很缺乏，尤其缺少面向具体语言（法语，其书写与读音差异大、静默字母多）的多维评估。因此作者希望从词汇、语义、声学多个角度，用一套综合指标来定性研究这些设计选择对法语ASR的影响。

Method: 1）系统搭建：基于Speechbrain，28个端到端ASR系统，统一结构为SSL模型 + 三层线性DNN + CTC层；CTC损失训练10个epoch，SSL层使用较低学习率；推理采用best path decoding。2）变量设置：(a) 分词策略——character、BPE、SentencePiece、Unigram，并变化子词表大小（150/250/500/750/1000），另设“BPE with graphemes”变体，即用144个法语音素字形（cross-referencing教学资源编译，如 aa, aen, cueil, illier, oi, tion 等）初始化BPE词表；(b) SSL模型——LeBenchmark的w2v2-FR-1k/3k/7k（法语1k/3k/7k小时）、w2v2-EN-53k（英文53k小时）、w2v2-xlsr（53语言）。3）数据：训练用ESTER1、ESTER2、EPAC、ETAPE、REPERE（约356小时广播音频），测试为REPERE 10小时。4）综合评估指标：词汇层WER、CER，以及作者提出的Unseen Word Error Rate (UWER，衡量训练集未出现但测试集出现的词的准确率，用于评估泛化)；语义层SemDist（基于CamemBERT/SentenceBERT的句子嵌入与参考转写的余弦相似度）；声学层PhonER（用G2P转换器PoemesProfonds将参考与假设转为音素序列后计算Levenshtein距离）。5）分析手段：按SSL模型语言/数据量、按分词算法与词表大小分组对比，并计算指标间系统级Spearman相关。

Result: （1）SSL模型影响：法语单语模型全面最优（w2v2-FR-7k: WER 16.56、CER 6.72、SemDist 10.45、UWER 75.19、PhonER 5.29）；英文模型即使数据量最大（53k小时）仍很差（WER 36.41），说明目标语言预训练至关重要；多语XLSR模型介于两者之间（WER 21.48），推测多语训练会稀释或覆盖语言特定信息。法语SSL训练数据量与各指标性能呈单调正相关（1k→3k→7k逐步提升）。（2）分词影响：所有子词分词器（BPE/SentencePiece/Unigram）在全部指标上优于字符分词（字符分词CER尚可但词边界与词汇准确率差）；词表越小对OOV泛化越好，大词表UWER明显下降（如Unigram 1000→150：UWER 78.91→73.12，WER 15.49→15.07）；最佳系统为Unigram+150词表（WER 15.07、CER 6.36、SemDist 9.33、UWER 73.12、PhonER 4.90），即“缩减词表”带来更好泛化；BPE with graphemes表现不佳，且各词表中grapheme占比越高的系统性能越差（Unigram在250/500/750/1000下grapheme占比最低而性能最好），表明子词单位更贴近语言学而非声学线索。（3）指标不一致性：尽管Unigram-150在各项指标上领先，指标间的系统级排序互相冲突（如SentencePiece下WER最优为500、CER/UWER/PhonER为150、SemDist为750）；Spearman相关显示WER与CER仅0.55，WER与SemDist 0.87，CER与SemDist 0.45，PhonER与UWER 0.80，表明以WER判定的“最优系统”未必在人类感知、语义或其他视角下最优，这是首次在系统级揭示该现象。

Conclusion: 法语端到端ASR中，SSL模型应在目标语言上预训练且训练数据量越大越好；子词分词优于字符分词，其中采用缩减词表的Unigram（150）性价比最高、可显著提升对未见词的泛化；而按音素字形（grapheme）定制词表并无增益，反而不如纯子词方案，说明端到端模型难以利用显式的声学-语言先验。更大的启示在于评估层面：词汇、语义、声学类指标在系统级给出相互矛盾的排序，标准WER不足以刻画转写质量，因此应针对任务开发多样化的内在与下游评估方法（含作者提出的UWER这类面向OOV泛化的指标与语义嵌入相似度），以更全面地衡量系统表现。作者指出未来可探索更多面向任务的自定义评估手段；本研究基于Speechbrain与28个系统的可复现配置（代码已开源）。

Abstract: The performance of end-to-end automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems enables their increasing integration into numerous applications. While there are various benefits to such speech-to-text systems, the choice of hyperparameters and models plays a crucial role in their performance. Typically, these choices are determined by considering only the character (CER) and/or word error rate (WER) metrics. However, it has been shown in several studies that these metrics are largely incomplete and fail to adequately describe the downstream application of automatic transcripts. In this paper, we conduct a qualitative study on the French language that investigates the impact of subword tokenization algorithms and self-supervised learning models from different linguistic and acoustic perspectives, using a comprehensive set of evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [34] [SERE: Structural Example Retrieval for Enhancing LLMs in Event Causality Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03701)
*Zhifeng Hao,Zhongjie Chen,Junhao Lu,Shengyin Yu,Guimin Hu,Keli Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SERE框架，通过概念路径度量、句法度量和因果模式过滤三种结构化检索策略，选择相关示例引导LLM进行因果推理，从而缓解LLM在事件因果识别任务中的因果幻觉问题，提升模型准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large Language Models exhibit limited effectiveness in Event Causality Identification due to biases in causal reasoning, specifically the tendency to overpredict causal relationships (causal hallucination), which hampers their performance in determining whether event pairs exhibit true causal relationships.

Method: SERE proposes three structural retrieval strategies: (1) Conceptual Path Metric measuring conceptual relationships via edit distance in ConceptNet; (2) Syntactic Metric quantifying structural similarity through tree edit distance on syntactic trees; (3) Causal Pattern Filtering filtering examples using LLM-based predefined causal structures. These strategies select relevant examples to guide LLMs in causal reasoning.

Result: The proposed SERE framework mitigates LLM bias and improves accuracy in ECI tasks by leveraging structured example retrieval based on conceptual, syntactic, and causal pattern metrics.

Conclusion: SERE framework effectively mitigates LLM bias in causal reasoning by selecting structurally relevant examples. Extensive experiments on multiple ECI datasets validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

Abstract: Event Causality Identification (ECI) requires models to determine whether a given pair of events in a context exhibits a causal relationship. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across various NLP tasks, their effectiveness in ECI remains limited due to biases in causal reasoning, often leading to overprediction of causal relationships (causal hallucination). To mitigate these issues and enhance LLM performance in ECI, we propose SERE, a structural example retrieval framework that leverages LLMs' few-shot learning capabilities. SERE introduces an innovative retrieval mechanism based on three structural concepts: (i) Conceptual Path Metric, which measures the conceptual relationship between events using edit distance in ConceptNet; (ii) Syntactic Metric, which quantifies structural similarity through tree edit distance on syntactic trees; and (iii) Causal Pattern Filtering, which filters examples based on predefined causal structures using LLMs. By integrating these structural retrieval strategies, SERE selects more relevant examples to guide LLMs in causal reasoning, mitigating bias and improving accuracy in ECI tasks. Extensive experiments on multiple ECI datasets validate the effectiveness of SERE. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/DMIRLAB-Group/SERE.

</details>


### [35] [SAM-NER: Semantic Archetype Mediation for Zero-Shot Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03706)
*Ruichu Cai,Juntao Gan,Miao Mai,Zhifeng Hao,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAM-NER框架，通过语义原型中介机制解决零样本命名实体识别中的跨域迁移问题。该框架通过实体发现、抽象中介和语义校准三个阶段，将实体投影到领域不变的原型空间，从而稳定跨域转移。实验表明该方法在CrossNER基准上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zero-shot NER suffers from domain and schema shifts where unseen label definitions misalign with LLM's intrinsic semantic organization, causing systematic semantic drift when target schemas are novel or semantically overlapping.

Method: SAM-NER proposes a three-stage semantic archetype mediation framework: (1) Entity Discovery using cooperative extraction and consensus-based denoising for high-coverage entity spans; (2) Abstract Mediation projecting entities into compact universal semantic archetypes distilled from ontological abstractions; (3) Semantic Calibration resolving archetype predictions to target types through constrained, definition-aligned inference with frozen LLM.

Result: SAM-NER consistently outperforms strong prior ZS-NER baselines on CrossNER benchmark in cross-domain settings.

Conclusion: The intermediate archetype space effectively bridges the gap between source domain entity semantics and target domain label definitions, providing a robust mechanism for cross-domain NER transfer.

Abstract: Zero-shot Named Entity Recognition (ZS-NER) remains brittle under domain and schema shifts, where unseen label definitions often misalign with a large language model's (LLM's) intrinsic semantic organization. As a result, directly mapping entity mentions to fine-grained target labels can induce systematic semantic drift, especially when target schemas are novel or semantically overlapping. We propose \textbf{SAM-NER}, a three-stage framework based on \emph{Semantic Archetype Mediation} that stabilizes cross-domain transfer through an intermediate, domain-invariant archetype space. SAM-NER: (i) performs \emph{Entity Discovery} via cooperative extraction and consensus-based denoising to obtain high-coverage, high-fidelity entity spans; (ii) conducts \emph{Abstract Mediation} by projecting entities into a compact set of universal semantic archetypes distilled from high-level ontological abstractions; and (iii) applies \emph{Semantic Calibration} to resolve archetype-level predictions into target-domain types through constrained, definition-aligned inference with a frozen LLM. Experiments on the CrossNER benchmark show that SAM-NER consistently outperforms strong prior ZS-NER baselines in cross-domain settings. Our implementation will be open-sourced at https://github.com/DMIRLAB-Group/SAM-NER.

</details>


### [36] [Rose-SQL: Role-State Evolution Guided Structured Reasoning for Multi-Turn Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03720)
*Le Zhou,Feng Yao,Fengcai Qiao,Bo Xu,Fangyuan Wang,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Rose-SQL是一个免训练的框架，通过Role-State表示和结构性同构检查，利用小规模大型推理模型实现高效的多轮Text-to-SQL解析，在多个模型规模上超越微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖不稳定的API推理或需要在小规模模型上进行昂贵的微调，而大型推理模型在多轮Text-to-SQL任务上的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了Role-State细粒度表示作为schema linking与SQL生成之间的结构蓝图，通过结构性同构检查追踪对话依赖，并利用小规模LRM的上下文学习实现零训练的准确上下文相关解析。

Result: 在Qwen3系列中，Rose-SQL在4B规模超越上下文学习基线，在8B和14B规模显著超越最先进的微调模型，并在其他推理骨干上表现一致。

Conclusion: Rose-SQL consistently outperforms baselines across multiple model scales (4B, 8B, 14B) and reasoning backbones, demonstrating the effectiveness of training-free in-context learning for multi-turn Text-to-SQL.

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) trained with Long Chain-of-Thought have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in code generation and mathematical reasoning. However, their potential in multi-turn Text-to-SQL tasks remains largely underexplored. Existing approaches typically rely on unstable API-based inference or require expensive fine-tuning on small-scale models. In this work, we present Rose-SQL, a training-free framework that leverages small-scale LRMs through in-context learning to enable accurate context-dependent parsing. We introduce the Role-State, a fine-grained representation that bridges the structural gap between schema linking and SQL generation by serving as a structural blueprint. To handle conversational dependencies, Rose-SQL traces the evolution of Role-State through historical context via structural isomorphism checks, guiding the model to infer the possible SQL composition for the current question through verified interaction trajectories. Experiments on the SParC and CoSQL benchmarks show that, within the Qwen3 series, Rose-SQL outperforms in-context learning baselines at the 4B scale and substantially surpasses state-of-the-art fine-tuned models at the 8B and 14B scales, while showing consistent gains on additional reasoning backbones.

</details>


### [37] [Segmenting Human-LLM Co-authored Text via Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03723)
*Mengchu Li,Jin Zhu,Jinglai Li,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将文本分割为人类撰写段落和LLM撰写段落的新任务，通过将文本分段与时序分析中的变点检测建立类比关系，开发加权算法和广义算法来处理检测分数的异质变异性，并在理论上证明了方法的极小极大最优性，实验表明该方法优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型(LLM)的快速发展，区分人类撰写文本与LLM生成文本变得至关重要。现有检测器通常只能对整篇文档给出二元分类结果，但在人机协作撰写的文本中，无法定位具体由人类或LLM撰写的段落，因此需要更精细的分割方法。

Method: 核心创新在于建立文本分割任务与时序变点检测之间的概念类比，将变点检测方法适配到LLM生成文本检测领域。具体包括：(1) 设计加权算法处理检测分数方差的异质性；(2) 开发广义算法框架；(3) 从理论角度证明所提方法的极小极大最优性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在一系列现有基线方法上展现出优异的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将变点检测技术迁移至文本分割领域，为人机协作文本的段落级溯源提供了有效解决方案，兼具理论保证和实证效果。

Abstract: The rise of large language models (LLMs) has created an urgent need to distinguish between human-written and LLM-generated text to ensure authenticity and societal trust. Existing detectors typically provide a binary classification for an entire passage; however, this is insufficient for human--LLM co-authored text, where the objective is to localize specific segments authored by humans or LLMs. To bridge this gap, we propose algorithms to segment text into human- and LLM-authored pieces. Our key observation is that such a segmentation task is conceptually similar to classical change point detection in time-series analysis. Leveraging this analogy, we adapt change point detection to LLM-generated text detection, develop a weighted algorithm and a generalized algorithm to accommodate heterogeneous detection score variability, and establish the minimax optimality of our procedure. Empirically, we demonstrate the strong performance of our approach against a wide range of existing baselines.

</details>


### [38] [Benchmarking Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models for Low-Resource Tajik Text Generation with the Tajik Web Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03742)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布目前最大的公开塔吉克语语料库（Tajik Web Corpus，31.9万篇文档、约11.1亿字符、1.685亿词），并在其1万篇子集上系统评测9种架构、17种参数高效微调（PEFT）配置对塔吉克语自回归续写任务的适配效果。结论是：Mistral-7B + QLoRA (r=8) 在质量与成本上最平衡（困惑度5.11，峰值显存14.21 GB，单卡训练约33分钟，母语者确认输出语法与话题连贯）；GPT-2 系小模型全量微调虽困惑度最低（GPT-2 Medium 3.48）却出现灾难性遗忘（输出英文或乱码）；编码器模型（XLM-RoBERTa、Persian BERT）困惑度约59–66，完全不适合生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语（属伊朗语族西南组，使用西里尔字母，约1500万使用者）严重缺乏数字文本资源，现有语料（OSCAR片段、Leipzig）规模小且脏乱，既往研究多聚焦于西里尔-波斯字母转写，无法支撑直接用塔吉克语生成文本。同时，LoRA/QLoRA 等 PEFT 方法在非拉丁字母、极低资源语言上的表现几乎未被系统研究，实践者缺乏在单卡（16–24 GB）预算下选择模型架构与微调策略的依据。

Method: 任务设定为提示续写（prompt continuation，给定塔吉克语前缀做自回归生成），因分类、问答等任务缺乏塔吉克语原生训练数据。数据：Tajik Web Corpus 按文档长度四分位分层抽样 1 万篇（均值3483字符），各模型用自身 tokenizer 截断至128 token，EOS 填充并在损失中屏蔽，训练/验证/测试按 8/10/10 划分（seed 42）。模型9个：解码器 DistilGPT-2、GPT-2、GPT-2 Medium、Phi-2、Qwen 2.5 1.5B Instruct、Mistral 7B v0.3；编码器-解码器 mT5-small；编码器 XLM-RoBERTa base、Persian BERT fa-zwnj-base（作为负对照）。策略：全量微调（仅 GPT-2 系可行）、LoRA、4-bit QLoRA（r=8/16，alpha=16/32，dropout 0.05；Mistral 额外作用于 gate/up/down 投影）。训练：AdamW（QLoRA 用 8-bit），恒定学习率 2e-4，≤2.7B 模型 3 轮、Mistral 2 轮，梯度检查点 + fp16，OOM 时自动折半批量并以梯度累积（step 2）维持等效批量；每配置 3 个种子（42/43/44）。评估：测试集困惑度与交叉熵损失（因 tokenizer 不同，困惑度仅在同族内比较）、母语者对生成续写的语法正确性与话题相关性定性评估、峰值显存与训练时间。硬件：单张 NVIDIA RTX 3090 24GB（mT5-small QLoRA 两个种子需 32GB 显卡）。

Result: GPT-2 Medium 全量微调困惑度最低（3.48±0.00），GPT-2 4.48，DistilGPT-2 5.03，但定性分析显示其输出为英文句子或西里尔乱码，属灾难性遗忘。Mistral-7B QLoRA r=8 得 5.11±0.03，r=16 得 5.03±0.03，差异经配对 t 检验 p>0.05 不显著，却多占约1GB显存（14.21 vs 15.28 GB），训练约33分钟（1987–1991 s）。Phi-2 QLoRA r=8 得 5.37，训练仅462 s，为有竞争力的轻量替代。Qwen 2.5 QLoRA 表现较差（7.35–7.95），作者归因于其西里尔语预训练薄弱。mT5-small QLoRA r=8 为 6.34±0.44。GPT-2 系从全量微调切换到 LoRA 出现明显退化（GPT-2 Medium 3.48→7.60/8.42；GPT-2 4.48→11.51/12.56；DistilGPT-2→13.05/14.16），说明低秩适配更适合大模型、小解码器需全量更新但会破坏预训练表示。编码器模型最差：XLM-RoBERTa QLoRA 59.34±2.70、Persian BERT 65.54±40.09，验证其不适合自回归生成并充当负对照。训练时间跨度 19.5 s（DistilGPT-2 LoRA r=16）至约33分钟（Mistral）。Mistral QLoRA 生成的塔吉克语续写语法正确、话题相关（正确答出杜尚别为首都、给出含塔吉克与25°C的天气描述、谈读书益处）。

Conclusion: 首次系统性 PEFT 基准显示：在极低资源非拉丁文字语言中，量化低秩适配能以有限预算获得连贯输出，而全量微调小模型虽数值困惑度更优却导致灾难性遗忘；困惑度不能单独作为生成质量指标，必须辅以输出检查与母语者定性评估。实践建议：(i) 采用 Mistral 7B + QLoRA r=8（单卡16GB可跑，约33分钟）；(ii) 避免对小型 GPT-2 做全量微调；(iii) 复用公开发布的语料与基准仓库。局限包括仅用约3%语料子集、超参固定未逐模型调优、OOM时自适应批量可能影响收敛、部分运行缺失显存日志、定性评估样本小且仅一位母语者、任务仅限提示续写。未来工作：扩展至全语料、分类与问答任务、多标注者大规模人工评估、逐架构超参优化，以及与 Soro 等基于持续预训练的语言专用模型对比。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the adaptation of generative large language models for the Tajik language, a low-resource language with Cyrillic script. To overcome the shortage of digital text resources, the author created and publicly released the Tajik Web Corpus, the largest open-access corpus of Tajik, comprising 319,298 documents (~1.11 billion characters). On a subsample of 10,000 documents, 17 configurations were benchmarked, covering autoregressive, encoder-decoder, and encoder-only models with three fine-tuning strategies: full fine-tuning, LoRA, and QLoRA (ranks 8 and 16). Quality was assessed via perplexity and cross-entropy loss; peak GPU memory and training time were also recorded. Best results were achieved by Mistral 7B with QLoRA (r=16): mean perplexity 5.03, standard deviation 0.03. Increasing rank from 8 to 16 gave statistically insignificant improvement while raising memory consumption. For small GPT-2 family models, full fine-tuning yielded lower perplexity (3.48 for GPT-2 Medium) than LoRA (7.60-8.42), but induced catastrophic forgetting. The encoder-only XLM-RoBERTa showed the worst results (perplexity 59.3). The novelty lies in creating the largest verified Tajik corpus and the first systematic analysis of PEFT effectiveness for Tajik text generation. Practical value lies in recommendations for architecture and fine-tuning strategy selection, optimizing computational costs without substantial quality loss.

</details>


### [39] [TriBench-Ko: Evaluating LLM Risks in Judicial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03792)
*Haesung Lee,Gyubin Choi,Eun-Ju Lee,So-Min Lee,Youkang Ko,Dogyoon Lim,Sung-Kyoung Jang,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TriBench-Ko 是作者公开发布的韩语司法工作流 LLM 风险评测基准，以「4 类司法任务 × 8 类部署风险」的矩阵形式评估 13 个大模型的可靠性，涵盖判例摘要、先例检索、法律争点抽取、证据分析四类任务，以及幻觉、遗漏、法条误用、人口统计偏差、过度迎合（谄媚）、提示敏感性、非确定性、裁判越权共八种风险维度。评测显示：遗漏（Omission）是各模型最普遍的失效模式（平均 F1 仅 0.450），先例检索任务最难（平均 F1 0.534，其中「先例检索×遗漏」平均 F1 0.293 为最差组合），gpt-5.4 总体最优（macro-F1 0.835），韩国模型 kt-midm-2.0-base-instruct 在遗漏维度反而最强（0.614）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律 LLM 基准（LegalBench、LawBench、LBOX Open、LEXam、AgentCourt 等）主要测量静态知识或代理任务（律考答题、分类），仅给出聚合准确率，无法揭示模型「如何失败」以及在输入结构、指令措辞变化下的行为稳定性；已有风险研究（Dahl et al. 2024 的 58–88% 幻觉率、JudiFair 的偏见评估）多为事后独立诊断，缺乏将任务能力与风险类型联合评估的统一框架。而韩国最高法院 2025 年《司法 AI 指南》及 EU AI Act（附件三将司法行政列为高风险）要求司法 AI 输出可验证、一致并从属于法官权威，亟需面向部署环节的、风险感知的评测基准。

Method: 基准设计：任务分类学（判例摘要、先例检索、法律争点抽取、证据分析）对应初审熟悉案情—法律检索—争议结构化—证据审查的司法流程阶段；风险分类学含 4 大类 8 维度：Inaccuracy（幻觉/遗漏/法条误用）、Bias（人口统计偏差/过度迎合）、Inconsistency（提示敏感性/非确定性）、Adjudicative Overreach（裁判越权）。数据源自韩国大法院综合法律信息门户与宪法法院官网，案例从韩国法曹考试标准判例研究语料中抽样，覆盖宪法、民法、刑法、民诉、刑诉、商法、行政法、劳动法、社会保障法、经济法 10 个领域，共 1,414 个二元判断项、31 个任务-风险对（先例检索×法条误用因结构性匹配任务而被有意留空）。标注为两阶段：一名标注者构建+一名前法官独立核验指令真实性，三名韩国律协认证律师复核法条正确性、文本完整性、管辖有效性与干扰项合理性，分歧项剔除。评测协议分 Single、Input Variant（保护属性改写）、Instruction Variant（引导性措辞）、Repeat（同一提示重复 5 次），变体/重复协议采用严格全对通过（all-or-nothing）计分。评估 13 个模型（闭源 API、韩国本土模型、开放权重模型），统一温度 0.0、最多 64 token、字符串匹配规范化输出为是/否，主指标为 macro-F1，并报告严格/平均准确率、成对一致率、响应偏置与校准等诊断指标。

Result: 13 个模型中 gpt-5.4 最佳（macro-F1 0.835），其后为 gpt-5.4-mini（0.781）、Qwen3.5-9B（0.771），kt-midm-2.0-base-instruct 总体第四（0.728）但在遗漏维度领先（0.614）；Llama-3.1-8B-it、A.X-3.1-Light 最弱（0.386/0.342）。风险层面：幻觉相对最易（头部模型 >0.877），遗漏最普遍（均值 0.450），法条误用次之；过度迎合均值 0.645；提示敏感性与非确定性是模型分化最大的维度之一，gpt-5.4 的低分主要源于输出波动，而 A.X-3.1-Light、Llama-3.1-8B-it 主要源于内容错误；裁判越权普遍不严重（Qwen3.5-9B 0.882、gpt-5.4 0.877 最高）。任务层面：判例摘要最易（0.699），先例检索最难（0.534），法律争点抽取中等但区分度高，证据分析相对可控且对法条误用较稳健。人口统计偏差中性别替换最难（Top4 模型 0.65–0.70），国籍与社会经济属性较稳。跨模型方差最大的是先例检索（std 0.219）及其提示敏感性/非确定性/幻觉组合。响应偏置分析显示头部模型 yes 率接近 0.5，其遗漏失败属真实保持能力不足；而多数弱模型 yes 率高达 0.97–1.00，遗漏指标与真实能力混淆。质性分析以大法院 2001다10113（因行为能力欠缺认定抵押设定合同无效并发回）为例，展示幻觉（捏造维持原判、将行为能力简化为 IQ 阈值）、遗漏（结论正确但遗漏原审推理与程序有效性）、裁判越权（主张无效优于可撤销的规范性论断）三类失效。

Conclusion: TriBench-Ko 将法律 LLM 评测从「聚合分数」推进到「任务×风险」的结构化评估，证明单一排行榜不足以判断部署就绪度，必须识别「在哪个任务、以何种风险方式失败」。核心结论是：当代 LLM 在司法场景中最普遍的隐患不是幻觉，而是「遗漏」——即输出方向正确却丢失法律构成性内容的保持能力不足，尤其在先例检索与法律信息整合类任务上；且整体排名与风险画像不等价，不同模型需要分层、有针对性的缓解策略（弱模型需多次采样与严格人工复核，缺失率主导则需专门的检索/保留校验机制）。作者主张司法 AI 输出必须可验证、一致且从属于司法权威，部署前需按风险类型进行严格专业检查。数据集与代码已开源于 github.com/holi-lab/TriBench-Ko。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into legal workflows. However, existing benchmarks primarily address proxy tasks, such as bar examination performance or classification, which fail to capture the performance and risks inherent in day-to-day judicial processes. To address this, we publicly release TriBench-Ko, a Korean benchmark designed to evaluate potential deployment risks of LLMs within the context of verified judicial task requirements. It covers four core tasks: jurisprudence summarization, precedent retrieval, legal issue extraction, and evidence analysis. It jointly assesses model behavior across multiple deployment risk categories, including inaccuracy (hallucination, omission, statutory misapplication), biases (demographic, overcompliance), inconsistencies (prompt sensitivity, non-determinism), and adjudicative overreach. Each item is structured to systematically assess both task performance and a specific risk type based on real judicial decisions. Our evaluation of a range of contemporary LLMs reveals that many models frequently manifest significant risks, most notably struggling with precedent retrieval and failing to capture critical legal information. We provide a comprehensive diagnosis of these LLMs and pinpoint critical areas where LLM-generated outputs in judicial contexts necessitate rigorous inspection and caution. Our dataset and code are available at https://github.com/holi-lab/TriBench-Ko

</details>


### [40] [Natural Language Processing: A Comprehensive Practical Guide from Tokenisation to RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03799)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一篇介绍系统性NLP实践课程的预印本，涵盖从词法处理到LLM微调的完整管道，包含12个动手实验，强调开源可复现性，并原创性地探索了Tajik和Tatar两种低资源语言的NLP应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合NLP教育中理论与实践的鸿沟，提供一个可复现的、端到端的现代NLP教学框架，涵盖从传统机器学习到最新LLM方法的完整技术栈，同时拓展低资源语言的NLP研究。

Method: 整合了12个动手实践课程，结合理论讲解与详细实现计划，使用统一进化的语料库进行实验，强制要求在公共仓库发布代码、模型和报告，采用开放权重模型而非商业API，特别关注Hugging Face生态系统。

Result: 成功设计了一个包含完整评估指标和透明评分标准的12模块教学体系，原创性地开发了Tajik和Tatar语的低资源语言NLP资源（子词分词器、词嵌入、词汇数据库和音译基准），为数据稀缺环境下的现代NLP适应提供了可参考的方案。

Conclusion: 该预印本通过12个实践课程构建了一个系统化的现代NLP教学框架，强调可复现性和开源模型的使用，特别是在低资源语言（Tajik和Tatar）上的应用创新，为从经典ML到LLM的系统性教学提供了独特的研究型教材范式。

Abstract: This preprint presents a systematic, research-oriented practicum that guides the reader through the entire modern NLP pipeline: from tokenisation and vectorisation to fine-tuning of large language models, retrieval-augmented generation, and reinforcement learning from human feedback. Twelve hands-on sessions combine concise theory with detailed implementation plans, formalised evaluation metrics, and transparent assessment criteria. The work is not a conventional textbook: it is designed as a reproducible research artefact where every session requires publishing code, models, and reports in public repositories. All experiments are conducted on a single evolving corpus, and the work advocates open-weight models over commercial APIs, with special attention to the Hugging Face ecosystem. The material is enriched by original research on low-resource languages, incorporating linguistic resources for Tajik and Tatar (subword tokenisers, embeddings, lexical databases, and transliteration benchmarks), demonstrating how modern NLP can be adapted to data-scarce environments. Designed for senior undergraduates, graduate students, and practising developers seeking to implement, compare, and deploy methods from classical ML to state-of-the-art LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [41] [Reproducing Complex Set-Compositional Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03824)
*Vincent Degenhart,Dewi Timman,Arjen P. de Vries,Faegheh Hasibi,Mohanna Hoveyda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于"集合组合式（set-compositional）信息检索"的可复现性研究：在统一协议下基准测试了词法、单向量稠密、后期交互、学习稀疏以及推理导向（ReasonIR、Search-R1、LogiCol、Set-Comp LSR）等12个检索/重排方法，并提出受控基准 LIMIT+（把 LIMIT 的原子属性谓词组合成 QUEST 风格模板）。结论是：在 QUEST 上最佳神经检索器 Recall@100≈0.42，是 BM25（0.20）的两倍多，但推理专用方法并不稳定占优；一旦移除语义捷径（迁移到 LIMIT+），LogiCol 从≈0.42 崩塌至 <0.02，而 BM25 升至≈0.96；按组合深度分层后所有方法单调退化，稠密方法崩塌而代数稀疏/词法方法稳定；受控重排下 LLM 与神经符号方法 nDCG@5 达 0.97/0.92，说明瓶颈在第一阶段候选生成而非排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的信息需求往往包含多重约束（交集、并集、排除），而深度研究查询与智能体查询改写使这类查询日益普遍。但现有检索系统主要在"语义相关性主导"的基准上训练评测，因此复杂查询基准上的分数提升难以解释：高分可能来自语义启发式与预训练世界知识的伪相关，而非真正满足组合约束。已有工作缺少两个缺口：(1) 缺乏在统一协议下对所有主流检索范式与新兴推理导向方法的系统比较；(2) 不清楚已报告的增益究竟是真实约束满足还是对数据语义线索的利用。因此作者提出三个研究问题：RQ1 各检索族在 QUEST/QUEST+Variants 上的表现；RQ2 这些增益在语义信号被削弱的受控环境中能否保持；RQ3 效果如何随约束类型与组合深度变化。

Method: 研究设计为"两部分评估"。(1) 可复现性研究：在统一索引、检索、评测流水线下对 QUEST（1,727 测试查询/325,427 文档）与 QUEST+Variants（11,854 查询/361,646 文档）评测六个第一阶段检索器（BM25；Contriever、E5-base-V2、E5-Mistral-Instruct、GritLM-7B、Promptriever、Qwen3-Embedding-8B；GTE-ModernColBERT；SPLADE-v3）与四个增强方法（ReasonIR-8B、Set-Comp LSR 零样本、LogiCol（E5-base 微调）、Search-R1（BM25 底座、最多 5 轮）），报告 Recall/nDCG@{5,20,100}；重排阶段在"全部 gold + 5 noise + 5 irrelevant"的受控候选集上评测 MonoT5、GPT-Oss-120B、Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct 与神经符号方法 OrLog（param+ 配置，LLaMA-70B 解析 + Qwen-2.5-7B 谓词打分 + ProbLog 聚合）。(2) 提出 LIMIT+：从 LIMIT（50,000 合成实体、1,848 属性）出发，解析实体属性集并构建属性→实体倒排索引，用七个 QUEST 风格模板（原子、A∪B、A∪B∪C、A∩B、A∩B∩C、A∖B、(A∩B)∖C）覆盖三类深度与四种逻辑算子，通过属性元组键去重，并按 gold 集大小 [1,200] 做五桶分层接受采样；最终得到 700 条查询（每模板 100 条），平均 gold 规模≈37（从 1 到 128.3），gold 集由集合运算精确导出并附模板/属性元数据，支持按约束类型与组合深度分层评测。附录提供复现与原论文分数的对比（QUEST/QUEST+ 高度一致，LIMIT 偏差较大）。代码与数据生成脚本公开。

Result: RQ1（QUEST/QUEST+）：神经检索器全面优于 BM25（Recall@100 0.197）；最佳为 GritLM-7B 与 LogiCol（均≈0.42），SPLADE 0.393、Qwen3-Embedding 0.384；LogiCol 在早期截断最佳（R@5=0.115、R@20=0.234），nDCG@100 最佳为 0.284；仅 0.1B 的 GTE-ModernColBERT 排序质量有竞争力（nDCG@100=0.229）但召回较低（0.294）。到 QUEST+Variants 各法绝对值约减半（LogiCol R@100=0.217，GritLM 0.211）但相对排序基本保持。推理导向方法未显出优势：ReasonIR-8B 与同规模稠密模型相当（R@100=0.364），Search-R1 仅略胜 BM25（0.228 vs 0.197），Set-Comp LSR 零样本 R@100=0.289 仍低于 SPLADE（0.393），说明朴素向量算术未捕捉集合语义。RQ2（LIMIT/LIMIT+）：格局剧变——LIMIT+ 上 BM25 最强（R@100=0.837，nDCG@20=0.79），Search-R1 0.829、GTE-ModernColBERT 0.733 紧随，而所有单向量稠密模型 <0.13，LogiCol 近零（0.017）；LIMIT+ 上 Set-Comp LSR 更稳健（nDCG≈0.22，nDCG@20≈0.22）而 LogiCol 仅 0.01，且 Set-Comp LSR 在 A∖B 上 nDCG@20≈0.37（约为 SPLADE 0.17 的两倍）、在 A∪B 上 0.31 vs 0.27，但在 A∩B/A∩B∩C 上崩塌至 0.09/0.01。重排实验：GPT-Oss-120B nDCG@5=0.889（QUEST）/0.970（LIMIT+），MAP 0.872/0.967；OrLog 0.870/0.920；Qwen-2.5-7B-Inst 0.751/0.858；MonoT5 严重失败（0.155/0.033，nDCG@20=0.402/0.033），表明瓶颈在第一阶段召回。RQ3（分层分析）：原子与析取模板较易，合取与排除模板明显更难（QUEST+ 上 LIMIT+ 合取模板稠密模型 nDCG@20 近零）；效果随谓词数从 1 增至 3 单调退化（QUEST 上 GritLM nDCG@20 由≈0.35 降至≈0.13；LIMIT+ 上 BM25/Search-R1 缓慢下降而稠密模型在深度 2–3 直接崩塌），表明聚合指标掩盖了显著的异质性与失败模式。

Conclusion: 总体结论：集合组合式检索的绝对性能仍然很低——即便最佳第一阶段检索器在 QUEST 上 Recall@100 ≤ 0.42，QUEST+ 上更低；提升主要来自与语义上下文的伪相关，而非真实的约束满足。三点启示：(1) 推理专用训练目标不足以应对集合组合式检索——LogiCol（110M）在 QUEST 上比肩 GritLM-7B 却在 LIMIT+ 崩塌，Search-R1 的 RL 改写几乎不及 BM25，而 Set-Comp LSR 的零样本代数框架在受控环境中泛化最稳，呼吁能区分语义相关性与组合结构的基准；(2) 瓶颈位于第一阶段候选生成而非排序能力，因此"宽召回第一阶段 + 约束感知重排"的多阶段架构是最有前景的方向；(3) 聚合指标掩盖关键失败模式，合取与否定模板远难于析取，且随组合深度单调退化，应把模板级细粒度评测作为复杂查询 IR 的标准实践。局限：LIMIT+ 为合成属性列表数据，简化了问题，价值在于受控诊断而非独立基准；重排评测使用保证含 gold 的受控候选集，属现实性能的上界；未评估生成式检索与 RAG 管线。后续工作包括将 LIMIT+ 扩展到 4 个以上谓词与更多算子（如对称差、包含关系）、系统研究 retrieve-then-reason 架构，并探索显式编码集合论结构的训练目标。

Abstract: Complex information needs may involve set-compositional queries using conjunction, disjunction, and exclusion, yet it remains unclear whether current retrieval paradigms genuinely satisfy such constraints or exploit `semantic shortcuts'. We conduct a reproducibility study to benchmark major retrieval families and reasoning-targeted methods on QUEST and QUEST+Variants, and introduce LIMIT+, a controlled benchmark where relevance depends on arbitrary attribute predicates and constraint satisfaction, and less on pretrained knowledge. Our findings show that (i) on QUEST, the best neural retrievers achieve an effectiveness that is more than double what can be achieved with BM25 (Recall@100 ${>}$0.41 vs.\ 0.20), but reasoning-targeted methods like ReasonIR and Search-R1 do not outperform general-purpose retrievers uniformly; (ii) on LIMIT+, gains fail to transfer, where the strongest QUEST method collapses from Recall@100${\approx}$0.42 to below 0.02, while classic lexical retrieval gains to ${\sim}$0.96. Lastly, (iii) stratifying by compositional depth reveals a consistent degradation across all methods, where algebraic sparse and lexical methods show more stable performance while dense approaches collapse. We release code and LIMIT+ data generation scripts to support future reproducibility and controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [42] [TRACE: A Metrologically-Grounded Engineering Framework for Trustworthy Agentic AI Systems in Operationally Critical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03838)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE是一个面向操作关键领域的跨域可信赖AI工程框架，通过四层架构、传统ML与LLM验证器的显式分离、状态化编排升级策略、有限人类监督，以及基于国际计量标准（GUM/VIM/ISO 17025）的信任度量体系，实现了LLM使用的有意设计决策而非架构默认，并引入计算精简比率（CPR）作为模型精简的核心设计原则，在临床、工业、司法三个差异化的治理场景中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可信赖AI系统缺乏统一的工程化框架，尤其在操作关键领域（医疗、工业、司法）中缺乏系统性方法。现有系统往往将LLM作为默认架构选择，缺乏对其使用必要性、风险和成本的显式评估机制，导致系统复杂度过高且信任度量缺乏计量学基础。

Method: 提出四层参考架构，核心为L2a（传统ML）与L2b（LLM验证器）的显式分离；L3层实现有状态的编排与升级策略；L4层确保有限的人类监督。构建基于GUM/VIM/ISO 17025的计量级信任度量套件，并引入计算精简比率（CPR）作为模型精简原则的量化指标。三个领域实例（临床决策支持、工业多域操作、司法AI助手）验证框架的跨域迁移能力。

Result: 成功构建了可跨域迁移的可信赖AI工程框架，通过L2a/L2b分离实现了LLM使用的有意设计，通过CPR量化了模型精简原则。三个不同治理环境下的实例验证了框架的通用性和适应性。

Conclusion: TRACE框架成功地将LLM的使用从架构默认转变为可量化的设计决策，通过CPR指标实现模型精简原则。三个跨域实例验证了该框架在不同治理环境下的通用性和适应性，为可信赖AI系统工程提供了可复用的方法论框架。

Abstract: We introduce TRACE, a cross-domain engineering framework for trustworthy agentic AI in operationally critical domains. TRACE combines a four-layer reference architecture with an explicit classical-ML vs. LLM-validator split (L2a/L2b), a stateful orchestration-and-escalation policy (L3), and bounded human supervision (L4); a metrologically grounded trust-metric suite mapped to GUM/VIM/ISO 17025; and a Model-Parsimony principle quantified by the Computational Parsimony Ratio (CPR). Three instantiations--clinical decision support, industrial multi-domain operations, and a judicial AI assistant--transfer the samearchitecture and metrics across principally different governance contexts. The L2a/L2b separation makes the use of large language models a deliberate design decision rather than an architectural default, with parsimony quantified through CPR. TRACE introduces CPR as a first-class design principle in trustworthy-AI engineering.

</details>


### [43] [MCJudgeBench: A Benchmark for Constraint-Level Judge Evaluation in Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03858)
*Jaeyun Lee,Junyoung Koh,Zeynel Tok,Hunar Batra,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MCJudgeBench是一个用于评估LLM法官在多约束指令遵循任务中断言级判断能力的基准测试。研究发现当前LLM法官评估存在的关键问题：整体响应层面的表现无法反映约束层面的可靠性，推理增强虽能提升正确率但不能保证稳定性，且正确性与一致性之间不存在正相关关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM法官评估仅关注整体响应判断，忽略了多约束指令遵循场景中每个独立约束的验证需求。LLM法官在约束级别的可靠性评估存在空白，需要新的评估框架来揭示法官可靠性的多维特征。

Method: 构建MCJudgeBench基准数据集，每个样本包含指令、候选响应、显式约束列表、每个约束的金标准标签（yes/partial/no）以及受控的响应扰动。设计了多种评估提示变体以测试法官稳定性，并使用正确性和不一致性双指标评估。

Result: 研究表明：1）强整体性能不保证各标签类别（尤其是稀缺的partial和no案例）检测同样可靠；2）高正确性不等于低不一致性；3）推理增强（CoT）提升正确率但不能统一改善稳定性；4）识别出随机解码导致的内在不一致和扰动导致的程序性不一致两种不同类型。

Conclusion: LLM法官评估应在约束级别进行，以揭示不同维度的失败模式。正确性与一致性是相互独立的评估维度，需分别考察。

Abstract: Multi-constraint instruction following requires verifying whether a response satisfies multiple individual requirements, yet LLM judges are often assessed only through overall-response judgments. We introduce MCJudgeBench, a benchmark for constraint-level judge evaluation in multi-constraint instruction following. Each instance includes an instruction, a candidate response, an explicit constraint list, per-constraint gold labels in {yes, partial, no}, and controlled response-side perturbations. The evaluation protocol further includes evaluation prompt variants to test judge stability. We evaluate proprietary and open-source LLM judges using both correctness and inconsistency metrics, distinguishing intrinsic inconsistency under stochastic decoding from procedural inconsistency under prompt and response perturbations. Our results show that judge reliability has multiple dimensions: strong overall performance does not guarantee equally reliable detection across label categories, especially for rarer partial and no cases. Judges with higher correctness do not always have lower inconsistency. Evaluation with reasoning improves correctness but does not uniformly improve stability. These findings motivate evaluating LLM judges at the constraint level to study these failure modes.

</details>


### [44] [CC-OCR V2: Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Literacy in Real-world Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03903)
*Zhipeng Xu,Junhao Ji,Zulong Chen,Zhenghao Liu,Qing Liu,Chunyi Peng,Zubao Qin,Ze Xu,Jianqiang Wan,Jun Tang,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对现有OCR基准测试与实际应用场景脱节的问题，提出了CC-OCR V2综合基准数据集，涵盖5大OCR核心任务和7,093个高难度样本。实验评估了14个先进LMM，结果显示当前模型性能与实际应用需求之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准测试与实际应用场景不对齐，假设条件过于理想化（同质采集条件），缺乏对实际应用中困难和边缘案例的覆盖。

Method: 提出了CC-OCR V2，一个包含5大核心任务（文本识别、文档解析、文档定位、关键信息提取、文档问答）、7,093个高难度样本的综合OCR基准测试，用于评估LMM在真实应用场景下的表现。

Result: 14个先进LMM在CC-OCR V2上的实验表明，当前模型远未达到实际应用要求，即使是最先进的模型在多样化任务和场景下也表现出显著的性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前LMM在基准测试上的性能与实际应用效果之间存在显著差距，现有模型难以满足实际企业文档处理需求。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently shown strong performance on Optical Character Recognition (OCR) tasks, demonstrating their promising capability in document literacy. However, their effectiveness in real-world applications remains underexplored, as existing benchmarks adopt task scopes misaligned with practical applications and assume homogeneous acquisition conditions. To address this gap, we introduce CC-OCR V2, a comprehensive and challenging OCR benchmark tailored to real-world document processing. CC-OCR V2 focuses on practical enterprise document processing tasks and incorporates hard and corner cases that are critical yet underrepresented in prior benchmarks, covering 5 major OCR-centric tracks: text recognition, document parsing, document grounding, key information extraction, and document question answering, comprising 7,093 high-difficulty samples. Extensive experiments on 14 advanced LMMs reveal that current models fall short of real-world application requirements. Even state-of-the-art LMMs exhibit substantial performance degradation across diverse tasks and scenarios. These findings reveal a significant gap between performance on current benchmarks and effectiveness in real-world applications. We release the full dataset and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/eioss/CC-OCR-V2.

</details>


### [45] [Steer Like the LLM: Activation Steering that Mimics Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03907)
*Geert Heyman,Frederik Vandeputte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PSR框架，通过将提示导向行为提炼为可解释的激活导向模型，弥补了激活导向与提示导向之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向方法性能通常不如基于提示的方法，需要理解这种性能差距的根源。

Method: 提出Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR)模型，从激活中估计令牌级导向系数，并训练模型模仿基于提示的干预行为。

Result: PSR模型在三个导向基准测试中优于现有激活导向方法，特别是在控制高连贯性生成方面。

Conclusion: PSR模型在三个基准测试中优于现有激活导向方法，尤其在控制高连贯性生成方面表现突出，并与提示工程具有竞争力。

Abstract: Large language models can be steered at inference time through prompting or activation interventions, but activation steering methods often underperform compared to prompt-based approaches. We propose a framework that formulates prompt steering as a form of activation steering and investigates whether distilling successful prompt steering behavior into simpler, interpretable models can close this gap. Our analysis reveals that popular activation steering methods are not faithful to the mechanics of prompt steering, which applies strong interventions on some tokens while barely affecting others. Based on these insights, we introduce Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR) models that estimate token-specific steering coefficients from the activations themselves and are trained to imitate prompt-based interventions. Experiments on three steering benchmarks across multiple language models show that PSR models outperform existing activation steering methods, especially when controlling for high-coherence completions, and also compare favorably to prompting on AxBench and persona steering.

</details>


### [46] [Atomic Fact-Checking Increases Clinician Trust in Large Language Model Recommendations for Oncology Decision Support: A Randomized Controlled Trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03916)
*Lisa C. Adams,Linus Marx,Erik Thiele Orberg,Keno Bressem,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Denise Bernhardt,Markus Graf,Marcus R. Makowski,Stephanie E. Combs,Florian Matthes,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 原子级事实核查（将AI建议分解为可单独验证的声明并链接到源指南文档）相比传统可解释性方法，能显著提升临床医生对AI的信任度，效果量达到0.94（大效应），信任比例从26.9%跃升至66.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生对AI治疗建议的信任度不足，影响AI在临床决策中的应用。传统可解释性方法（如特征重要性、注意力图等）可能无法有效建立信任，需要探索新的透明度机制。

Method: 随机试验设计，纳入356名临床医生，产生7,476个信任评分。对比原子级事实核查方法与多种传统透明度机制对临床医生信任的影响。

Result: 原子级事实核查产生大效应量（Cohen's d = 0.94），临床医生信任比例从26.9%提升至66.5%。传统透明度机制呈剂量-反应梯度改善，效应量为0.25至0.50。

Conclusion: 在高风险临床决策中，将AI建议分解为可单独验证并链接到源指南的声明，比传统可解释性方法能产生更高的临床医生信任度。

Abstract: Question: Does atomic fact-checking, which decomposes AI treatment recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guideline documents, increase clinician trust compared to traditional explainability approaches?
  Findings: In this randomized trial of 356 clinicians generating 7,476 trust ratings, atomic fact-checking produced a large effect on trust (Cohen's d = 0.94), increasing the proportion of clinicians expressing trust from 26.9% to 66.5%. Traditional transparency mechanisms showed a dose-response gradient of improvement over baseline (d = 0.25 to 0.50).
  Meaning: Decomposing AI recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guidelines produces substantially higher clinician trust than traditional explainability approaches in high-stakes clinical decisions.

</details>


### [47] [The Counterexample Game: Iterated Conceptual Analysis and Repair in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03936)
*Daniel Drucker,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究语言模型是否能通过迭代的"反例-修复"循环进行哲学概念分析。实验涵盖20个概念和数千个循环，发现LM判断接受的反例数量是人类的2倍，但人机判断具有中等一致性；延长迭代仅增加定义长度而不提升准确性；部分概念本身难以获得稳定定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念分析（通过反例精炼定义）是哲学方法论的核心。研究旨在探索语言模型能否执行这一任务，评估其进行高水平迭代哲学推理的能力。

Method: 设计迭代分析-修复链：LM生成器提出定义→另一LM或人类生成反例→修复器改进定义，循环往复。在20个哲学概念上运行数千个反例-修复周期，由专家人类和LM裁判分别评估反例有效性。

Result: LM裁判接受的反例数量约为人类的2倍，但人类间及人机间的逐项有效性判断呈中等一致性；扩展迭代仅使定义冗长化，准确率未获提升；部分概念本质上抗拒稳定定义。

Conclusion: 语言模型可参与哲学推理，但反例-修复循环很快出现收益递减；该范式可作为评估LLM持续高水平迭代哲学推理能力的有效测试用例。

Abstract: Conceptual analysis -- proposing definitions and refining them through counterexamples -- is central to philosophical methodology. We study whether language models can perform this task through iterated analysis and repair chains: one model instance generates counterexamples to a proposed definition, another repairs the definition, and the process repeats. Across 20 concepts and thousands of counterexample-repair cycles, we find that, although many LM-generated counterexamples are judged invalid by both expert humans and an LM judge, the LM judge accepts roughly twice as many as humans do. Nonetheless, per-item validity judgments are moderately consistent across humans and between humans and the LM. We further find that extended iteration produces increasingly verbose definitions without improving accuracy. We also see that some concepts resist stable definitions in general. These findings suggest that while LMs can engage in philosophical reasoning, the counterexample-repair loop hits diminishing returns quickly and could be a fruitful test case for evaluating whether LMs can sustain high-level iterated philosophical reasoning.

</details>


### [48] [Feature-Augmented Transformers for Robust AI-Text Detection Across Domains and Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03969)
*Mohamed Mady,Johannes Reschke,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对AI生成文本检测器在分布偏移下的脆弱性，提出了一套"部署导向"的固定阈值评估协议（仅在HC3 PLUS验证集上标定一次决策阈值，测试时全程不变），并提出DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn架构：在[CLS]表征之外，用可学习注意力模块融合30项手工语言学特征（可读性、词汇多样性、困惑度、连贯性、文体计量等）。该模型在M4上取得85.97%平衡准确率（人类召回81.38%、AI召回90.57%），5种子宏平均83.15±1.04%，在同一协议下超越Fast-DetectGPT等零样本基线最高+7.22分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式AI文本检测器在源分布（in-domain）上可达接近满分的性能（HC3 PLUS 上99.5%平衡准确率），但实际部署中检测器面对的是风格、领域与生成管线均不同的文本，分布偏移会使性能显著退化。现有文献缺少两类评估要素：（1）不使用目标域标签的"部署现实"操作点——多数工作报告的是逐目标重调阈值后的乐观分数；（2）类别级（生成器级）错误剖析——单一聚合指标掩盖了"保人类文本"与"广覆盖生成器"之间的非对称失效模式。同时，主流检测器多依赖BERT/RoBERTa等较早骨干与表层线索，对释义、翻译、摘要等语义不变改写尤为脆弱。

Method: 监督二分类检测，训练集为HC3 PLUS（英文，train 148,040条，AI侧为GPT-3.5-Turbo，含语义不变改写变体）。基线为BERT-base、RoBERTa-base（微调+线性头）；另引入DeBERTa-v3-base骨干（其ELECTRA式替换token检测预训练被假设为对表层线索偏移更鲁棒）。特征增强模型采用双分支：文本分支输出h_[CLS]∈R^768；特征分支从62维手工特征（困惑度8、熵10、burstiness 6、重复性10、词汇8、连贯性6、可读性6、文体计量8）中，通过互信息与点二列相关的等权平均全局排序一次性选出top-30（仅用源域训练集，避免目标泄漏，RobustScaler中位数/IQR归一化），经重要性网络u(x)=W2·ReLU(LN(W1 f_k))、softmax得动态逐样本权重a(x)，加权后投影为z_f∈R^128，与h_[CLS]拼接为896维向量送入MLP分类头。训练：AdamW、3 epoch、lr 2e-5（特征头1e-3）、batch 16、max_len 512、linear warmup、H:R权重1.5:1.0。阈值协议：在val_qa∪val_si上以0.01步长在[0.10,0.90]网格搜索最大化平衡准确率得τ*，并在所有测试分布固定（最佳模型τ*=0.60）。评估三套件：HC3 PLUS（test_qa/test_si）、M4英文子集（78,766条，按wikipedia/wikihow/reddit/arxiv/peerread五域与八个生成器族分解）、AI-Text-Detection-Pile（1,392,011条），另加Gemini 2.0 Flash/3.0 Pro生成与新建Academic-Text-arxiv-gpt-gemini（669,008条）外部测试；对照Fast-DetectGPT、Log-Rank、RADAR零样本基线（同一固定阈值协议）、5随机种子稳定性与特征类别单留一/单类别消融。

Result: （1）域内：BERT 98.26/85.97、RoBERTa 99.54/86.58、BERT+FeatAttn 98.83/86.16、DeBERTa+FeatAttn 99.47/87.21（test_qa/test_si，BA%），语义不变改写明显更难。（2）M4域级：BERT 宏平均79.6、RoBERTa 77.9、BERT+FeatAttn 81.90、DeBERTa+FeatAttn 82.39，且领域依赖极强（BERT在arxiv仅69.4、RoBERTa在wikipedia仅68.9；DeBERTa+FeatAttn在peerread达93.0）。（3）最佳模型DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn：HC3 QA 99.63%、HC3 SI 86.85%、M4整体85.97%（H-R 81.38%、AI-R 90.57%、ROC-AUC 0.9437），对最难生成器BLOOMZ召回94.59%、LLaMA 96.25%。（4）5种子：M4宏平均83.15±1.04%（H-R 80.05±4.63、AI-R 86.24±2.85）。（5）同一固定阈值下M4宏平均BA：本文84.50 vs Fast-DetectGPT 77.28、Log-Rank 73.17、RADAR 67.82，最高达+7.22分。（6）外部：AI-Text-Detection-Pile上71.86% BA（AI-R 88.19%、H-R 55.53%，假阳性偏高）；Gemini 2.0 Flash 23,463条AI召回99.63%，Gemini 3.0 Pro 1,000条降至82.70%；Academic Text整体81.09%（GPT-3.5 100%、Gemini 93.25%、arXiv人类召回75.57%）。（7）消融：单类别仅可读性83.15最佳；留一法显示可读性(-1.65)、词汇(-1.52)贡献最大；特征增强最大增益在arXiv（+6.15 BA）。（8）固定vs自适应阈值：HC3 SI上自适应仅+0.48% BA但完全翻转误差画像（人类召回升、AI召回降）。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：源分布上的接近满分性能不能可靠预测偏移下的可靠性；语义不变改写是高强度压力测试，固定阈值的验证集标定操作点更贴近部署约束，域级与生成器级分解比单一聚合分数更具信息量。方法层面，手工语言学特征的注意力融合加现代DeBERTa-v3骨干显著优于早期BERT/RoBERTa骨干（最高+7.22分于零样本基线），但特征增强会加剧"保留人类文本"与"覆盖多样生成器"之间的操作点权衡：DeBERTa路线偏向高AI召回（Bloomz/LLaMA/Dolly2上提升明显），而BERT路线更善于保留人类文本（人类召回92.95%）。作者主张后续检测器评估应同时报告语义不变压力测试、验证集固定阈值、生成器级错误画像与类别级特征分析，并指出双重用途风险（对抗者可利用已暴露的弱点、过激检测器易误杀人类文本）。

Abstract: AI-generated text is nowadays produced at scale across domains and heterogeneous generation pipelines, making robustness to distribution shift a central requirement for supervised binary detectors. We train transformer-based detectors on HC3 PLUS and calibrate a single decision threshold by maximising balanced accuracy on held-out validation; this threshold is then kept fixed for all downstream test distributions, revealing domain- and generator-dependent error asymmetries under shift. We evaluate in-domain on HC3 PLUS, under cross-dataset transfer to the multi-domain, multi-generator M4 benchmark, and on the external AI-Text-Detection-Pile. Although base models achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance (up to 99.5% balanced accuracy), performance under shift is brittle and strongly model-dependent. Feature augmentation via attention-based linguistic feature fusion improves transfer, with our best model (DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn) achieving 85.9% balanced accuracy on M4. Multi-seed experiments confirm high stability. Under the same fixed-threshold protocol, our model outperforms strong zero-shot baselines by up to +7.22 points. Category-level ablations further show that readability and vocabulary features contribute most to robustness under shift. Overall, these results demonstrate that feature augmentation and a modern DeBERTa backbone significantly outperform earlier BERT/RoBERTa models, while the fixed-threshold protocol provides a more realistic and informative assessment of practical detector robustness.

</details>


### [49] [Logical Consistency as a Bridge: Improving LLM Hallucination Detection via Label Constraint Modeling between Responses and Self-Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03971)
*Hao Mi,Qiang Sheng,Shaofei Wang,Beizhe Hu,Yifan Sun,Zhengjia Wang,Hengqi Zeng,Yang Li,Danding Wang,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 LaaB（Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge），一个把「模型内部隐状态/Logits/注意力等微观神经特征」与「LLM 自评式宏观符号判断」统一起来的事实性幻觉检测框架。核心洞察是：LLM 对自己回答所作的自我评判本身也是一次生成行为，也可能是幻觉化的，因此可以被另一套幻觉检测器再次检测；并且响应与自评判之间存在天然的逻辑约束——当自评判说"Yes"（支持响应）时两者事实性标签应一致，当说"No"（否定响应）时两者标签应相反。LaaB 据此把符号标签"反向映射"回特征空间（meta-judgment），构建响应检测器 D_r 与自评判检测器 D_j 两个对等模块，并用带逻辑约束的互学习（Huber 损失对齐 + 置信度感知加权 + 梯度范数自适应平衡系数）联合优化；训练采用"异步轮转 + 联合微调"两阶段策略，推理时仅部署 D_r，因此不增加推理开销。在 TriviaQA、MMLU、NQ_Open、HaluEval 四个数据集、Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct、Qwen-2.5-32B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 四个开源模型上，对比 8 个基线（Self-Judge、SelfCheckGPT、Eigen-Score、SAPLMA、Logits Lens、Lookback Lens，附录另加 LapEigvals、TSV），LaaB 在绝大多数设定下带来稳定提升（如 Llama-3.1-8B 上 SAPLMA 平均 macF1 从 73.93 提升至 75.20，Acc 从 75.41 提升至 77.52），且在留一数据集（leave-one-dataset-out）跨域评测中同样表现出更强的分布迁移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 存在事实性幻觉，影响其在真实场景中的可靠性，现有的幻觉检测方法主要沿两条路线展开，但各自只覆盖了幻觉的一个侧面：(1) 基于内在模式（intrinsic patterns）的检测器，从生成过程中提取隐状态、预测 Logits、注意力分布等微观信号来量化不确定性，代表工作有 SAPLMA、Logits Lens、Lookback Lens、SelfCheckGPT、EigenScore 等，其问题是缺乏语义校准，容易漏掉"高置信度幻觉"；(2) 基于自我评判（self-judgment）的检测器，通过事实性提示词诱导 LLM 口头判断答案是否属实，代表工作有 Self-Judge、Self-Refine、multi-agent debate，其问题是存在自我偏好偏差（self-preference bias）与"过度思考"问题，容易产生"二次幻觉/评估性幻觉"（evaluative hallucination）。作者认为这两类行为本质上是耦合的，分别建模孤立处理会浪费其相互依赖性，因此提出核心研究问题：如何有效整合微观内在信号与宏观自评判信号以获得更准确的幻觉检测。为此需要解决的关键挑战是：如何为一个联合可学习框架同时容纳神经特征与符号判断——这正是 LaaB 用"逻辑一致性"作为桥梁来解决的核心问题。

Method: 整体架构为三部分（对应论文第 4 节与图 2）：
(1) 响应幻觉建模（Response Hallucination Modeling）：以 MLP 分类器 D_r 接收从响应 O_r 生成过程中提取的内在特征 F_r ∈ {H_r, P_r, A_r}。H_r 为 (Q_r,O_r) 拼接序列在验证集最优层 K_val,r 的末 token 隐状态（按分位数 [1/8,1/4,…,1] 网格搜索层）；P_r 依据 Logits Lens 假设取层维概率，经单个 Transformer 层 + 平均池化得到序列级特征；A_r 改编自 Lookback 比例，将上下文切分为 system prompt / query / response trigger / 前序 token 四段，按 KL 散度排序选 top-P（P=0.85）个注意力头。训练用交叉熵 L_CE,r。
(2) 自评判幻觉建模（Self-Judgment Hallucination Modeling）：用事实性评估提示词诱导 LLM 输出 O_j ∈ {"Yes","No"}，不把 O_j 当作真值，而是视为带可学习事实性标签 L_j 的生成物，训练第二个检测器 D_j 预测 L_j。特征 F_j ∈ {H_j,P_j,A_j}：H_j 取判断末 token 最优层隐状态；P_j 取判断首 token 的 Logits Lens 表示，并聚合 "Yes"/"No" 同义词集合概率，按公式(1)以 [P_yes ⊕ (P_yes−P_no)]（或对称的 No 分支）强化对比；A_j 将判断上下文切为 Framing/Query/Response/Eval_Query/Format/Trigger 六段计算注意力比例。
(3) 逻辑约束互学习（Logic-Constrained Mutual Learning）：依据语义定义逻辑依赖——O_j="Yes"（肯定）时 L_r 与 L_j 同向，"No"（否定）时 L_r = 1−L_j 异向；据此定义逻辑损失 L_Logic，用 Huber 损失(δ=0.5)对齐两检测器的标量概率（Yes 分支对齐 S_r,hallu 与 S_j,hallu；No 分支对齐 S_r,hallu 与 S_j,real）。为防止弱检测器误导强检测器，引入置信度感知加权：L_Logic,r = log(1+S_j(L_j)/S_r(L_r))·L_Logic，J 侧对称，即用"对同伴 ground-truth 类的置信度/对自身 ground-truth 类的置信度"之比缩放。总目标 L_* = L_CE,* + α_*·L_Logic,*，其中 α_* 按末层参数梯度范数比动态计算（式6）。训练分两阶段：阶段一 D_r 与 D_j 以"轮转异步"方式互为同伴训练，先收敛者冻结并回滚到最优状态；阶段二恢复两者最优参数做联合微调（lr=1e-6，α 取两侧自适应权重均值）。推理时仅部署 D_r，判定分布 S_r = D_r*(F_r)，无需生成自评判，故不增加推理成本。
此外特征提取还有工程细节：Lookback 比率按 anchor/context 分段平均池化并沿段维归一化，序列级比率按头维与层维组织；自评判同义词组包含 Yes/True/Correct 及其大小写与下划线变体，No/False/Incorrect 同理，并取与目标 LLM 词表的交集。实验侧数据自建：先让 LLM 作答、再让其自评，再通过"文本模式匹配 → NLI 蕴含评分（nli-deberta-v3-base）→ GPT-4o-mini 标注"的三级自动标注流水线给响应与判断打事实性标签，不确定样本人工核验（抽样 200 条一致率 93.50%；整体抽样 800 条一致率 96.125%），数据集按 7:1:2 划分。

Result: 1) 总体性能（表3）：在 4 数据集 × 4 LLM × 8 基线的绝大多数设定下，LaaB 均带来一致提升，且推理成本基本不变。例：Llama-3.1-8B 上 SAPLMA 平均 macF1 73.93→75.20、Acc 75.41→77.52；Logits Lens 68.15→69.96 / 69.16→72.87；Lookback Lens 72.03→73.54 / 73.70→75.74；Qwen-2.5-32B 上 SAPLMA 平均 74.89→76.39 / 78.20→80.17。附录 G 中 LaaB 同样能提升 LapEigvals 与 TSV，体现出与多种检测器的兼容性和通用性。2) 方法学结论：基于隐状态的 SAPLMA 整体优于基于 Logits 与注意力的方法，并从 LaaB 中获益更多；所有内在模式类方法普遍优于 Self-Judge 与采样一致性类基线，说明内部表征比离散 token 输出携带更丰富的事实性信号。3) 变体分析（表4）：仅用 D_j 推断（D_j 单独）明显弱于 LaaB（如 Logits Lens 在 TriviaQA macF1 仅 45.17），说明响应内在模式仍是主力信号、D_j 主要起补充作用；推理时同时用 D_r+D_j 融合仅带来边际增益却近乎翻倍推理成本，表明判断视角的知识已在训练期蒸馏进 D_r。4) 跨数据集泛化（表5，leave-one-dataset-out，Llama-3.1-8B）：SAPLMA 平均由约 65.8 提升到约 69.0（如 MMLU 56.19→59.31、HaluEval 67.49→70.25），作者解释为逻辑一致性约束抑制了仅被单一信号支持的、数据集特有的伪特征。5) 进一步分析（§5.5）：Sankey 迁移图显示原先被 D_r 判错的相当部分样本被纠正（黄色→紫色流），同时 D_r 原有的正确预测基本被保留，且出现从"两者均错"到正确的意外微弱增益，作者归因于联合优化引入的弱监督信号；长度分桶准确率显示 LaaB 在各长度区间普遍提升，长文本区间（(20,30] 与 >30）增益更大，最大提升出现在 MMLU 的 >30 token 子集，解释为自评判把响应级事实性压缩为单个 Yes/No token，缓解了长序列下的表征稀疏与噪声问题。6) 效率（表7，A800、batch 128）：Attns Based 训练最快 0.40 s/epoch；Hidden Based 推理延迟最低 0.0215 ms/instance；Logits Based 参数量最少（155K/25K）但训练 1.63 s/epoch、推理 0.0347 ms/instance 反而最高，说明 LaaB 不显著增加推理代价。作者同时坦诚局限：强制 Yes/No 答案空间会引入噪声；两个视角同时判错的样本原则上无法修正，且逻辑约束是软约束、不能保证逐样本完全一致；方法依赖生成方（服务商）可访问模型内部状态，不适用于黑盒第三方场景。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：LLM 的自评判不是可信的"真值源"，而是一个同样可被检测的生成行为；利用响应与自评判之间由语义决定的"标签同向/反向"逻辑约束，可以把符号判断反向映射（meta-judgment）到特征空间，从而以互学习方式把微观神经不确定性与宏观符号推理两类互补信号统一起来。LaaB 作为一个即插即用的"框架级"增强，能稳定提升多种幻觉检测基线（含隐藏状态、Logits、注意力、谱特征、隐空间干预等）在多模型多数据集上的 macro-F1 与准确率，并显著改善留一数据集的跨域鲁棒性；由于推理阶段只保留响应检测器，检测视图的知识通过互学习被蒸馏，因而几乎没有额外推理开销，兼具精度与效率。论文还指出两条衍生发现：内在（隐状态）信号仍是幻觉检测最有效的主干；将长响应压缩为单 token 判断可以缓解长上下文的表征稀疏问题，这与长文幻觉检测、多模态扩展、与外部事实核查流水线结合等未来方向相衔接。整体而言，本文提供了"以逻辑一致性为桥梁统一两类检测范式"的清晰思路，方法新颖、实验覆盖较广（4 数据集 × 4 LLM × 8 基线 + 2 附加基线），但增益幅度多为数个百分点，且依赖模型内部访问权限与自评判标注质量，其普适性与可复现性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to factual hallucinations, risking their reliability in real-world applications. Existing hallucination detectors mainly extract micro-level intrinsic patterns for uncertainty quantification or elicit macro-level self-judgments through verbalized prompts. However, these methods address only a single facet of the hallucination, focusing either on implicit neural uncertainty or explicit symbolic reasoning, thereby treating these inherently coupled behaviors in isolation and failing to exploit their interdependence for a holistic view. In this paper, we propose LaaB (Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge), a framework that bridges neural features and symbolic judgments for hallucination detection. LaaB introduces a "meta-judgment" process to map symbolic labels back into the feature space. By leveraging the inherent logical bridge where response and meta-judgment labels are either the same or opposite based on the self-judgment's semantics, LaaB aligns and integrates dual-view signals via mutual learning and enhances the hallucination detection. Extensive experiments on 4 public datasets, across 4 LLMs, against 8 baselines demonstrate the superiority of LaaB.

</details>


### [50] [Safety and accuracy follow different scaling laws in clinical large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04039)
*Sebastian Wind,Tri-Thien Nguyen,Jeta Sopa,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sebastian Bickelhaup,Michael Uder,Harald Köstler,Gerhard Wellein,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 SaFE-Scale 框架与 RadSaFE-200（200 道放射学多选题、含逐选项高风险/不安全/证据矛盾标注），在 34 个本地开源 LLM × 6 种部署条件（闭卷、洁净证据、冲突证据、标准 RAG、代理式 RAG、最大上下文）共 40,800 次评测中发现：临床 LLM 的安全性与准确率遵循不同的"缩放"路径——洁净（oracle 级）证据带来最大联合收益（准确率 73.5%→94.1%，高风险错误 12.0%→2.6%，危险过度自信 8.0%→1.6%），而检索、长上下文与推理时算力（自洽、三模型集成）仅部分改善指标，并引入"同步失败"；部署条件对方差的解释力（43/45/38%）远高于模型家族（9/8/17%），置信度不能区分安全与危险输出。因此结论是：临床安全是部署属性，而非模型规模或准确率缩放的被动结果；应按证据管线而非"更大模型"来采购与审计临床 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床 LLM 的部署普遍沿"缩放"路径进行——增大参数量、加长上下文、加重检索复杂度、增加推理时算力——并隐含假设"平均准确率更高即更安全"。但医学场景中少数自信的、高风险的或与证据矛盾的错误，其临床后果远大于平均基准分。本文动机在于：(1) 现有医学 LLM 安全评测多把错误视为等价，仅以平均准确率为主要终点，无法捕捉错误的后果结构；(2) 缺乏对"实际部署轴"（证据质量、检索策略、上下文构造、推理时算力）与安全性的系统性对照测量；(3) 缺少在模型运行前即由临床医师定义的逐选项风险标注，使"错误"能被分层为高风险、不安全与证据矛盾。作者因此主张把安全评测的重心从模型规模转向部署条件。

Method: 框架 SaFE-Scale：固定基准、选项、提示格式、答案抽取与评分规则，仅沿五个实用"缩放"轴（模型家族/参数量、证据质量、检索策略、上下文暴露、推理时算力）变化。基准 RadSaFE-200：200 道文本放射学多选题，来自 RadioRAG（104 题，源自 RSNA Case Collection）、RaR（65 题，慕尼黑工业大学考试题，五选项）、New-31（31 题，新写，基于 Radiopaedia 病例页），共 865 个选项；每题含医师撰写的 clean evidence（中位 32 词）、conflict evidence（加一句干扰句，中位 46.5 词）、9 类题型标签与 15 类亚专科多标签；每个选项由一名认证放射科医师标注 high-risk / unsafe / contradiction 三个二元标签（unsafe 是 high-risk 的严格子集，contradiction 仅对照 clean evidence）。865 选项中 33% 高风险、10% 不安全、40% 矛盾；138/200 题含至少一个高风险选项。模型面板：34 个本地部署开源 LLM（Qwen、Llama、Gemma、MedGemma、DeepSeek、Mistral、GPT-OSS 七族，0.5B–685B，含 dense 与 MoE、通用与医学适配），vLLM 部署于 H100/H200/MI300X/B200。主实验 34×6×200 = 40,800 次评测；每单元采 1 次 greedy 生成（用于准确率与三项安全结局）加 k 次随机采样（k=20，DeepSeek-V3.2/Llama-4-Scout=5，DeepSeek-R1=3）经 Mistral-Small-4 验证器映射到选项字母后多数投票。定义 entropy-normalized 置信度 P=1−H(p)/log(|A|+1)，危险过度自信 = 错误 ∧ 选项 high-risk/unsafe ∧ P≥0.80。推理时算力二级实验：8 模型 × 3 条件 × 单次 vs 自洽（K=20）；4 个预设三模型集成（Dense Mid、Frontier、Qwen scale、Cross scale）× 3 条件，定义同步失败 = 三成员同选一错误选项。统计：以问题为重采样单位做 1000 次 bootstrap（配对索引复用），模型平均后取均值；两因素 OLS 方差分解（家族、部署条件、家族×条件）以平方和占比报告；问题级最坏情形按高风险→不安全→矛盾率排序。附加检查：第二读者独立重标全部 200 题 865 选项（原始一致率 59.8/79.4/61.7%，Cohen's κ 0.29/0.22/0.05，PABAK 0.20/0.59/0.23）；169 题无泄漏子集敏感性分析；危险过度自信阈值 θ∈{0.60…0.99} 敏感性分析；亚专科分层、模型规模分层延迟分析。

Result: 1) 证据质量主导安全：闭卷→洁净证据，34/34 模型准确率与高风险错误同向改善，准确率 73.5%→94.1%（+20.6 点），高风险错误 12.0%→2.6%，不安全 1.8%→0.2%，矛盾 12.7%→2.3%，危险过度自信 8.0%→1.6%；洁净证据下家族间准确率收敛至 90.4–97.0%，家族内高风险错误标准差由 3.8 降至 1.0 点。2) 冲突证据仅降 1.6 点准确率却使高风险错误升约三分之一、危险过度自信升约一半，且高风险错误上的置信度不降反升。3) 检索未复制该安全画像：标准 RAG 准确率 76.0%、高风险错误 9.6%、危险过度自信 5.7%；代理式 RAG 准确率升至 78.1%、矛盾降至 9.0%，但高风险错误 10.3%、危险过度自信 8.0% 高于标准 RAG；闭卷→代理式 RAG 过渡中 14/34 模型改善、18/34 恶化。4) 最大上下文提示准确率仅 73.5%→74.0%，延迟 18.0→27.0 秒（+50%），无安全收益。5) 置信度不可作安全信号：正确与高风险答案的置信度差中位数在所有条件下均远低于 30 点（闭卷 94.9 vs 87.8；洁净证据 98.5 vs 85.4），高风险错误置信度基本落在 y=x 对角线上，错误答案置信度与准确率正相关（斜率 +0.40）。6) 方差分解：部署条件解释准确率/高风险错误/危险过度自信的 43%/45%/38%，模型家族仅 9%/8%/17%，家族×条件交互 6%/4%/8%。7) 自洽：24 个（模型，条件）对的全部指标变化均在 ±1 点内（平均 <0.5 点），无法接近洁净证据效果；危险过度自信仅在自洽下可定义，闭卷与标准 RAG 下达两位数。8) 集成：相对面板均值准确率与高风险错误改善，但相对最优成员在准确率、高风险错误与危险过度自信上为负、unsafe 为零；同步失败闭卷 5.9%、标准 RAG 6.5%（Frontier 达 9.0%），多数高风险错误为三成员一致错误。9) 最坏情形集中：闭卷下 15 道高危题的高风险错误率 52.9–97.1%，最高排名题 34/34 全错、33/34 高风险、34/34 矛盾；这些题在六条件下平均仍有多题 >60%，且与集成同步失败高度重叠。10) 鲁棒性：无泄漏 169 题子集各条件指标与全量差约 1 点以内且条件排序不变；θ=0.60–0.99 各阈值下洁净/冲突证据始终最低风险。

Conclusion: 在本文的文本放射学多选评测范围内，临床 LLM 安全性不能由准确率缩放推断：部署条件是安全性的主导决定因素，其解释力超过模型家族与参数量；唯一能同时提升准确率与全部安全结局的杠杆是高质量、可控的证据，且即便是 oracle 级洁净证据，其残余错误的置信度仍然很高、无法用作部署过滤。标准/代理式 RAG、长上下文与推理时算力（自洽、集成）只改善部分指标，agentic RAG 甚至提高危险过度自信，集成引入同步失败这一新型失效模式；临床危害集中于少数复发难题，与集成失败重叠，因此应按题目级最坏情形而非平均分来验收。作者的实践建议是：临床团队应把可控的证据管线（语料、检索、答案前置校验）作为规格与审计对象，在本机构自身病例与真实部署条件下测量四项安全结局，模型升级在复测前不得记录为安全改进。同时明确局限：单一 200 题纯文本多选基准、题型不平衡（诊断类占 59%）、不涉及影像/自由文本/纵向决策；安全标签为临床判断型标注且存在读者依赖（第二读者阈值更宽/矛盾更严）；洁净与冲突证据是受控构造而非真实噪声检索；置信度是重复采样稳定性而非校准概率；规模分析为描述性、非参数化 scaling law 且未隔离预训练数据/架构/对齐等混杂变量；未拟合幂律形式。结论的普适性限于本文范围，作者将 SaFE-Scale 定位为可在影像推理、自由文本报告及其他临床专科中重新实例化的方法学模板。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs are often scaled by increasing model size, context length, retrieval complexity, or inference-time compute, with the implicit expectation that higher accuracy implies safer behavior. This assumption is incomplete in medicine, where a few confident, high-risk, or evidence-contradicting errors can matter more than average benchmark performance. We introduce SaFE-Scale, a framework for measuring how clinical LLM safety changes across model scale, evidence quality, retrieval strategy, context exposure, and inference-time compute. To instantiate this framework, we introduce RadSaFE-200, a Radiology Safety-Focused Evaluation benchmark of 200 multiple-choice questions with clinician-defined clean evidence, conflict evidence, and option-level labels for high-risk error, unsafe answer, and evidence contradiction. We evaluated 34 locally deployed LLMs across six deployment conditions: closed-book prompting (zero-shot), clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Clean evidence produced the strongest improvement, increasing mean accuracy from 73.5% to 94.1%, while reducing high-risk error from 12.0% to 2.6%, contradiction from 12.7% to 2.3%, and dangerous overconfidence from 8.0% to 1.6%. Standard RAG and agentic RAG did not reproduce this safety profile: agentic RAG improved accuracy over standard RAG and reduced contradiction, but high-risk error and dangerous overconfidence remained elevated. Max-context prompting increased latency without closing the safety gap, and additional inference-time compute produced only limited gains. Worst-case analysis showed that clinically consequential errors concentrated in a small subset of questions. Clinical LLM safety is therefore not a passive consequence of scaling, but a deployment property shaped by evidence quality, retrieval design, context construction, and collective failure behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [51] [Human-Provenance Verification should be Treated as Labor Infrastructure in AI-Saturated Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03210)
*Erin McGurk,David Khachaturov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper）。核心主张：在生成式/智能体AI饱和的市场中，经核验的"人类在场"（human provenance）会成为类似凡勃伦商品的地位属性，产生"人类溯源溢价"（human-provenance premium）；因此AI治理应将人类溯源核验体系视为"劳动基础设施"（labor infrastructure），而非面向消费者的奢侈真实性标签。论文提出"表演性人性"（performative humanity）三分法——关系性在场劳动、审美溯源劳动、问责劳动——并以"构成性人类在场"（constitutive human presence）作为评估人机混合工作的标准。据此推导出一个有条件的"哑铃型"价值捕获结构假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式与智能体AI将许多标准化认知、创造与协调任务的生产边际成本压至极低，使"够用"（good-enough）合成替代品大规模可及，从而削弱了支撑中等层知识工作的稀缺性溢价（scarcity premium）。论文不主张AI凭空创造人类劳动的位置性价值，而是主张AI把原本局限于手工艺、关系性与奢侈市场的"人类溯源溢价"普遍化、激进化。由此产生两个未被现有文献充分回答的问题：(1) 为何人类劳动价值不是均匀崩塌，而是其价值基础被重组（从"输出本身有价值"转向"可见的人类在场有价值"）；(2) 溢价在经济的何处沉淀、由谁获取。政策动机在于：若溯源体系仅为高端消费市场设计，将加剧价值集中与劳动者议价权流失，因此需要一套面向劳动者的、可携带、隐私保护、可审计的溯源基础设施。

Method: 采用跨学科的理论综合与概念建构（立场论文范式），无实证数据集或实验。理论来源包括：任务导向的技能极化文献（Autor、Acemoglu & Restrepo）、技能偏向技术变迁、信息经济学中信息商品近零边际复制成本（Shapiro & Varian）、平台资本主义与租集中的政治经济学（Srnicek、Parrinello et al.）、凡勃伦 conspicuous consumption 与 Leibenstein/Bagwell-Bernheim 稀缺— conspicuous 需求、Spence 信号理论、Hirsch 位置性竞争、Darby & Karni 的信任品（credence goods）与形式化认证制度条件、Hochschild 情感劳动。实证材料以文献综述形式引用：Noy & Zhang（ChatGPT 专业写作随机实验）、Brynjolfsson et al.（客服AI辅助）、职业暴露研究、Fuchs et al. 手作标签效应、Bellaiche et al. 作者身份标签效应、Mandel & Imas 拍卖中"AI参与即贬值"的非线性心理污染效应、Cara 平台禁AI投稿、WGA West 披露条款。附录B给出Spencian分离均衡的简式形式化：条件 c_A > π > c_H 支撑"人制品"标签的分离均衡，AI饱和下 π 上升而 c_A 下降，故溯源基础设施的验证能力需与模仿技术同步才可维持均衡。附录C以示意图对比采猎者、中世纪庄园、俄国农奴制、工业/战后、工业后沙漏型到AI哑铃型的六种劳动秩序。附录A表格对四项核心主张给出证据等级分层（机制层面已支持／尚属合理但未直接证明／有界领域支持／前瞻性预测／规范性且未检验）。

Result: 1) 机制成立：AI以高固定成本、近零边际成本提供"够用"替代品，压低标准化认知/创意劳动的定价基准，经验依赖型工资梯度被压缩；因此被消灭的是稀缺性租金而非岗位本身。2) 提出表演性人类三分法及各自风险：关系性在场（被注意/被回应的关系本身为商品；受AI陪伴型产品侵蚀，但专业治疗、教育、高信任顾问场景结构性更强；伴随情感抽取与监控风险，且承担者多为女性、移民、低收入劳动者，溢价分配存疑）；审美溯源（价值源于"人类痕迹"叙事；最易被合成仿冒侵蚀，故验证基础设施成为承重结构）；问责劳动（价值源于承担法律与道德责任，因现行监管要求持牌人担责而具"法律而非经济"支撑的结构性地板，但受监管变动威胁）。3) 区分人类溯源溢价与凡勃伦效应：现有研究只支持"人类标签带来支付意愿水平上移"（preconditions），价格越高需求越多的宏观凡勃伦命题仍是前瞻性预测；Cara等仅为市场分割的初步证据而非持久溢价证据。4) 机制由三项结构属性相互放大：位置性属性、信任品属性（买方无法仅凭产出判断是人还是系统）、以及稀缺性。5) 经济含义：哑铃结构两端不对称——左端AI基础设施所有者的绝对租金持续扩大（且尾部向下延伸，依赖应用层与集成者未必获租）；右端人类在场溢价单位价值高但容量受限；中端被商品化。参照Acemoglu-Restrepo"so-so自动化"，劳动收入份额下降会压缩高端人类溢价的付费基础，故该市场主要由资本份额受益者与需要人类问责的机构支付，被挤压的中产在结构上被定价出局。6) 提出五项劳动基础设施设计原则：过程溯源而非仅内容溯源；混合工作中的人类角色证明（区分人类作者/审阅者/问责方/关系参与者）；隐私保护而非持续监控；跨平台与跨职业机构可携带；真实性评分与排序的可审计性，并附加"不得将溯源等同于AI内容检测"的负面要求（水印与检测技术仍存争议，NIST将其视为尚未解决的技术生态）。7) 回应四类反对意见：AI更多是互补而非替代（承认，但任务级互补不等于职业不贬值）；真实性可被模拟（以两阶段认证——先是社会性认证（熟人、委托、知名匠人），后是制度性认证（认证机构、水印、可信中介）——化解为时序而非致命矛盾）；核验沦为监控（以最小必要过程记录、情感监控与认证不相容来划界）；溢价被平台攫取或具排他性（要求可携带性、排序审计、劳动者集体治理，并指出全球南方、非正式与照护劳动者可能最难负担认证成本）。

Conclusion: 论文结论：AI饱和不会使劳动需求简单崩塌，而是重组劳动赖以保有价值的社会基础；AI治理的核心问题不是"人类劳动能否在自动化中存活"，而是"哪些形式的人类在场在经济、法律与社会上仍可辩护"。若溯源基础设施仅为溢价消费市场而建，将强化哑铃结构；若按劳动基础设施来建（跨平台可携带、可审计、隐私保护、可表示人类角色/同意/问责/报酬），则可能保全那些判断、问责、照护与作者身份构成其价值创造之组成部分的劳动者的议价权。论文强调哑铃假说是有条件预测而非必然：两项关键约束是（a）人类溢价层可能"单位价值深、就业容量浅"，只重分配价值而无法吸纳被挤出的中层；（b）最可能提供关系性在场与照护的劳动者，恰是因照护劳动的性别化与种族化组织而最难获取该溢价的人群。任何忽略这两点的政策论述都会高估人类溯源市场的福利收益。后续研究议程包括：将溯源体系按劳动基础设施设计；对高暴露职业评估AI是创造互补的人类任务还是在规模化替代既有判断；对真实性评分系统实施审计以确认其衡量真实服务质量而非强制平台可识别的情感表演规范；并让三类人类在场劳动的代表直接参与设计对其劳动进行核验、排序与定价的体系。

Abstract: We argue that AI-saturated markets are likely to create Veblen-good premiums, which we term human-provenance premiums, for verified human presence, and hence AI governance should treat human-provenance verification as labor infrastructure. Generative and agentic AI systems lower the cost of many standardized cognitive, creative, and coordination tasks, weakening the scarcity premiums that have supported much middle-tier knowledge work. We argue that this pressure may produce an asymmetric barbell-shaped structure of value capture in advanced economies: high-volume synthetic production controlled by owners of AI infrastructure at one pole, and scarce, high-status human labor valued for verified human presence at the other.
  We advance three claims. First, AI compresses the value of standardized middle-tier labor by making good-enough synthetic substitutes scalable at low marginal cost, hollowing out the middle of the skill distribution currently categorized by knowledge work. Second, this compression reallocates demand for human labor toward work valued for its visible human character. We term this performative humanity and distinguish three forms of labor: relational presence, aesthetic provenance, and accountability. Third, as these premiums depend on credible verification, AI governance should treat human-provenance systems as labor infrastructure rather than as luxury authenticity labels.
  To evaluate hybrid human-AI work, we propose constitutive human presence as the relevant standard: human labor retains premium value when human judgment, attention, accountability, authorship, or relational participation is not incidental to the output but constitutive of what is being purchased.

</details>


### [52] [Cheap Expertise: Mapping and Challenging Industry Perspectives in the Expert Data Gig Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03295)
*Robert Wolfe,Aayushi Dangol*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析五家人工智能数据标注组织及其CEO的公开传播内容，揭示了AI行业如何构建专业知识未来的愿景：即AI专业知识廉价且高效，人类专业知识被重新定位为可被提取和定价的资源，而机构专业知识则需被"解放"以融入AI系统。该研究警示这一趋势可能重塑白领工作形态和专业知识的社会价值体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI行业对专家标注数据需求的激增，一个以数据标注为形式的"专家零工经济"正在兴起。该研究旨在探究这一新兴行业如何通过公共传播渠道塑造并合理化其对"专业知识未来"的愿景，以及这种愿景对人类专家和社会制度可能产生的影响。

Method: 研究者收集并分析了五家行业数据标注组织及其CEO在社交媒体（Twitter/X、LinkedIn等）和播客公开露面中的沟通内容，采用质性文本分析方法探索这些组织对未来专业知识愿景的叙事框架与话语策略。

Result: 研究发现AI行业对专业知识前景的描述呈现三个核心隐喻：①AI专业知识是"廉价的"——因能提供比人类专业知识更高的投资回报率而具有优势；②人类专业知识被视为"可提取的资源"——其价值可相对于AI专业知识被评判和定价；③机构专业知识（如高校和企业持有的）被认为需要"解放或改革"——以便整合进最新AI系统。

Conclusion: 研究提出了若干"挑衅性"问题，呼吁学界与政策制定者思考社会应如何应对AI驱动的专家零工经济及其"廉价专业知识"愿景。暗示需要重新审视现有专业制度设计，以应对行业对专业知识价值的重新定义和再定价。

Abstract: Demand for expert-annotated data on the part of leading AI labs has created an expert gig economy with the potential to reshape white collar work and society's understanding of expertise. In this research, we study the vision for the future of expertise described in the public communication of five industry data annotation organizations and their CEOs, as reflected on social media feeds and public appearances on podcasts. We find that the industry envisions AI expertise as cheap, meaning that it can offer a better return on investment than human expertise. Human expertise, meanwhile, is viewed as an extractable resource, the value of which can be judged relative to AI expertise. Finally, institutional expertise (such as that created or possessed by universities and corporations) is viewed as in need of liberation or reform, such that it can be incorporated into the latest artificial intelligence systems. Our findings have implications for human experts, whose professional lives may be transformed and revalued by this industry, as well as for societal institutions that mediate expertise. We close this work with a series of provocations intended to elicit consideration of how society can best approach an AI-driven expert gig economy and the cheap expertise it intends to produce.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Distributive Justice: Hermeneutical Fairness in Ad Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03419)
*Camilla Quaresmini,Valentina Breschi,Jessica Leoni,Viola Schiaffonati,Mara Tanelli,Giulia De Pasquale*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文引入 Miranda Fricker 的认知不正义概念来分析在线广告公平性，提出认知公平约束和认知感知效用成本，将其整合到分配公平广告分配框架中，通过模拟发现分配约束与认知公平需要协同考虑，纯效用导向分配会损害弱势群体利益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告的公平性通常被形式化为分配正义问题，但广告仍可能通过内容本身以及接收者对广告的解释和接受方式造成伤害。传统的分配正义框架无法捕捉这种认知层面的不公正。

Method: 基于1986-1987年艾滋病广告评估调查的探索性相关模式，提出群体层面的认知公平约束和认知感知效用成本，并将其整合到现有的分配公平广告分配框架中，通过控制变量模拟探索经济效用、分配公平约束与认知成本之间的权衡。

Result: 模拟结果显示：纯效用导向分配导致对弱势群体的投放不足；分配约束在高认知风险情况下可在适度效用损失下降低认知成本；不加分配约束地加权认知成本可能产生集中于弱势群体的政策。这表明分配约束与认知公平需要协同考虑。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，应将在线广告的公平性分析扩展到分配公平之外，包含解释和接受的认知条件。单纯基于效用的分配会导致对弱势群体投放不足；分配约束可在适度效用损失下降低认知成本；而不加分配约束地加权认知成本可能产生集中于弱势群体的政策。这项研究为在线广告公平性研究开辟了新的认知正义维度。

Abstract: Fairness in online advertising is often formalized as a distributive justice problem, aiming to ensure that impressions, opportunities, or outcomes are allocated comparably across protected groups. Yet online advertising can still produce harms arising from ads' content and from how recipients interpret and uptake them. To capture this dimension, we draw on Miranda Fricker's notion of hermeneutical injustice. We model ad delivery as a mechanism that distributes interpretative resources and can fail in two ways: relevant concepts can be withheld through systematic under-exposure, leading to hermeneutical deprivation; and recipients may experience hermeneutical distortions when saturated with low-uptake or skewed framings. Grounded in exploratory correlational patterns from the AIDS Advertising Evaluation surveys (1986-1987), we introduce a group-level hermeneutical fairness constraint and a hermeneutically aware utility cost. We integrate them into a benchmark, utility-driven ad allocation framework that already enforces distributive justice, yielding a distributively fair, hermeneutically aware framework that prevents deprivation and distortion from concentrating within protected groups. Through controlled simulations, we explore trade-offs between economic utility, classical distributive fairness constraints, and hermeneutical cost. The results show that purely utility-based allocation drives under-delivery to the disadvantaged group. When the hermeneutical stakes of withholding ads are high, distributive constraints reduce hermeneutical cost at modest utility loss. Conversely, weighting hermeneutical cost without distributive constraints can yield policies concentrated on the disadvantaged group. These findings motivate expanding fairness analyses of online advertising beyond distributive notions to include epistemic conditions of interpretation and uptake.

</details>


### [54] [Brainrot: Deskilling and Addiction are Overlooked AI Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03512)
*Ilias Chalkidis,Anders Søgaard*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: The paper identifies a gap in AI safety and alignment research: existing work focuses on discrimination, harmful content, information hazards, and malicious use cases, while largely ignoring public concerns about cognitive and mental health risks from over-reliance on GenAI (e.g., deskilling, critical thinking atrophy, and addiction). The authors quantify this gap and propose extending safety and alignment work, alongside information campaigns and regulation, to address these overlooked risks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The AI safety and alignment literature has primarily focused on a limited set of harms (discrimination, harmful content, information hazards, malicious use cases), while the public discourse also highlights threats to cognition, mental health, and human welfare — such as deskilling, critical thinking atrophy, and addiction to GenAI systems. These latter risks are largely unaddressed in existing AI safety research.

Method: The paper (1) quantifies the discrepancy between current AI safety focus areas and public concerns, and (2) discusses initial approaches for addressing cognitive and mental health risks within the safety and alignment framework, including information campaigns and regulatory measures.

Result: N/A — This is an abstract describing the scope and direction of the paper rather than reporting completed results.

Conclusion: The paper proposes that safety and alignment work should expand to include cognitive and mental health concerns, and suggests information campaigns and regulation as mitigation strategies.

Abstract: The scope of AI safety and alignment work in generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) has so far mostly been limited to harms related to: (a) discrimination and hate speech, (b) harmful/inappropriate (violent, sexual, illegal) content, (c) information hazards, and (d) use cases related to malicious actors, such as cybersecurity, child abuse, and chemical, biological, radiological, and nuclear threats. The public conversation around AI, on the other hand, has also been focusing on threats to our cognition, mental health, and welfare at large, related to over-relying on new technologies, most recently, those related to GenAI. Examples include deskilling associated with cognitive offloading and the atrophy of critical thinking as a result of over-reliance on GenAI systems, and addiction associated with attachment and dependence on GenAI systems. Such risks are rarely addressed, if at all, in the AI safety and alignment literature. In this paper, we highlight and quantify this discrepancy and discuss some initial thoughts on how safety and alignment work could address cognitive and mental health concerns.
