In this setting, systems must consider multiple, context-dependent objectives, such as helpfulness, truthfulness, safety, privacy, calibration, non-manipulation, user preference, reversibility, and stakeholder impact, while determining which objectives are active, which are soft preferences, and which must function as hard or quasi-hard constraints. These examples are not intended as an exhaustive taxonomy: different domains and deployment settings may activate different objective dimensions and different conflict-resolution procedures. Our framework models AI behavior as a context-dependent choice rule over candidate actions, objective estimates, active constraints, stakeholders, uncertainty, and conflict-resolution procedures. We outline an implementation pathway based on decomposed objective representations, context-to-objective routing, hierarchical constraints, deliberative policy reasoning, controlled personalization, tool-use control, diagnostic evaluation, auditing, and post-deployment revision.

</details>


### [104] [From Intent to Execution: Composing Agentic Workflows with Agent Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03986)
*Kishan Athrey,Ramin Pishehvar,Brian Riordan,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化创建多智能体系统的框架，通过LLM规划器、两阶段信息检索智能体推荐器和批判智能体等模块，替代了传统的手动组合、选择和创建过程。实验结果表明，该方法在召回率、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统（MAS）的构建需要手动编排计划、手动选择智能体、手动创建执行图，这一过程费时费力且缺乏自动化支持。

Method: 提出了一个自动化框架，包含以下模块：LLM驱动的规划器、自然语言任务描述、动态调用图、智能体任务映射编排器、以及智能体推荐器（采用两阶段信息检索系统，包括快速检索器和基于LLM的重排序器）。此外，还引入了批判智能体对智能体和工具推荐进行整体性重新评估。

Result: 该方法在召回率上优于现有最优方法，且具有更好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。批判智能体的加入进一步提升了召回率得分。

Conclusion: 综合审查和修订基于任务的智能体选择是构建端到端多智能体系统的重要步骤。批判智能体的加入进一步提升了召回率，证明了对智能体和工具推荐进行整体性重新评估的必要性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built using AI agents fulfill a variety of user intents that may be used to design and build a family of related applications. However, the creation of such MAS currently involves manual composition of the plan, manual selection of appropriate agents, and manual creation of execution graphs. This paper introduces a framework for the automated creation of multi-agent systems which replaces multiple manual steps with an automated framework. The proposed framework consists of software modules and a workflow to orchestrate the requisite task- specific application. The modules include: an LLM-derived planner, a set of tasks described in natural language, a dynamic call graph, an orchestrator for map agents to tasks, and an agent recommender that finds the most suitable agent(s) from local and global agent registries. The agent recommender uses a two-stage information retrieval (IR) system comprising a fast retriever and an LLM-based re-ranker. We implemented a series of experiments exploring the choice of embedders, re- rankers, agent description enrichment, and supervising critique agent. We benchmarked this system end-to-end, evaluating the combination of planning, agent selection, and task completion, with our proposed approach. Our experimental results show that our approach outperforms the state-of-the- art in terms of the recall rate and is more robust and scalable compared to previous approaches. The critique agent holistically reevaluates both agent and tool recommendations against the overall plan. We show that the inclusion of the critique agent further enhances the recall score, proving that the comprehensive review and revision of task-based agent selection is an essential step in building end-to-end multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [105] [An Agent-Oriented Pluggable Experience-RAG Skill for Experience-Driven Retrieval Strategy Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03989)
*Dutao Zhang,Tian Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience-RAG Skill，通过将检索策略选择封装为可复用的智能体技能，实现动态适配不同任务的检索需求，在多个基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成系统假设单一固定检索流程足以应对所有异构任务，但事实问答、多跳推理、科学验证等不同任务表现出不同的检索偏好需求。

Method: 提出Experience-RAG Skill，一个位于智能体与检索器池之间的面向智能体的可插拔检索编排层。该技能通过分析当前场景、查询经验记忆、选择合适的检索策略，并向智能体返回结构化证据来实现检索编排。

Result: 在固定候选池条件下，于BeIR/nq、BeIR/hotpotqa和BeIR/scifact三个数据集上达到0.8924的整体nDCG@10，优于固定单检索器基线，并与自适应RAG风格路由方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 检索策略选择可以被封装为可复用的智能体技能，而非硬编码到上层工作流中。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems often assume that one fixed retrieval pipeline is sufficient across heterogeneous tasks, yet factoid question answering, multi-hop reasoning, and scientific verification exhibit different retrieval preferences. We present Experience-RAG Skill, an agent-oriented pluggable retrieval orchestration layer positioned between the agent and the retriever pool. The proposed skill analyzes the current scene, consults an experience memory, selects an appropriate retrieval strategy, and returns structured evidence to the agent. Under a fixed candidate pool, Experience-RAG Skill achieves an overall nDCG@10 of 0.8924 on BeIR/nq, BeIR/hotpotqa, and BeIR/scifact, outperforming fixed single-retriever baselines and remaining competitive with Adaptive-RAG-style routing. The results suggest that retrieval strategy selection can be productively encapsulated as a reusable agent skill rather than being hard-coded in the upper workflow.

</details>


### [106] [SymptomAI: Towards a Conversational AI Agent for Everyday Symptom Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04012)
*Joseph Breda,Fadi Yousif,Beszel Hawkins,Marinela Cotoi,Miao Liu,Ray Luo,Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen,Mike Schaekermann,Samuel Schmidgall,Xin Liu,Girish Narayanswamy,Samuel Solomon,Maxwell A. Xu,Xiaoran Fan,Longfei Shangguan,Anran Wang,Bhavna Daryani,Buddy Herkenham,Cara Tan,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,John B. Hernandez,Quang Duong,Yun Liu,Zach Wasson,Dimitrios Antos,Bob Lou,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Anupam Pathak,Nichole Young-Lin,Jake Sunshine,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymptomAI是对话式AI患者访谈系统，在约1.4万人的Fitbit用户中验证，其诊断准确率显著超过临床医生，并证明系统化的症状采集优于用户自主描述。该研究还揭示了可穿戴设备生理数据与疾病状态的强关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于精心策划的医学案例研究，但这些复杂场景难以反映患者在日常生活中报告症状的真实情况。需要评估语言模型在真实世界人群中的表现。

Method: 通过Fitbit应用部署SymptomAI对话式AI智能体，采用随机对照设计（N=13,917）。子集中1,228名参与者提供临床诊断标签，517名参与者的对话由临床医生小组进行注释（共250多小时）。在盲法随机比较中评估AI的DDx准确性，并与给定相同对话的独立临床医生进行对比。同时分析了超过500,000天的可穿戴设备数据与约400种疾病的相关性。

Result: SymptomAI的DDx准确性显著优于独立临床医生（OR=2.47, p<0.001）；采用专门症状访谈的智能体策略表现显著优于基线用户引导对话（p<0.001）；急性感染与生理指标变化存在强相关性（如流感OR>7）。

Conclusion: 尽管受限于自我报告的真实标签，但研究结果表明，专门且完整的症状访谈优于用户引导的症状讨论（这是大多数消费者LLM的默认模式）。

Abstract: Language models excel at diagnostic assessments on currated medical case-studies and vignettes, performing on par with, or better than, clinical professionals. However, existing studies focus on complex scenarios with rich context making it difficult to draw conclusions about how these systems perform for patients reporting symptoms in everyday life. We deployed SymptomAI, a set of conversational AI agents for end-to-end patient interviewing and differential diagnosis (DDx), via the Fitbit app in a study that randomized participants (N=13,917) to interact with five AI agents. This corpus captures diverse communication and a realistic distribution of illnesses from a real world population. A subset of 1,228 participants reported a clinician-provided diagnosis, and 517 of these were further evaluated by a panel of clinicians during over 250 hours of annotation. SymptomAI DDx were significantly more accurate (OR = 2.47, p < 0.001) than those from independent clinicians given the same dialogue in a blinded randomized comparison. Moreover, agentic strategies which conduct a dedicated symptom interview that elicit additional symptom information before providing a diagnosis, perform substantially better than baseline, user-guided conversations (p < 0.001). An auxiliary analysis on 1,509 conversations from a general US population panel validated that these results generalize beyond wearable device users. We used SymptomAI diagnoses as labels for all 13,917 participants to analyze over 500,000 days of wearable metrics across nearly 400 unique conditions. We identified strong associations between acute infections and physiological shifts (e.g., OR > 7 for influenza). While limited by self-reported ground truth, these results demonstrate the benefits of a dedicated and complete symptom interview compared to a user-guided symptom discussion, which is the default of most consumer LLMs.

</details>


### [107] [Redefining AI Red Teaming in the Agentic Era: From Weeks to Hours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04019)
*Raja Sekhar Rao Dheekonda,Will Pearce,Nick Landers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍基于Dreadnode SDK的AI红队智能体系统，通过自然语言接口将红队工作从周级压缩到小时级。该系统统一了传统ML模型对抗样本测试和生成式AI越狱测试的框架，涵盖45+攻击方法、450+转换操作和130+评分器。在Meta Llama Scout上验证实现85%攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI红队方法需要操作者手动编写特定库的代码，工作流程繁琐（需数周时间构建），且结果不满意时需重建流程，导致实际探测目标的时间远少于构建工作流的时间。

Method: 构建基于45+攻击方法、450+转换操作和130+评分器的统一框架；通过自然语言接口让操作者描述目标，系统自动完成攻击选择、转换组合、执行和报告生成。

Result: 在Meta Llama Scout上实现85%攻击成功率，严重度达1.0，且无需人工开发代码。

Conclusion: 通过Dreadnode SDK和AI智能体架构，将AI红队攻击从周级工作压缩到小时级；在Llama Scout上实现85%攻击成功率，证明框架有效性。

Abstract: AI systems are entering critical domains like healthcare, finance, and defense, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. While AI red teaming is a primary defense, current approaches force operators into manual, library-specific workflows. Operators spend weeks hand-crafting workflows - assembling attacks, transforms, and scorers. When results fall short, workflows must be rebuilt. As a result, operators spend more time constructing workflows than probing targets for security and safety vulnerabilities.
  We introduce an AI red teaming agent built on the open-source Dreadnode SDK. The agent creates workflows grounded in 45+ adversarial attacks, 450+ transforms, and 130+ scorers. Operators can probe multi-agent systems, multilingual, and multimodal targets, focusing on what to probe rather than how to implement it.
  We make three contributions: 1. Agentic interface. Operators describe goals in natural language via the Dreadnode TUI (Terminal User Interface). The agent handles attack selection, transform composition, execution, and reporting, letting operators focus on red teaming. Weeks compress to hours. 2. Unified framework. A single framework for probing traditional ML models (adversarial examples) and generative AI systems (jailbreaks), removing the need for separate libraries. 3. Llama Scout case study. We red team Meta Llama Scout and achieve an 85% attack success rate with severity up to 1.0, using zero human-developed code

</details>


### [108] [OpenSeeker-v2: Pushing the Limits of Search Agents with Informative and High-Difficulty Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04036)
*Yuwen Du,Rui Ye,Shuo Tang,Keduan Huang,Xinyu Zhu,Yuzhu Cai,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文表明，仅通过监督微调（SFT）配合高质量训练数据，学术团队即可训练出超越工业级大厂的搜索智能体。通过三项数据合成改进（扩大知识图谱、扩展工具集、严格低步数过滤），使用仅10.6k数据点训练的OpenSeeker-v2在4个基准上超越Tongyi DeepResearch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界训练前沿搜索智能体需要大量资源（预训练+持续预训练+SFT+强化学习），门槛过高。学术团队缺乏资源跟进。本文旨在探索是否可以通过简化的SFT方法+高质量轨迹数据达到同等甚至更好的效果。

Method: 三项简单数据合成改进：(1) 扩大知识图谱规模以支持更丰富的探索；(2) 扩展工具集规模以提供更广泛的功能；(3) 严格低步数过滤以确保数据质量。仅使用10.6k数据点进行SFT训练。

Result: OpenSeeker-v2（30B ReAct范式）在4个基准上达到SOTA：BrowseComp 46.0%、BrowseComp-ZH 58.1%、Humanity's Last Exam 34.6%、xbench 78.0%，全面超越Tongyi DeepResearch（43.4%、46.7%、32.9%、75.0%）。

Conclusion: 高质量、高难度的信息性轨迹配合简单SFT可达到出人意料的强大效果。OpenSeeker-v2是首个在同等模型规模和范式下、由纯学术团队仅用SFT训练达到SOTA的搜索智能体。研究表明前沿搜索智能体研究可以被更多社区 접근。

Abstract: Deep search capabilities have become an indispensable competency for frontier Large Language Model (LLM) agents, yet their development remains dominated by industrial giants. The typical industry recipe involves a highly resource-intensive pipeline spanning pre-training, continual pre-training (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). In this report, we show that when fueled with informative and high-difficulty trajectories, a simple SFT approach could be surprisingly powerful for training frontier search agents. By introducing three simple data synthesis modifications: scaling knowledge graph size for richer exploration, expanding the tool set size for broader functionality, and strict low-step filtering, we establish a stronger baseline. Trained on merely 10.6k data points, our OpenSeeker-v2 achieves state-of-the-art performance across 4 benchmarks (30B-sized agents with ReAct paradigm): 46.0% on BrowseComp, 58.1% on BrowseComp-ZH, 34.6% on Humanity's Last Exam, and 78.0% on xbench, surpassing even Tongyi DeepResearch trained with heavy CPT+SFT+RL pipeline, which achieves 43.4%, 46.7%, 32.9%, and 75.0%, respectively. Notably, OpenSeeker-v2 represents the first state-of-the-art search agent within its model scale and paradigm to be developed by a purely academic team using only SFT. We are excited to open-source the OpenSeeker-v2 model weights and share our simple yet effective findings to make frontier search agent research more accessible to the community.

</details>


### [109] [An Information-Geometric Framework for Stability Analysis of Large Language Models under Entropic Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24076)
*Hikmat Karimov,Rahid Zahid Alekberli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受热力学启发的「Kerimov–Alekberli 稳定性框架」，用效用 U、外部不确定性（熵）S 与两个内部结构代理（内部整合 I_int、对齐反思能力 C_a）构造复合稳定性评分，通过在分母中引入「内部阻尼项」B=0.5·I_int+0.5·C_a 实现熵项的非线性衰减，得到 E*=U−S/(1+B)。基于 IST-20 基准的 4 模型×20 场景共 80 个观测，E* 在全部观测上均高于线性基线 E=αU−βS，平均提升 0.0299（95% CI 0.0247–0.0351），且提升幅度在高熵条件下更大。作者明确声明这只是可解释的建模抽象与基准层面的概念验证，不主张物理定律或机器伦理学理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被部署到高风险与运维场景后，仅凭聚合准确率难以刻画系统可靠性：模型在名义条件下表现优异，但在歧义、部分信息、竞争约束与推理不稳定等扰动下可能输出"流畅但不可靠"的内容（幻觉、不一致、对提示敏感）。同时，AI 治理框架（NIST AI RMF、OECD、UNESCO、EDPS 等）普遍要求超越能力指标，转向评估鲁棒性、可控性、校准与压力条件下的行为。因此需要一个能把外部不确定性与内部结构一并纳入的、紧凑且可解释的评估透镜，以补充而非取代现有基准。作者同时借用了四条文献线索：信息论与计算热力学（Shannon、Landauer、Bennett）、复杂系统的内部组织与整合（Tononi、Friston）、AI 伦理与治理、以及 LLM 可靠性/事实性实证研究。

Method: 框架构建：（1）基线线性式 E=αU−βS，U 为归一化任务效用，S 为归一化外部熵/无序度；（2）内部屏障项 B=γ·I_int+λ·C_a，I_int 为内部一致性/连贯代理，C_a 为校准、拒答与自我调节等修正性对齐行为代理，二者均非直接可观测内部状态，而是由模型输出与元数据操作化得到的基准代理；（3）阻尼分母 D=1+B=1+0.5·I_int+0.5·C_a；（4）广义稳定性 E*=U−S/D，使熵的影响被内部结构削弱。方法设定：二次分析，不重新设计或执行 IST-20 基准，仅对既有基准输出与结构化元数据做确定性后处理；系数为基准协议事先固定（ex ante），非本样本拟合，定位为确认性-描述性而非预测性分析。统计分析：描述统计、模型级聚合、80 观测上的 E* 与 E 配对比较（配对 t 检验与 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验双报告）、熵/阻尼分母/稳定性之间的相关分析、阻尼系数敏感性分析（γ,λ∈{0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0} 网格，基准设定 γ=λ=0.5，α、β 固定）。

Result: 描述统计（n=80）：Ū=0.9745(SD 0.0180)、S̄=0.0639(SD 0.0514)、Ī_int=0.8785、C̄_a=0.9018、D̄=1.8901(SD 0.0448)、Ē=0.9106(SD 0.0654)、Ē*=0.9404(SD 0.0426，即离散度更小)、Δ̄=0.0299(SD 0.0234，最小值 0.0010)。配对比较：E* 在全部 80 个观测上均高于 E，配对 t 检验 p=2.22×10⁻¹⁸，Wilcoxon p=7.84×10⁻¹⁵。模型级：Grok-3 Ē*=0.9830（Δ̄=0.0055）、GPT-4o 0.9620（0.0215）、DeepSeek-V3 0.9424（0.0246）、Gemini-1.5 0.8744（0.0679，因熵负荷最高 S̄=0.1480）。相关：r(S,E)=−0.9809、r(S,E*)=−0.9545、r(D,E*)=0.3242 (p=0.0033)、r(Δ,S)=0.9997（作者承认部分为代数结构性的"一致性检验"而非独立发现）。敏感性：非平凡参数下 Δ̄ 在 0.0113–0.0407 之间单调随阻尼系数上升；γ=λ=0 时 Δ=0，单独任一系数为 0 时另一非零仍使全部 80 观测 Δ>0；模型排序在所有设定下保持一致。

Conclusion: 结论：框架在受控压力基准下能生成稳定、可解释、可区分的评分行为——高名义性能不足以刻画压力下的可靠性，标准条件下表现相近的系统在引入不确定性/扰动后可能分化；非线性阻尼形式在不确定压力越高时越具区分度。作者将本文定位为「基于基准的概念验证」，明确不主张机器行为的物理定律、不主张 AI 对齐的完整理论、不主张物理等价，也不声称验证了对真实部署失效或伤害的预测能力。局限：I_int 与 C_a 完全依赖基准自定义代理（替代操作化可能改变数值与排序，缺乏独立验证）；样本仅 80 个观测、4 个模型，泛化性有限；无外部标注的失效/事故数据，故只能作为基准评分模型而非运维风险预测器；Δ 与 S 的强相关部分是构造性的。未来方向：跨多基准更大规模验证（推理、事实性、工具使用、对抗提示）；以校准误差、自一致性率、拒答精确率、检索验证成功率等独立可测量替换基准代理；用部署时序遥测检验稳定性得分能否追踪漂移与事故风险；把固定启发式系数改为学习式或贝叶斯参数化；最终检验该类紧凑状态式指标能否成为运行时监督、比较审计与可信 AI 保证的组件。数据与代码仅"经合理请求可提供"，尚未公开可复现包。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes and operational settings, evaluation strategies based solely on aggregate accuracy are often insucient to characterize system reliability. This study proposes a thermodynamic inspired modeling framework for analyzing the stability of LLM outputs under conditions of uncertainty and perturbation. The framework introduces a composite stability score that integrates task utility, entropy as a measure of external uncertainty, and two internal structural proxies: internal integration and aligned reective capacity. Rather than interpreting these quantities as physical variables, the formulation is intended as an interpretable abstraction that captures how internal structure may modulate the impact of disorder on model behavior. Using the IST-20 benchmarking protocol and associated metadata, we analyze 80 modelscenario observations across four contemporary LLMs. The proposed formulation consistently yields higher stability scores than a reduced utilityentropy baseline, with a mean improvement of 0.0299 (95% CI: 0.02470.0351). The observed gain is more pronounced under higher entropy conditions, suggesting that the framework captures a form of nonlinear attenuation of uncertainty. We do not claim a fundamental physical law or a complete theory of machine ethics. Instead, the contribution of this work is a compact and interpretable modeling perspective that connects uncertainty, performance, and internal structure within a unied evaluation lens. The framework is intended to complement existing benchmarking approaches and to support ongoing discussions in AI safety, reliability, and governance.

</details>


### [110] [The Kerimov-Alekberli Model: An Information-Geometric Framework for Real-Time System Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24083)
*Hikmat Karimov,Rahid Zahid Alekberli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Kerimov-Alekberli模型，通过建立非平衡热力学与随机控制之间的形式同构，将AI安全重新定义为信息几何框架，利用KL散度和Fisher信息度量量系统异常，并基于Landauer原理证明对抗扰动通过增加信息熵做功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全研究依赖启发式规则，缺乏严格的物理基础。现有方法无法量化伦理违规的物理本质，需要从热力学角度建立可量化的安全范式。

Method: 1) 建立非平衡热力学与随机控制的形式同构；2) 定义系统异常为黎曼流形上的偏差；3) 使用KL散度作为主度量，Fisher信息度量作为动态阈值；4) 基于Landauer原理证明对抗扰动的物理做功机制。

Result: 在NSL-KDD数据集和无人机轨迹仿真中，基于FPT触发器实现实时检测，获得高准确率、低FPR等良好性能指标。

Conclusion: 该研究为AI安全提供了严格的热力学基础，将伦理违规锚定在可量化的物理做功和熵信息上，实现从启发式规则向热力学稳定性范式的转变。

Abstract: This study introduces the Kerimov-Alekberli model, a novel information-geometric framework that redefines AI safety by formally linking non-equilibrium thermodynamics to stochastic control for the ethical alignment of autonomous systems. By establishing a formal isomorphism between non-equilibrium thermodynamics and stochastic control, we define systemic anomalies as deviations from a Riemannian manifold. The model utilizes the Kullback-Leibler divergence as the primary metric, governed by a dynamic threshold derived from the Fisher Information Metric. We further ground this framework in the Landauer Principle, proving that adversarial perturbations perform measurable physical work by increasing the system's informational entropy. Validation on the NSL-KDD dataset and unmanned aerial vehicle trajectory simulations demonstrated that our model achieves effective real-time detection via the FPT trigger, with strong performance metrics (e.g., high accuracy and low FPR) on benchmark datasets. This study provides a rigorous physical foundation for AI safety, transitioning from heuristic, rule-based ethical frameworks to a thermodynamics-based stability paradigm by grounding ethical violations in quantifiable physical work and entropic information.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [MARS-DA: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Risk-Aware Multi-Agent Bidding in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03142)
*Jiayi Chen,Xuan Zhang,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 MARS-DA：一个“经理—工人”式的分层多智能体强化学习框架，用于电力现货市场双结算（日前 DA / 实时 RT）环境下的风险感知报价。作者同时开源了一个基于 PJM 2018–2025 真实数据的高保真 Gymnasium 环境 StrategicBiddingEnv。框架核心是顶层 Meta-Controller（PPO）按市场状态动态混合两个冻结的子策略输出——保守的 Safe Agent（专注 DA 收益）与激进的 Speculator Agent（专注 RT 套利），并用带二次惩罚项的凹型风险调整奖励（λ_risk=5.0）驱动“自动熔断式”的逆势对冲。实验显示其在两个测试期均取得最优风险调整收益，Sharpe 分别为 0.978 与 0.270，最大回撤 2.84% 与 8.12%，远优于 Vanilla PPO/SAC、CVaR-PPO 与 MPC 启发式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新能源渗透率上升使批发电价波动加剧，双结算机制将发电商暴露于日前（DA）锁价与实时（RT）偏差结算之间的跨期风险。现有路线各有缺陷：随机/鲁棒优化等模型方法依赖显式不确定性模型与反复重优化，可扩展性与适应性差；深度强化学习方法多为扁平结构、固定策略，易过拟合特定市场状态且难以在线权衡风险—收益，无法处理市场状态切换（regime shift），也缺乏对 DA–RT 价差随机性的建模。此外，电力系统已有基准环境（Grid2Op、RL2Grid）聚焦电网运行或单一市场参与，缺少双结算协同报价的标准化测试床，使通用方法与风险敏感方法的比较缺乏统一平台。

Method: 1) 环境：StrategicBiddingEnv，基于 PJM DataMiner API 采集 2018–2022 五年数据（DA/RT LMP、实际负荷与 DA 负荷预测），并融合 Open-Meteo 气象（温度、风速）与 EIA 天然气日价作为价格领先指标；2024/9–2025/9 作为第二测试期。物理对象为 100 MW 燃气机组，含最小稳燃、爬坡率、启停成本与约束违规罚金。动作是连续分配比 α∈[0,1]，q_DA=α·P_max，q_RT=P_max−q_DA，即未锁定 DA 的容量自动进入 RT 形成投机敞口；利润含两段结算收入、边际成本、启动成本与罚金；状态包含 DA 价格、24h 滚动波动率、负荷预测、机组开停与最小上/下时限计数、上一出力以及时间周期编码。数据按时间顺序严格划分以防泄漏（缺失 <4h 用线性插值，更长用季节平均）。
2) 分层架构：Manager-Worker。所有模块基于 PPO。第一阶段“University Phase”用“利润感知角色强制”预训练异质子策略：Safe Agent 奖励 r_safe=π·α−|π|(1−α)λ_role，惩罚其使用 RT；Speculator 奖励 r_spec=π(1−α)−|π|αλ_role，惩罚其锁定 DA，以避免“盲目追求电量份额”的非盈利行为。预训练后冻结参数。
3) Meta-Controller：输出混合权重 w=[w_safe, w_spec]，和为 1，最终动作 A_t=w_safe·a_safe+w_spec·a_spec；用 PPO clipped 目标（ε=0.2）与风险调整奖励 r_meta=π/1000−(λ_risk/2)(π/100)²（λ_risk=5.0），惩罚利润幅值、抑制“彩票型”赌博行为，形成对收益方差的代理。
4) 评估：10 次独立运行 × 200 episode，指标为 Sharpe、Sortino、最大回撤 MDD、Regime Alignment Score 与分配熵；基线为 Vanilla PPO(NNSF)、Vanilla SAC、CVaR-PPO(α=0.05)、RollingOpt(MPC)，并做异质/同质、动态/静态权重、3 智能体、最佳单智能体四组消融。

Result: 测试期 1（2022，高燃料价与疫后复苏）：MARS-DA Sharpe 0.978、Sortino 3.142、最大回撤 2.84%，风险调整指标最优（CVaR-PPO 0.697、RollingOpt 0.646、NNSF-PPO 0.545、Vanilla SAC 0.424），且 RollingOpt 回撤高达 36.78%；累计收益 60.1M 略低于其他方法（67.6M），说明其以略低绝对收益换取更低尾部风险。测试期 2（2024–2025，分布外、高波动、更高新能源渗透）：MARS-DA 累计收益 4.45M 最高，Sharpe 0.270 最高，Sortino 1.808 最高，MDD 仅 8.12%，而各基线 MDD 均超过 55%（CVaR-PPO 55.91%、RollingOpt 61.63%、SAC 55.48%、NNSF 60.18%）；滚动 Sharpe 恢复最快、峰值约 0.55。学习曲线上 MARS-DA 收敛更快、渐近 Sharpe 约 1.0–1.1，高于基线 0.85–0.95。机制分析：分配熵 0.517 表明系统确实在两个子策略间切换而非坍缩；Regime Alignment Score 为 −0.075，Speculator 权重与波动率呈弱负相关，说明学到的是逆势对冲——极端波动（价格负偏、可能暴跌）时迅速把权重切向 Safe Agent，充当自动熔断器，而在中等波动区间才启用投机。消融显示：静态 50/50 加权虽总收益更高（77.5M vs 64.3M）但 Sharpe 降至 0.458、回撤升至 3.69%，证明增益来自状态依赖的动态切换而非简单集成；同质化（Vanilla SAC）Sharpe 0.424；加入 Neutral 第三个智能体后降至 0.601、回撤 4.04%（协调复杂度上升、年末性能崩溃），说明双智能体是策略多样性与训练稳定性的最佳平衡；相较最佳单智能体 CVaR-PPO（0.697）分层系统仍占优，表明 Meta-Controller 能获取保守策略在稳定期错过的投机上收益。

Conclusion: MARS-DA 通过“去中心化专用子策略 + 集中式动态加权”的分层设计，把出价策略学习与市场状态适应解耦，克服了单体 RL 策略的脆弱性与刚性优化模型的自适应性不足，在 PJM 真实数据上取得最优风险调整回报，并成为唯一在分布外压力测试中避免灾难性亏损的方法；其韧性来源于学到的逆势对冲（极端波动时自动削减投机敞口）。论文的意义在于：开源了双结算市场的高保真基准环境，使通用与风险敏感 RL 可被公平比较；并表明层次化风控结构是新能源主导的高波动电力市场中实现自主、安全、盈利参与的可行路径。需要指出的局限：消融章节数值与主表存在不一致（如静态权重对比中出现 Sharpe 0.920、MDD 0.90% 与表 1 的 0.978/2.84%、表 2 的 0.458/3.69% 并存），Regime Alignment Score 的定义在评估指标段与结果段也不一致；此外动作仅由分配比 α 决定而机组开停状态仅存在于状态中，启停成本与物理约束的建模仍较简化，环境与单一燃气机组的简化假设也限制了结论的外推性。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of renewable energy has introduced substantial volatility into wholesale electricity markets, complicating the optimal bidding strategies for power producers. Traditional Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches often struggle to balance profit maximization with risk management, frequently overfitting to specific market conditions or failing to account for the stochastic spread between Day-Ahead (DA) and Real-Time (RT) settlements. To address these challenges, this paper makes two primary contributions. First, we introduce and open-source a high-fidelity gymnasium environment for two-settlement electricity market bidding. Grounded in extensive empirical data from the PJM Interconnection, the environment explicitly models the interplay between DA commitments and RT deviations, providing a standardized testbed for general and risk-sensitive agents. Second, we propose MARS-DA (Multi-Agent Regime-Switching for Day-Ahead markets), a novel hierarchical framework that orchestrates distinct sub-policies for risk management and profit seeking. MARS-DA utilizes a top-level Meta-Controller to dynamically blend the actions of two specialized base agents: a "Safe Agent" that optimizes for reliable DA allocation and a "Speculator Agent" that targets volatile RT arbitrage opportunities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MARS-DA achieves superior risk-adjusted returns compared to state-of-the-art baselines while maintaining robust regime alignment during periods of extreme market volatility.

</details>


### [112] [Enwar 3.0: An Agentic Multi-Modal LLM Orchestrator for Situation-Aware Beamforming, Blockage Prediction, and Handover Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03215)
*Ahmad M. Nazar,Abdulkadir Celik,Asmaa Abdallah,Mohamed Y. Selim,Daji Qiao,Ahmed M. Eltawil*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Enwar 3.0 is an environment-aware reasoning framework for vehicular mmWave networks that uses multi-modal sensing and agentic LLMs to perform predictive beamforming, blockage detection, and handover management. The system detects sensor impairments in real-time and coordinates specialized agents for beam selection and environment perception.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maintaining robust mmWave connectivity in vehicular networks faces challenges from environmental dynamics, sensor degradation, and link variability, requiring real-time adaptation capabilities.

Method: The framework integrates: (1) a synthetic degradation pipeline for training sensor health classifiers; (2) a primed LLM with chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting and human-in-the-loop feedback; (3) multi-agent orchestration for beam selection, blockage forecasting, and environment perception; (4) dynamic sensor-specific model loading based on environmental context. Evaluated across 15 sensor combinations.

Result: Sensor degradation classifier achieves >99% accuracy; beam selection accuracy exceeds 88%; blockage F1-scores surpass 98%; reasoning correctness reaches 87% on complex decision prompts.

Conclusion: Enwar 3.0 establishes a scalable foundation for LLM-integrated wireless systems capable of real-time reasoning, perception, and adaptation in vehicular environments.

Abstract: Maintaining robust millimeter-wave (mmWave) connectivity in vehicular networks requires real-time adaptation to environmental dynamics, sensor degradation, and link variability. This paper presents Enwar 3.0, an environment-aware reasoning framework that unifies multi-modal sensing, agentic large language models (LLMs), and context-driven model selection for predictive beamforming, blockage detection, and handover management. Building upon prior iterations of Enwar, the proposed architecture integrates a classifier-driven assessment of sensor health with a primed LLM that orchestrates multiple specialized agents through structured, task-aware prompting. A novel synthetic degradation pipeline enables the training of a sensor degradation classifier that detects real-time impairments across camera, radar, LiDAR, and GPS inputs, achieving over 99% accuracy. The LLM, trained via chain-of-thought (CoT) priming and human-in-the-loop feedback, coordinates agent calls for beam selection, blockage forecasting, and environment perception while dynamically loading sensor-specific models based on environmental context. Extensive evaluations across 15 sensor combinations demonstrate that Enwar 3.0 delivers state-of-the-art performance in both predictive accuracy and interpretability, with beam selection accuracy exceeding 88%, blockage F1-scores surpassing 98%, and reasoning correctness reaching 87% on complex decision prompts. This work establishes a scalable foundation for LLM-integrated wireless systems that reason, perceive, and adapt in real-time.

</details>


### [113] [Coordination as an Architectural Layer for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03310)
*Maksym Nechepurenko,Pavel Shuvalov*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体LLM系统的协调层抽象为可独立配置的架构元素，通过固定LLM、工具和提示模板仅改变协调配置的对照实验，验证Murphy分解可识别配置签名。实验覆盖100个Polymarket市场，3/5预测成立，2种配置主导Pareto前沿，证明协调层值得作为独立研究对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在生产中失败率达41%-87%，主因是协调缺陷而非基础模型能力；现有方法仅做失败模式枚举或提供声明式编排框架，缺乏从协调配置到可预测失败模式签名的原则性映射。

Method: 信息控制实验设计：固定LLM、工具、输出上限和提示模板，仅改变协调配置（5种），以每题总计算量为内生架构输出；采用Brier分数的Murphy分解分离校准与判别力；100个Polymarket二元市场测试+Bootstrap功效投影；Foresight Arena实时部署验证。

Result: 3/5预指定预测方向成立；2种配置在Pareto前沿占优（成本-质量权衡）；Bootstrap区间可分离共识对齐与其它配置，但Bonferroni校正后配对检验不显著（n=100）；Pareto前沿存在且可识别。

Conclusion: 协调应作为可配置的架构层独立处理；Murphy分解可分离校准与判别能力；五种协调配置留下可区分特征；3/5预测得到验证，2种配置在Pareto前沿占优；作为方法论验证的首次实例化，非跨模型通用结论。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems fail in production at rates between 41% and 87%, mostly due to coordination defects rather than base-model capability. Existing responses split between cataloguing failure modes empirically and shipping declarative orchestration frameworks as engineering tools; neither delivers a principled mapping from coordination configuration to predictable failure-mode signature. We argue that coordination should be treated as a configurable architectural layer, separable from agent logic and from information access, enabling architectural reasoning rather than only engineering productivity.
  We instantiate this with an information-controlled design on prediction markets: a single LLM, fixed tools, fixed per-call output cap, and fixed prompt template across five reference coordination configurations, with total compute per question treated as an endogenous architectural output. The Murphy decomposition of the Brier score separates calibration from discriminative power, so configurations leave distinguishable signatures even when aggregate scores coincide.
  On 100 Polymarket binary markets resolved after the model's training cutoff (claude-opus-4-6) we report Murphy signatures, a cost-quality Pareto frontier, category-conditioned analysis, and a bootstrap power-projection. Three of five pre-specified predictions are upheld in direction; two configurations dominate the Pareto frontier within this regime; exploratory bootstrap intervals separate consensus alignment from others, though pairwise tests do not survive Bonferroni correction at n=100. We also deploy the same configurations as live agents on Foresight Arena under web-search-enabled conditions, as an on-chain replication channel accumulating in parallel. Harness, trace dataset, and production agents are released. We position this as a methodology-validating first instantiation, not a general cross-model claim.

</details>


### [114] [MemFlow: Intent-Driven Memory Orchestration for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03312)
*Jiayi Chen,Yingcong Li,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MemFlow 是一个免训练（training-free）的“意图路由 + 确定性证据编译”记忆编排框架：Router Agent 按查询意图把请求分发到三个专门记忆层级（Profile Lookup / Targeted Retrieval / Deep Reasoning），Memory Agent 在模型之外完成检索、时间排序、冲突消解等确定性预处理并按层级感知预算打包上下文，Answer Agent 生成答案，Validator Agent 在答案缺乏证据支撑时升级到更重层级重试。在冻结的 Qwen3-1.7B 上，MemFlow 于 LongMemEval、LoCoMo、LongBench（N=4,236）取得 52.4% 平均准确率，接近全上下文 SLM 基线的 2 倍（+23.4pp），较 RAG/ReAct 高 +6.2pp，而最终答案上下文仅约 2,223 token（比 ReAct 少 3.9×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程多轮记忆代理存在三类部署难题：全上下文提示导致上下文溢出与"中间遗忘"；统一平面检索（RAG）对偏好、时间线、知识更新、多会话综合等结构性差异的查询一刀切，暴露于噪声证据；开放式 agent 循环（ReAct 类）在 sub-3B 规模下易产生幻觉工具调用与断裂推理轨迹。作者的核心论点是：SLM 记忆失败在很大程度上源于“记忆操作错配”（intent-routing mismatch）——不仅要知道检索什么，更要知道该查询在结构上需要哪一种记忆操作（检索模式、证据变换、上下文预算）。而 SLM 自身无法可靠地通过开放式推理自我编排这些操作。因此需要把记忆规划从 SLM 内部外化到一个有界、可确定执行的多智能体流水线中。已有工作各有缺口：提示压缩（LongLLMLingua）任务无关，Self-RAG/CRAG 仍把编排权交给模型，MemGPT/Mem0/Zep 依赖模型主导的记忆访问或相似度检索，MEM1 需任务特定强化学习；针对冻结 SLM 同时处理意图分类、检索专门化、上下文预算与接地验证的训练无关框架仍然缺失。

Method: 形式化上，MemFlow 令 O 为有限的有类型记忆操作集合，每个操作规定检索模式、确定性证据变换、打包预算、答案提示与校验/升级规则；Router 估计 π(q)∈O，之后执行路径完全确定。单次查询最多 4 次 SLM 调用。

（1）Router Agent：采用三层级联（规则层→单次 SLM 分类→关键词启发式兜底），将查询划分为七类意图标签：profile-injection、targeted-extraction、temporal-reasoning、conflict-resolution、broad-summarization、constraint-validation、state-tracking。路由器不感知下游工具，仅输出意图标签与 requires_rag/requires_reasoning 标志，路由准确率 87.7%。

（2）Memory Agent（完全在 SLM 之外执行）：三级检索体系，穷尽且互斥地划分七个标签。Tier 1 Profile Lookup：对 profile-injection 零检索——稳定偏好/身份事实检索反而有害（会引入冲突或过期表述），故在摄入时预编译结构化用户画像直接加载。Tier 2 Targeted Retrieval：多趟实体感知检索，主混合 BM25+dense（RRF 融合，bge-small-en-v1.5 稠密编码器，base top-k=8）后按实体分别再检索并去重合并。Tier 3 Deep Reasoning：五类标签在 SLM 看到证据前先做确定性预处理——时序类用 Chronological Sorter 与 Date Math Calculator，冲突类用 Recency Filter 标记过期事实，摘要类强制会话多样性（防冗余）并做 map-reduce 聚合，约束类过滤 always/never/must 等模态与规则语言，状态追踪保留时间顺序的状态变更候选。

（3）Context Packer：按优先级三槽位分配（Tier-1 锁定事实永不被截断 → Tier-3 预计算摘要溢出时先缩减 → Tier-2 原始块最后截断），高优先级未用预算动态再分配给低优先级；额外四项增强：按重排分数相关性排序、精度敏感标签每块仅保留 top-2 句、Jaccard≥0.85 去重、动态预算再分配；每标签独立预算（如 targeted-extraction 6,000 token/每块300词，broad-summarization 8,000 等）。

（4）Answer Agent：单次 SLM 调用 + 七套标签专属系统提示（时序问题给时间线框定、profile 给偏好注入框定等）；时序/摘要类问题可发出受限 TOOL: 调用（如 days_between、count_occurrences），由框架确定性执行并注入 TOOL_RESULT，最多 3 轮工具轮次，模型无法自由选工具或陷入循环。

（5）Validator Agent：按成本由低到高三级级联——硬失败检测（空答案/ESCALATE_REQUIRED/not found，零成本直接升级）、短答案直通（≤6 词、纯数字、布尔直接跳过接地检查）、轻量 SLM 接地裁判（Qwen3-1.7B，max_new_tokens=8，无 CoT，输出 yes/no，无法解析则用 token 重叠启发式 τ=0.07）。校验失败时按标签升级链一次性重路由到更重层级（如 profile-injection→targeted-extraction、targeted-extraction→conflict-resolution、broad-summarization→targeted-extraction 等）；若仍失败则返回校准弃答。

另有 Active Context Check：若查询与最近 8 轮 token 重叠≥0.6，直接返回高重叠连续片段（覆盖约 2% 查询）。整体为零训练、权重冻结、可插拔到不同 backbone。

实验设计：三个基准 LongMemEval（500 题，oracle split）、LoCoMo（1,986 题，600 轮双人对谈/第三方人物查询）、LongBench v1（1,750 题，9 个文档级子任务），合计 4,236 题。基线包括 Direct QA（short 2,333 token / full 9,800 token）、RAG（同一混合检索器，top-k=8，2,266 token）、ReAct（同一检索与工具，最多 5 轮推理，8,732 token）、MemGPT、Memobase（Mem0 开源实现），以及 GPT-4o（9.8k 匹配预算与 120k 天花板）作为前沿参照。评测统一用 GPT-4o-mini 二值判定（对 300 条预测与 GPT-4o 一致率 85.7%，与人工标注一致率 89.6%），并说明 EM/F1 会严重低估开放式记忆 QA。

Result: 主结果：MemFlow 平均 52.4%（LongMemEval 61.8 / LoCoMo 51.2 / LongBench 51.1），为所有 SLM 系统最高，较 Direct QA(full) +23.4pp，较 RAG 与 ReAct（均 46.2%）均 +6.2pp；MemGPT 30.9%、Memobase 仅 9.6%（其画像压缩~260 token 破坏了情节回忆）。效率上答案上下文 2,223 token，与 RAG（2,266）相当、仅为 ReAct（8,732）的 1/3.9；但全流水线平均 10,167 prompt token（路由 6,954 占 58%、答案 2,550、校验 1,087、升级 2,289×14.9%、固定包装 314），略高于 ReAct——即以编排开销换取更紧凑可靠的最终推理上下文。与匹配预算 GPT-4o 差距 8.9pp；在 single-session-preference(+3.3pp)、temporal-reasoning(+3.8pp) 及 LoCoMo adversarial(+8.7pp 对 ReAct、+4.3pp 对 GPT-4o 匹配) 上反超；主要差距集中在 knowledge-update(-25.6pp) 与 conflict-resolution 类（路由过预测：527 条被判为冲突 vs 78 真冲突）。

跨 backbone 泛化：Qwen3-1.7B 29.0→52.4(+23.4)、SmolLM2-1.7B 19.6→43.0(+23.4)、Qwen3-0.6B 28.3→41.4(+13.1)、LLaMA-3.2-1B 19.4→39.1(+19.7)、Gemma-3-1B 23.4→29.4(+6.0)，五个模型全部提升，且直接提示下输出退化/格式崩溃的模型获益最大。

消融（按影响排序）：移除标签化检索与预处理 −18.7pp（最大）；以平面 RAG 替换分层检索 −9.5pp；绕过路由 −8.4pp；移除确定性工具 −8.4pp；移除升级/校验 −7.7pp；用简单拼接替换打包器 −7.6pp；各消融 token 成本基本稳定（±5%），说明增益来自证据"质量"而非计算量。升级统计：98.0% 触发校验，14.9% 触发升级，仅 3.4%（146/4,236）最终采用升级后答案；被升级查询本身准确率仅 10.3%（多为信息本身缺失的难例）。路由端到端准确率：LoCoMo 88.6%、LongBench 90.9%、LongMemEval 72.4%（该基准问题类型本体与动作标签难对齐）。定性案例展示 45× 压缩（9,935→220 token）、仅 711 token 正确作答、校验拒绝后重检索（1,571→2,294 token）挽回、以及对不存在信息正确弃答。

Conclusion: 结论与局限：MemFlow 说明，有界编排（结构化意图路由 + 确定性证据编译 + 接地验证）能让冻结 SLM 更有效地利用有限上下文，在特定长程记忆场景下缩小与前沿模型的差距，且不需要微调、RL 或基准特定训练；作者认为"结构化记忆路由可在一定程度上替代模型容量"。作者也明确承认若干局限：全流水线编排开销不小且主要来自路由提示（占 58% token，可借助前缀/KV 缓存在部署中缓解）；单一互斥意图标签无法覆盖复合型查询（如同需时间推理与冲突消解）；跨会话演化事实（知识更新、冲突消解）仍然困难，路由过预测是主要待改进点；LLM 裁判给出的是语义正确性估计而非严格词法分数，且仅在英文数据集上验证。外部记忆系统（MemGPT/Memobase）的上下文长度为检索元数据估算，属系统级参照而非严格效率对齐的对比。广义影响方面，作者强调其模块化设计利于端侧/边缘设备上的持久化个人助理部署与隐私友好（历史留本地），同时提醒持久用户画像若缺乏知情同意与数据治理会带来行为画像与操纵风险，SLM 精度在高风险决策（医疗、法律、金融）下仍需人工监督。未来方向：降低路由开销，并把校验、升级与工具派发改用学习到的决策策略（以答案正确性为奖励信号），同时保持有界执行路径与紧凑最终答案上下文。

Abstract: Modern language agents must operate over long-horizon, multi-turn histories, yet deploying such agents with Small Language Models (SLMs) remains fundamentally difficult. Full-context prompting causes context overflow, flat retrieval exposes the model to noisy evidence, and open-ended agentic loops are unreliable under limited reasoning capacity. We argue that a substantial portion of SLM memory failure arises from mismatched memory operations: different query types demand categorically different retrieval strategies, evidence transformations, and context budgets that SLMs cannot reliably self-orchestrate through open-ended reasoning. We introduce MemFlow, a training-free memory orchestration framework that externalizes memory planning from the SLM. A Router Agent classifies each query by intent and dispatches it to the Memory Agent, which executes one of three specialized tiers (Profile Lookup, Targeted Retrieval, or Deep Reasoning) and assembles the resulting evidence under a dynamic, tier-aware token budget. An Answer Agent then generates a response from this compact context, and a Validator Agent optionally retries with a heavier memory tier when the response is not supported by the provided evidence. This route-then-compile design avoids tool-selection hallucination and reasoning loops while keeping the answer context compact. Evaluated on a frozen Qwen3-1.7B backbone across long-horizon memory benchmarks - LongMemEval, LoCoMo, and LongBench - MemFlow improves accuracy by nearly 2x over full-context SLM baselines. These results suggest that structured intent routing and deterministic evidence preparation can make limited-capacity models substantially more effective in resource-constrained long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [115] [Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent Architecture for Traceable, Risk-Aware Human-AI Decision Support in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04003)
*Danny Hoang,Ryan Matthiessen,Christopher Miller,Nasir Mannan,Ruby ElKharboutly,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体知识分析架构 MAKA，用于自由曲面航空零件（Ti-6Al-4V 转子叶片）CNC 加工中可溯源、风险可控的人机协同决策支持：以中央智能体做意图路由、分析智能体仅调用确定性数值工具、知识图谱智能体做证据检索、批评智能体做物理合理性/安全边界/溯源完整性校验，全部结论经人工审批后再落地 CAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由曲面、弱刚性薄壁航空零件（如 Ti-6Al-4V 转子叶片）的微米级公差要求，必须把虚拟加工路径跟踪误差、切削力/工件挠曲仿真、扫描三维检测偏差场与工艺知识统一解释为"有界且可论证的补偿动作"，并保留审计溯源。现有工业流程中，CAM 离线规划、参数调优、SPC 稀疏检验三者相互割裂，靠人工解读串联，缺乏一致的多源证据融合、溯源跟踪与风险边界约束；通用 LLM 助手虽擅长文本与工具调用，但缺少机器运动学与切削力学显式表征，存在幻觉、数值推理不稳、溯源与可重复性无保证等问题，难以承担高后果决策。因此需要一种将关键数值计算约束到确定性工具、把领域知识检索与物理约束校验显式化的架构，并在资源受限的边缘/近边缘部署条件下实现可问责的人机协同。

Method: 架构：MAKA 由四类角色组成八步闭环——(1) 自然语言查询；(2-3) 中央智能体（CA）用语言模型路由器+JSON-Schema 校验+确定性启发式回退，输出结构化路由指令；(4) 分析智能体（AA）仅通过确定性工具库计算，其输出必须附带工具调用列表、工具输出与"数值→工具输出 ID"的溯源映射；(4') 知识图谱智能体（KGA）在符号图上做嵌入检索（预池化+μ+zσ 自适应阈值+有界邻居扩展/beam），返回主张→三元组 ID 证据；(5-7) 批评智能体（CRA）执行四类检查（意图对齐、工具接地完整性、KG 证据接地、物理可行性与边界动作），输出 accept/revise/escalate，设有迭代预算 L 以保证终止，超预算即升级人工；(8) 返回建议与审计轨迹。物理方法：以扫描配对法向偏差构造厚度向合成偏差 v=δP+δS 与单面偏差 s=v/2；将 RMVM 路径跟踪误差投影到同一 CAD 坐标系检验键得到 p_k；非路径项 u=s−p 按零件序号做逐对仿射拟合 u=c_k+b_k(n−1)+ε，得漂移率 b_k、末件漂移量 w_d=b_k(N−1)、残余系统项 c_k 与变异性代理 w_v=sqrt(Σε²/(N−2))；用 |·| 幅度加 ε-floor 计算稳定归因分数 φ^p, φ^c, φ^d 与变异性指标 ψ^v；补偿几何在固定 25° 刀具倾角下由符号修正量 Δ_k 映射为轴向/径向偏置 t_l=Δcosθ、t_r=Δsinθ，由 CRA 依据 KG 约束与车间限值设界。知识图谱由 4 篇 Ti-6Al-4V 文献经本地 gpt-oss-120b 分块抽取 2701 三元组、3401 实体、1151 关系，PostgreSQL 存储。实验：CCAT 用 4 轴 Trak VMC10si 加工 16 片叶片（30 检验点、15 对压力/吸力面配对），GOM ATOS ScanBox 检测；评价含 L1/L2/L3 工具编排基准（75 题/级对照专家脚本）、critic 有无配对消融（首路由丢弃 0.3 工具提示的受控退化）、KG 与 no-KG 配对 QA（75 题开放问答+等价选择题）、以及数字孪生 what-if 端到端分阶段补偿验证（ThirdWave 挠曲场 + RMVM 路径场 + 检验统计）。

Result: 工具编排：L1 单步上 MAKA 与单模型均高（GPT-OSS-20b、Ministral3-14b 达满分），MAKA 对弱模型最高 +20%（Qwen3-VL-8b）；随深度增加差距拉大，L2 最高提升 87.5 个百分点，L3 至少 32%，主要修复序列错乱、非法参数与中间状态丢失。Critic 消融（6 模型×75 配对题，450 配对试验、423 例退化）：no-critic 的工具选择 F1 局限于 0.2492–0.3137，critic 启用后升至 0.5722–0.7511，均值 0.2919→0.6697；绝对增益 +0.2690～+0.4374；平均 58.67% 配对试验工具质量改善、62.22% 减少缺失必需工具、61.19% 退化试验完全恢复，Qwen3-VL-30b 最强（78.67%/84.00%/81.08%）。KG 消融：开放问答数值得分均值 0.3462→0.4736（+0.1274，GPT-OSS-20b +0.2418 最佳），单题耗时 0.7305s→2.6496s；选择题准确率 0.4733→0.5733（GPT-OSS-20b 达 0.7067），耗时 0.2413s→1.6002s。端到端：分阶段（刀路补偿→挠曲导向参数→磨损感知更新）在高保真数字孪生中把预测面偏差从 10^-2 in 量级降至大部分表面 ±0.001~10^-3 in；全链路推理与检索仅 4.3 s，相对分钟至小时级加工节拍可忽略。

Conclusion: 多智能体分解在组合式工具使用上是可靠性必需品而非实现便利：单模型在依赖工具链上失效，MAKA 通过分离意图路由、确定性计算与有状态多步执行消除非法参数绑定、调用顺序错误与中间状态丢失。Critic 是显式恢复层，在路由受噪条件下防止早期错误传播；KG 智能体提供证据链接的数值依据与约束性警示，抑制幻觉并提升定量正确性，同时带来相对加工节拍可忽略的延迟开销。作者强调结论边界：偏差分解中的残余项是"残余系统柔度项"，不可唯一辨识为挠曲；变异性代理仅作为风险/升级指示，不等同于物理磨损状态；仿真与实测呈拓扑（空间）一致而非逐点数值一致；数字孪生结果构成部署前可验证基线，而非声称物理闭环补偿已实现。后续方向包括推广到更多零件族（仅需替换工具层）、细粒度磨损物理建模、真实机床闭环验证与更完整的溯源/责任框架。

Abstract: High-precision CNC machining of free-form aerospace components requires bounded compensations informed by inspection, simulation, and process knowledge. Off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) assistants can generate text, but they do not reliably execute risk-constrained multi-step numerical workflows or provide auditable provenance for high-stakes decisions. We present multi-agent knowledge analysis (MAKA), a human-in-the-loop decision-support architecture that separates intent routing, tools-only quantitative analysis, knowledge graph retrieval, and critic-based verification that enforces physical plausibility, safety bounds, and provenance completeness before recommendations are surfaced for human approval. MAKA is instantiated on a Ti-6Al-4V rotor blade machining testbed by fusing virtual-machining path-tracking error fields, cutting-force and deflection simulations, and scan-based 3D inspection deviation maps from 16 blades. The analysis decomposes deviation into an evidence-linked pathing component, a drift-based wear proxy capturing systematic evolution across parts, a residual systematic compliance term, and a variability proxy for instability-aware escalation. In a three-level tool-orchestration benchmark (single-step through $\geq$3-step stateful sequences), MAKA improves successful tool execution by up to 87.5 percentage points relative to an unstructured single-model interaction pattern with identical tool access. Digital twin what-if studies show MAKA can coordinate traceable compensation candidates that reduce predicted surface deviation from order $10^{-2}$in to approximately $\pm 10^{-3}$in over most of the blade within the simulation environment, providing a pre-deployment verification signal for risk-aware human decision-making.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Tutti: Making SSD-Backed KV Cache Practical for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03375)
*Shi Qiu,Yifan Hu,Xintao Wang,Wenhao Zhu,Jianqin Yan,Hao Chen,Kaiqiang Xu,Kai Chen,Yiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Tutti是一个高效的SSD后端KV缓存解决方案，通过消除CPU干预，实现GPU与SSD之间的高速数据传输。与最先进的GDS方案LMCache相比，Tutti将TTFT降低78.3%，吞吐量提升2倍，服务成本降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型上下文增长，KV缓存远超GPU HBM和CPU DRAM容量，需卸载到NVMe SSD。但现有方案因GPU内存碎片化导致大量小型随机I/O，CPU成为瓶颈，即使使用GPU Direct Storage(GDS)仍需CPU介入发起I/O。

Method: (1) GPU原生对象抽象支持批量KV缓存传输和管理；(2) 重构GPU存储栈，引入GPU io_uring支持异步GPU直连对象I/O；(3) 提出了slack感知的I/O调度避免GPU资源竞争。CPU仅负责异步加载每层I/O内核到GPU一次。

Result: 与GDS启用的LMCache相比，Tutti在严格SLO约束下TTFT降低78.3%，可实现请求率提升2倍，服务成本降低27%。Tutti达到接近DRAM后端LMCache的推理性能，同时提供近乎无限的容量。

Conclusion: 通过消除CPU干预和引入GPU io_uring等创新设计，Tutti成功饱和NVMe SSD带宽，将GPU停顿降至接近零，为LLM服务中的KV缓存卸载提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM serving relies on prefix caching to improve inference performance. As growing contexts push key-value (KV) cache footprint far beyond GPU HBM and CPU DRAM capacity, KV cache is increasingly offloaded to NVMe SSDs. Unfortunately, restoring KV cache from SSDs suffers from poor I/O performance and incurs significant GPU stalls. This is primarily because the fragmented GPU memory layout results in a massive number of tiny random I/Os, rendering the low-parallelism CPU a severe bottleneck even with GPU Direct Storage (GDS), which still relies on CPU intervention to initiate each I/O and thus remains CPU-centric. This paper presents Tutti, an efficient SSD-backed KV caching solution that eliminates CPU intervention from the critical data and I/O control paths between HBM and SSDs. At the core of Tutti is a GPU-centric KV cache object store, in which the CPU is only responsible for asynchronously loading I/O kernels once per layer to the GPU. Tutti saturates NVMe SSD bandwidth and reduces GPU stalls to near zero through the following designs: (i) we provide a GPU-native object abstraction that enables bulk KV cache transfers and management; (ii) we re-architect the GPU storage stack by introducing GPU io_uring to support asynchronous GPU direct object I/O; and (iii) we propose slack-aware I/O scheduling to avoid GPU resource contention. We have implemented Tutti and integrated it to vLLM. Extensive evaluation shows that compared to the state-of-the-art GDS-enabled, SSD-backed LMCache, Tutti reduces TTFT by 78.3% under strict SLO constraints and improves the achievable request rate by 2x. The serving cost is reduced by 27%. Tutti achieves nearly the same inference performance as DRAM-backed LMCache, while providing almost infinite capacity.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Beyond Similarity Search: A Unified Data Layer for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03275)
*Venkata Krishna Prasanth Budigi,Siri Chandana Sirigiri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于PostgreSQL+pgvector的统一数据层解决方案，解决RAG生产环境中的数据陈旧、租户泄漏和查询组合爆炸三大核心问题，实验表明可降低92%查询延迟并消除跨租户数据泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在生产环境中暴露出原型性能与实际可靠性之间的差距，根源在于传统分离式数据层存在的三个核心问题：数据陈旧、租户数据泄漏和查询组合爆炸。

Method: 使用PostgreSQL内置向量搜索(pgvector)和HNSW索引构建统一数据层，在50,000份文档上进行受控基准测试，对比传统分离式系统架构。

Result: 统一数据层实现了显著改进：日期过滤查询延迟降低92%，租户范围查询降低74%，零同步不一致，跨租户数据泄漏完全消除，同步代码减少93%。

Conclusion: 研究证明PostgreSQL+pgvector的统一数据层可有效解决RAG系统的三个核心问题，建议采用混合分层架构来平衡性能与成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems have become the standard architecture for grounding large language models in organizational knowledge. Yet production deployments consistently expose a gap between clean prototype performance and real-world reliability. This paper identifies three root causes of that gap: data staleness, tenant data leakage, and query composition explosion. All three trace back to the conventional split-system data layer. We propose and evaluate a unified data layer built on PostgreSQL with native vector search (pgvector) and HNSW indexing. Controlled benchmarks on 50,000 documents show 92% latency reduction for date-filtered queries, 74% for tenant-scoped queries, zero synchronization inconsistency, and complete elimination of cross-tenant data leakage with 93% less synchronization code. We additionally discuss a recommended hybrid tier architecture

</details>


### [118] [RAG over Thinking Traces Can Improve Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03344)
*Negar Arabzadeh,Wenjie Ma,Sewon Min,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究证明检索思考轨迹（问题解决过程中生成的中间思维轨迹）可显著提升推理任务性能，提出的T3方法将其结构化后作为检索语料，在多个强模型和基准测试中超越非RAG基线和标准网页检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为RAG对推理密集型任务（如数学和代码生成）收益有限，本研究挑战这一假设，认为局限在于语料选择而非RAG本身。

Method: 提出检索思考轨迹（thinking traces）而非传统文档，并开发T3方法将思考轨迹转换为结构化、易检索的表示形式，形成retrieve-then-generate管道。

Result: 在AIME 2025-2026、LiveCodeBench、GPQA-Diamond等基准上均取得一致提升，例如Gemini-2.5-Flash在AIME上相对提升+56.3%；同时可降低最多15%的推理成本。

Conclusion: Thinking traces是一种有效的推理任务检索语料，T3方法通过结构化、紧凑化或诊断性表示可进一步解锁更强性能提升，且几乎不增加额外推理成本。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective for knowledge-intensive tasks, but is widely believed to offer limited benefit for reasoning-intensive problems such as math and code generation. We challenge this assumption by showing that the limitation lies not in RAG itself, but in the choice of corpus. Instead of retrieving documents, we propose retrieving thinking traces, i.e., intermediate thinking trajectories generated during problem solving attempts. We show that thinking traces are already a strong retrieval source, and further introduce T3, an offline method that transforms them into structured, retrieval-friendly representations, to improve usability. Using these traces as a corpus, a simple retrieve-then-generate pipeline consistently improves reasoning performance across strong models and benchmarks such as AIME 2025--2026, LiveCodeBench, and GPQA-Diamond, outperforming both non-RAG baselines and retrieval over standard web corpora. For instance, on AIME, RAG with traces generated by Gemini-2-thinking achieves relative gains of +56.3%, +8.6%, and +7.6% for Gemini-2.5-Flash, GPT-OSS-120B, and GPT-5, respectively, even though these are more recent models. Interestingly, RAG on T3 also incurs little or no extra inference cost, and can even reduce inference cost by up to $15%$. Overall, our results suggest that thinking traces are an effective retrieval corpus for reasoning tasks, and transforming them into structured, compact, or diagnostic representations unlocks even stronger gains. Code available at https://github.com/Narabzad/t3.

</details>


### [119] [Revisiting General Map Search via Generative Point-of-Interest Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03397)
*Dong Chen,Shuai Zheng,Haoyang Shao,Hongsheng Wu,Muhao Xu,Yeyu Yan,Ruifang Li,Zhenfeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GenPOI是一个创新的生成式POI检索框架，旨在解决通用地图搜索中用户查询信息不足的问题。该框架将异构搜索上下文和POI统一为结构化序列，利用LLM进行空间感知的候选生成，并创新性地提出了Geo-Semantic POI Tokenization和proximity-aware constrained generation策略，在千万级POI数据集上验证了其卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统POI检索方法过度依赖浅层语义匹配，难以处理用户查询中信息不足的问题；同时现有检索范式难以有效整合异构上下文信息来推断复杂的搜索意图。

Method: 提出Geo-Semantic POI Tokenization方法，将每个POI表示为包含语义和地理上下文信息的紧凑token序列，以增强LLM的空间理解能力；同时采用proximity-aware constrained generation策略约束LLM的解码空间，确保生成结果的有效性和地理相关性。

Result: 在腾讯地图超过千万量级的工业级数据集上验证了该框架的优越性能。

Conclusion: GenPOI通过在腾讯地图超过千万量级的POI数据集上的实验验证了其优越性。该框架利用LLM强大的上下文建模能力，有效解决了传统方法难以处理的上下文依赖复杂查询问题，同时通过约束解码策略确保了生成结果的有效性和地理空间相关性。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) retrieval aims to identify relevant candidates from massive-scale POI databases, serving as a cornerstone for diverse location-based services. However, in general map search scenarios, conventional POI retrieval methods are increasingly challenged by underspecified user queries due to their excessive reliance on surface-level semantic matching. Meanwhile, such queries are often highly context-dependent and personalized, yet existing retrieval paradigms struggle to effectively synergize heterogeneous contexts for complex search intent inference. To address these limitations, we revisit general map search from a generative perspective and propose GenPOI, an innovative Generative POI retrieval framework tailored for general search on maps. It seamlessly unifies heterogeneous search contexts and POIs into structured sequences, leveraging the powerful contextual modeling of Large Language Models (LLMs) for spatial-aware candidate generation. Consequently, this generative paradigm effectively solves more challenging queries through profound context dependency modeling and search intent reasoning. Specifically, accounting for the unique geospatial nature of map scenarios, GenPOI introduces a novel Geo-Semantic POI Tokenization to represent each POI as a compact token sequence encoding both semantic and geographic context, thus grounding the LLM's spatial understanding. Additionally, a proximity-aware constrained generation strategy is employed to restrict the decoding space of the LLM, ensuring the validity and geospatial relevance of the generated results. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets from Tencent Map, comprising POIs at the scale of over 10 million, demonstrate the superior performance of GenPOI.

</details>


### [120] [Aspect-Aware Content-Based Recommendations for Mathematical Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03861)
*Ankit Satpute,André Greiner-Petter,Noah Gießing,Olaf Teschke,Moritz Schubotz,Akiko Aizawa,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次针对数学领域探索基于内容的研究论文推荐（CbRPR），提出专家驱动的"aspect（关系维度）驱动"洞察，构建 GoldRiM（80 篇种子、420 对专家标注）与 SilverRiM（212K 种子、532K 对自动标注）两个面向数学的 aspect 感知数据集，并提出 aspect 条件化异构图神经网络 AchGNN，联合建模文本语义、引用结构与作者学术血脉，在两个数据集所有 aspect 上超越 GraphCL、Qwen3-7B-FT、SciBERT-FT 等基线（GoldRiM General MRR 0.593，相对 GraphCL 提升约 26%），并在 Papers with Code 上验证了跨域可迁移性，已部署于 MaRDI 平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容型论文推荐（CbRPR）已在计算机科学、生物医学领域取得进展，但在数学领域几乎空白。研究动机基于三点观察：(1) 数学论文之间建立于抽象、符号压缩与演绎推理，其"相关性"是概念性的而非表层文本或引用重叠所能体现——例如定理的对偶表述、证明策略的推广、定理的限制/推广，在词面与引用上几乎无重叠；(2) LLM 嵌入在高度符号化、语义密集的数学文本上表现欠佳，单独使用嵌入不足以刻画数学相关性；(3) 作者共现是"学术血脉/概念谱系"的稳健代理信号（数学家在小领域内深耕、复用证明风格、基础结果常以作者名被引用）。由此提出两个研究问题：RQ1 数学论文的内容相关性究竟由什么构成；RQ2 文本与作者血脉的联合建模如何影响数学 CbRPR。

Method: 方法分两阶段。(1) 领域奠基：通过与 zbMATH Open 合作，收集资深专家（30+ 年代数几何评审经验）标注的 GoldRiM（80 种子/420 对），并对全部 420 对做定性分析，最初得到 66 个细粒度标签，经与专家迭代讨论合并为 4 个高层 aspect：Specialization/Restriction (32.14%)、Modification/Generalization (40%)、Results (17.86%)、Prove/Cases (10%)；同时用词面重叠（2-gram Jaccard 均值仅 0.031）、Qwen3-7B 嵌入相似度、引用邻近度（23% 无引用路径、43% 仅 1 条边，文献耦合召回 34%、共被引 35%）三项统计分析证明单纯文本/嵌入/引用信号均不足以刻画专家相关性。进而从 zbMATH Open 约 312K 篇摘要、786K 条摘要内引用中，用 Qwen2.5-14B 少样本抽取关系短语，得 212K 种子、532K 对、7,326 个细粒度标签，再经 k-means 对齐到 GoldRiM 的 4 类本体（分布为 37.83/29.30/21.04/11.83%），构成 SilverRiM。(2) 模型 AchGNN：构建论文—作者异构无向图，三类边为 aspect 标注边、引用边、作者边；节点用 Qwen3-7B 编码标题+摘要（移除公式内引用）与作者名初始化；采用 GraphSAGE 式关系特定消息传递（L=2，论文邻居 15、作者邻居 5）；aspect 条件化仅作用于打分层而非消息传递，以避免多 aspect 纠缠：交互向量 z=[h_q‖h_c‖h_q⊙h_c‖e_as] 经 MLP 得分数；辅以 aspect 多类分类交叉熵损失；总损失为 BPR 排序损失 + λ·aspect 损失（λ=0.2）。评测采用 aspect 独立的训练/测试划分与 aspect-specific 检索，指标为 P@10、R@10、MRR，检索语料为 380 万篇 zbMATH Open 英文文档。

Result: GoldRiM 上 AchGNN 全面领先：General P/R/MRR = 0.341/0.425/0.593，较 GraphCL（0.271/0.352/0.471）相对提升约 26%，为 SciBERT-FT（MRR 0.475）的五倍以上；四个 aspect 上均最优（如 Modification/Generalization MRR 0.468）。SilverRiM 上同样最优（General MRR 0.410，R 0.634），但因噪声与每种子平均仅 2.50 条推荐，精确率偏低、相对优势缩小，且 MRR 低于 GoldRiM，说明 SilverRiM 存在排序质量稀释。基线分析显示：MathIR 方法（Approach0、DPR、Mabowdor）表现不佳；SPECTER 优于 SciBERT 但微调后反而不如 SciBERT-FT；Qwen3-7B 为最佳基座嵌入模型，Qwen3-7B-FT 仍不及图方法，说明瓶颈不在监督量而在表示能力；GraphCL 优于纯嵌入，验证结构信号价值。消融显示：移除 Specialization/Restriction 监督退化最大（因其数据占比最高），移除 Prove/Cases 影响最小且溢出效应有限，说明 aspect 打分解耦有效；AchGNN_noMath（去公式）仅小幅下降，表明增益主要来自文本与结构而非公式本身；作者血缘消融与随机化作者（随机化更差）均造成大幅退化，证明作者提供的是语义谱系而非单纯图连通性；超参上中等邻居规模最优（15/5、L=2），过大导致过平滑。PwC 上 AchGNN 与 SciBERT-FT 互有胜负（General P 0.486 vs 0.569，Method P 0.512 vs 0.407），显示方法可迁移但并非全面超越。

Conclusion: 数学领域的内容型论文推荐本质上是"aspect 驱动"的：相关性来自定理推广/限制、对偶表述、证明技术复用等概念关系，而非词面或引用相似度，这既解释了既有 CbRPR 在数学上失效的原因，也界定了领域特有 aspect 分类体系的必要性。贡献有三：首个面向数学 aspect 感知 CbRPR 的专家驱动数据集 GoldRiM 与大规模自动派生数据集 SilverRiM（填补 CS/BM 主导的现有数据集缺口，且 aspect 体系与既有数据集重叠有限）；首个将 aspect 条件化引入异构引用—作者图的 AchGNN，证明结构化、关系化的建模优于纯语义嵌入微调；以及面向部署与可复现性的开源数据集与代码、MaRDI 平台上线。局限与展望：GoldRiM 由单一专家标注、仅覆盖代数几何，存在偏差与规模限制；SilverRiM 标签噪声大、稀疏；图限于 zbMATH Open，未覆盖 arXiv 等外部文献；后续计划在 MaRDI 上开展用户交互评估，结合协同过滤与内容信号的混合推荐，并在更多子领域、更多评审者上验证和精炼 aspect 分类体系（如引入方法相似性、历史影响等新 aspect），以及跨数据集扩展引用与作者网络以提升泛化性。

Abstract: Content-based research paper recommendation (CbRPR) has seen advances in computer science and biomedicine, but remains unexplored for mathematics, where paper relatedness is more conceptual than explicit textual or citation-based similarity. Mathematics papers may be connected through shared proof techniques, logical implications, or natural generalizations, yet exhibit minimal textual or citation overlap, rendering existing CbRPR ineffective. To address this gap, we first conduct an expert-driven study characterizing mathematical recommendations, revealing that relevance is inherently \textit{aspect}-driven. Grounded in this insight, we introduce GoldRiM (small, expert-annotated) and SilverRiM (large, automatically derived), the first datasets for \textit{aspect}-aware CbRPR in mathematics. Recognizing that LLM embeddings of mathematical content alone yield suboptimal representation, we propose AchGNN, an \textit{aspect}-conditioned heterogeneous GNN that jointly models textual semantics, citation structure, and author lineage. Across GoldRiM and SilverRiM, AchGNN consistently outperforms prior \textit{aspect}-based CbRPR methods, achieving substantial gains across all evaluated \textit{aspects}. We conduct ablation studies to analyze the contributions of individual \textit{aspect} supervision, authorship lineage, and graph-structural signals to AchGNN's performance. To assess domain generality, we further evaluate AchGNN on the \textit{Papers with Code} dataset of machine learning publications, demonstrating that our \textit{aspect}-aware approach effectively transfers beyond mathematics. We deploy our system on the MaRDI platform to help mathematicians with recommendations and release datasets and code publicly for reproducibility.

</details>


### [121] [Domain-Adaptive Dense Retrieval for Brazilian Legal Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04005)
*Jayr Pereira,Roberto Lotufo,Luiz Bonifacio*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究巴西法律检索的异构性问题，比较Qwen3-Embedding-4B模型在三种训练设置下的表现：基础模型、法律数据微调、混合训练（法律+SQuAD-pt）。混合训练在保持法律检索性能的同时显著提升了跨类型检索的鲁棒性，尤其在问答类检索任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西法律检索任务具有异构性特点，涵盖案例法、立法文本和问答型检索，这使得训练密集检索器时存在领域专业化与泛化能力之间的权衡困境。

Method: 基于Qwen3-Embedding-4B模型，采用三种训练设置进行对比：1）无微调的基础模型；2）仅使用法律数据训练；3）混合训练（法律数据+SQuAD-pt葡萄牙语监督数据集）。评估在JUÁ排行榜的五个法律数据集及Quati数据集上进行。

Result: 法律专用模型在专业化法律任务上表现最佳；混合训练设置在保持法律检索能力的同时实现了更好的整体平衡，在六个数据集上NDCG@10从0.414提升至0.447，MRR@10从0.586提升至0.595，MAP@10从0.270提升至0.308。Quati数据集上混合模型明显优于法律专用模型。

Conclusion: 法律专用训练与混合训练各具优势：前者更适合领域专业化，后者在跨检索类型（尤其是问答型检索）上更具鲁棒性。研究表明针对异构检索任务，混合训练是更平衡的选择。

Abstract: Brazilian legal retrieval is heterogeneous, covering case law, legislation, and question-based search. This makes training dense retrievers a trade-off between stronger domain specialization and broader robustness across retrieval types of search. In this paper, we explore this trade-off using three training setups based on Qwen3-Embedding-4B: a base model with no fine-tuning, a version trained only on legal data, and a mixed setup that combines legal data with SQuAD-pt supervised dataset. We evaluate these models on five legal datasets from the JUÁ leaderboard, along with Quati dataset as an extra Portuguese retrieval benchmark to test out-of-domain generalization. The legal-only model performs best on the most specialized legal tasks. The mixed setup keeps strong performance on legal data while offering a better overall balance, improving average NDCG@10 from 0.414 to 0.447, MRR@10 from 0.586 to 0.595, and MAP@10 from 0.270 to 0.308 across all six datasets. The biggest improvement appears on Quati, where the mixed model clearly outperforms the legal-only one. Overall, the results show that legal-only and mixed training lead to different strengths: the first is better for specialization, while the second is more robust across different types of search, especially question-based ones. Both adapted models are available on Hugging Face

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [FINER-SQL: Boosting Small Language Models for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03465)
*Thanh Dat Hoang,Thanh Trung Huynh,Matthias Weidlich,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Tong Chen,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FINER-SQL proposes a reinforcement learning framework with fine-grained execution feedback (memory reward + atomic reward) to train small language models (3B) for Text-to-SQL, achieving competitive accuracy with LLMs while being cost-efficient and privacy-preserving.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models for Text-to-SQL suffer from high computational cost, long inference latency, and data privacy concerns, making them impractical for real-world deployment. While small language models (SLMs) offer efficient on-premise deployment, they struggle with weak reasoning and poor instruction following. Existing reinforcement learning methods using sparse binary rewards (0/1) provide insufficient learning signals when SQLs are incorrect, leading to unstable or collapsed training.

Method: FINER-SQL is a scalable and reusable reinforcement learning framework built on group relative policy optimization (GRPO). It replaces sparse binary supervision with dense and interpretable rewards offering continuous feedback: (1) Memory reward aligns reasoning with verified traces for semantic stability, (2) Atomic reward measures operation-level overlap to grant partial credit for structurally correct but incomplete SQLs. This transforms discrete correctness into continuous learning for stable, critic-free optimization.

Result: On BIRD and Spider benchmarks, FINER-SQL achieves up to 67.73% and 85% execution accuracy respectively with only a 3B model, matching much larger LLMs while reducing inference latency to 5.57s/sample.

Conclusion: FINER-SQL provides a cost-efficient and privacy-preserving path toward high-performance Text-to-SQL generation, enabling stable training of SLMs without critics through continuous dense rewards.

Abstract: Large language models have driven major advances in Text-to-SQL generation. However, they suffer from high computational cost, long latency, and data privacy concerns, which make them impractical for many real-world applications. A natural alternative is to use small language models (SLMs), which enable efficient and private on-premise deployment. Yet, SLMs often struggle with weak reasoning and poor instruction following. Conventional reinforcement learning methods based on sparse binary rewards (0/1) provide little learning signal when the generated SQLs are incorrect, leading to unstable or collapsed training. To overcome these issues, we propose FINER-SQL, a scalable and reusable reinforcement learning framework that enhances SLMs through fine-grained execution feedback. Built on group relative policy optimization, FINER-SQL replaces sparse supervision with dense and interpretable rewards that offer continuous feedback even for incorrect SQLs. It introduces two key reward functions: a memory reward, which aligns reasoning with verified traces for semantic stability, and an atomic reward, which measures operation-level overlap to grant partial credit for structurally correct but incomplete SQLs. This approach transforms discrete correctness into continuous learning, enabling stable, critic-free optimization. Experiments on the BIRD and Spider benchmarks show that FINER-SQL achieves up to 67.73\% and 85\% execution accuracy with a 3B model -- matching much larger LLMs while reducing inference latency to 5.57~s/sample. These results highlight a cost-efficient and privacy-preserving path toward high-performance Text-to-SQL generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/thanhdath/finer-sql.

</details>


### [123] [In-memory Multidimensional Indexing Using the skd-tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03640)
*Achilleas Michalopoulos,Dimitrios Tsitsigkos,Nikos Mamoulis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为slicing kd-tree（skd-tree）的新型多维数据索引结构。该结构通过将每个节点的空间划分为多个切片并压缩分割器，结合数据并行性和SIMD指令优化，显著降低了树的深度和查询计算复杂度。同时提出了创新的自顶向下构建算法、平衡机制和更新算法，在真实和合成数据集上的实验表明其性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着主存数据库的发展，需要高效索引多个维度以支持多维范围查询和最近邻查询。现有数据结构（R树、kd树、四叉树、网格分区）以及最近提出的多维学习索引都存在一定局限性，亟需更高效的解决方案来提升查询性能。

Method: 通过将每个节点的空间划分为多个切片来压缩分割器，结合数据并行性，减少树的层数并限制查询计算量。设计了三项核心贡献：(1)创新的自顶向下构建算法，(2)支持树平衡的内节点和叶节点设计，(3)新型更新算法。在遍历过程中，每个节点仅应用少量SIMD指令完成查询处理。

Result: skd-tree通过切片压缩大幅降低树的层数，结合SIMD指令实现常数级计算量优化，在多维范围查询和KNN查询中展现了显著的性能提升，优于现有主流索引方法。

Conclusion: 实验评估表明，skd-tree在真实和合成数据集上相比现有方法实现了优异的性能表现。这种通过切片压缩和SIMD并行化来优化kd-tree的方法，为主存数据库中的多维数据索引提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the problem of indexing multi-dimensional data in memory for the efficient support of multi-dimensional range queries and nearest neighbor queries. This is a classic problem in main-memory databases, where there is a need for indexing multiple columns simultaneously. Established data structures include the R-tree, kd-tree, quad-tree, and grid-based partitioning. More recently, multi-dimensional learned indexes have also been proposed to address this problem. We propose slicing kd-tree (skd-tree), a variant of the kd-tree, where each node partitions the space of its subtree into multiple slices across a single splitting dimension. By compressing the splitters of the partitions and with the help of data-parallelism, we (i) radically reduce the number of levels of the tree and (ii) limit the number of computations required for multi-dimensional range and proximity queries. The nodes of the skd-tree resemble the nodes of a main-memory B+-tree, however, a different dimension is used at each level. Our novel range and kNN algorithms on the skd-tree apply only a small constant number of SIMD instructions at each node during tree traversal. Our contributions also include a novel top-down construction algorithm, different types of inner and leaf nodes that warrant tree balancing, and a novel update algorithm. Our skd-tree achieves strong performance compared to existing methods, according to our experimental evaluation on real and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [124] [ConRAD: Conformal Risk-Aware Neural Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03806)
*Sonia Horchidan,Fabian Zeiher,Xiangyu Shi,Vasiliki Kalavri,Henrik Boström,Ioannis Kontoyiannis,Paris Carbone*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ConRAD 是首个在神经图数据库（NGDB）查询引擎内部原生提供声明式召回保证的框架：用户只需给出召回目标（如≥90%），系统即通过 Conformal Risk Control 自动为多跳查询计划的每个算子推导预测阈值，在有限样本、分布无关的统计有效性下严格满足风险预算，同时最大化端到端精度。核心三项技术是：将召回目标形式化为风险约束优化问题；用分位数空间标量化（quantile-space scalarization）把 k 维阈值搜索压缩为单一单调参数 η；以及提出“conformal gate”这一物理算子，在图证据充分时完全绕过神经推理。在三个 KG 基准、三种查询拓扑与 5%–40% 缺失率下，所有召回预算均被严格满足（最大向下偏差 0.0464），精度达到或超过需人工调参且无保证的最佳静态基线，并在近完整图区域将神经调用降至 0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱在开放世界假设（OWA）下存在不完整性，神经图数据库通过将精确图遍历与概率性神经链接预测组合来恢复缺失事实。但现有基础模型仅优化单点准确率，其分数未校准；一旦多跳查询流水线中推理算子被组合，统计误差会非线性累积：早期假阴性不可逆地剪除有效子树，假阳性则向下游倾泻噪声。实践者普遍采用手动调静态阈值或 top-k 的启发式做法，既无法适应算子异质性与局部稀疏性，也没有任何数学保证，实际上把“查询优化器”的角色推给应用开发者。在药物安全监测等安全关键场景中，缺少对结果完整性的形式化统计界限使这类系统不可用。此外，标准 Conformal Risk Control (CRC) 只校准单个预测器的标量阈值，且要求嵌套性（nestedness），而多算子阈值空间随算子数指数增长、非可分解，无法直接应用。

Method: （1）问题形式化：将用户声明的召回目标（等价于 FNR ≤ α）映射为多算子阈值向量 λ ∈ [0,1]^k 的约束优化：min C(λ)=E[|Ŷ|] s.t. R(λ) ≤ α。风险为端到端 FNR；因假阴性不可恢复而假阳性可被下游过滤，故 FNR 为硬约束，基数/精度为优化目标。（2）分位数空间标量化：定义映射 λ_j(η) = Q̂_j(η^{γ_j})，其中 Q̂_j 为各算子在校准集上的经验分位函数，用以归一化不同算子不可比的分数尺度；γ_j 为逐算子指数，控制“前松后紧”或“前紧后松”策略（如谓词下推式剪枝）。定理 4.1 证明在逐点打分假设（标准链接预测打分函数成立，不适用于 top-k 排序式选择）下，任何逐分量单调标量化保证全局单调性 Ŷ(η_b) ⊆ Ŷ(η_a)，从而 CRC 的嵌套性与有限样本有效性得以保留。（3）conformal gate：一种新的物理算子，将检索事实与神经预测统一到同一分数尺度 S(τ)（检索事实经一致性哈希 H 映射到 [δ,1]，神经分数缩放到 [0, δ−ε)），从而在 λ_i ≥ δ 时以纯检索模式执行（零推理成本、可下采样以抑制基数膨胀），λ_i < δ 时切换为混合模式；δ 不影响接受/拒绝行为。（4）校准流程（Algorithm 1）：离线将校准集划分为优化集 D_opt（2000 条）与验证集 D_valid（2000 条），按算子计算分数与经验分位函数，在 100 点 η 网格上遍历 5 种候选 γ 策略，用 CRC 修正项 n/(n+1)·R̂ + B/(n+1) ≤ α 选出最紧 η*，再在 D_valid 上按基数挑选最优阈值向量，存入 (拓扑, α) → λ̂ 查找表；运行时按拓扑与风险预算查表，将逻辑 DAG 编译为 conformal gate 物理计划。实现为 Neo4j v5.20.0 + PyTorch UltraQuery（单张 NVIDIA H100 NVL），δ=0.5，哈希用归一化 MD5；支持 EPFO 片段（存在量词、合取、析取），三种拓扑 3p / 2u / 2ip；不支持否定与递归查询（破坏单调性与固定维度阈值向量假设）。

Result: 在 FB15k-237、NELL-995、YAGO3-10 三个基准、3 种查询拓扑、5%/20%/40% 缺失率及召回目标 0.6–0.9 下的完整评估：（1）有效性：所有配置均严格满足风险预算，最大向下偏差 0.0464（YAGO3-10, 2ip, target 0.70），最大向上偏差 0.0711（NELL-995, 3p, target 0.70），与有限样本校准和网格量化一致；2u 因并联冗余跟踪最紧，3p 最难（误差逐跳复合）。（2）精度：在召回 ≥0.60 约束下，ConRAD 在 FB15k-237 3p 达 0.96（hybrid 最佳 0.95）、2ip 达 0.93（hybrid 0.92）、YAGO3-10 3p 达 0.87（hybrid 0.85），仅 YAGO3-10 2ip 略低（0.83 vs 0.85）；而 neo4j 纯检索在所有数据集的 3p/2ip 上均失败（召回 0.36–0.59），neural 基线虽满足召回但精度崩塌。（3）弃答率：静态基线在 40% 缺失率下最多 60% 查询返回空集，ConRAD 保持 <30%（target 0.9 时仅 3.3%），因弃答以零召回计入风险，CRC 优化天然规避之。（4）效率：conformal gate 在 5% 缺失率、target 0.6 时神经调用降至 0%，target 0.7/0.9 时升至 34.9%/48.2%；20% 缺失率下 0.8/0.9 目标均为 50%；前跳多为纯检索、后跳因前沿扩张切至混合。离线校准总耗时最多约 1.5 小时（YAGO3-10 3p 3711s），其中分数计算占主导（如 YAGO3-10 2u 超过 99%），阈值优化仅需数秒；运行时开销为每候选三元组一次哈希/标量投影与浮点比较，为常数级。（5）鲁棒性：替换为更弱的 UltraQuery-T 后，仅需离线重校准即可在全部拓扑保持召回保证（最大向下偏差 0.0169），精度随模型质量等比下降（0.70/0.96/0.66），说明安全契约独立于预测器精度。

Conclusion: ConRAD 将神经数据库的可靠性从“开发者手动调参的超参数”转变为“查询引擎原生的声明式约束”，是首个在 NGDB 中对组合式随机查询计划提供形式化正确性保证的框架。其做法是将 CRC 从标量扩展到多算子拓扑：分位数空间标量化在保持嵌套性的同时把 k 维搜索降为 1 维，conformal gate 则在检索证据充分处消除不必要的神经推理，实现召回硬约束与精度优化目标的有效分离。实验证明其在不同拓扑、缺失率与预测器强度下均严格满足召回预算，精度达到或超过无保证且需手工搜索的最佳静态基线，同时在近完整区域将神经调用降至零。作者承认其固有限制与未来方向：仅提供边际（而非条件）保证且依赖可交换性假设，在严重分布漂移下会退化；不支持零样本迁移到新拓扑（需保守回退如 union bound，或依赖已校准分支的模块化组合）；标量化搜索只保证给定单调轨迹上的最优而非全局最优；放弃与否决/递归查询；当前优化目标是精度而非计算开销。未来工作包括以延迟/IO 为目标的计算驱动优化、结合 Weighted CP 与 Adaptive Conformal Inference 的连续在线重校准，以及向 RAG、learned join、多阶段检索等更广泛 DAG 型预测管线的推广。

Abstract: Querying incomplete knowledge graphs with neural predictors is powerful but dangerous. Errors compound across multi-hop pipelines with no formal bound on the completeness of results. We introduce ConRAD, the first framework to enforce declarative recall guarantees natively within a neural graph database query engine. Given a user-specified risk budget, ConRAD automatically derives per-operator prediction thresholds that satisfy the recall target with finite-sample, distribution-free statistical validity via Conformal Risk Control, while maximizing end-to-end precision. To scale calibration across multi-operator query topologies, we introduce a quantile-space scalarization that reduces intractable high-dimensional threshold searches to a single parameter. We further design the conformal gate, a novel physical operator that dynamically bypasses neural inference when local graph evidence suffices, eliminating unnecessary model inferences in dense graph regions. Evaluated across three benchmarks and three query topologies, ConRAD strictly satisfies all risk budgets, with empirical recall falling below the target by at most 0.046 across all settings. It reduces neural invocations to zero in near-complete graph regions, and achieves precision that matches or exceeds best-case static baselines that offer no guarantees and require manual threshold search.

</details>


### [125] [Inconsistent Databases and Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03954)
*Yasir Mahmood,Jonni Virtema,Timon Barlag,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文建立了不一致数据库修复与SETAFs之间的形式化对应关系。研究表明，子集极大修复在声明约束下对应多种论证扩展语义，而在LAV元组生成依赖下仅对应preferred扩展。不同IC类型决定了对论证框架复杂度的需求：某些IC可简化为传统AF，另一些则需SETAFs的集合攻击机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不一致数据库的子集极大修复与论证框架可接受论证集之间的深层联系，特别是当IC包含声明约束和LAV元组生成依赖时的对应关系。

Method: 通过建立数据库修复与SETAFs语义之间的映射关系，证明修复与各种扩展语义（naive、preferred、stable）之间的对应性，并分析不同IC类型对映射的影响。

Result: ①声明约束下，子集极大修复对应naive扩展，也等于SETAFs中的preferred和stable扩展；②元组生成依赖下，修复对应preferred扩展，预处理后可得唯一stable和naive扩展；③两类约束混合时，仅preferred语义能刻画修复；④函数依赖和包含依赖无需集合攻击，可映射为传统AF。

Conclusion: SETAFs在建模包含声明约束和LAV元组生成依赖的数据库修复方面优于传统AFs，但并非所有IC都需要集合攻击，函数依赖和包含依赖可映射到传统AFs。

Abstract: The connection between subset-maximal repairs for inconsistent databases involving various integrity constraints and acceptable sets of arguments within argumentation frameworks has recently drawn growing interest. In this paper, we contribute to this domain by establishing a new connection when integrity constraints (ICs) include denial constraints and local-as-view tuple-generating dependencies. It turns out that SET-based Argumentation Frameworks (SETAFs), an extension of Dung's argumentation frameworks (AFs) allowing collective attacks, are needed. It is known that subset-maximal repairs under denial constraints correspond to the naive extensions, which also coincide with the preferred and stable extensions in the resulting SETAFs. Our main findings establish that repairs under the considered fragment of tuple-generating dependencies correspond to the preferred extensions. Moreover, for these dependencies, additional preprocessing allows computing a unique extension that is stable and naive. Allowing both types of constraints breaks this relationship, and even the pre-processing does not help as only preferred semantics captures these repairs. Finally, while it is known that functional dependencies do not require set-based attacks, we prove the same regarding inclusion dependencies. Thus, one can translate inconsistent databases under these restricted classes of ICs to plain AFs with attacks only between arguments.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [ARIS: Autonomous Research via Adversarial Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03042)
*Ruofeng Yang,Yongcan Li,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARIS是一个开源自主研究框架，通过跨模型对抗协作解决长时研究中的隐式证据失败问题，具有执行层（65+技能、MCP集成、研究wiki）、编排层（五工作流）和保证层（三阶段声明检查、五轮编辑、证明检查）的三层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统的性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于其周围的框架（harness）。对于长时研究工作流，主要失败模式不是可见的崩溃，而是看似合理但实际缺乏证据支持的成功：运行时间较长的智能体会产生声称，其证据支持可能不完整、报告错误或被无声地继承自执行者的表述。

Method: ARIS采用跨模型对抗协作作为默认配置：执行模型驱动研究进展，来自不同模型家族的审查模型批判中间产物并请求修订。三层架构包括：(1)执行层：65+可复用Markdown定义技能、MCP模型集成、持久化研究wiki、确定性图表生成；(2)编排层：五个端到端工作流，支持可调努力程度和可配置审查模型路由；(3)保证层：实验声明支持检查的三阶段流程（完整性验证、结果-声明映射、声明审计）、五轮科学编辑流程、数学证明检查、渲染PDF视觉检查。

Result: 报告展示了ARIS的架构设计、保证机制和早期部署经验。原型自改进循环记录研究痕迹并提出框架改进建议，这些建议仅在审查模型批准后才被采纳。

Conclusion: ARIS是一个用于自主研究的开源研究框架，通过三层架构（执行层、编排层、保证层）解决LLM智能体在长时研究工作流中的隐式失败问题，特别是证据支持不完整或被错误继承的问题。

Abstract: This report describes ARIS (Auto-Research-in-sleep), an open-source research harness for autonomous research, including its architecture, assurance mechanisms, and early deployment experience. The performance of agent systems built on LLMs depends on both the model weights and the harness around them, which governs what information to store, retrieve, and present to the model. For long-horizon research workflows, the central failure mode is not a visible breakdown but a plausible unsupported success: a long-running agent can produce claims whose evidential support is incomplete, misreported, or silently inherited from the executor's framing. Therefore, we present ARIS as a research harness that coordinates machine-learning research workflows through cross-model adversarial collaboration as a default configuration: an executor model drives forward progress while a reviewer from a different model family is recommended to critique intermediate artifacts and request revisions. ARIS has three architectural layers. The execution layer provides more than 65 reusable Markdown-defined skills, model integrations via MCP, a persistent research wiki for iterative reuse of prior findings, and deterministic figure generation. The orchestration layer coordinates five end-to-end workflows with adjustable effort settings and configurable routing to reviewer models. The assurance layer includes a three-stage process for checking whether experimental claims are supported by evidence: integrity verification, result-to-claim mapping, and claim auditing that cross-checks manuscript statements against the claim ledger and raw evidence, as well as a five-pass scientific-editing pipeline, mathematical-proof checks, and visual inspection of the rendered PDF. A prototype self-improvement loop records research traces and proposes harness improvements that are adopted only after reviewer approval.

</details>


### [127] [ARISE: A Repository-level Graph Representation and Toolset for Agentic Fault Localization and Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03117)
*Shahd Seddik,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ARISE通过多粒度程序图（含语句级定义-使用边）和数据流切片API，增强LLM智能体在仓库级故障定位和自动修复中的语义精度。实验表明该方法显著提升定位和修复性能，且改进来源于数据流图而非工具模式设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的系统提供仓库的结构表示（文件、类、函数及其关系），但未建模过程内变量值的流动方式，导致智能体缺乏函数级和行级定位所需的语义精度。

Method: 提出多粒度程序图，将结构关系扩展到语句级节点，通过过程内定义-使用边连接；通过三层工具API暴露该图，将数据流切片作为一等原生、可查询的智能体原语，允许模型在单次调用中追踪定义或消费感兴趣变量的语句。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite（300个真实GitHub问题，11个Python仓库）上使用Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct作为骨干模型。相比未修改的SWE-agent基线，ARISE将Function Recall@1提升17.0分，Line Recall@1提升15.0分。这些定位改进直接转化为修复成功。

Conclusion: ARISE在SWE-bench Lite上达到22.0% Pass@1（66/300），比SWE-agent提升4.7个百分点。消融实验证实改进来自数据流图而非工具模式，大语言模型可直接消费结构化切片输出而无需自然语言摘要层。图构建器和切片API被设计为框架无关的即插即用工具集，可用于未来APR研究。

Abstract: Repository-level fault localization (FL) and automated program repair (APR) require an agent to identify the relevant code units across files, follow call and data dependencies, and generate a valid patch. Existing graph-based systems provide structural representations of repositories (files, classes, functions and their relationships) but do not model how variable values flow within procedures, leaving agents without the semantic precision needed for function- and line-level localization. We present ARISE (Agentic Repository-level Issue Solving Engine), which augments an LLM-based agent with a multi-granularity program graph that extends structural relationships down to statement-level nodes connected by intra-procedural definition-use edges. ARISE exposes this graph through a three-tier tool API, which brings data-flow slicing as a first-class, queryable agent primitive that allows the model to trace, in a single call, which statements define or consume a variable of interest. We evaluate on SWE-bench Lite (300 real GitHub issues, 11 Python repositories) using Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct as the backbone. Compared to the unmodified SWE-agent baseline, ARISE improves Function Recall@1 by 17.0 points and Line Recall@1 by 15.0 points. These localization gains translate directly into repair success, with ARISE achieving 22.0% Pass@1 (66/300), a 4.7 percentage-point improvement over SWE-agent. Controlled ablations confirm that the improvement is driven by the data-flow graph rather than the tool schema, and that large code models consume structured slice output directly without requiring a natural-language summarization layer. The graph builder and slicing API are designed as a framework-agnostic, drop-in toolset for future APR research.

</details>


### [128] [Kerncap: Automated Kernel Extraction and Isolation for AMD GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03208)
*Cole Ramos,Keith Lowery*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Kerncap 是 AMD 开源的 GPU kernel 自动化提取与隔离工具：它在 HSA 运行时层拦截 HIP 与 Triton 的 kernel dispatch，做"地址空间闭包"式的虚拟地址保真设备内存快照，自动定位 kernel 源码，并生成可编辑、可重编译、可自验证的自包含 reproducer 工程。在 llama.cpp 案例中，把"数小时的人工 kernel 隔离"压缩为一条命令，端到端 edit-recompile-validate 循环比传统全应用流程快 13.6×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU kernel 调优最有效的形式是"隔离迭代"：编辑单个 kernel、单独重编译、单独验证。但真实应用（LLM 推理、HPC 模拟）规模巨大，隔离一个 kernel 反而成为主要成本。论文指出 kernel 不是自包含产物，reproducer 必须同时恢复三个紧耦合组件：(1) 定义（编译后的 kernel 二进制 + 全部头文件/翻译单元依赖 + 原始编译 flag）；(2) 运行时状态（grid/block 配置、kernel 参数、通过指针间接可达的所有设备内存内容）；(3) 环境（可忠实重放原执行的构建配置与 dispatch 机制）。手工隔离需重建编译 flag、dispatch 配置和运行时输入，极易因缺失头依赖、误判 dispatch 配置或内存捕获不全而失效，开发者因而退化为"全量重建 + 全应用重跑"的慢循环（llama.cpp 单次 CMake 全量重建 128 s，约为 8.3 s 基线运行时的 15×）。此外，作者指出该类工作此前存在空白：NVIDIA 的 Nsight Compute / CUPTI Checkpoint API 虽做 kernel/状态重放，但闭源、仅 NVIDIA 硬件，且把捕获状态当作内部重执行缓冲，而非开发者可读、可编辑、可重编译的产物；rr/CRIU 只覆盖 CPU 侧状态；rocprofv3 只做 tracing，torch.compile 的 TORCH_COMPILE_DEBUG 不捕获运行时张量输入。

Method: 系统为 C++/Python 混合实现，暴露 profile / extract / replay 三条 CLI 命令，内部为五阶段流水线：

1) 运行时拦截：libkerncap.so 经 LD_PRELOAD 加载，向 rocprofiler-sdk 注册 intercept callback，在 HSA 初始化时拿到活的 HsaApiTable 并替换函数指针——拦截 hsa_queue_create（创建 intercept queue 并注册 per-packet 回调）、内存分配（pool/VMEM 的 reserve/map/free）以维护"指针→大小"映射、hsa_executable_get_symbol_by_name 以关联 kernel object 与 mangled symbol、code object reader 以截获 HSACO 二进制。为在 kernel 执行后而非执行前快照，Kerncap 将 dispatch packet 的 completion signal 替换为新 signal、转发包并阻塞等待，完成后再做内存快照并递减原 signal 以放行应用。
2) Triton 桥接：Triton 最终也走 HSA dispatch，故复用同一拦截库；但 HSA 看不到 Triton 层元数据（用户可见 kernel 名、类型化签名、tl.constexpr 值、张量 dtype/stride、autotuner 配置）。为此通过 sitecustomize.py 在解释器启动时挂钩 Triton JIT 编译路径（可随 multiprocessing.spawn 传播到 vLLM 的 EngineCore worker），把 HSACO 的 SHA-256 与上述元数据写入 name_map.json；调度时用 amd_comgr 在 executable load 阶段解析 AMDGPU code-object metadata 取 kernarg 各 slot 的 offset/size/value_kind/type，与 name_map.json 交叉引用还原类型化调用；捕获完成时写 capture_complete 哨兵文件，CLI 的 watchdog 线程随即 SIGTERM/SIGKILL 宿主进程组，实现早期终止。
3) 地址空间闭包（VA-faithful 快照）：kernel 执行后遍历全部被跟踪分配，按原始虚拟地址分块（默认 64 MiB，KERNCAP_SNAPSHOT_CHUNK_BYTES 可调）用 hsa_memory_copy 流式落盘为 memory/region_{base}.bin，同时写 memory_regions.json（基址、大小、pool/VMEM 类型、是否含 kernarg）。由于在原虚拟地址恢复，内嵌设备指针（T** 等）关系"免费"保留，无需 DWARF 或指针追逐。此外捕获 HSA_SYMBOL_KIND_VARIABLE 模块变量（如 Kokkos 的 kokkos_impl_hip_constant_memory_buffer）到 module_vars/，并以 executable SHA-256 消歧同名符号；元数据文件先于拷贝写出以容忍竞态释放。
4) 源码发现：HIP 路径以 demangled 名 + mangled symbol + source dir 为输入，优先 DWARF 路径——用 nm 在 compile_commands.json 列出的目标文件中定位含该 mangled symbol 的 .o，再用 llvm-dwarfdump（回退 readelf）读行表提取翻译单元内全部源文件（这是 Kokkos 等框架生成 kernel 唯一可行的路径）；无调试信息时回退两遍 grep（严格匹配 __global__ 定义，再宽松匹配基名）；递归追踪 #include 依赖（最深 5 层）；翻译单元歧义（如 llama.cpp 的 mmq-instance-*.cu）用 nm 按精确 mangled symbol 消歧；编译 flag（-D/-I）取自匹配条目，缺失时扫描源码中的 HIP/ROCm/AMD 条件宏推断。Triton 路径走 Python AST：解析所有 .py，查找带 @triton.jit/@triton.autotune 的 FunctionDef，按名匹配，追踪 ImportFrom（相对/绝对），识别 __init__.py 包结构。
