Method: 框架：教师 p_T 与学生 p_S 由同一参数 p_θ 通过不同上下文实例化。学生仅接收目标低资源 query x_tgt；教师额外获得英语平行 query x* 与 CoT 指令 C（要求英语推理、用目标语言作答），从而激发模型自身的高资源安全推理。采样策略统一为 q(·)：on-policy 用学生 rollout（y~p_S），off-policy 用教师 rollout（y~p_T）。训练目标为 token 级散度（主实验用反向 KL）最小化，仅更新学生，教师冻结。DPSW：教师视角 w_t^T = 1 − 归一化 top-K 熵/logK（K=32，衡量教师对安全关键 token 的置信度）；学生视角 w_t^S = 1 − p_S(y*_t)（衡量与教师选择不一致的风险）；w_t = w_t^T·w_t^S 并按序列长度归一化，stop-gradient 后加权散度。训练数据仅用 XSafety 的多语言 query（EN/ZH/AR/BN），测试用 MultiJail、PKU-SafeRLHF（GPT-4o 评审，辅以 GPT-5.5、GLM-5.2），通用能力用 MMMLU（5-shot）、MGSM（0-shot CoT）。基线含 SFT、DPO/rDPO/KTO/ORPO/R-DPO/SimPO、PolyRefuse、Self-Defense、SDRRL、MPO。

Result: 1) 安全性：Qwen-3-8B 在 MultiJail 十语言平均 ASR 由 21.68% 降至 9.90%(off)/10.04%(on)；LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct 由 21.21% 降至 2.92%(off)/2.03%(on)；LLaMA-2-7B-chat 由 22.03% 降至 8.86%(off)/2.48%(on)，全面优于全部基线（最佳基线 MPO/SDRRL 约 9–14%）。2) 未见语言泛化稳健：训练仅含 4 语，IT/VI/KO/TH/SW/JV 仍显著提升（LLaMA-3 斯瓦希里语 69.52%→6.03%/4.13%）。3) 通用能力保持：MMMLU/MGSM 平均分与原始模型相当或更优（Qwen-3 MMMLU 64.65→66.88/66.76；LLaMA-3 48.17→48.16）。4) DPSW 消融在所有模型/基准上一致有效（Qwen-3 on-policy：10.04/7.62 vs 11.09/9.10）；安全关键 token 的 w_t、w_t^T、w_t^S 均显著高于非关键 token（GPT-4o 语义 span 标注，标注者不见权重）。5) 其他发现：反向 KL 优于 FKL/JSD；冻结教师优于 EMA 更新（α=0.05 致安全与能力双崩）；教师侧英语 query 与 CoT 缺一不可；直接用 ground-truth 拒答会引发捷径学习并使 LLaMA-3 的 MMMLU 从 48.17 跌至 18.58。6) 成本：外部响应数据成本 $0（DPO 类 $1200、SFT $650、SDRRL $450）；单 epoch 训练时间高于 SFT/DPO（2.1–3.0 GPU-h），但在等 30 分钟单卡预算下 ASR 仍最低；教师-学生 KL 在未见语言上下降 29.7%–32.7%。7) 局限：Qwen 系列在斯瓦希里语残余高 ASR 主因是 invalid 响应（低资源生成能力弱，unsafe 仅约 6.7%）而非真实安全失败；人工校验基于英文翻译后的响应，评审仍依赖 LLM 评委。

Conclusion: MSD 提供了一个 response-free、可扩展的跨语言安全对齐范式：把「已有护栏」而非「外部标注数据」作为迁移对象，通过自蒸馏 + token 级自适应加权实现低成本、高泛化的多语言安全对齐。局限在于其上限受基座模型在高资源语言的护栏强度与 ICL 能力约束，并依赖高质量的平行 query 翻译质量；后续可探索更强的翻译鲁棒性与多教师/多语言组合。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit severe multilingual safety misalignment: they possess strong safeguards in high-resource languages but remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in low-resource languages. Current safety alignment methods generally rely on high-quality response data for each target language, which is expensive and difficult to generate. In this paper, we propose a cross-lingual safeguard transfer framework named Multilingual Self-Distillation (MSD). This framework transfers an LLM's inherent safety capabilities from high-resource (e.g., English) to low-resource (e.g., Javanese) languages, overcoming the need for response data in any language. Our framework is flexible and can be integrated with different self-distillation strategies. Specifically, we implement two concrete methods -- on-policy MSD and off-policy MSD -- both of which enable effective cross-lingual safety transfer using only multilingual queries. Furthermore, we propose Dual-Perspective Safety Weighting (DPSW), a divergence measure to optimize the distillation objective. By jointly considering the perspectives of both the teacher and the student, DPSW adaptively increases the penalty weights on safety-critical tokens while reducing the weights on non-critical tokens. Extensive experiments on representative LLMs across diverse multilingual jailbreak and utility benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently achieves superior multilingual safety performance. Notably, it generalizes effectively to more challenging datasets and unseen languages while preserving the model's general capabilities.

</details>


### [179] [Structured Diffusion Bridges: Inductive Bias for Denoising Diffusion Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02973)
*Eitan Kosman,Gabriele Serussi,Chaim Basking*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散桥的跨模态转换框架，将配对监督视为可选启发式约束而非必要前提。该方法在有配对、半配对和无配对数据的场景下均表现一致，仅需少量配对即可达到接近全配对数据下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态转换任务本质上是欠约束的，多个跨模态映射可能产生相同的边缘分布。现有方法大多依赖完全配对的数据集，这限制了其适用范围。

Method: 提出一种扩散桥框架，通过对齐约束(alignment constraints)来表征和限制可行解空间，将配对监督作为可选的启发式信息而非必要条件。

Result: 在合成数据和真实跨模态翻译基准数据集上验证了该方法，涵盖无配对、半配对和全配对三种设置，表现稳定。

Conclusion: 扩散桥是构建不完全依赖配对数据的跨模态转换方法的灵活基础，仅需少量配对即可达到接近全配对质量，且适用于完全无配对场景。

Abstract: Modality translation is inherently under-constrained, as multiple cross-modal mappings may yield the same marginals. Recent work has shown that diffusion bridges are effective for this task. However, most existing approaches rely on fully paired datasets, thereby imposing a single data-driven constraint. We propose a diffusion-bridge framework that characterizes the space of admissible solutions and restricts it via alignment constraints, treating paired supervision as an optional heuristic rather than a prerequisite. We validate our method on synthetic and real modality translation benchmarks across unpaired, semi-paired, and paired regimes, showing consistent performance across supervision levels. Notably, \textbf{it achieves near fully-paired quality with a substantial relaxation in pairing requirements, and remaining applicable in the unpaired regime}. These results highlight diffusion bridges as a flexible foundation for modality translation beyond fully paired data.

</details>


### [180] [Mixed-Precision Information Bottlenecks for On-Device Trait-State Disentanglement in Bipolar Agitation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03039)
*Joydeep Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MP-IB是首个将混合精度量化作为信息瓶颈的框架，用于从语音中分离双相障碍患者的稳定身份特征与波动情感状态。核心创新在于利用精度差异实现8倍信息不对称：FP16编码说话者身份(1024 bits)，INT4捕获激越状态(128 bits)。在Bridge2AI-Voice数据集(833人)上，speaker-independent条件下rho=0.117，优于94M参数的WavLM-Adapter、beta VAE和手工特征。在CREMA-D上零样本迁移AUC=0.817，身份泄露接近随机(EER=0.42)，延迟23.4ms，模型仅617KB，可部署于廉价边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双相障碍患者的激越状态需要持续监测，但语音生物标志物面临两个核心挑战：(1)需从语音中分离稳定的说话者身份特征与波动的情感状态；(2)边缘设备资源受限，需实现实时推理。当前方法（如自监督学习继续训练、beta VAE解耦、手工韵律特征）难以平衡性能与隐私/效率。

Method: 提出MP-IB框架，核心创新是将混合精度量化作为信息瓶颈：FP16 trait head (1024 bits) 编码说话者身份，INT4 state head (128 bits) 捕获激越状态，实现8倍信息不对称。辅以动态精度调度和多尺度时间融合技术。

Result: Bridge2AI-Voice数据集(833人，4会话/人，严格speaker-independent CV)：rho=0.117 (95% CI: [0.089, 0.145], p=0.003)，较WavLM-Adapter(rho=-0.042)、beta VAE(rho=0.089)、手工韵律(rho=0.031)提升2.8-15.9个百分点。CREMA-D零样本：AUC=0.817。身份泄露抑制至接近随机(EER=0.42, MIA-AUC=0.52)。延迟23.4ms，模型617KB。

Conclusion: MP-IB通过精度驱动的信息瓶颈首次实现了临床级激越检测与隐私保护的统一，在保持竞争力的同时达到亚毫秒级延迟和亚MB级体积，可部署于低于20美元的边缘设备。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of bipolar disorder agitation via voice biomarkers requires disentangling stable speaker traits from volatile affective states on resource-constrained edge devices. We introduce MP-IB, the first framework to treat mixed-precision quantization as an information bottleneck for clinical trait-state separation. The core insight is that numerical precision itself controls capacity: an FP16 trait head (1,024 bits) encodes speaker identity, while an INT4 state head (128 bits) captures agitation, yielding 8x information asymmetry without adversarial training. We augment this with Dynamic Precision Scheduling and Multi-Scale Temporal Fusion. On Bridge2AI-Voice (N=833, 4 sessions/participant, strict speaker-independent CV), MP-IB achieves rho = 0.117 (95\% CI: [0.089, 0.145], p=0.003 vs. chance), outperforming 94M-parameter WavLM-Adapter with in-domain SSL continuation (rho = -0.042), beta VAE disentanglement (rho = 0.089), and hand-crafted prosody (rho = 0.031) by 2.8--15.9 points absolute. Zero-shot transfer to CREMA-D achieves AUC=0.817. Identity leakage is suppressed to near-random (EER=0.42, MIA-AUC=0.52). End-to-end latency is 23.4 ms with a 617 KB footprint, enabling real-time monitoring on sub 20 dollar devices.

</details>


### [181] [TCD-Arena: Assessing Robustness of Time Series Causal Discovery Methods Against Assumption Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03045)
*Gideon Stein,Niklas Penzel,Tristan Piater,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TCD-Arena是一个用于评估时间序列因果发现算法鲁棒性的模块化测试框架，通过约3000万次实验揭示了33种假设违背下算法的鲁棒性表现，并发现集成方法有望改善鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现（CD）框架在科学探索中具有重要价值，但实际应用受到以下限制：(1)对强且通常不可验证的假设的依赖；(2)缺乏对算法鲁棒性的可靠性能评估。这两个问题阻碍了因果发现方法的实际推广和可信度。

Method: 构建了一个模块化、可高度定制和可扩展的测试工具包TCD-Arena，系统性地对33种不同的假设违背情形进行渐进式严重程度测试；进行了约3000万次独立因果发现实验来评估算法的鲁棒性表现；同时研究了因果发现集成方法在应对多种假设违背时的表现。

Result: 通过大规模实验（约3000万次因果发现尝试），揭示了33种假设违背情形下算法的细致鲁棒性特征，并发现因果发现集成方法具有提升整体鲁棒性的潜力。

Conclusion: TCD-Arena提供了首个系统化的时间序列因果发现算法鲁棒性评估框架，通过大规模实验揭示了不同算法的鲁棒性特征差异，为因果发现方法在真实复杂数据条件下的可靠应用提供了重要参考。集成方法展现出改善鲁棒性的潜力，表明在应对多假设违背场景时，多方法协同可能优于单一方法。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) is a powerful framework for scientific inquiry. Yet, its practical adoption is hindered by a reliance on strong, often unverifiable assumptions and a lack of robust performance assessment. To address these limitations and advance empirical CD evaluation, we present TCD-Arena, a modularized, highly customizable, and extendable testing kit to assess the robustness of time series CD algorithms against stepwise more severe assumption violations. For demonstration, we conduct an extensive empirical study comprising around 30 million individual CD attempts and reveal nuanced robustness profiles for 33 distinct assumption violations. Further, we investigate CD ensembles and find that they have the potential to improve general robustness, which has implications for real-world applications. With this, we strive to ultimately facilitate the development of CD methods that are reliable for a diverse range of synthetic and potentially real-world data conditions.

</details>


### [182] [Neuron-Anchored Rule Extraction for Large Language Models via Contrastive Hierarchical Ablation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03058)
*Francesco Sovrano,Gabriele Dominici,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MechaRule通过"激动剂"神经元定位与分组测试方法，将可解释AI中的符号规则提取与语言模型的电路机制研究相结合，在算术和越狱任务中实现了高效规则相关神经元识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current XAI approaches suffer from a gap: global rule-extraction methods lack grounding in model circuitry, while mechanistic interpretability depends on hand-crafted hypotheses and expensive neuron-level interventions. A bridge between symbolic rule extraction and circuit-level understanding is needed.

Method: The pipeline combines: (1) Regime-conditional strength predicate with confidence-guided conservative pruning for adaptive group testing, achieving Θ(k log(N/k) + k) complexity; (2) Data split alignment strategy prioritizing faithful splits over spectral splits for reliable agonist localization. Ablation experiments on Qwen2 and GPT-J for arithmetic and jailbreak tasks demonstrate effectiveness.

Result: MechaRule achieves 96.8% recall of high-effect brute-force agonists, and suppressing localized agonists reduces arithmetic accuracy by up to 71.1% and jailbreak success by 8.8%.

Conclusion: MechaRule provides a theoretically grounded and empirically validated approach to link symbolic rule extraction with mechanistic interpretability, enabling reliable identification of agonist neurons that can be suppressed to disrupt rule-related behaviors.

Abstract: A key goal of explainable AI (XAI) is to express the decision logic of large language models (LLMs) in symbolic form and link it to internal mechanisms. Global rule-extraction methods typically learn symbolic surrogates without grounding rules in model circuitry, while mechanistic interpretability can connect behaviors to neuron sets but often depends on hand-crafted hypotheses and expensive neuron-level interventions. We introduce MechaRule, a pipeline that grounds rule extraction in LLM circuits by efficiently localizing sparse neurons called agonists, whose activation neutralization disrupts rule-related behaviors. MechaRule rests on two empirical observations. First, within a fixed baseline/flip regime, sparse agonist effects can be approximately monotone and saturating: a few dominant neuron activations can overtop weaker ones at coarse scales, while overlapping neurons flip many of the same examples. This motivates viewing localization as adaptive group testing driven by a regime-conditional strength predicate with confidence-guided conservative pruning, yielding Theta(k log(N/k) + k) interventions over N candidates when k << N neurons are agonists under the monotone-overtopping abstraction. Second, agonists emerge more reliably when ablations are verified through data splits aligned with close-to-faithful rule behavior; spectral splits remain a useful rule-free fallback, while unfaithful splits degrade localization. Empirically, overtopping appears mainly in learned, task-aligned regimes: on arithmetic and jailbreak tasks across Qwen2 and GPT-J, MechaRule recalls 96.8% of high-effect brute-force agonists in completed comparisons, and suppressing localized agonists reduces arithmetic accuracy and jailbreak success by up to 71.1% and 8.8%, respectively.

</details>


### [183] [OGPO: Sample Efficient Full-Finetuning of Generative Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03065)
*Sarvesh Patil,Mitsuhiko Nakamoto,Manan Agarwal,Shashwat Saxena,Jesse Zhang,Giri Anantharaman,Cleah Winston,Chaoyi Pan,Douglas Chen,Nai-Chieh Huang,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer,Sergey Levine,Abhishek Gupta,Hongkai Da,Paarth Shah,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OGPO（Off-policy Generative Policy Optimization）：用离策略 TD 学习训练 Q 函数集成，并把 Q 值当作生成式控制策略（GCP，扩散/流匹配）内部“去噪 MDP”的终止奖励，再以 PPO 式的零阶（zeroth-order）策略梯度对整条去噪链做全策略微调，从而兼具 off-policy 的样本效率与 on-policy 的表达力。其改进版 OGPO+ 引入成功缓冲区行为克隆正则化，OGPO+CA 引入双边保守优势以抑制 critic 过估计。在 Robomimic/Kitchen/Adroit/LIBERO 上达到 SOTA，样本效率约为 on-policy 的 DPPO 的 10 倍，并可在无专家数据的在线缓冲下将弱 BC 初始化策略微调到接近满成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式控制策略（GCP，如扩散/流匹配策略）已成为机器人学习的强参数化，但其在线 RL 微调存在“样本效率 vs 策略改进幅度”的两难：(1) 以 off-policy critic 为核心的方法（DSRL 噪声引导、EXPO 残差修正、QC/PA-RL 的 Best-of-N+BC）数据复用好、样本效率高，但只能在预训练分布的支撑集内做局部修改，基策略差或需高精度/长时序任务时崩溃，且无法扩展动作支撑集以保证探索；(2) 强调最终性能的方法（DPPO、ReinFlow、RL-100）把 GCP 视作双层（bi-level）MDP，用 on-policy 策略梯度贯穿整个去噪过程，改进激进但环境样本效率极低。作者指出机器人领域存在“样本代价不对称”：环境交互轨迹昂贵，而 GCP 内部去噪轨迹纯计算、几乎免费，因此应把环境层与去噪层解耦——在环境 MDP 中用 off-policy TD 学习（昂贵样本高复用），在去噪 MDP 中用 on-policy RL 做提取（廉价计算、表达力强）。

Method: 1）形式化：把 GCP 表示为去噪 MDP（状态 a_{t,k}、动作 a_{t,k-1}），与环境 MDP 组成双层 MDP；OGPO 在每次去噪轨迹末端“截断”双层 MDP，用 Q_targ 作为动作轨迹级终止奖励。2）Critic：M 个 Q 网络集成，TD 损失用 action-chunk 的 h 步回报回传（Q-chunking 风格），target 取 min/mean/subsample 聚合，EMA 更新；可选 Q-方差缩减（对 N_vr 个下一动作取平均 TD 目标）。3）策略提取：对回放缓冲中采样 N_batch 个状态，每个状态并行采样 N_group 条去噪轨迹（GRPO 式“context/response”类比），用 Q 的组内均值作价值基线（无需独立 value 网络），优势 Â = Q_targ − V̂（GRPO 的标准差归一化被作者发现有害而弃用），损失为 PPO 裁剪目标，其重要性比率为沿去噪链各步似然比之积（Annealed Importance Sampling，K≤10 使轨迹级 IS 稳定），梯度因此传播到去噪每一步，实现全策略微调；零阶更新避免了对 Q 的 ∇_a 反传与跨去噪步 BPTT 的梯度爆炸。4）流策略的 ODE→SDE 转换：每步注入高斯噪声以获得非奇异似然，并用 Albergo 等的随机插值边缘分布校正（分数漂移项经策略重参数化为 c = σ_init²(π_θ(x_τ,τ)τ − x)/2，配合 σ_τ = σ_init√(1−τ) 的锥形噪声调度）。5）稳定化组件：OGPO+ 用成功缓冲区 D_succ ⊆ D_roll 做 BC 正则（对 flow 为 CFM 损失，对 diffusion 为去噪分数匹配），作者证明其为前向 KL 的 ELBO/“模式保持屏障”；OGPO+CA 用集成的组内优势构造保守优势（仅当全部成员优势同号才更新，取最悲观幅值）；可选 Best-of-N 推理（用 Q 作验证器，仅边际收益）。6）实现细节：动作分块 h、EMA 参考策略、N_group=32、PPO clip ε=0.01、UTD=1。

Result: （1）样本效率：OGPO+ 相对 on-policy 的 DPPO 在 Robomimic Square/Transport 上达到同等最终成功率所需环境样本约少 10 倍；相对 off-policy 基线 DSRL/EXPO/QC 也有数量级提升，且几乎不需任务特定调参（论文称是唯一能把弱初始化（≤50% 成功率）BC 策略在“在线回放缓冲无专家数据”条件下微调至接近满成功率的方法）。（2）表达能力：全策略微调显著优于噪声引导（DSRL）与残差修正（EXPO），后者在基策略差/无离线数据时不稳定或崩溃；对 Adroit 稠密奖励任务 OGPO 弱于 EXPO。（3）机制分析：UMAP 与 ∇_a Q 分析显示 OGPO 选择性“拉伸”动作方差——沿 critic 梯度方向收缩/锐化，沿与 critic 梯度正交方向保留方差，从而同时获得高成功率、快完成与轨迹级多模态探索（PushT 上验证）；作者假设这源于对整个生成链每一步的微调（FPO++ 也有类似但较弱的效应）。（4）失效模式与稳定化：vanilla OGPO 存在 critic 过利用，表现为成功率—完成速度的紧张（轨迹长度骤降、成功率震荡/平台），且在像素输入下更严重（图像 critic 使策略无法改进）；OGPO+ 的成功缓冲正则一致改善样本效率与渐近性能，OGPO+CA 使 Q 估计更贴近真值、抑制 offline→online 的“性能凹陷”，是像素任务稳定训练的关键。附加增益项：Q-方差缩减在像素任务上有效、状态任务几乎无差；GRPO 标准差归一化、WSRL+CalQL 离线预训练均不如目标化干预。（5）提取目标消融：PPO 式似然比最优且最稳；AWR-FM 在 Robomimic 上失败；AW-OGPO/OFPO++ 较差，OFPO++ 在 Transport 长时序上崩溃；去掉负优势梯度在 Square/Toolhang 影响小但在 Transport 明显变差（负优势对抑制预训练次优模式必要）；BPTT 直接反传 Q 与去噪链梯度不稳、收敛差。（6）可扩展性：OGPO 可直接用于 diffusion 策略（Square 上验证），并在 LIBERO 像素+语言任务上有效。

Conclusion: OGPO 通过“环境层 off-policy TD 学习 + 去噪层 on-policy PPO 式零阶提取”的解耦设计，在生成式控制策略的全量微调上同时获得样本效率与策略表达力，是目前最简洁有效的方案之一；其三个关键经验结论是：(a) 更好的策略提取本身（而非更好的 critic）即可显著提升稳定性和样本效率；(b) 简单的 PPO 是最优、最稳且调参最少的提取目标；(c) 全策略微调不会导致模式坍缩，反而能增加动作多样性与探索。配套的稳定化手段（成功缓冲 BC、保守优势、Q 方差缩减）优于降低学习率、悲观更新或熵/距离正则等通用做法。局限：估计 Q 所需的并行去噪 rollout 对大 VLA 推理成本极高，可能限制扩展；稠密奖励类任务（如 Adroit）表现不如残差类方法；关于“为何保留方差”的完整解释仍待后续研究。此外该文所标注日期与部分引用（如 2026 年文献）存在时序异常，读者宜以可复现代码为准。

Abstract: Generative control policies (GCPs), such as diffusion- and flow-based control policies, have emerged as effective parameterizations for robot learning. This work introduces Off-policy Generative Policy Optimization (OGPO), a sample-efficient algorithm for finetuning GCPs that maintains off-policy critic networks to maximize data reuse and propagate policy gradients through the full generative process of the policy via a modified PPO objective, using critics as the terminal reward. OGPO achieves state-of-the-art performance on manipulation tasks spanning multi-task settings, high-precision insertion, and dexterous control. To our knowledge, it is also the only method that can fine-tune poorly-initialized behavior cloning policies to near full task-success with no expert data in the online replay buffer, and does so with few task-specific hyperparameter tuning. Through extensive empirical investigations, we demonstrate the OGPO drastically outperforms methods alternatives on policy steering and learning residual corrections, and identify the key mechanisms behind its performance. We further introduce practical stabilizers, including success-buffer regularization, conservative advantages, $χ^2$ regularization, and Q-variance reduction, to mitigate critic over-exploitation across state- and pixel-based settings. Beyond proposing OGPO, we conduct a systematic empirical study of GCP finetuning, identifying the stabilizing mechanisms and failure modes that govern successful off-policy full-policy improvement.

</details>


### [184] [Adaptive Negative Scheduling for Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03076)
*Adnan Ali,Jinlong Li,Syed Muhammad Israr,Ali Kashif Bashir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdNGCL通过引入硬度感知调度器(HANS)解决图对比学习中静态负采样的局限性，将负样本选择建模为损失门控、预算约束的过程，在三个难度层级(硬/中等/易)间动态调整步长，并在9个基准数据集上验证了7个第一、2个第二的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图对比学习方法依赖静态负采样，未能考虑训练过程中负样本的动态信息量和计算成本，导致效率和效果之间的权衡不理想。

Method: 提出AdNGCL框架，包含HANS调度器，将负样本选择建模为损失门控的预算约束优化问题；在硬/中/易三个层级进行负样本选择；基于对比损失趋势动态调整步长；维护全局和类别级预算；周期性刷新样本以保持多样性。

Result: 在9个图基准数据集上，AdNGCL在7个数据集上取得最佳准确率，2个数据集上取得次优，同时提供明确的计算成本控制能力。

Conclusion: 预算感知的损失敏感调度策略是提升新兴计算智能应用中表示学习鲁棒性和效率的有效方法。

Abstract: Graph contrastive learning (GCL) has become a central paradigm for self-supervised representation learning in computational intelligence, with applications spanning recommendation, anomaly detection, and personalization. A key limitation of existing methods is their reliance on static negative sampling, which fails to account for the dynamic informativeness and computational cost of negatives during training. We propose AdNGCL, an adaptive negative scheduling framework with a hardness-aware scheduler (HANS) that formulates negative selection as a loss-gated, budget-constrained process across hard, intermediate, and easy strata. The scheduler dynamically adjusts step sizes based on contrastive loss trends under both global and per-category budgets, while periodically refreshing samples to maintain diversity without exceeding compute constraints. Experiments on nine benchmark graph datasets demonstrate that AdNGCL consistently advances state-of-the-art performance, achieving the best accuracy on seven datasets and second-best on the remaining two, while offering explicit control over computational cost. These results highlight the value of budget-aware, loss-sensitive scheduling as a general strategy for improving the robustness and efficiency of representation learning in emerging computational intelligence applications.

</details>


### [185] [Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction for Memory-Bounded EEG Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03085)
*Chengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对EEG信号的UICL问题，提出ADaCoRe方法，通过形态学感知的选择、压缩和重建，实现内存高效的自适应学习，在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing UICL methods for EEG personalization store full past samples, undermining the continual learning goal of avoiding retraining under strict memory constraints.

Method: ADaCoRe combines four components: (1) saliency-driven keyframe protection to identify important EEG morphologies, (2) rational polyphase compression for efficient storage, (3) adjoint reconstruction with verbatim overwrite on protected indices, and (4) prototype-confidence selection for adaptive exemplar maintenance.

Result: ADaCoRe consistently outperforms recent baselines across three benchmarks under tight buffer regimes, with performance gains of at least +2.7 ACC on ISRUC and +15.3 ACC on FACED datasets.

Conclusion: ADaCoRe proposes a memory-efficient UICL pipeline that preserves key EEG morphology during compression, consistently outperforming baselines on three benchmarks with gains of +2.7 ACC (ISRUC) and +15.3 ACC (FACED) under tight memory constraints.

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) signals provide millisecond-level temporal resolution but their analysis is limited by remarkable noise and inter-subject variability, making robust personalization difficult under limited annotations. Unsupervised Individual Continual Learning (UICL) has been proposed to address this practical challenge, where a model pretrained on a labeled cohort must adapt online to unlabeled subject streams under strict memory constraints. However, existing UICL methods typically store full past samples, which undermine the continual learning goal of avoiding retraining. Observing that EEG signals exhibit well-structured morphologies to be exploited via morphology-aware selection, compression, and reconstruction, here we propose Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction (ADaCoRe) for UICL. This is a memory-efficient pipeline composed of saliency-driven keyframe protection, rational polyphase compression, adjoint reconstruction with verbatim overwrite on protected indices, and prototype-confidence selection for adaptive exemplar maintenance. Across three representative benchmarks, ADaCoRe consistently outperforms recent strong baselines under tight buffer regimes (eg., the performance gains are at least +2.7 and +15.3 ACC on ISRUC and FACED datasets, respectively). Ablation studies quantify compression-fidelity trade-offs and highlight the contribution of each design, while visualizations confirm the preservation of key EEG morphology during compression and reconstruction.

</details>


### [186] [Attribution-Guided Masking for Robust Cross-Domain Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03091)
*Shubham Harkare,Arvind Yogesh Suresh Babu,Yash Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预训练Transformer模型在跨领域情感分类中表现严重退化，本文提出 Attribution-Guided Masking (AGM) 方法，通过在微调时动态检测和惩罚高度归因的虚假tokens来缓解这一问题，在零样本迁移设置下取得了与多个强基线方法相当的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer模型在领域内情感分类任务上准确率很高，但在迁移到领域外数据时性能严重下降。研究假设这种泛化差距是由模型对领域特定虚假tokens（如@mentions、hashtags、俚语等）的依赖所驱动的。

Method: 提出 Attribution-Guided Masking (AGM)，核心是梯度-based 归因掩码损失 (L_mask)，可选择性地与反事实对比损失结合以增强领域不变表征。整个方法无需目标领域标签或人工标注，在微调阶段动态检测并惩罚高度归因的虚假tokens。

Result: 在四个不同领域、八个随机种子的严格零样本迁移设置下评估，AGM 在最难迁移任务（Sentiment140）上达到 Δ=0.244，与 DANN(0.264)、DRO(0.248)、Fish(0.247)、IRM(0.238) 等基线方法性能相当，且提供了token级别的可解释性。消融实验证实归因引导掩码是关键组件。

Conclusion: AGM 通过抑制对领域特定tokens的归因，将模型依赖转向领域不变的情感标记，有效提升了跨领域泛化能力。归因引导掩码是提升泛化性能的关键因素，替换为随机token选择会导致性能下降。

Abstract: While pre-trained Transformer models achieve high accuracy on in-domain sentiment classification, they frequently experience severe performance degradation when transferring to out-of-domain data. We hypothesize that this generalization gap is driven by reliance on domain-specific spurious tokens. After demonstrating that post-hoc-token-level attribution drift fails to predict this gap, we propose Attribution-Guided Masking (AGM), a training time intervention that dynamically detects and penalizes highly attributed spurious tokens during fine-tuning. AGM's core component is a gradient based attribution masking loss ($\mathcal{L}_{mask}$), which can optionally be combined with a counterfactual contrastive loss to enforce domain-invariant representations, all without requiring target-domain labels or human annotation. Evaluated in a strict zero-shot transfer setting across four diverse domains with eight random seeds, AGM achieves competitive generalization compared to five strong baselines on the hardest transfer (Sentiment140): $Δ$ = 0.244 versus DANN (0.264), DRO (0.248), Fish (0.247), and IRM (0.238), while uniquely providing token-level interpretability into which features drive the generalization gap. Our qualitative analysis confirms that AGM suppresses attribution on domain-specific tokens such as @mentions, hashtags, and slang, shifting reliance toward domain-invariant sentiment markers. Our ablation study further confirms that attribution-guided masking is the critical component: removing it or replacing it with random token selection consistently degrades performance on difficult transfers.

</details>


### [187] [When Prompts Interact: Assessing Prompt Arithmetic for Deconfounding under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03096)
*Zhecheng Sheng,Yongsen Tan,Xiruo Ding,Trevor Cohen,Serguei Pakhomov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HyPA方法，通过在软提示上进行任务算术运算，结合任务提示与去线性化混淆提示，以提升模型对分布偏移的鲁棒性，同时保持较好的任务性能。该方法仅需调整少量虚拟令牌，参数效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类模型常依赖混淆变量（捷径特征）来获得良好的分布内性能，但在分布偏移时性能急剧下降。传统任务算术方法虽能消除不需要的信号，但需要全量微调，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic (HyPA)方法，在软提示上进行任务算术运算，将任务提示与线性化混淆提示相结合以抵消虚假相关性。该方法仅需调整少量可训练的虚拟令牌，实现参数高效。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，HyPA在分布偏移下始终改善了鲁棒性与性能的权衡。分析表明，HyPA通过减少混淆信号对预测的影响或在表示中抑制混淆信号来缓解混淆问题。

Conclusion: HyPA是一种参数高效且有前景的方法，能够在评估的设置中提升对混淆分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In classification tasks, models may rely on confounding variables to achieve strong in-distribution performance, capturing spurious features that fail under distribution shift. This shortcut behavior leads to substantial degradation in out-of-distribution settings. Task arithmetic offers a potential solution by removing unwanted signals via subtraction of secondary model updates, but it typically requires full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive. Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient alternative by adapting models through a small set of trainable virtual tokens. Task arithmetic on the resulting prompts presents an appealing alternative to operations on entire models, but the extent to which this approach can limit reliance on spurious features remains to be established. In this work, we study whether composing soft prompts through task arithmetic improves robustness to confounding shifts. We propose Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic (HyPA), which combines task prompts with linearized confounder prompts to counteract spurious correlations. Across multiple benchmarks, HyPA consistently improves the robustness-performance trade-off relative to prompt-arithmetic baselines under distribution shift. We further analyze how HyPA affects hidden representations and find evidence consistent with it mitigating confounding either by reducing the influence of confounder signals on predictions or by suppressing them in the representation. These results establish HyPA as a parameter-efficient and promising approach for improving robustness under confounding shifts in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [188] [Pose Tracking with a Foundation Pose Model and an Ensemble Directional Kalman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03105)
*Tianlu Lu,Asif Sijan,Thomas Noh,Huaijin Chen,Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出集成方向卡尔曼滤波器(EnDKF)，利用方向统计学处理姿态估计中方向不确定性问题，通过单位四元数表示姿态。实验表明该方法在合成系统和数字孪生头部追踪场景中均能显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准卡尔曼滤波器假设难以准确描述方向不确定性，导致姿态估计性能受限。

Method: 提出集成方向卡尔曼滤波器(EnDKF)，采用单位四元数表示姿态，结合方向统计学和集成方法进行联合位置与姿态估计。

Result: 在合成恒速-恒角速度系统和基于FoundationPose算法的数字孪生头部追踪场景中均验证了显著误差降低。

Conclusion: EnDKF有效解决了方向不确定性问题，在姿态追踪任务中优于传统卡尔曼滤波方法。

Abstract: This paper introduces the ensemble directional Kalman filter (EnDKF), an ensemble-based Kalman filtering approach for pose tracking that jointly estimates an object's position and attitude using ideas from directional statistics. The EnDKF integrates a unit-quaternion attitude representation to move beyond canonical Kalman filter mean and covariance assumptions that poorly capture directional uncertainty. Experiments on a synthetic constant-velocity constant-angular-velocity system and a digital-twin head-tracking scenario using the FoundationPose algorithm demonstrate a significant reduction in error as opposed to merely using measurements.

</details>


### [189] [Gated Subspace Inference for Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03109)
*Stephen J. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用token激活流形低有效秩特性加速Transformer语言模型推理的方法。通过激活向量分解、低秩缓存和门控机制，在无需重训练和架构修改的情况下实现3-10倍线性层加速，同时保持模型输出的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型在推理时面临内存带宽瓶颈问题，特别是线性层权重的读取操作成为计算效率的主要限制因素。该方法利用了每个层中token激活流形的低有效秩（low effective rank）这一关键观察。

Method: 核心方法包括三个步骤：1) 将每个激活向量分解为子空间分量和残差分量；2) 通过缓存的低秩权重映像在降低的内存带宽下计算子空间分量上的线性层输出；3) 应用逐token门控机制决定是否计算残差校正。该门控机制可确保输出分布在可控容差范围内保持不变。

Result: 在三个模型家族（GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B、OPT 6.7B）上验证结果显示：线性层权重读取加速3.0x至10.5x；困惑度比低于1.00；top-1 token一致率超过98%；在特定操作点可实现逐字符完全一致的输出。

Conclusion: 该方法无需重训练、无需架构修改、不涉及注意力机制的近似。在AMD MI300X上对GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B、OPT 6.7B三个模型家族的验证表明，在线性层权重读取上可实现3.0x至10.5x的加速，同时保持困惑度比低于1.00、top-1 token一致率超过98%。在GPT-J 6B上以k=256、ε=0.05为操作点时，加速模型与基线的输出达到逐字符完全一致。

Abstract: A method is presented for accelerating inference in transformer language models by exploiting the low effective rank of the token activation manifold at each layer. The method decomposes each activation vector into a subspace component and a residual, computes the linear-layer output on the subspace component via a cached low-rank weight image at reduced memory bandwidth, and applies a per-token gate that determines whether the residual correction is computed or skipped. The gate ensures that the output distribution is preserved to within a controllable tolerance. Validation on three model families (GPT-2 124M, GPT-J 6B, OPT 6.7B) on AMD MI300X demonstrates effective speedups of 3.0x to 10.5x on linear-layer weight reads with perplexity ratios below 1.00 and top-1 token agreement above 98%. The method requires no retraining, no architectural modification, and no approximation of the attention mechanism. At the operating point (k = 256, ε = 0.05) on GPT-J 14 6B, the accelerated model produces character-for-character identical output to the baseline.

</details>


### [190] [Cascade Token Selection for Transformer Attention Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03110)
*Stephen J. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"级联令牌选择"（cascade token selection），把 ADA（Activation Decorrelation Attention）在每一层选代表令牌的开销从 O(T²d) 降到 O(Trd)：第 l 层的代表集被继承到第 l+1 层，用一次 r×r 的代表集内部 Gram 和一次 (T−r)×r 的交叉 Gram 做验证，并只做少量增删。实验在 AMD MI300X 上对 GPT-2 124M / GPT-J 6B / OPT 6.7B 验证：Gram 运算节省 22%–63%，相邻层代表集平均 Jaccard 重叠 0.83–0.94，深层替换率仅 3%–18%。核心发现是：信息令牌集合是输入的结构性属性，能在网络深度上连贯传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADA 通过 Gram 阈值把注意力从 O(T²) 压到 O(r²)（r ≪ T），但每层选代表集需计算 T×T Gram 矩阵，代价 O(T²d)，与它想削减的注意力代价同阶（T=2048、d=4096 时约 17 GFLOP/层，与注意力本身相当），导致中等序列长度下净加速被选择开销抵消。核心观察是代表集在深度方向高度相干：transformer block 是 Lipschitz 连续的近似等距映射，层 l 近似重复的令牌在 l+1 仍近似重复、去相关的仍去相关，因此无需每层重算完整 Gram；这与 GSI 在隐藏维 (d 轴) 上的基底继承级联互为正交对应。

Method: 算法层面：第 0 层用标准 ADA 计算全 T×T Gram 并顺序贪心选代表（阈值 γ_t = max_{s<t}|G_st| < 1−τ² 即代表，τ=0.3）；随后各层执行 Algorithm 1——行归一化 X̂；(a) 在继承的代表集上算 r×r Gram，剔除已变冗余者（移除）；(b) 在非代表集与有效代表集之间算 (T−r)×r 交叉 Gram，挑选新去相关者（新增）；合并得 R^(l+1)。代价 r²d + (T−r)rd = Trd，比独立选择省 T/r 倍。理论上给出 Proposition 3（深度冗余持久性：经残差连接的 block 使 Λ^(l)≈1+λ，配合行归一化界 ‖x̂_s−x̂_t‖ ≤ Λ‖x_s−x_t‖/min‖x‖，在 Λ‖x_s−x_t‖/min‖x^(l+1)‖ < √2·τ 时冗余关系保持）与 Proposition 4（级联保守性：级联集是独立集的严格超集，绝不漏掉真代表，只可能多包含"假代表"）。用 Jaccard 指数量化深度相干、用替换率 η=(增+删)/r 量化更新幅度，并与 GSI 做 d 轴/T 轴的正交对比（GSI 省 96% 基底更新、级联省 22%–63% Gram 运算）。

Result: 三个模型族在 T=512、τ=0.30 下的结果：GPT-2 124M 节省 22%（Jaccard 0.863），GPT-J 6B 节省 52%（0.940），OPT 6.7B 节省 63%（0.825），节省随模型深度增加（首层全 Gram 成本被更多层摊薄）；GPT-J 选择成本从 30.1 GFLOP 降至 14.7 GFLOP，与实测 52% 一致。代表集数量在深层的级联/独立比为 1.11–1.32，注意力 FLOPs 增加 ≤22%（262² vs 237²），换来选择 FLOPs 减半；且因是独立集的超集，注意力精度不低于独立选择。深层层替换率降至 3%–18%，早期层高达 190%（层 0→1 的结构性跃迁）。发现 OPT 异常：层 0 因学习式位置编码 r=512，层 1 塌缩为 r=1，层 1–9 压缩 ≥13.8×，这既解释其最高节省率，也说明该方法不依赖特定位置编码方案。预测 T=8192 时节省达 95%（约 2.7 TFLOP → 150 GFLOP），选择加速 18×。与 GSI 组合：GPT-J 6B 上线性层有效加速 15.6×、选择加速 2.1×、注意力计算加速 6.2×，有效参数 386M/6B、有效令牌 205/512。

Conclusion: 级联令牌选择通过跨层继承代表集并用交叉 Gram 验证更新，把 ADA 的选择开销从 O(T²d) 降至 O(Trd)，且不损失代表质量（保守超集），使 ADA 在实际序列长度下能兑现其理论压缩。论文更深层的结论是：训练后 transformer 的信息集中在隐藏维与令牌维两个方向的低维流形上，该流形随深度缓慢旋转，因此"信息令牌集合"是输入的结构性属性而非逐层偶然现象；GSI（d 轴基底继承）与级联 ADA（T 轴集合继承）正交且相乘，合成系统覆盖整个前向传播。后续方向：跨深度自适应阈值 τ^(l)（可由替换率免费提供信号）、长上下文（2048–8192）实测标定 r 随 T 的增长、与 GSI 的联合级联、GEMM 核级实现（选择核与注意力核跨层流水线并行）、以及按注意力头分别选择代表集（h×Tr_h d_h 的权衡待测）。

Abstract: A method is presented for reducing the cost of representative token selection in transformer attention layers by exploiting the coherence of the representative set across depth. Activation Decorrelation Attention (ADA) selects $r \ll T$ representative tokens at each layer via a Gram threshold and computes attention on the compressed $r \times r$ problem, but the selection requires a $T \times T$ Gram matrix at every layer. The cascade mechanism introduced here inherits the representative set from layer $l$ to layer $l+1$, validates it via a $(T - r) \times r$ cross-Gram computation, and updates it with a small number of additions and removals. The cost of the selection step drops from $O(T^2 d)$ to $O(T r d)$ per layer. Validation on three model families (GPT-2 124M, GPT-J 6B, OPT 6.7B) on AMD MI300X demonstrates Gram operation savings of $22\%$ to $63\%$ with mean Jaccard overlap of $0.83$ to $0.94$ between consecutive layers. The cascade reveals that the set of informative tokens is a structural property of the input that propagates coherently through the depth of the network: the same tokens carry the non-redundant information at layer $l$ and at layer $l+1$.

</details>


### [191] [Taming the Curses of Multiagency in Robust Markov Games with Large State Space through Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03125)
*Jingchu Gai,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分布鲁棒马尔博弈（RMG）的数据效率问题，首次提出在具有大/无限状态空间的RMG中利用线性函数近似打破多主体诅咒的算法，涵盖生成模型和在线交互两种设置，填补了该领域在可扩展算法上的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MARL面临两大核心挑战：（1）环境不确定性带来的鲁棒性问题；（2）数据效率问题，即状态和动作空间随智能体数量指数增长导致的"多主体诅咒"。现有RMG算法仅局限于表格设置，仅能处理小规模问题，而大规模（或无限）状态空间的RMG问题尚未被有效解决。

Method: 本文采用线性函数近似（LFA）处理一般RMG问题，针对全变差距离定义的不确定集，分别在生成模型设置和新型在线交互设置下开发了可证明数据高效的算法。研究通过理论分析证明这些算法能够打破多主体诅咒。

Result: 本文提出的算法首次在具有大（可能无限）状态空间的RMG问题上打破了样本复杂度的多主体诅咒，且该结果不依赖于不确定集的具体构建方式，具有普适性。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在具有大（可能无限）状态空间的RMG问题上打破了样本复杂度的多主体诅咒，为大规模RMG问题提供了首个可证明数据高效的算法解决方案。该成果不依赖于不确定集的构建方式，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) holds great potential but faces robustness challenges due to environmental uncertainty. To address this, distributionally robust Markov games (RMGs) optimize worst-case performance when the environment deviates from the nominal model within a uncertainty set. Beyond robustness, an equally urgent goal for MARL is data efficiency -- sampling from vast state and action spaces that grow exponentially with the number of agents potentially leads to the curse of multiagency. However, current provably data-efficient algorithms for RMGs are limited to tabular settings with finite state and action spaces, which are only computationally manageable for small-scale problems, leaving RMGs with large-scale (or infinite) state spaces largely unexplored. The only existing work beyond tabular settings focuses on linear function approximation (LFA) for a restrictive class of RMGs using vanish minimal value assumption and still suffers from sample complexity with the curse of multiagency. In this work, we focuses on general RMGs with LFA. For uncertainty sets defined by total variation distance, we develop provably data-efficient algorithms that break the curse of multiagency in both the generative model setting and a newly proposed online interactive setting. To our knowledge, our results are the first to break the curse of multiagency of sample complexity for RMGs with large (possibly infinite) state spaces, regardless of the uncertainty set construction.

</details>


### [192] [Instance-Level Costs for Nuanced Classifier Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03135)
*Kabir Kang,Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出归一化超额成本(NEC)指标，用于评估分类错误时考虑实例级误分类成本。研究发现大多数模型错误集中在低成本的模糊样本上，NEC远低于标准错误率，但成本敏感训练在不同场景下的效果差异显著，仅当成本可从输入特征预测时才有明显收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类将所有错误视为等同，但在内容审核、医学筛查、安全关键应用中，明确案例的错误成本远高于模糊案例。需要一种能根据错误严重程度加权的评估指标。

Method: 提出归一化超额成本(NEC)指标，通过投票边缘、决策阈值距离或置信度评分来推导每样本成本；当成本均匀时退化为标准错误率。在文本、图像和表格基准数据集上进行评估，并探索了多种成本敏感训练方法（损失加权、采样策略、回归）

Result: NEC通常显著低于错误率（如5%错误率可对应1.8% NEC），表明大多数错误集中在模糊样本；成本敏感训练收益不一致——仅在成本可从输入特征预测时（如合成控制）有改善，而真实数据集收益甚微或无。

Conclusion: NEC提供了一种实用的实例级误分类成本推导和评估方法，即使成本敏感训练收益有限；研究揭示模型错误主要集中在模糊样本上，这对实际部署中的风险评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: Standard classification treats all errors equally, but in content moderation, medical screening, and safety-critical applications, mistakes on clear-cut cases are far more costly than errors on ambiguous ones. We propose normalized excess cost (NEC), a metric that weights classification errors by per-example costs and reduces to standard error rate when costs are uniform. Costs can derive from annotator vote margins, distance from decision thresholds, or confidence ratings. Across text, image, and tabular benchmarks, we find that NEC is often substantially lower than error rate -- models with 5\% error rate can achieve 1.8\% NEC -- revealing that most mistakes concentrate on ambiguous, low-cost examples. However, incorporating costs into training via loss weighting, sampling strategies, or regression yields inconsistent benefits: improvements appear only when costs are predictable from input features, as in our synthetic control, while real-world datasets show mixed or negligible gains. Our framework provides a practical methodology for deriving and evaluating instance-level misclassification costs, even when cost-sensitive training offers limited benefit.

</details>


### [193] [OCRR: A Benchmark for Online Correction Recovery under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03153)
*Adrian Grassi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OCRR（在线修正恢复率）基准测试，用于衡量分类系统在用户纠正流下的恢复速度。该基准通过流式语料库、预言机或随机纠正机制，评估9种基线算法和7种有界存储变体的性能。实验表明，hash-chained append-only substrate在保持原分布准确率的同时能有效恢复新类别准确率，显著优于其他持续学习基线和LoRA微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准仅在训练时冻结模型评估性能，无法衡量真实系统中面对分布偏移时的在线恢复能力。缺乏衡量系统在用户纠正流下恢复速度的基准测试。

Method: 提出OCRR基准测试，将语料库流式通过分类系统，对错误预测应用预言机或随机纠正，记录新类别准确率和原分布准确率随纠正次数变化的曲线。评估了来自5个家族的9种基线算法和7种有界存储变体，包括标准在线学习基线（river）、持续学习方法（EWC、A-GEM、LwF）、检索/参数混合方法（kNN-LM）、参数高效微调（LoRA on DeBERTa-v3-large）和hash-chained append-only substrate。

Result: 在Banking77和CLINC150数据集上，substrate是唯一能同时实现新类别准确率（88.7 ± 2.9%）和原分布准确率（95.4 ± 0.8%）的系统，在相同内存预算下比最佳持续学习基线高出32.6个百分点，比LoRA-DeBERTa-v3-large在保留率上高出84.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 分类准确率在ANN recall@5从0.69降至0.23（10k到10M语料规模）时保持稳定在99%，表明substrate的margin-band多数投票机制对检索不完美具有鲁棒性，纯top-k召回率指标无法预测此特性。

Abstract: Static benchmarks measure a model frozen at training time. Real systems face distribution shift: new categories, paraphrased queries, drift: and must recover online via user corrections. No existing benchmark measures recovery speed under correction streams. We introduce OCRR (Online Correction Recovery Rate): a benchmark that streams a corpus through a classification system, applies oracle or stochastic corrections to wrong predictions, and reports two curves: novel-class accuracy and original-distribution accuracy versus correction count. We evaluate the substrate alongside nine baseline algorithms from five families plus seven bounded-storage variants of the substrate for the Pareto sweep, including standard online-learning baselines (river), continual-learning methods (EWC, A-GEM, LwF), retrieval/parametric hybrids (kNN-LM), parameter-efficient fine-tuning of a 1.5 B-parameter encoder (LoRA on DeBERTa-v3-large), and a hash-chained append-only substrate (Substrate). On Banking77 and CLINC150, under oracle and sparse correction policies, the substrate is the only system that simultaneously recovers novel-class accuracy (88.7 +/- 2.9 %) and retains original-distribution accuracy (95.4 +/- 0.8 %) beating the next-best published continual-learning baseline by 32.6 percentage points at equal memory budget, and beating LoRA-on-DeBERTa-v3-large by 84.6 percentage points on retention. We further find that classification accuracy remains stable at 99 % even as approximate-nearest-neighbour recall@5 degrades from 0.69 to 0.23 across 10 k to 10 M corpus scales, suggesting the substrate's margin-band majority vote is robust to retrieval imperfection in a way that pure top-k recall metrics do not predict. Code and data are available at https://github.com/adriangrassi/ocrr-benchmark.

</details>


### [194] [Pairwise matrices for sparse autoencoders: single-feature inspection mislabels causal axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03160)
*Michael A. Riegler,Birk Sebastian Frostelid Torpmann-Hagen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出成对矩阵协议改进SAE可解释性，发现标准单特征协议遗漏的三类关键现象：倒U型响应、非线性联合效应、方向依赖的一致性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE协议仅验证单特征steering，无法捕捉特征间交互效应和复杂非线性行为

Method: 成对矩阵协议：协同扫描steering系数与联合条件，结合匹配几何控制实验

Result: 三个关键发现：①"AI免责声明"特征呈倒U型响应②三近正交特征联合抑制产生损害，单特征抑制无影响③一致性与方向模式相关而非幅度

Conclusion: 特征交互是非线性的，联合抑制揭示单特征分析遗漏的功能依赖；方法可迁移至其他模型定位因果特征

Abstract: The standard sparse-autoencoder (SAE) interpretability protocol labels each feature from its top-activating contexts and validates by single-feature steering. We propose the pairwise matrix protocol, co-varying steering coefficient with joint condition, and report three findings the standard one-corner protocol misses on Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct, replicated on Gemma-2-2B-it. First, a feature labelled "AI self-disclaimer" from its top contexts produces an inverted U-shape under a coefficient sweep: at c=+500 the model substitutes a fluent contemplative-philosopher voice for the disclaimer. Two further features anchor the criterion (one monotonic, one pure breakdown). Second, three near-orthogonal cluster-specific features that individually steer a philosophy-of-mind register, jointly suppressed at c=-500, damage grounded composition on recipes and engine explanations as well as introspective prompts; single-feature suppression at the same magnitude leaves controls intact. Third, a matched-geometry comparison of single-feature, joint, and random-direction perturbations (norm ~1.55, cosine ~0.64) yields three distinct output regimes: single-feature substitutes strategy filler, random direction substitutes diverse content, joint suppression alone produces placeholder text. Coherence loss is direction-pattern-dependent, not magnitude-dependent. All three findings reproduce on Gemma with model-specific damage signatures; the matched-geometry control is CI-separated by ~10x. The pipeline also locates a top causally responsible feature in Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct.

</details>


### [195] [Global and Local Topology-Aware Attention with Persistent Homology and Euler Biases for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03163)
*Usef Faghihi,Amir Saki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"拓扑感知注意力"框架：把持久同调（H0–H2）、锚定 Euler 特征变换（AET）与核希尔伯特（RKHS）几何以可加偏置矩阵的形式直接注入注意力 logit，而非仅作为附加特征；再配一个由验证集门控（α_loc 可置 0）的局部 Hilbert/持久同调残差，保证全局方法与 Zeng 式局部 H0 基线在函数类上被严格包含（Proposition 1）。在 7 个数据集单元 × 3 种子 × 3 时序划分（共 189 个配对单元）上，三类架构（轻量注意力/Ridge、PatchTST、TimeSeriesTransformer）均取得显著为正的配对效应（平均相对 RMSE 降幅 12.5% / 23.5% / 47.8%，p 分别为 7.2e-4、3.5e-5、<1e-4）。核心结论是：拓扑应被视为"由验证集选择的、依赖架构的归纳偏置"，而非普遍更优的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 注意力以缩放点积计算 token 间交互，其结构中没有任何显式机制去保留连通分支、环（loop）、壳状/空腔（shell/void）、方向性 Euler 签名或非线性度量邻域。而科学时间序列恰恰蕴含这类几何结构：季节性轨迹会形成闭合环、波动率regime切换表现为收益窗口几何形态的变化、轴承退化则通过振动特征的稀疏突变而非平滑均值漂移来演化。现有的拓扑+预测工作（如 Zeng 等的局部持久同调方法、CrossTopoNet）多把拓扑摘要当作外部特征流或辅助表征进行拼接融合，无法改变 token 交互图本身；而全局持久同调摘要又是多对一映射，无法覆盖全部局部拓扑信息（两个窗口可具有相同的全局持久图但完全不同的局部图集合）。因此需要在注意力 logit 层面注入全局拓扑偏置，同时保留一个显式的局部残差分支，二者尺度互补。

Method: **1. 全局拓扑感知注意力**。A_global = softmax(QK^T/√d_h + Σ_k λ_k(X)B^{H_k}(X) + λ_E(X)B^{AET}(X) + Σ_k λ_{K,k}(X)B^{KH_k}(X))。其中：H0 偏置为三尺度（0.5σ、σ、2σ）高斯径向亲和的加权和（多尺度连通性）；H1 偏置基于两跳闭合证据 C_ij=(1/(N−2))Σ_k A_ik A_kj ·(1−A_ij)，在半径集合 {0.7σ, σ, 1.4σ} 上平均（捕捉环闭合）；H2 为"中心化半径 + 局部稀疏度"的壳敏感代理（明确声明非精确 H2 持久性）；AET 用训练集方向/阈值上的可微图 Euler 统计 χ̃=Σm_i−Σ_{i<j}a_ij m_i m_j 构成方向性 Euler 偏置，补足 Betti 数不变的方向重分布信息；KH_k 通道以高斯核诱导的希尔伯特距离 d_H²=k(x,x)+k(y,y)−2k(x,y) 替代欧氏距离计算 Vietoris–Rips 持久同调。Vietoris–Rips 复形定义、持久性寿命摘要 S_k=log(1+Σ(d−b)/(median d+ε)) 给出。

**2. 自适应拓扑温度**：η_c=softplus(α_c)，按拓扑模式 m 选择通道集合，α_c 在训练集训练、验证集早停，用于让模型自行路由各拓扑通道的权重。

**3. 验证门控局部残差**：局部覆盖（子窗长 8、步长 4）上计算 7 类图 D^{0,+}（原序列一维路径过滤）、D^{0,−}（取负序列）、D^1、D^2 及 RKHS 版 KH_0/KH_1/KH_2，以有限寿命统计（top-4 寿命、总/均值/标准差/最大寿命、有限条数）向量化，经仅用训练集拟合的 16 维注意力投影得 R_loc；并定义归一化局部 PH 对比度 C_ab（观测性、非因果）驱动局部权重 γ_m。最终预测 ŷ_final=(1−α_loc)ŷ_global+α_loc ŷ_local，其中 α_loc 仅在验证集固定网格上选取，且需满足 RMSE_val(global+local) < RMSE_val(global) − δ_loc·max{1, RMSE_val(global)}，否则 α_loc=0，从而逐位精确还原全局模型。Proposition 1 证明 Zeng 式局部 H0 预测类 F_Z ⊆ F_L；另有 F_global ⊆ F_aug。

**4. 无泄漏协议**：按时序划分训练/验证/测试；scaler、AET 方向与阈值、PH 归一化器、RKHS 距离尺度、局部注意力投影均只在训练窗上拟合；拓扑仅由输入窗 X_t 计算，绝不涉及未来目标；Ridge 惩罚、拓扑模式、拓扑强度、动态温度、局部/全局混合权重仅在验证集选择；测试集只用一次。

**5. 实验设置**：三类架构（轻量注意力+Ridge 头、PatchTSTForRegression、TimeSeriesTransformerForPrediction），每个架构配对评估；7 个非退化数据集单元（3 个合成：高阶拓扑压力测试、循环 H1、壳/空腔 H2；4 个真实：月度 CO2、S&P 500 收益窗口→5 日年化实现波动率、NASA IMS 轴承集1/集2）；第三个 IMS 文件夹因目标近退化被剔除。精确持久同调在可行时启用（点数上限 28，最大边分位 0.60），否则使用平滑代理。

**6. 复杂度**：距离矩阵 O(N²p)；精确持久性在 c≤28 子集上最坏 O(c⁴) 个单纯形、边界矩阵约化 O(S_c³)；平滑 H0/H2 为 O(N²p)；平滑 H1 两跳代理朴素为 O(N³)；AET 为 O(RNp+RQN²)。

Result: **主审计（每架构 63 个配对单元，7 数据集×3 种子×3 时序划分，共 189 单元）**：
- 轻量注意力/Ridge：46 改善 / 17 恶化 / 0 持平，平均相对 RMSE 降幅 12.5%，95% bootstrap CI [-5.3%, 27.7%]（跨零），d_z=0.44，配对随机化 p=7.2e-4。
- PatchTST：33 改善 / 10 恶化 / 20 保留基线，降幅 23.5%，CI [14.1%, 33.2%]，d_z=0.55，p=3.5e-5。
- TimeSeriesTransformer：47 改善 / 6 恶化 / 10 持平，降幅 47.8%，CI [38.4%, 57.0%]，d_z=0.47，p<1e-4。
- 合计 189 单元中 126 改善、33 恶化、30 由验证门控保留基线。

**数据集异质性（PatchTST 细分）**：高阶拓扑压力测试 93.5%（9/9 单元全改善）、壳/空腔 H2 67.2%（9/9 全改善）、IMS 集1 12.4%、IMS 集2 3.4%；而 S&P/FRED 收益形状 −0.7%、CO2 −7.6%、循环 H1 −3.4%，多为弱效应或负效应，门控频繁回退基线。代表性格切分上轻量模型 7 个数据集中 6 个改善；TimeSeriesTransformer 在循环基准上出现唯一明确恶化（0.1180→0.1498）。

**轻量架构消融（Table 4，相对 Zeng 式 H0 与经典注意力）**：局部 vs Zeng-style 平均 +19.2%，全局 vs Zeng-style +24.3%，全局 vs 经典平均 +30.3%（其中高阶拓扑 84.2%、IMS 集2 47.4%、CO2 27.4%、S&P 24.6%；IMS 集1 −8.5%、壳/空腔 局部 −0.3%）。被选中的拓扑模式随数据集变化：高阶拓扑选 AET+局部 Hilbert/PH，CO2 选 RKHS-full，S&P 选 AET，IMS 集2 选 RKHS 动态，循环基准选混合动态 blend。

**作者自述局限**：精确持久同调对长窗口昂贵；平滑 H1/H2 仅为可解释近似（H2 应视为壳敏感代理）；O(N³) 的平滑 H1 与稠密 O(N²) 偏置限制长序列可扩展性；未提供与非拓扑基线的系统性 wall-clock 基准；数据集广度有限；Zeng 式基线为受控适配而非完整复现；第三个 IMS 数据集因目标退化被排除。

Conclusion: **论文主张**：拓扑应被视为"由验证集选择的、依赖架构的归纳偏置"，而非普遍替换标准注意力的方案。门控设计（α_loc 可置 0）在误差上保留基线预测能力，同时在几何信号可预测时释放多阶几何信息；通过加性 logit 偏置与门控残差的形式，可直接嵌入 Transformer 类预测架构而不丢弃点积注意力。

**分析性评价**：
1. *证据强度*：三类架构的配对符号检验均为显著正值（p≤7.2e-4），且 d_z≈0.44–0.55 属中等效应；但 bootstrap CI 在轻量模型上跨零，说明"相对降幅均值"这一指标本身不稳定。作者以符号配对检验为主、相对降幅均值为辅，是恰当的表述方式。
2. *均值可能被合成基准主导*：Table 1 中高阶拓扑基准的基线 RMSE≈0.497 而拓扑版≈0.038（降幅>90%），单此一项对均值贡献极大，且该数据集目标由构造方式显式依赖 H1 几何。真实数据上的收益明显更温和甚至为负（PatchTST 在 CO2、S&P、循环基准上为负），故"拓扑提升预测"的强表述应限于"目标由几何决定"的场景。
3. *门控机制的双面性*：门控使拓扑模型在验证集上"只在其更优时保留"，这既解释了 30 个保留基线单元，也意味着配对比较包含一定"自我筛选"成分；恶化单元（33 个）主要出现在门控失效处，说明门控本身并非可靠的安全网。
4. *公平性与泄漏控制*：无泄漏协议设计较为严格（scaler、拓扑校准、混合权重均限于训练/验证），并明确把 Zeng 基线限制在原始缩放窗的标量 H0 摘要、不给原始窗口或 patch 特征，公平性论证合理。但第三个 IMS 数据集被剔除的判定标准（目标方差过低）与"排除负结果"的动机需要在更广数据上复核。
5. *方法学局限*：精确持久同调在 c≤28 上限下只能提供粗略拓扑，且 H2 实为代理；平滑 H1 的 O(N³) 成本与缺少 wall-clock 对照，使"可扩展性优势"这一隐含论证尚未闭合；也未与 DLinear/TimesNet 等强深度基线比较，故不能排除收益来自注意力偏置的正则化/容量效应而非拓扑语义。
6. *可复现性*：论文声明随包提供 notebook、种子、超参网格、训练校准文件与全部结果 CSV，protocol 描述清晰，复现门槛较低。

**总体判断**：这是一篇方法动机清晰、协议严谨、结论克制的"机制+证据"型工作，其价值主要在于提出并验证了"在注意力 logit 层面注入拓扑 + 验证门控局部残差"的设计范式及其两条包含关系（函数类包含与基线误差保持），而非宣称拓扑是通用增益；其统计结论应解读为"在几何可预测的任务上、且在可被验证集确认时有效"的受控、受门控的正向效应。

Abstract: Scientific time series often encode predictive geometric structure, including connectivity, cycles, shell-like geometry, directional changes, and nonlinear neighborhoods, that standard dot-product attention does not explicitly represent. We introduce a topology-aware attention framework that adds such structure to attention logits using persistent homology (H0-H2), anchored Euler characteristic transforms, and kernel-Hilbert channels. A validation-gated local residual captures local topological signals, including a Zeng-style local H0 component, only when held-out validation data support the correction. Exact Vietoris-Rips computations and smooth topological surrogates are evaluated under a no-leakage protocol with train-only calibration, validation-only selection, and test-only reporting.
  We evaluate guarded topology-aware variants across three architecture families: lightweight attention/Ridge, PatchTSTForRegression, and TimeSeriesTransformerForPrediction. Experiments include synthetic benchmarks isolating higher-order topology and real datasets covering CO2, S&P 500 return-window geometry, and NASA IMS bearing degradation. The audit uses matched paired comparisons across seven dataset units, three random seeds, and three chronological splits, giving 63 paired units per architecture and 189 paired units overall. Topology-aware models show positive paired effects when geometry is predictive, with heterogeneous magnitude across datasets and architectures. Lightweight attention/Ridge improves in 46 of 63 units, with mean relative RMSE reduction of 12.5% and paired randomization p=7.2e-4; PatchTST improves in 33 units and retains the baseline in 20 units, with 23.5% reduction and p=3.5e-5; and TimeSeriesTransformer improves in 47 units, with 47.8% reduction and p<1e-4. The results support topology as a validation-selected, architecture-compatible inductive bias.

</details>


### [196] [Enhancing AI-Based ECG Delineation with Deep Learning Denoising Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03183)
*Jeff Breeding-Allison,Emil Walleser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: An autoencoder-based neural network is proposed for canine ECG denoising, effectively suppressing diverse noise sources while preserving critical cardiac waveform morphology for downstream clinical delineation tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Canine ECG analysis is challenging due to various noise sources (respiration, muscle activity, poor lead contact, external electrical artifacts) that obscure clinically relevant cardiac electrical activity. Classical filtering and wavelet-based methods struggle to handle diverse noise patterns while preserving morphological features essential for accurate ECG delineation.

Method: Autoencoder-based neural network trained to reconstruct clean cardiac signals from noisy inputs, enabling effective noise suppression while preserving diagnostically important waveform morphology.

Result: The model shows strong denoising performance across both noisy and clean ECG recordings, indicating robustness to varying signal conditions.

Conclusion: The proposed autoencoder model demonstrates robust denoising capabilities while preserving critical ECG morphological features, making it suitable for preprocessing canine ECG signals before clinical delineation analysis.

Abstract: Evaluating canine electrocardiograms (ECGs) is challenging due to noise that can obscure clinically relevant cardiac electrical activity. Common sources of interference include respiration, muscle activity, poor lead contact, and external electrical artifacts. Classical signal denoising techniques, such as filtering and wavelet-based methods, struggle to suppress diverse noise patterns while preserving morphological features critical for accurate ECG delineation. We propose an autoencoder-based neural network model and training strategy for ECG denoising as a preprocessing step for canine ECG analysis. The model is trained to reconstruct clean cardiac signals from noisy inputs, enabling effective noise reduction without degrading diagnostically important waveforms. Our approach demonstrates strong performance across both noisy and clean ECG recordings, indicating robustness to varying signal conditions and suitability for downstream delineation tasks.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond Activation Alignment: The Geometry of Neural Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03222)
*Amirhossein Yavari,Farnaz Zamani Esfahlani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出 RSA/CCA/CKA 等激活对齐方法只刻画全局线性读出的一致性，无法反映表征对局部（小）刺激扰动的敏感性。作者提出基于「局部可解码信息」的互补框架：把局部扰动辨别任务二阶矩化，并用「投影期望 Fisher/pullback 算子」作为该任务族在数据集层面的最小完备摘要（命题1），再以 SPD 流形上的对数谱（仿射不变）距离比较这些正则化签名，得到 S-RAS 相似度及其对所有提升后任务值的统一乘性证书（定理1），另给出紧致最坏常数 d_∞（定理2/3）与对后处理平移的不变性。实验在 Tiny10/ResNet-18/ViT 模型库与小鼠视觉皮层静态光栅数据上验证：可恢复跨随机训练网络的对应层、类条件探针可迁移到留出图像与模型、能分离标准与鲁棒训练、并揭示刺激坐标族对生物匹配的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSA、CCA、CKA 等激活对齐方法被广泛用于比较生物与人工表征；已有理论将其解释为「在广泛全局任务族上最优线性读出的一致性」。但作者指出两点局限：(1) 全局读出的一致性并不决定系统如何利用局部刺激证据——两个表征可以在激活空间上高度对齐，却对小扰动的敏感度截然不同；(2) 已有局部几何方法（导数、Jacobian、Fisher/pullback 度量）虽能量化「哪些刺激方向被放大/抑制」，却未规定扰动族的选择、如何跨刺激聚合、以及何种数据集层面摘要才足以刻画相应的辨别任务。已有工作还表明基于激活的相似度（如 CKA）对后处理式的点云平移+均值中心化并不不变。因此核心问题是：两个表征能否在给定刺激/任务坐标族内，把相同的小扰动变成同等可辨别。

Method: 将表征视为可微映射 f: R^d→R^m（含层级复合 Φ_n），在刺激坐标 s∈Ω⊂R^q（ψ 参数化）下定义局部坐标扰动辨别任务：观测者从 N(h_f(s),Σ) 与 N(h_f(s+η),Σ) 中判别，线性化后 d'²=η^T I(s) η，I(s)=J_h(s)^T Σ^{-1} J_h(s)。定义「投影二阶矩局部任务族」：固定正交列 P∈R^{q×k}，扰动 s→s+Pz（C_z=E[zz^T]⪰0），任务值 T=Tr(C_z F)，其中 F=E_s[P^T J^T Σ^{-1} J P]∈S_+^k（各向同性时 F=σ^{-2}G，G 为投影期望 pullback 度量）。命题1（借助 PSD 迹分离引理）证明 F 是该二阶矩任务族的最小完备数据集级摘要；仅有 span(P) 而非具体基重要，故满足基不变性。进一步分解增益 γ=Tr(G)/k、形状 Ĝ=G/Tr(G)，以及类条件对比 ΔG_y、ΔĜ_y；命题2 用 Rayleigh–Ritz/Ky Fan 说明对比方向是「分布级共享方向」而非逐图优化方向，并给出 Jensen 型不等式区分于 eigen/principal distortion。比较层面：先做迹标定 SPD 提升 Ã=A+ε_reg Tr(A)/k·I（ε_reg=1e-4），再用仿射不变度量 d_AIRM=‖log(A^{-1/2}BA^{-1/2})‖_F 定义 d_S-RAS,P=(1/√k)d_AIRM 与分数 S-RAS_P=exp(−d)。定理1 给出 e^{−d}A⪯B⪯e^{d}A，从而对任意 C⪰0 有 e^{−d}Tr(CA)≤Tr(CB)≤e^{d}Tr(CA)，即 S^{√k}T̃(f)≤T̃(g)≤S^{−√k}T̃(f)；附录 A.5 给出紧致常数 d_∞=‖log M‖_op=sup_C|log Tr(CB)/Tr(CA)| 及 d_∞≤d_AIRM≤√k d_∞。附录还证明基于 Jacobian 的签名对局部常值平移不变（Lemma 4），并给出循环网络作为展开映射或平衡映射（含隐式微分 J=(I−DW)^{-1}D du/dx）的推广。

Result: （1）层对应（Tiny10，10 模型×8 层，n=1024）：S-RAS 完整版在随机族 K=16 为 71.7%，K=768 达 85.1%；PCA 基族达 93.8%，接近线性 CKA 94.4% 与 RBF CKA 94.0%（作者说明激活基线在原始准确率上仍最优，本实验为一致性核查）。形状版 S-RAS 为 49.2→55.7（随机）/78.1（PCA）；逐点 AIRM 随 K 增大反而退化（73.8→45.6 / 83.2→46.3）；MSA 型谱比基线接近随机（15.3/39.9→14.2/29.2），且其谱比距离对共形增益不变、只依赖极端广义特征值（Prop.3/4）。诊断显示 S-RAS 相似度随层距衰减更快、top-margin 更大。（2）类条件留出探针（K=32、每侧 2 个探针）：对比导出探针的留出图像分离度 1.10，随机对比 0.43、池化敏感度 0.31、标签置换零假设 0.03；10 个留出模型划分全部胜出（单侧配对 Wilcoxon p=9.77e-4）；留出模型分类 AUC 0.874 vs 0.795/0.625/0.474；效应在 K=16 即出现、对探针数与幅度稳健。（3）鲁棒训练（固定 ResNet-18，标准 vs PGD/TRADES/MART）：分离度分别 0.99/1.22/1.14（p=9.77e-4）；PGD vs TRADES 0.28，TRADES vs MART 0.22，PGD vs MART −0.01，说明探针并非对所有训练目标对都敏感。（4）Allen 静态光栅（q=3：θ、ρ=log2 频率、φ；匹配细胞数 60/45）：跨区 top-1 上完整 Fisher 0.458 优于 CKA 0.288、形状 Fisher 0.259、朴素几何 0.222（随机 0.167），经 FDR 校正 q=8.0e-4；VISp 深度上 CKA 0.483、完整 Fisher 0.466、形状 0.397、朴素 0.293（对角 AUC 0.549 vs 0.547）。坐标族选择显著影响结果：ρ+φ 达 0.500、θ+φ 0.486 均高于全坐标 0.458（面积队列），VISp 深度中 θ+φ 达 0.569。辅助分析中「基线+形状 Fisher」的 CV AUC 最高（0.646 vs 基线 0.589），说明方向分配信息不能被激活/解码/映射基线完全覆盖。

Conclusion: 本文提出了与激活对齐互补的「局部可解码信息」框架：局部扰动辨别任务族由投影期望 Fisher/pullback 算子最小完备刻画，比较这些签名用 SPD 对数谱几何（S-RAS），并附带对所有提升任务值的统一乘性证书（紧致形式由 d_∞ 给出）。核心结论是相似性本质上是「族相对」的而非普适的：不同扰动族给出不同的对应关系与匹配结果，模型与生物数据均显示该族依赖性。实证上 S-RAS 能恢复跨独立训练网络的层对应结构、对远距离非对角匹配抑制更锐利（但原始 top-1 准确率未普遍超过强激活基线），更强的证据来自针对性诊断：类条件有限探针可迁移到留出图像与模型、鲁棒训练对比揭示训练目标导致的证据使用差异、静态光栅记录揭示依赖坐标族的生物敏感度结构。作者亦明确限定：该框架是局部、数据集级、二阶矩的，无法刻画逐图最优、高阶效应与强非线性有限扰动行为；Fisher 型判别解释依赖刺激无关噪声假设；比较是样本匹配而非算力匹配，更大架构上的基准（子采样、低秩族、分布式 JVP 批处理）仍待完成。

Abstract: Activation-alignment measures such as Representational Similarity Analysis (RSA), Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA), and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) are widely used to compare biological and artificial neural representations. Recent theoretical work interprets many of these methods as assessing agreement between optimal linear readouts over broad families of global tasks. However, agreement at the level of global readouts does not determine how a system uses local stimulus evidence. Specifically, representations may align in activation space yet differ in their sensitivity to small perturbations. To address this challenge, we introduce a complementary framework based on local decodable information, which focuses on a representation's ability, under noise, to discriminate small perturbations within a specified stimulus-coordinate subspace. Building on Fisher information and local representation geometry, we summarize each representation using the expected projected pullback/Fisher metric over that subspace. This formulation induces a second-moment family of local discrimination tasks, for which the resulting operator provides a minimal, complete dataset-level summary of expected discriminability. We compare these regularized signatures using a log-spectral distance on the manifold of symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrices, yielding the Spectral Riemannian Alignment Score (S-RAS) and a uniform multiplicative certificate over the corresponding family of lifted task values. Empirically, this framework enables the recovery of corresponding layers across independently trained artificial neural networks, supports transferable class-conditional probes, reveals controlled dissociations between standard and robust training, and uncovers stimulus-coordinate family effects across mouse visual cortex using the Allen Brain Observatory static gratings dataset.

</details>


### [198] [Self-Mined Hardness for Safety Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03226)
*Prakhar Gupta,Garv Shah,Donghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"自挖掘难度"（Self-Mined Hardness）方法：不用人工整理的越狱数据集，而是用目标模型自身 rollout 的有害率（harmful rate）给每个对抗提示打分，取最难的一半，配对模型自己未被越狱的回复做监督微调；在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 与 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct 上把 WildJailbreak 攻击成功率从 11.5%/20.1% 降到 1–3%，但"形似越狱的良性提示"拒答率飙至 74–94%；再以 1:1 交错混入同样数量的 adversarially-framed benign 提示，可把该拒答率回落至 30–51%（8B）/52–72%（3B），代价是 ASR 上升 2–6 个百分点；混合条件下训练"最难一半"比"随机一半"再降低 35–50%（约 3pp）的残余 ASR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调通常依赖人工策划的对抗数据集（WildJailbreak 人工攻击语料、BCT 的手工 clean/biased 对），成本高、可扩展性差且难以覆盖模型自身的失败模式。作者希望：①无需外部干净数据集或手工设计的对抗家族，直接从目标模型自身行为中挖掘出"哪些提示对它最难"；②同时解决安全微调带来的过拒（overrefusal）副作用——模型学会了"凡是长得像越狱就拒答"，而非"只在真正有害时才拒答"。因此需要一种可控的样本选择机制与专门针对过拒的缓解手段。

Method: 1) 难度定义：对 WildJailbreak adversarial_harmful 中采样的 5000 条提示，每条用目标模型在 T=1 下采 64 次 rollout，由 WildGuard、MD-Judge v0.1、Llama-Guard-3 三判官多数投票判有害，定义 harmful rate hr(p)=有害 rollout 占比；仅保留 0<hr(p)<1 的"合格池"E（既有学习信号又有非越狱 rollout 可作监督目标），8B 得 2388 条、3B 得 2488 条。2) 规范配对：每条合格提示随机选一条非越狱 rollout 作为 safe_target，所有基线复用同一 (prompt→target) 映射，使 Hard 与 Random 的差异只来自提示选择。3) 对抗式良性监督：从 adversarial_benign 采样 3000 条，每条采 4 次 rollout（仅需 1 条不拒答即可，8B 保留率约 91%），用 WildGuard 判拒答，取合格集 B（大小 n）并配对不拒答的 compliant_target。4) 五个 LoRA 基线（r=16, α=32, target {q_proj,v_proj}, lr 1e-4, cosine, bs 10, 1 epoch, 顺序采样）：Hard（E 按 hr 降序）、Random（E 随机排列）、Control（等量 vanilla benign，计算量对照）、Hard-Mixed（Hard 与 B 严格 1:1 交错 [H0,B0,H1,B1,…]）、Random-Mixed（Random 与 B 交错）。5) 报告点为合格池 50% 检查点（底层一半过于简单：8B 底半 76% 的 hr≤5%、39% 处于 1/64 经验下界；对应阈值 τ≈0.094/0.125），严格交错使 Hard@50% 与 Hard-Mixed@50% 见到完全相同的前 k 条对抗提示。6) 评测：3 个安全集（WildJailbreak eval、WildGuardMix adversarial-harmful、ClearHarm，报三判官多数投票 ASR）与 3 个过拒集（WildJailbreak/WildGuardMix 的 adv-benign 及 plain benign，报 WildGuard 平均拒答率）。

Result: 8B（50% 检查点）：纯训练 Hard 把 WildJailbreak ASR 从 11.5% 降到 2.1%，WildGuardMix 降到 0.3%，ClearHarm 0.1%；但两个 adv-benign 集拒答率从 20–22% 升到 78–84%，plain benign 从 4.5% 升到 32–36%。计算量匹配的 Control 仅升 8–10pp（8B），说明过拒来自对抗数据本身而非 LoRA 副作用。混合后 Hard-Mixed：ASR 升至 5.1%（+3.0pp），adv-benign 拒答降至 49.0%/51.2%（−22~35pp），plain benign 降至 14.3%。3B：Base ASR 20.1%；Hard 纯训练 ASR 0.9–1.1%，但 adv-benign 拒答高达 90–94%，plain benign 43.5–55.1%；Hard-Mixed ASR 3.4%（+2.3pp），adv-benign 降至 66.2%/72.5%，plain benign 19.0%。最难一半 vs 随机一半：混合条件下 Hard 在安全上占优（8B 低 2.8pp，3B 低 3.4pp，摘要称残余 ASR 降 35–50%），但过拒上 Random-Mixed 反而更好（8B 低 11–19pp，3B 低 13.8–18.0pp）；纯条件下差距小且 3B 上反转（−0.2pp，属噪声，ASR 已触底）。结论：混合基线在 (ASR, 过拒) 的 Pareto 前沿上严格优于任一纯基线；难样本优先只是把权衡点向安全侧平移，并非全面占优；3B 残余拒答更高，说明模型容量也影响"形似越狱 vs 真有害"的判别能力。

Conclusion: 本文给出一套自包含的安全微调流水线：对抗训练数据与监督目标全部来自目标模型自身 rollout，无需外部干净集或手工对抗族设计；五个基于规范 prompt→target 配对的受控基线分离了"提示选择"与"良性交错"两种效应。纯对抗微调产生明确的安全–过拒权衡（计算匹配对照无法解释），1:1 混入 adversarially-framed benign 提示以 2–6pp 的 ASR 代价换回可观的合规性；最难一半 vs 随机一半在两个失效模式上方向相反，说明样本选择只是平移 (ASR, overrefusal) 权衡点。局限：仅在 Llama-3 家族验证，跨家族泛化未知；最佳混合基线仍拒答 30–51%（8B）/52–72%（3B）的形似越狱良性提示，未回到基座水平；50% 截断点在查看难度分布后选定，存在事后合理化问题；难度分数仅刻画频率、未刻画严重度与语义多样性；被丢弃的"始终越狱"提示可由更强安全教师生成安全回答后再改写为目标模型分布以纳入利用。

Abstract: Safety fine-tuning of language models typically requires a curated adversarial dataset. We take a different approach: score each candidate prompt's difficulty by how often the target model's own rollouts are judged harmful, then fine-tune on the hardest prompts paired with the model's own non-jailbroken rollouts. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, this approach cuts the WildJailbreak attack success rate from 11.5% and 20.1% down to 1-3%, but pushes refusal on jailbreak-shaped benign prompts from 14-22% to 74-94%. Interleaving the same hard prompts 1:1 with adversarially-framed benign prompts (prompts that look like jailbreaks but have benign intent) cuts that refusal back down to 30-51% on 8B and 52-72% on 3B, at a cost of 2-6 percentage points of attack success rate. Within the mixed regime, training on the hardest half of the eligible pool rather than a random half cuts the remaining ASR by 35-50% (about 3 percentage points) on both models.

</details>


### [199] [Text-Conditional JEPA for Learning Semantically Rich Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03245)
*Chen Huang,Xianhang Li,Vimal Thilak,Etai Littwin,Josh Susskind*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TC-JEPA通过引入文本条件机制改进I-JEPA，利用细粒度文本调节器（跨注意力）减少掩码特征预测的不确定性，从而学习更语义化的视觉表示，在下游任务中表现优异，尤其在细粒度视觉理解和推理任务上超越对比学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Image-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (I-JEPA) faces challenges due to inherent visual uncertainty at masked positions, where feature prediction may fail to learn semantic representations.

Method: TC-JEPA modulates predicted patch features using a fine-grained text conditioner that computes sparse cross-attention over input text tokens, making patch features predictable as a function of text and more semantically meaningful.

Result: TC-JEPA improves downstream performance and training stability with promising scaling properties, and outperforms contrastive methods on diverse tasks, particularly those requiring fine-grained visual understanding and reasoning.

Conclusion: TC-JEPA achieves improved downstream performance and training stability with promising scaling properties, establishing a new vision-language pretraining paradigm based on feature prediction that outperforms contrastive methods, especially on fine-grained visual understanding and reasoning tasks.

Abstract: Image-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (I-JEPA) offers a promising approach to visual self-supervised learning through masked feature prediction. However with the inherent visual uncertainty at masked positions, feature prediction remains challenging and may fail to learn semantic representations. In this work, we propose Text-Conditional JEPA (TC-JEPA) that uses image captions to reduce the prediction uncertainty. Specifically, we modulate the predicted patch features using a fine-grained text conditioner that computes sparse cross-attention over input text tokens. With such conditioning, patch features become predictable as a function of text, thus are more semantically meaningful. We show TC-JEPA improves downstream performance and training stability, with promising scaling properties. TC-JEPA also offers a new vision-language pretraining paradigm based on feature prediction only, outperforming contrastive methods on diverse tasks, especially those requiring fine-grained visual understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [200] [Posterior-First Neural PDE Simulation: Inferring Hidden Problem State from a Single Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03247)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对单场观测下神经PDE模拟的不确定性问题，提出后验优先范式：通过先推断问题状态的后验分布再预测，解决传统端到端方法的状态模糊和预测退化问题，在PDEBench上实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经PDE模拟器在部署时仅接收单一场观测，传统场到未来预测器会将不同潜在问题状态映射到同一确定性子空间，丢失决策所需的不确定性信息，导致可靠预测和下游决策失败。

Method: 提出后验优先的神经PDE模拟框架：首先推断最小任务充分问题状态的后验分布，然后基于该后验进行预测。通过合成精确模糊实验验证理论预测的模糊壁垒存在，并在隐藏元数据的PDEBench任务上评估方法效果。

Result: 实验表明：点估计与后验方法的差距与理论预测的模糊壁垒一致；后验恢复方法将PDEBench任务的pooled rollout nRMSE从0.175降至0.132，消除了59.4%的直接-to-oracle差距。

Conclusion: Posterior-first方法显著优于传统单场预测方法，在PDEBench上pooled rollout nRMSE从0.175降至0.132，消除了59.4%的直接预测差距。建议单次观测的神经PDE模拟应采用后验优先范式而非端到端场-未来预测。

Abstract: Neural PDE simulators often receive only a single observed field at deployment. In this setting, a field-to-future predictor can collapse distinct latent problem states into the same deterministic interface, losing the ambiguity needed for reliable rollout and downstream decisions. We propose posterior-first neural PDE simulation: first infer a posterior over the minimal task-sufficient problem state, then condition prediction on that posterior. The resulting theory connects the object, the learning target, and the failure mode: Bayes downstream values factor through this posterior, refinement labels make it learnable by proper scoring rules, and deterministic collapse incurs an ambiguity barrier whenever the true posterior is non-Dirac. Synthetic exact-ambiguity experiments show that point-versus-posterior gaps track the predicted barrier. On metadata-hidden PDEBench tasks, posterior recovery reduces pooled rollout nRMSE from 0.175 to 0.132, closing 59.4% of the direct-to-oracle gap. These results suggest that single-observation neural PDE simulation should be posterior-first rather than monolithic field-to-future prediction.

</details>


### [201] [Ortho-Hydra: Orthogonalized Experts for DiT LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03252)
*Seunghyun Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对DiT上LoRA多风格微调中的style bleed问题，提出Ortho-Hydra方法。通过从预训练权重奇异向量中划分per-expert不相交输出子空间，解决了HydraLoRA零初始化导致的专家对称演化冷启动失效问题，使路由器能在训练早期就产生有效专业化信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在多风格数据上微调时存在style bleed问题：单一低秩残差无法表示多个不同的艺术家特征，优化器收敛到其平均值。现有HydraLoRA虽采用MoE架构，但全零初始化导致路由器收到来自各头相同的梯度，始终保持均匀先验，专家呈置换对称演化，实际上训练成本是单rank-r LoRA的E倍而效果等价。

Method: 提出Ortho-Hydra重新参数化方法：结合OFT风格的Cayley正交共享基与per-expert不相交输出子空间。具体从预训练权重的top-(Er)左奇异向量中划分出各专家的专属输出空间，使路由器per-expert评分在第0步即为非简并状态。

Result: 实验表明：两种HydraLoRA基线（零初始化共享基变体、σ=0.1高斯抖动）在前1k步内均未脱离均匀先验；Ortho-Hydra在前几百步内即开始去均匀化。路由器动态分析验证了冷启动机制的改善。

Conclusion: Ortho-Hydra通过不相交输出子空间的设计，在第0步就为路由器提供非简并的per-expert评分，使专业化在任意专家训练前就获得梯度信号，有效解决了HydraLoRA的冷启动问题。该方法在保持多风格数据路由能力的同时，避免了专家对称演化导致的训练效率损失。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion transformers (DiT) on multi-style data suffers from \emph{style bleed}: a single low-rank residual cannot represent several distinct artist fingerprints, and the optimizer converges to their average. Mixture-of-experts LoRA in the HydraLoRA style replaces the up-projection with $E$ heads under a router, but when every expert is zero-initialized the router receives identical gradient from each head and remains at the uniform prior. The experts then evolve permutation-symmetrically, and the network trains as a single rank-$r$ LoRA at $E{\times}$ the cost. We present \textbf{Ortho-Hydra}, a re-parameterisation that combines an OFT-style Cayley-orthogonal shared basis with per-expert \emph{disjoint output subspaces} carved from the top-$(Er)$ left singular vectors of the pretrained weight. Disjointness makes the router's per-expert score non-degenerate at step~$0$, so specialization receives gradient signal before any expert has trained. We test the predicted deadlock on a DiT pipeline by comparing two HydraLoRA baselines, a zero-initialized shared-basis variant and the original $σ{=}0.1$ Gaussian-jitter mitigation, against Ortho-Hydra under a matched optimiser, dataset, and step budget. Neither baseline leaves the uniform prior within the first $1\text{k}$ steps; Ortho-Hydra begins de-uniformising within the first few hundred. End-task generation quality on multi-style data is out of scope; we report the construction, the cold-start mechanism, and the routing dynamics it changes. Code: https://github.com/sorryhyun/anima_lora.

</details>


### [202] [The Right Answer, the Wrong Direction: Why Transformers Fail at Counting and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03258)
*Gabriel Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并论证：大语言模型在简单计数任务上的失败并非"内部没有表示计数"，而是"表示计数所在的子空间与输出头（lm_head）的数字 token 行几乎正交"，即一个**几何读出瓶颈**。作者在 Pythia/Qwen3/Mistral 三个模型族上用线性探针（内部 R²>0.99）与 logit-lens 证明计数信息确实存在于残差流中，但探针方向与数字行的 |cos|≤0.032（与随机方向 0.013 无显著差异，置换检验 p=0.79）。因果定位手段是"仅改写 lm_head 的 9 行数字行"（36,864 参数）：在受约束的 digit-restricted next-token 协议下把准确率从 10–24% 提升到 60.7–100%，但在无约束自回归生成中仍为 0%，说明部署级失败还包含上游注意力路由问题；真正可部署的修复是注意力 Q/V 上的 rank-16 LoRA（7.67M 参数），在真实贪心生成下达到 83.1%±7.2%，且生成与全词表 next-token 之间 gap=0.000，logit-lens 上正确数字的词表排名中位数从 ~5.6 万降至 1。作者强调该结论有清晰边界：MMLU/GSM8K 无此瓶颈（答案需多步推导、不存在于提示边界），DROP 仅部分迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型能做多步推理、通过资格考试、写可用代码，却在"数一数这段话里有多少只猫"这种只需逐 token 阅读、无需外部知识的任务上表现极差（最强模型在受控提示下不干预时准确率 ≤24%）。此前工作（Razeghi 等 2022 的计数退化、Stolfo 等 2023 的激活修补定位注意力头）只记录了现象，未回答"为什么偏偏是计数在答案直接可读时崩溃"。作者提出的假设是：失败发生在输出头（readout）而非表示层——模型把计数编码在一个输出头读不出来的方向上。这一假设可证伪：若瓶颈真在数字行的读出几何，则"只修数字行"应在受约束解码下奏效而在自由生成下失效，同时"修上游路由"应在两种协议下都奏效；两条预测均被实验验证，从而把"模型知道答案但无法表达"这一常见的定性观察推进到可定位（36,864 个参数）、可修复、可解释几何结构的层面。

Method: 基准与模型：全因子合成计数基准（6 计数 × 3 干扰项 × 4 长度 × 3 间距 = 216 条件 × 20 样本 = 4,320 提示，70/30 分层），另建 8 类实体 × 8 模板的自然语言计数（540 提示，含 3 个完全留出实体类型）；模型覆盖 Qwen3-8B/14B、Mistral-7B-v0.1、Pythia-410M/160M。协议被显式分为三种并逐处标注：digit-restricted next-token（诊断用）、full-vocab next-token（152K）、unconstrained greedy generation（部署相关），外加 instruct 模式；多数结果为 3 seed（42/11/77）× 200 测试提示，头条生成数为 5 seed。
测量手段：(1) 逐层 ridge 探针读出计数（R²、每层 |cos(probe, 数字行)|，并用 LDA/mean-diff/PCA/随机方向/置換检验/TOST 做稳健性）；(2) logit-lens（对 h^(ℓ) 施加最终 RMSNorm + lm_head，取 layer 0–35、entity-mean 与 last-token 两种位置，记录数字 argmax 命中率与正确数字的全词表 rank）；(3) 直接分析 lm_head 权重矩阵：数字行范数分位数（12–29 百分位）、结构 token（空格/换行/句点/逗号/冒号/破折号）与数字簇质心余弦 >0.5、数字簇内部平均余弦 0.735。
干预与对照：(a) 仅微调 9 行数字行（36,864 参数，ridge 与 Adam、margin-hinge 与 optimized 目标）；(b) DPS（Diagnostic Probe Steering，layer-2 ridge 探针把软/硬高斯或 +100 注入数字 logit；hard DPS/probe-round 作为上界诊断）；(c) LoRA rank-16 作用于注意力 Q/V（7.67M），以及 Q-only/K/V/O/FFN 单点消融；(d) 零训练范数重标定（×2、×3）；(e) 负对照：打乱行-数字映射、行位置随机、随机探针方向、乱序标签探针、shuffled-row 探针。
消融/边界分析：计数幅度分层（1–2 vs 4–7）、logit-rank 与修复准确率的相关性、类内隐藏态方差（实体计数 0.84 vs 列表长度 0.56）、9 行扩到 59 行的容量消融、4 种提示格式稳健性、instruct 模式、14B 上"单独行修复 vs 单独 DPS vs 叠加"的解耦实验，以及 MMLU/GSM8K/DROP 负对照。

Result: 1) 表示确实存在：Qwen3-8B 各层探针 R²≥0.99（最佳 0.997，层 2 起即可读），logistic 分类器在末层可达 100%；logit-lens 在 entity-mean 位置峰值仅 23.4%、last-token 位置峰值 41.6%（层 26），与探针形成约 4×/2.4× 差距。
2) 几何错位：探针方向与数字行 |cos| 全局均值 0.016（95% CI 0.015–0.016），每层 ≤0.032，随机基线 0.013±0.011，置换检验 p=0.79；对比"模型确实会输出的特征"其 |cos| 为 0.100–0.115（高维不构成障碍）；随机方向探针效果与基线一致。数字 token 在末位出现的概率 ≤1.5%（计数/算术/随机文本均如此），因此训练梯度不会把数字行转向计数方向——作者给出零空间不动点解释，并用消融验证：算术微调 3000 步后 |cos| 几乎不变（0.0096），而计数微调使其上升 3.2×（0.0074→0.0280）。
3) 因果定位：9 行修复在 digit-restricted 下达到 60.7%（实体计数，最难）至 100%（加法），probe-round 上界 96.8–100%，hard DPS 与 probe-round 完全一致（98.7%），soft DPS 退回基线；full-vocab 修复 60.3%±2.8%。行修复在贪心生成中为 0.0%（8/15 token 内无数字），logit-masked 生成恢复到 59.2%，证明是"格式/路由失配"而非表示错误；打乱行映射退化到 11.1%/14.0%。
4) 修复天花板：数字行范数位于 12–29 百分位，84% 词表更"响"；范数重标定可把 full-vocab 从 0% 提到 26.5% 但不超过受限基线；~60–67% 的上限被归因于词表竞争 + 实体计数的类内方差 0.84；Adam 微调仅 +10.8pp、扩到 59 行无增益。
5) 可部署修复：LoRA Q/V rank-16 达到 full-vocab next-token 91.7%±4.5%、真实贪心生成 83.1%±7.2%（多任务混合 5 seed；仅实体计数 3 seed 为 97.0/96.5/94.5），生成与 next-token gap=0.000；机制证据：layer 2 探针方向几乎不变（0.0089→0.0070），layer 35 探针 R² 0.974→0.998，logit-lens 正确数字 rank 中位数 ~55,980→1（seed 42 单轨迹 54,332→838），logit-lens 准确率 9.3%→71.8%。跨任务同向：字符计数 rank 32,265→16、加法 21,186→1。
6) 泛化与边界：instruct 模式首 token 数字准确率仅 22%（探针 R²≥0.9996），9 行修复后 99.9%；自然语言计数 88.7%→97.6%（DPS）/96.3%（probe-round），|cos| 仍 0.015；NL 与合成探针方向彼此近正交（|cos|=0.114），解释跨域修复失败与 ~65–68% 结构上限；多位计数（10–20）基线 0%、DPS 100%；多数投票 |cos|=0.003、DPS 达 100%；最大值抽取 R²=0.757、DPS 11.1%→40.0%。MMLU（70.2%，行修复反降至 55.6%，|cos| 0.31–0.48）与 GSM8K（探针 R²≈−0.1）构成干净零效应，DROP 仅 +8pp（部分迁移）。14B 上 |cos|=0.011，为随机基线的 0.57×；单独行修复 58.2%、单独 DPS 10.8%（近基线），另一套匹配分布的自然语言联合管线（9 行 + 硬 DPS）达 90.3%±1.5%。

Conclusion: 计数失败应被重新理解为"几何读出瓶颈"而非表示失败：模型确实在线性可解码的子空间中精确编码了计数，但该方向与 lm_head 数字行近乎正交，输出通道几何失配。其学术价值在于把"模型知道却无法表达"这一常见论断量化为可测量的正交性（|cos|≤0.032）、可定位的最小因果探针（36,864 参数）、可解析的 ~60% 修复天花板（数字行范数分位数 + 词表竞争），并由此给出可证伪的干预处方：受约束解码下改数字行足够，全词表/自由生成下必须同时修注意力 Q/V 路由。方法论启示是：在假设"推理失败"之前，应先检验"表示–输出对齐"。
需注意的边界与保留：结论最强地适用于低词表、答案在提示边界已预编码的聚合任务，不外推至 GSM8K 式多步推理或开放式生成；9 行修复被作者明确界定为诊断工具（自由生成 0%），不可当作开放文本补丁；DPS/probe-round 属上界诊断；头条 83.1% 为多任务混合池化值（方差受任务混合驱动），实体单任务约 95–97%；文中大量 legacy 协议数字（93.8%、97.5%、38.8% 等）与 harmonized 头条数字不可横向比较（作者以 Table 3/协议映射表标注）；"rank 降至 1"的表述实为多 seed 池化中位数，单 seed（42）仍为 838，作者已披露该口径差异。

Abstract: Large language models often fail at simple counting tasks, even when the items to count are explicitly present in the prompt. We investigate whether this failure occurs because transformers do not represent counts internally, or because they cannot convert those representations into the correct output tokens.
  Across three model families, Pythia, Qwen3, and Mistral, ranging from 0.4B to 14B parameters, we find strong evidence for the second explanation. Linear probes recover the correct count from intermediate layers with near-perfect accuracy ($R^2>0.99$), showing that the information is present. However, the internal directions that encode counts are nearly orthogonal to the output-head rows for digit tokens ($|\cos|\leq0.032$). In other words, the model stores the count in a form that the digit logits do not naturally read out.
  We localize this failure with two interventions. Updating only the digit rows of the output head (36,864 parameters) substantially improves constrained next-token digit prediction (60.7 to 100.0% across four tasks), but it does not fix autoregressive generation. By contrast, a small LoRA intervention on attention Q/V weights (7.67M parameters) improves upstream routing and achieves 83.1% +/- 7.2% in true greedy autoregressive generation. Logit-lens measurements confirm the mechanism: the correct digit's vocabulary rank drops from 55,980 to 1, a 50,000x improvement. Additional norm, logit-lens, and cross-task analyses show that the bottleneck generalizes across character counting, addition, and list length, while remaining absent from broader multi-step reasoning benchmarks, including MMLU, GSM8K, and DROP.
  These results identify counting failure as a geometric readout bottleneck rather than a failure of internal representation: the model knows the count but the output pathway is geometrically misaligned with the tokens needed to express it.

</details>


### [203] [Do LLMs have core beliefs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03255)
*Anna Sokol,Marianna B. Ganapini,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨LLM是否具有类似人类认知的"核心信念"。通过开发的ADT框架在5个领域测试发现，当前所有LLM在对话压力下都无法维持稳定的世界观，显示出与人类认知的根本差异——缺乏核心承诺这一关键组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于LLM是否具有人类水平认知的争论中，学界较少关注人类认知的结构性组成部分——核心信念（core beliefs）。这些真理性的基础承诺通常难以被推翻，因为放弃它们意味着现实观的根本性转变。研究旨在探索LLM是否具有类似人类的核心信念机制。

Method: 开发了"对抗对话树"（Adversarial Dialogue Trees, ADTs）探测框架，对LLM进行系统性对抗测试。该方法通过多轮对话压力测试模型是否能维持其核心立场。测试覆盖5个知识领域：科学、历史、地理、生物和数学。

Result: 大多数LLM在对话压力下无法维持稳定的世界观。虽然部分新模型显示出更好的稳定性，但所有模型最终都会在对抗性对话压力下失败，无法维持关键承诺。

Conclusion: 当前所有LLM都缺乏人类水平认知的关键组成部分。尽管新一代模型的辩论能力有所提升，但在对话压力下仍无法维持核心信念的稳定性。

关键发现：
- 5个领域（科学、历史、地理、生物、数学）测试中，大多数LLM无法维持稳定的世界观
- 较新的模型虽然稳定性有所改善，但仍会在对话压力下失败
- 模型在论证技巧上有代际改进
- 核心承诺（core commitments）概念的缺失是LLM与人类认知的根本差异

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has sparked debate about whether these systems exhibit human-level cognition. In this debate, little attention has been paid to a structural component of human cognition: core beliefs, truths that provide a foundation around which we can build a worldview. These commitments usually resist debunking, as abandoning them would represent a fundamental shift in how we see reality. In this paper, we ask whether LLMs hold anything akin to core commitments. Using a probing framework we call Adversarial Dialogue Trees (ADTs) over five domains (science, history, geography, biology, and mathematics), we find that most LLMs fail to maintain a stable worldview. Though some recent models showed improved stability, they still eventually failed to maintain key commitments under conversational pressure. These results document an improvement in argumentative skills across model generations but indicate that all current models lack a key component of human-level cognition.

</details>


### [204] [Two Calls, Two Moments, and the Vote-Accuracy Curve of Repeated LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03379)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套仅用每例两次带标注调用即可获得的、无分布假设且精确（sharp）的多数投票（majority vote）精度上下界：用两次调用识别潜在成功概率的一、二阶矩（μ, ν）及同例正确性相关 ρ，通过"三支撑点极值化 + 二次对偶"把无限维矩问题化为有限凸规划，给出三票预算的闭式区间（宽度 ≤ 1/8）与改进判据、无限票端点的尖锐界，并给出Wald投影的有限样本置信版本、最大熵/LDGP点预测补全；在 QNLI/QQP 上的 LLM 实验表明单次精度不能排序投票收益，且出现投票后的排名反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样（self-consistency、pass@k、推理扩展）是当前消耗测试时算力的标准手段，但"重复调用能提升多少精度"并不由单次调用精度决定：一致性不等于正确性——模型可能稳定答对，也可能稳定答错；低单次精度的模型反而可能保留更多可被投票平均掉的随机性。然而，要刻画投票精度曲线（vote-accuracy curve）通常需要每例大量重复调用，成本高昂。因此核心动机是：能否用极少的验证开销（每例仅 2 次带标注调用）就精确刻画"重复推理的收益曲线"，从而指导是否启动投票、增加到多少票；以及为什么单次精度不足以排序推理策略。

Method: 设定条件独立（conditional-i.i.d.）模型：每例潜在成功概率 q_i ~ π，条件于 q_i 时各次调用独立为 Bernoulli(q_i)。两次调用的成对正确性表识别 μ=E[Q]、ν=E[Q²]，以及同例正确性相关 ρ=(ν−μ²)/(μ(1−μ))；可行性集恰为 μ²≤ν≤μ，构成矩类 M(μ,ν)。关键技术：(1) 尖锐界——对任意有限预算 2n+1，L_n/U_n 由矩约束下 ∫P_n dπ 的极值给出，其极值元在 [0,1] 上至多三点支撑（Winkler–Carathéodory 有限矩极点定理），并有二次上/下包络对偶证书（凸包支撑超平面 + 交换法在端点与驻点处检查多项式松弛），界为精确而非离散化；(2) 三票闭式界——由 m3=E[Q³] 的界 ν²/μ ≤ m3 ≤ ν−(μ−ν)²/(1−μ) 得 L1=ν+2(μ−ν)²/(1−μ)、U1=3ν−2ν²/μ，宽度 2μ(1−μ)ρ(1−ρ)≤1/8，改进判据 ρ<1−1/(2μ)，等价于 P(B1≠B2)>2P(B1=B2=0)；(3) 无限票端点 V_∞(π)=π((1/2,1])+½π({1/2})，通过二次主序 ψ_a(q)=(q−a)²/(1/2−a)² 与 3q−2q² 给出显式上界 G(μ,ν)（两分支），下界由 Q↦1−Q 反射；(4) 有限样本推断——对 (μ̂,ν̂) 构造可行 Wald 椭圆区域并投影各预算的界与增益界（凸最小化，Bonferroni 校正）；(5) 点补全——[0,1] 上二次指数最大熵密度与 LDGP（正态卵形/Probit）难度模型唯一矩匹配，给出全曲线预测。实验：Llama 3.1-8B、Phi-4-mini、Qwen2.5-7B 经 Ollama 在 QNLI/QQP 上 18 个任务×模型×温度设定（每设定 8192 例、5 次调用），另设两个开发集选定、独立留出确认的协议 Q04/N05（6144 例 + 预设 1024 例第三次调用），以及 GSM8K 九次调用补充评估。

Result: 理论上：一次调用无法保证任何正增益（确定性律 δ_0/δ_1 使 V_n=μ）；两次调用给出每个有限预算的尖锐区间，三票宽度至多 1/8；无限票端点亦可尖锐界定但对 q=1/2 附近潜在质量敏感（μ=1/2 时宽度 1−ρ，ρ→0 时趋于 1，而三票宽度→0）。实验上：18 个直接提示设定的三票/五票经验精度全部落在其 95% Wald 投影区内（兼容性检验）；Q04 与 N05 的三票增益下界分别为 +0.70 与 +0.72 个百分点（名义联合置信 95%），第三次调用观测增益为 +3.22（CI [1.70,4.75]）与 +4.65（[3.38,5.93]），均超过半个百分点目标；N04（Qwen, T=0.2，两次调用分歧率约 0.6%）的三票/五票增益上界仅 +0.46/+0.47，排除达标可能；Llama 在 QQP 上把温度由 0.2 升至 2.0 使单次精度下降而五票增益由 −0.06 升至 +2.57；出现投票诱导的排名反转（N09 单次落后 N06 0.86，五票反超 1.04）；LDGP 点预测 MAE 为 0.21/0.22，优于区间中点的 0.36/0.35；GSM8K 九票经验增益 +0.85 但两票区间未能定号；缓存复用破坏条件独立性的诊断显示区间被压缩为一点而实际精度偏差可达 0.78 个百分点。

Conclusion: 每例两次带标注调用即可把"可重复性"转化为关于测试时算力分配的证据：其联合正确性表唯一确定所需矩，从而给出每个有限票数的分布无关尖锐精度界、票数间增益界与改进判据，并可经 Wald 区域投影纳入验证不确定性，支持同时进行多预算比较与预算选择。无限票端点有独立且尖锐的几何（紧有限预算界不意味着紧的无限外推）。实验显示单次精度与重复性提供的是互补信息：温度调整与随机化模型混合可制造不被单次精度排序的投票收益，甚至引发排名反转，因此模型选择与调用预算必须联合考虑。论文还提出后续方向：利用样本特征估计条件矩以指导逐例调用预算，并把回归不确定性传播进这些界中。

Abstract: Repeated sampling is a standard way to spend test-time compute, but its benefit is controlled by the latent distribution of correctness across examples, not by one-call accuracy alone.
