Our results show that TabSurv consistently outperforms on average established classical and deep learning baselines, such as RSF, DeepSurv, DeepHit, SurvTRACE. Notably, deep ensembles with Weibull parametrization instead of non-parametric models achieve the highest average rank by C-index. Overall, our study clarifies how modern tabular neural networks can be adapted and trained to tackle survival analysis problems, offering a strong and reliable approach. The TabSurv implementation is publicly available.

</details>


### [261] [Integrating Feature Correlation in Differential Privacy with Applications in DP-ERM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03945)
*Tianyu Wang,Luhao Zhang,Rachel Cummings*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出特征相关性感知的差分隐私框架 CorrDP：将标准 DP 的统一隐私约束按"敏感/不敏感特征之间的统计相关程度（以全变差距离 TV 度量）"分级放宽，在相关度低的不敏感特征上少加噪声、同时仍保证不泄露敏感特征的条件分布；据此设计了 CorrDP-Laplace 机制与 CorrDP-SGD（含 TV 距离的样本内估计版本），在 m_s 个敏感特征时可将 DP-ERM 的效用从 Õ(√m/(nε)) 提升到 Õ(√m_s/(nε))，并给出近匹配下界与合成/真实数据实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准（全局）差分隐私对所有特征施加统一隐私约束，忽略了现实中特征敏感度的异质性——例如医疗数据中血压比粗化的年龄段更敏感；但这些特征通常相互相关（如年龄影响血压范围），因此对二者施加同等噪声既损失效用又扭曲数据。已有的半敏感 DP（semi-sensitive DP）把不敏感特征直接当作公开，忽略了"泄露年龄可暴露血压条件分布"这一隐私风险，存在隐私漏洞；而属性隐私/Pufferfish 等相关工作关注的是个体间相关或数据集级统计保护，而非特征间相关性的行级隐私。作者因此提出两个核心问题：(1) 对于含不敏感特征且其与敏感特征可能相关的数据集，应采用何种隐私机制；(2) 放宽 DP 定义能在多大程度上改善隐私–效用权衡。

Method: 1) 形式化 CorrDP：定义相邻数据库（仅敏感或仅不敏感分量不同），并以数据集相关的"敏感度距离" d(D,D') 取代统一约束：P(A(D)∈R) ≤ e^{ε/d(D,D')}·P(A(D')∈R)+δ；距离用敏感特征条件分布的全变差距离 d(D,D')=max_I TV(P_{X^S|e^I}, P_{X^S|(e')^I})，满足公理：d∈[0,1]；仅敏感分量变化时 d=1（退化为标准 DP）；仅不敏感分量变化时 d∈[0,1)；二者独立时 d=0。2) 相关敏感度与机制：定义相关敏感度 Δ_C f=min{Σ_{j∈S}Δf_j+Σ_{j∈U}Δf_j·TV(j), Δf}，据此构造 CorrDP-Laplace 机制（噪声尺度 Δ_C f/ε），证明其为 (ε,0)-CorrDP 且精度优于标准 Laplace。3) CorrDP-SGD：在标准 DP-SGD 基础上为每个坐标设定异方差噪声——敏感坐标 σ_i²=(log(1/δ)+1)L²T/(n²ε²)，不敏感坐标乘以 max{TV(i), m_s²/m²}；用矩会计（Rényi 散度 + 高斯机制）证明隐私，用 Shamir 2013 的 SGD 分析给效用。4) 距离未知时的样本内估计：用 TV̂_D(i) 加高置信上界项 2c₂√(log((m−m_s)/δ))/n^γ 替代真实 TV(i)，并证明所得机制为 (ε,2δ)-CorrDP 且效用同阶；给出离散/直方图/联合高斯等满足误差率 γ 与稳定性 (O(1/n) 敏感度) 的具体估计器。5) 扩展：全连接神经网络的逐层噪声标定（首层按特征相关性缩减）、连续敏感度评分 s_i、标签隐私尺度、Hellinger/Wasserstein 距离替换 TV，以及对属性隐私、Label DP、度量 DP 的对比分析。

Result: 理论上：CorrDP-Laplace 满足 (ε,0)-CorrDP，其高精度偏差界优于标准 Laplace；CorrDP-SGD 满足 (ε,δ)-CorrDP，效用 R(θ_priv)=Õ(√((m_s+min{Σ_{i∈U}TV(i), m_s/4})·log(1/δ))/(nε))；当 Σ_{i∈U}TV(i)=Θ(m_s) 时退化为 Õ(√m_s/(nε))，相对标准 DP 的 Õ(√m/(nε)) 提升约 √(m/m_s) 倍；并给出近匹配下界 Ω(min{1, √(m_s+max_k k·TV^(k)²)/(nε)})；PL 条件下可达 Õ(m_s/(n²ε²))，神经网络中效用从 √(m_θ)/(nε) 改善为 √(m_θ−(m−m_s)d_0)/(nε)。TV 估计版保持同阶效用，隐私仅损失一个 δ 因子。实验上（合成高斯数据 m=100, n=2000, m_s=10；Adult、Sepsis、Credit Card、Medical Cost 数据集，涵盖逻辑回归、线性回归与两层神经网络），CorrDP 在所有设置下都显著优于 Standard DP-SGD 与 Partial 基线，且与隐私更弱的 Semi 基线相当（Adult 上甚至优于 Semi），高隐私区优势最明显；改变敏感特征比例（0.05m~0.8m）结论定性不变。

Conclusion: CorrDP 提供了一个把特征相关性纳入隐私约束的行级 DP 松弛框架：既允许不敏感特征少加噪声，又通过 TV 距离确保不会通过相关特征泄露敏感特征的信息，并配套给出可计算的距离估计流程与从 GLM 到神经网络的算法与效用改进（CorrDP-SGD 相对标准 DP-SGD 最高可获得约 √(m/m_s) 的效用增益）。局限包括：框架在敏感/不敏感划分清晰时最有效（边界模糊情形需用附录中的连续敏感度松弛）；隐私与效用保证依赖对特征空间和模型结构的额外假设（Assumption 3.4 等）；高维下 TV 距离估计计算开销较大，仅需不过松的上界。后续方向包括改进距离估计效率、扩展到强凸/非凸与其它一阶隐私算法，以及推广到假设检验等推断性任务的理论保证。

Abstract: Standard differential privacy imposes uniform privacy constraints across all features, overlooking the inherent distinction between sensitive and insensitive features in practice. In this paper, we introduce a relaxed definition of differential privacy that accounts for such heterogeneity, allowing certain features to be treated as insensitive even when correlated with sensitive ones. We propose a correlation-aware framework, $\textsf{CorrDP}$, which relaxes privacy for insensitive features while accounting for their correlations with sensitive features, with the correlations quantified using total variation distance. We design algorithms for differentially private empirical risk minimization (DP-ERM) under the $\textsf{CorrDP}$ framework, incorporating distance-dependent noise into gradients for improved theoretical utility guarantees. When the correlation distance is unknown, we estimate it from the dataset and show that it achieves a comparable privacy-utility guarantee. We perform experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets and show that $\textsf{CorrDP}$-based DP-ERM algorithms consistently outperform the standard DP framework in the presence of insensitive features.

</details>


### [262] [Pretrained Model Representations as Acquisition Signals for Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03964)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Shikha Surana,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Zachary Weller-Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练MACE模型的潜在空间信息进行主动学习采集，直接从预训练模型提取NTK核和激活核作为采集信号，无需辅助不确定性头、贝叶斯训练或委员会集成，在反应化学基准测试中达到相同性能目标所需数据量减少38%（能量）和28%（力）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应化学MLIP训练受限于量子化学标签的高成本和过渡态配置的稀缺性。现有主动学习方法通常需要复杂的辅助组件（不确定性头、贝叶斯训练、委员会集成），本文探索预训练MLIP的潜在空间是否已包含有效采集所需信息，以简化流程。

Method: 从预训练MACE势能中直接导出两种采集信号：有限宽度神经切线核（NTK）和基于隐藏层潜在空间特征的激活核。提出基于相似性的采集策略，利用预训练模型诱导的相似性空间保留化学有意义结构。

Result: 两种核方法一致优于固定描述符基线、委员会分歧和随机采集，达到性能目标所需数据平均减少38%（能量误差）和28%（力误差）。预训练模型诱导的相似性空间保留了化学有意义的结构，提供了比随机初始化或固定描述符核更可靠的残差不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 预训练将潜在空间几何与模型误差对齐，产生实用且充分的采集信号用于反应MLIP微调，无需额外的不确定性建模组件。这简化了主动学习流程，使其更易于应用于反应化学系统。

Abstract: Training machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for reactive chemistry is often bottlenecked by the high cost of quantum chemical labels and the scarcity of transition state configurations in candidate pools. Active learning (AL) can mitigate these costs, but its effectiveness hinges on the acquisition rule. We investigate whether the latent space of a pretrained MLIP already contains the information necessary for effective acquisition, eliminating the need for auxiliary uncertainty heads, Bayesian training and fine-tuning, or committee ensembles. We introduce two acquisition signals derived directly from a pretrained MACE potential: a finite-width neural tangent kernel (NTK) and an activation kernel built from hidden latent space features. On reactive-chemistry benchmarks, both kernels consistently outperform fixed-descriptor baselines, committee disagreement, and random acquisition, reducing the data required to reach performance targets by an average of 38% for energy error and 28% for force error. We further show that the pretrained model induces similarity spaces that preserve chemically meaningful structure and provide more reliable residual uncertainty estimates than randomly initialised or fixed-descriptor-based kernels. Our results suggest that pretraining aligns latent-space geometry with model error, yielding a practical and sufficient acquisition signal for reactive MLIP fine-tuning.

</details>


### [263] [Flow Sampling: Learning to Sample from Unnormalized Densities via Denoising Conditional Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03984)
*Aaron Havens,Brian Karrer,Neta Shaul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Sampling框架，通过条件于噪声样本的扩散模型和流匹配方法，从已知能量函数定义的未归一化密度中进行高效采样，并自然推广到黎曼流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非归一化密度采样是生成建模的重要变体，但评估能量函数代价高昂，需要学习高效的采样器。

Method: 提出Flow Sampling框架，条件于噪声样本而非数据样本，回归去噪扩散漂移项；利用插值过程减少能量函数评估次数；推导出常曲率黎曼流形上的闭合漂移公式。

Result: 在合成基准、小分子肽、大规模分子构象生成和球面分布等任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Flow Sampling提供了一种高效、可扩展的未归一化密度采样方法，具备流形泛化能力。

Abstract: Sampling from unnormalized densities is analogous to the generative modeling problem, but the target distribution is defined by a known energy function instead of data samples. Because evaluating the energy function is often costly, a primary challenge is to learn an efficient sampler. We introduce Flow Sampling, a framework built on diffusion models and flow matching for the data-free setting. Our training objective is conditioned on a noise sample and regresses onto a denoising diffusion drift constructed from the energy function. In contrast, diffusion models' objective is conditioned on a data sample and regresses onto a noising diffusion drift. We utilize the interpolant process to minimize the number of energy function evaluations during training, resulting in an efficient and scalable method for sampling unnormalized densities. Furthermore, our formulation naturally extends to Riemannian manifolds, enabling diffusion-based sampling in geometries beyond Euclidean space. We derive a closed-form formula for the conditional drift on constant curvature manifolds, including hyperspheres and hyperbolic spaces. We evaluate Flow Sampling on synthetic energy benchmarks, small peptides, large-scale amortized molecular conformer generation, and distributions supported on the sphere, demonstrating strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [264] [A Closed-Form Adaptive-Landmark Kernel for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04046)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PALACE是一种基于持久同调的自适应分类引擎，通过覆盖理论和Lebesgue数准则提供四个封闭形式理论保证（ distortion bound、最优权重、分类率、预测证书），在多个数据集上表现最优或具有竞争力，尤其在领域膨胀场景下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久同调分类方法PLACE使用固定参数网格，缺乏数据自适应性。当数据分布集中时，均匀网格存在效率低下问题。该研究旨在通过自适应调整预算(budget)、半径(radii)、带宽(bandwidth)三个超参数（各≤5个选择），构建数据自适应的分类引擎。

Method: 方法核心基于覆盖理论：利用landmark覆盖上的Lebesgue数准则推导出四个封闭形式保证。(i) 结构下界λ(τ;ν)控制失真；(ii) 通过训练标签推导最优权重w_k=K^(-1/2)和最远点采样策略；(iii) 核-RKHS分类率及Mahalanobis margin作为排名器；(iv) 非渐近(Pinelis)和渐近(Gaussian)形式的预测证书，无需校准分割。

Result: 理论保证：分类率O((k-1)√K/(γ√m_min))，二元必要性阈值m=Ω(√K/γ)。实证表现：Orbit5k达91.3%±1.0%（匹配Persformer）；COX2和MUTAG领先所有图方法；DHFR在1pp内接近ECP。8倍领域膨胀时自适应方法保持94%准确率，均匀网格降至25%。

Conclusion: PALACE是首个封闭形式图分类方法在多个基准数据集上达到最优或竞争水平，证明了数据自适应机制在持久同调分类中的有效性。Mahalanobis margin(mean Spearman ρ≈+0.60)和预测证书机制为实际应用提供了可解释性和可靠性保证。

Abstract: We introduce PALACE (Persistence Adaptive-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), the data-adaptive companion to PLACE, paying a small cross-validation tier on three knobs (budget, radii, bandwidth; $\leq 5$ choices each). A cover-theoretic core (Lebesgue-number criterion on the landmark cover) yields four closed-form guarantees. (i) A structural lower distortion bound $λ(τ;ν)$ on $\mathcal{D}_n$ under cross-diagram non-interference, with a $(D/L)^2$ budget reduction over the uniform grid when diagrams concentrate. (ii) Equal weights $w_k = K^{-1/2}$ maximizing $λ$, and farthest-point-sampling positions $2$-approximating the optimal $k$-center covering radius; both derived from training labels alone, no gradient training. (iii) A kernel-RKHS classification rate $O((k-1)\sqrt{K}/(γ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ with binary necessity threshold $m = Ω(\sqrt K/γ)$ from a matching Le Cam lower bound, and a closed-form filtration-selection rule. The kernel-Mahalanobis margin $\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$ is the strongest closed-form ranker across the chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ\approx +0.60$); the isotropic surrogate $\hatγ/\sqrt{K}$ admits a selection-consistency rate, and $\widehatλ$ from (i) provides an independent data-level signal (positive on COX2 and PTC). (iv) A per-prediction certificate, in non-asymptotic Pinelis and asymptotic Gaussian forms, with no calibration split. Empirically, PALACE is the strongest closed-form diagram-based method on Orbit5k ($91.3 \pm 1.0\%$, matching Persformer), leads every diagram-based competitor on COX2 and MUTAG, and is competitive on DHFR (within 1 pp of ECP). At $8\times$ domain inflation, adaptive placement maintains $94\%$ while the uniform grid collapses to chance ($25\%$ on 4-class data).

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [A Poisson Process for Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03071)
*Amit Ganz Rozenman,Ariel Kulik,Roy Schwartz,Mohit Singh*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了单调次模函数在拟阵独立约束下的最大化问题，提出一种基于随机泊松过程的新颖简单方法，无需离散化或舍入，仅需少量单元素交换即可达到(1-1/e)的紧近似比，并应用于次模福利最大化和一般/可分离分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题已研究超过十年：2008年连续贪婪算法获得紧近似保证，2012和2024年非遗忘局部搜索技术也达到相同保证。现有方法较为复杂，作者寻求更简洁的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于随机泊松过程的新方法，与现有两种技术不同，无需离散化或舍入，在执行极少的单元素交换操作即可完成。

Result: 该方法达到了(1-1/e)的紧近似比，并成功应用于次模福利最大化问题和一般/可分离分配问题，获得快速算法。

Conclusion: 该方法以极简的方式达到了最优近似保证，为次模优化领域提供了一种简洁有效的新范式。

Abstract: We study the problem of maximizing a monotone submodular function subject to a matroid independence constraint. For more than a decade, a rich body of work has studied this problem. Initially, a tight approximation of $ (1-\frac{1}{e})$ was given using the continuous greedy algorithm [Calinescu-Chekuri-Pal-Vondr{á}k STOC`2008] and later non-oblivious local search techniques were able to match this tight approximation guarantee [Filmus-Ward FOCS`2012] and [Buchbinder-Feldman FOCS`2024].
  We propose a new and remarkably simple approach to this problem that is based on a stochastic Poisson process. Our approach matches the tight $ (1-\frac{1}{e})$ approximation guarantee and it differs from the known two techniques since it does not require discretization or rounding while performing very few single element swaps. We also present applications of our approach and obtain fast algorithms for submodular welfare maximization, and for the general and separable assignment problems.

</details>


### [266] [Dynamic Detours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03225)
*Daniel Dadush,Michał Pilipczuk,Amadeus Reinald,Marek Sokołowski,Michał Włodarczyk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文设计了支持边插入和删除的动态图数据结构，能够高效回答长路径查询、绕行查询和奇偶路径查询。其核心发现是：与已知的长度受限短路径查询下界不同，长路径查询可以高效处理（摊销时间$O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$或$2^{O(k^3)} \log n$级别）。这与三角形猜想失败时短路径查询不存在$O(n^{o(1)})$时间数据结构的已知条件_lower bound形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文研究的动机来自于对动态图上路径查询数据结构的探索。给定参数k，需要在完全动态无向图（支持边插入和删除）中回答以下查询：(1)长(u,v)路径：判断是否存在长度至少为k的路径；(2)长(u,v)绕行：判断是否存在长度至少为dist_G(u,v)+k的路径；(3)偶/奇(u,v)路径：判断是否存在偶/奇长度的路径。

Method: 论文的主要技术贡献是"延迟边插入"(delayed edge insertion)机制，该机制在双连通分量(biconnected components)的层面上局部运作。这种技术使得在动态更新过程中能够高效地维护和查询路径信息。

Result: 论文实现了以下摊销时间复杂度：对于前两类查询，更新或回答查询的时间为$2^{O(k^3)} \log n + O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$；对于奇偶路径查询，时间为$O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$，其中n为图的顶点数。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了动态图数据结构中"长路径查询比短路径查询更容易处理"这一反直觉现象。虽然已知短路径（如长度至多2的路径）查询在条件下难以获得亚线性时间，但长路径（长度至少k）和奇偶路径查询却可以高效实现。这挑战了该领域的传统认知，并为动态图算法的研究方向提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Fix a parameter $k\in \mathbf{N}$. We give dynamic data structures that for a fully dynamic undirected graph $G$, updated over time by edge insertions and edge deletions, can answer the following queries:
  - Long $(u,v)$-path: Given $u,v\in V(G)$, is there a path from $u$ to $v$ of length at least $k$?
  - Long $(u,v)$-detour: Given $u,v\in V(G)$, is there a path from $u$ to $v$ of length at least $\text{dist}_G(u,v)+k$?
  - Even/odd $(u,v)$-path: Given $u,v\in V(G)$, is there a path from $u$ to $v$ of even/odd length?
  The amortized time of executing an update or answering a query is $2^{O(k^3)} \log n + O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$ in the first two cases, and $O(\log^2 n \log^2 \log n)$ in the last, where $n$ is the number of vertices of $G$.
  The first result is in sharp contrast with known conditional lower bounds for reporting paths of length at most $k$. Specifically, there is no data structure supporting queries about $(u,v)$-paths of length at most two in time $n^{o(1)}$ unless the Triangle Conjecture fails. Our main technical contribution is a mechanism of "delayed edge insertion" that works locally on the level of biconnected components.

</details>


### [267] [Provable Accuracy Collapse in Embedding-Based Representations under Dimensionality Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03346)
*Dionysis Arvanitakis,Vaggos Chatziafratis,Yiyuan Luo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 证明了嵌入维度与精度之间的尖锐权衡：除非嵌入维度接近真实维度，否则精度会骤然崩塌至50%的平凡基线，并基于Unique Games Conjecture证明即使在一维可实现的情况下，多项式时间算法也无法克服这一精度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中，基于欧氏空间R^d的嵌入表示是核心方法，主要目标是使用最小维度来忠实捕获数据关系。关键问题在于维度选择如何影响表示精度。

Method: 研究标准对比学习设置中的锚点-正样本-负样本三元组(i,j,k)，这些三元组编码距离比较dist(i,j) < dist(i,k)，假设这些三元组可由未知D维真实嵌入实现。

Result: 存在常数c<1，使得维度至多cD的任何嵌入都会违反一半的三元组，精度低至仅50%，等同于忽略输入的一维平凡解。

Conclusion: 除非嵌入维度d接近真实维度D，否则精度会发生骤然崩塌。即使数据几乎可在一维实现，在Unique Games Conjecture下，多项式时间算法也无法超越50%的平凡基线。

Abstract: Embedding-based representations in Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^d$ are a cornerstone of modern machine learning, where a major goal is to use the \emph{smallest dimension} that faithfully captures data relations. In this work, we prove sharp dimension--accuracy tradeoffs and identify a fundamental information-theoretic limitation: unless the embedding dimension $d$ is chosen close to the ground-truth dimension $D$, accuracy undergoes a sudden collapse. Our main result shows that this phenomenon arises even in standard contrastive learning settings, where supervision is limited to a set of $m$ anchor--positive--negative triplets $(i,j,k)$ encoding distance comparisons $\mathrm{dist}(i,j) < \mathrm{dist}(i,k)$. Specifically, given triplets realizable by an unknown ground-truth embedding in $D$ dimensions, we prove that there exists constant $c < 1$, such that \emph{every embedding of dimension at most $cD$ violates half of the triplets}, yielding accuracy as low as a trivial one-dimensional solution that ignores the input. We complement our information-theoretic bounds with strong computational hardness results: under the Unique Games Conjecture, even if the given triplets are nearly realizable in $D=1$ dimension, no polynomial-time algorithm -- \textit{regardless of its dimension} -- can achieve accuracy above the trivial $50\%$ baseline.

</details>


### [268] [An Optimal Algorithm for Cardinality-Constrained Diameter Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03431)
*Chao Xu,Mingdong Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究基数约束直径划分问题，提出O(n²)最优算法（改进自O(n² log n)），通过将问题归约为最大生成树瓶颈2-着色问题求解，并证明下界；同时对欧几里得权重给出亚二次算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进已有的O(n² log n)算法，解决基数约束直径划分问题

Method: 将问题归约为最大生成树上的瓶颈2-着色问题，通过树动态规划求解；同时对欧几里得权重利用几何结构特性

Result: 提出O(n²)算法和匹配的Ω(n²)下界；同时输出所有基数的最优解；欧几里得权重下实现亚二次时间

Conclusion: 算法达到最优且匹配下界；欧几里得权重下可实现亚二次时间

Abstract: Cardinality-constrained diameter partitioning asks for a partition of $n$ items into two classes of prescribed sizes that minimizes the larger of the two class diameters. We give an $O(n^2)$ algorithm and a matching $Ω(n^2)$ lower bound if we can only query the weight between two elements. The algorithm computes the optimum for every cardinality simultaneously, improving Avis's $O(n^2\log n)$. The reduction is to a bottleneck 2-coloring problem on the maximum spanning tree, solved by a standard tree DP. For a single cardinality with Euclidean weights, we obtain a subquadratic time algorithm in any fixed dimension.

</details>


### [269] [Counting Small Balanced (p,q)-bicliques in Signed Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03603)
*Mekala Kiran,Apurba Das,Suman Banerjee,Tathagata Ray*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化研究签名二部图中固定尺寸「平衡 (p,q)-双团」的精确计数问题：将 BCList++ 扩展出带符号基线 SBCList++，并提出楔形中心的 BBWC（按符号配置分桶楔形、组合数计数）与基于顶点剪枝的 BBVP（锚点固定 + 候选集提前剪枝）两种精确算法；在 9 个真实数据集上 BBVP 相对基线取得平均约 561×（摘要版称 636×）加速，并能在基线超时的大规模数据集上完成计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二部图广泛用于用户-商品、药物-靶点等关系建模，但真实交互常带极性（激活/抑制、喜欢/讨厌），需用带正负号的二部图刻画，因此对签名二部图做 motif 分析具有实际意义。现有工作存在三点不足：(1) 平衡蝴蝶 (2,2)-双团是最小的非平凡签名 motif，但只能捕捉局部结构，无法刻画同一分区内多于两个顶点的高阶关系；(2) 已有的最大（带符号）双团、signed bitruss 等算法是枚举/规模约束型算法，若要统计恰好 (p,q) 尺寸的平衡双团，必须先生成大量更大的双团再后置过滤，效率极低；BCList++ 虽能高效统计无符号 (p,q)-双团，但完全不考虑边符号，直接套用会枚举出海量最终被丢弃的不平衡候选；(3) 已有 balanced (2,k) 双团算法固定一侧为 2，符号依赖局限于同一顶点对，而 (p,q) 中蝴蝶数量随 p,q 组合式增长，要求所有内部蝴蝶同时满足平衡条件，需全局符号一致性，难度陡增。同时这类固定尺寸结构在药物-靶点网络中有直接价值（p 个药物对 q 个靶点表现一致的调控行为，可用于组合疗法设计、跨数据集模块对比）。

Method: 问题定义：带符号二部图 G=(U,V,E)，每条边带 +/− 标签；(p,q)-双团为两侧分别 p、q 个顶点的完全二分子图，若其内部不含不平衡蝴蝶（即每个 4-环含偶数条负边）则为平衡双团；关注 p>1、q>1。核心结构性质（Lemma 8/10）：平衡 (p,q)-双团中所有 signed p-wedge 属于同一类型——楔形类型由 κ=c₁c₂…c_{p−1} 编码，cᵢ∈{s,d} 表示边 (v₁,u₁) 与 (v₁,wᵢ) 符号相同(s)或相异(d)，共 2^{p−1} 种类型。三种算法：(1) SBCList++（基线）：将 Yang 等的 BCList++ 扩展以处理边符号，枚举全部 (p,q)-双团后再过滤不平衡者；(2) BBWC（楔形中心，Algorithm 1）：取较小分区 S 为锚点集，对每个 u∈S 按顶点优先级 ρ(u)（度优先、ID 次之）枚举 signed p-wedge，按类型放入 2^{p−1} 个桶，桶内记录「锚点侧顶点集 → 公共邻居数」，最后对每个楔形累加 C(共同邻居数, q)；最坏时间复杂度 O(m·Δ·C(Δ,p))；(3) BBVP（顶点剪枝，Algorithm 2）：以 u 为锚点，用 cnt[w] 统计 u 与 w 的共享邻居数，构造候选集 C={w : cnt[w]≥q}，若 |C|<p−1 直接跳过 u；再对 C 中 (p−1) 元组求公共邻居集合 T=∩L[w]，|T|<q 则丢弃；最后对 T 中每个 v 计算楔形类型 κ 并按类型用组合数 C(x[κ], q) 计数，从而避免同一顶点组被多个中心重复枚举（示例中楔形枚举由 6 次降为 3 次三元组）。

Result: 实现于 C++（Intel Xeon E5-1630 v4，32GB RAM），在 9 个真实数据集（Drug_Human、Senate、House、Astro、NIPS、Jester150、KDD Cup、AOL、Epinions；含原生带符号与按 0.7/0.3 概率合成符号两类）上评估，单次运行限时 5 小时，超时记为 INF。(1) p=q=3 运行时间：BBWC 全面优于基线——DH 0.755s→0.05s（15.1×）、SE 1937s→3.77s（561×）、AS 11.2×、KDD 2.8×，且 HO/NIPS/JE 上基线超时而 BBWC 可完成；但在 AOL、EP 等大分区分数据集上 BBWC 因楔形枚举爆炸而超时。(2) BBVP 在所有数据集上一致优于前两者，平均相对 BBWC 加速约 10.7×（HO 24.7×、AS 18.8×、DH 5×、SE 3.8×），并在基线与 BBWC 均超时的 AOL、EP 上成功完成（1764s / 8346s）；相对基线在基线可完成的 4 个数据集上平均加速约 561×（摘要中报告 636×，正文为 561×，两者存在数值口径差异）。(3) 内存：BBWC、BBVP 均低于基线（基线依赖额外辅助数组避免频繁子图构建），BBVP 内存最省（如 EP 2.31MB、SE 0.007MB vs 基线 0.68MB）。(4) 参数扩展 p∈{3,4,5}、q∈{3..7}：基线随 q 增大性能显著劣化，BBWC/BBVP 因已分离符号楔形并直接做组合选择而保持稳定。数据集上平衡 (3,3)-双团计数跨度极大（DH 92,818 到 House 约 1.01e11、Epinions 约 4.98e14），验证了规模适应性。

Conclusion: 本文提出并研究带符号二部图中平衡 (p,q)-双团的精确计数问题，指出这是此前未被研究的空白。BBWC 通过在枚举阶段即施加符号平衡约束（利用「同一平衡双团内所有 signed p-wedge 同型」的引理，将候选空间按 2^{p−1} 种符号配置分桶后做组合计数）避免后置过滤；BBVP 进一步以顶点为单位锚定与剪枝，用共享邻居计数构造必要条件候选集，显著削减冗余楔形枚举。二者均优于改编自 BCList++ 的基线 SBCList++，在运行时间与内存上全面占优，且能扩展到基线无法处理的最数据集，表明固定尺寸的平衡双团可高效、精确地在大规模真实网络中被计数。不足与展望：目前为串行实现，尚未利用多核/GPU；尚未处理动态（增量）带符号二部图；实验中部分数据集的符号为概率合成而非真实极性标签，可能影响结论普适性；此外摘要与正文给出的加速比（636× vs 561×）不一致，有待澄清。

Abstract: Two disjoint sets of entities and their relationship can be modelled as a bipartite graph. Real-life examples include drug-target interaction in biological networks, user-item relationships in e-commerce networks, etc. Motif-based analysis is essential for understanding the structure of large-scale networks, and bipartite graphs are no exception. In contrast to unsigned graphs, motif analysis in signed bipartite graphs has received limited attention. The smallest non-trivial motif in a signed bipartite graph is a balanced (2,2)-biclique, often called a balanced butterfly, which captures only local patterns and cannot reveal higher-order relationships. Bipartite motifs have been studied in the literature in the context of signed bipartite graphs, such as maximal biclique, bitruss, and so on. None of these works addresses bipartite motifs with fixed-sized vertex sets, which are often relevant in practical situations. In this work, we study the balanced (p,q)-biclique counting problem for small values of p and q. As a baseline, we first adapt and extend the state-of-the-art BCList++ algorithm for unsigned bipartite graphs to incorporate edge signs, which we call SBCList++. We then propose two efficient algorithms: BBWC, a wedge-centric approach that enforces balance constraints during enumeration, and BBVP, a vertex-based pruning approach that directly enumerates feasible vertex sets. Extensive experiments on large real-world datasets demonstrate that the vertex-based pruning algorithm, BBVP, significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving an average speedup of 636$\times$ over SBCList++ (where p=q=3).

</details>


### [270] [Exact and Approximate Algorithms for Polytree Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03622)
*Juha Harviainen,Frank Sommer,Manuel Sorge*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了多棵树（Polytree, 骨架为森林的贝叶斯网络）的结构学习问题，在精确算法与近似算法两个方向取得进展：精确方面，新提出动态规划算法解决无入度约束的多棵树学习（O(3^n)），并通过改进状态枚举把入度有界 k 的情形加速到 O((2+ε)^n)；近似方面，给出 PT_≤ 的 (k+1)-近似、PT_≤^+ 的 2-近似、PT_comp 的 2q-近似多项式时间算法，并证明这些界在 P≠NP 与唯一游戏猜想下几乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯网络（BN）的结构学习即使是分值可分解（局部可加）的情况也已被证明是 NP-hard（Chickering 1995），且推理为 #P-hard，因此实践中常依赖如 K2 的启发式算法。多棵树（Polytree，骨架为森林的 DAG）是受限的 BN 类：可解释性更强、推理仅需多项式时间（Pearl 1989），在显微图像分割等场景已有应用，但其结构学习即使在每个节点至多 2 个父节点时仍 NP-hard（Dasgupta 1999），已知最快算法需指数时间（Grüttemeier 等 2021a），仅"分支（branching）"特例（每节点一个父节点）可多项式求解（Edmonds 1967）。因此需要弄清：施加哪些限制（入度界、分值函数性质、组件规模界）能让问题变得可解或可近似，以及最优算法的运行时间与近似因子在何种意义上已接近最优。

Method: **问题设定**：Polytree Learning (PT)：给定节点集 V 与局部可加的局部分值 f_v（采用 non-zero 编码，未指定者为 −∞，并归一化 f_v(∅)=0），求骨架为森林的最高分 DAG；变体记号 PT_≤（入度上界 k）、PT_≤^+（可加局部分值）、PT_comp（每个连通分支至多 q 条弧）。

**1. 精确算法（Theorem 3.1）**：新增一个只能取空父集的辅助节点 u，并把每个候选父集 S 复制为 S 与 S∪{u}，把 PT 归约为"学习最高分的连通多棵树"。定义 DP 状态 Q[S,T]（节点集为 S、仅 T 中节点可拥有非空父集的最优连通多棵树的得分），递推为：选取 v∈T 猜测其父集 D_v∈F_v；按 D_v∩T 是否为空/恰含一个节点分两种情形转移，其余为 −∞。正确性通过 DFS 访问序 σ 归纳证明（DFS 序使 D_opt 在 S_i 上的诱导子图连通多棵树），并反向证明所有非 −∞ 的 DP 路径都对应连通多棵树。状态数 3^n，单状态 O(|F|·n^{O(1)})。

**2. 加速到 (2+ε)^n（Theorem 3.3 + Lemma 3.2）**：引理证明存在节点序（DFS 总是走向最小未探索子树，即"轻子树优先"DFS）使得 |S_i|−|T_i| = O(k log n)：因为 D_v ⊄ T_i 的节点数至多 O(log n)（sz(v) > sz(u)+sz(w) ≥ 2sz(w) 的"倍增"论证，k-叉树情形达到紧）。于是只需考虑满足 |S|−|T| = O(k log n) 的 DP 状态，状态数 2^n · n^{O(k log n)}；再由 |F| = O(n^k) 得总时间 O((2+ε)^n |I|^{O(1)})（任意 ε>0）。

**3. 运行时间下界（Theorem 3.5）**：从 k'-Set Partitioning（与 k'-Set Cover 在 SCC 下同下界）归约到 PT_≤：为宇宙元素建"宇宙节点"，建 t' = ⌈εt⌉ 个"选择节点"，加一个连接节点 p；选择节点的候选父集为至多 1/ε 个互不相交子集的并再加 {p}，其分值等于并集大小；构造规模 n ≤ (1+ε)n'+2，父集大小为常数 k = k'/ε。得分达到 n' 当且仅当原问题可解，从而 PT_≤ 与 PT 在 SCC 下不能快于 2^{(1−ε)n}。

**4. 近似算法**：Theorem 4.1——对 PT_≤ 采用贪心：反复为尚未定父集的节点加入分值最高的、且不破坏多棵树（无环）性质的父集。分析中用"虚拟边"计数：i 步后最多 (k+1)i 个最优父集被阻挡（前 i 个节点 + 每连通分支至多 |C|−1 条虚拟边），故第 i 步总能加入分值 ≥ f_{(k+1)i+1} 的父集，得 (k+1)-近似，并给出达到该因子的坏局部最优实例（图2）。Theorem 4.2——对 PT_≤^+（可加分值）改为逐边贪心加入最优单条边（同时满足无环与入度 ≤ k），否则会陷入坏局部最优；每步最多阻挡 2i 条最优边（分支连通性阻挡 i 条 + 入度阻挡 i 条），得 2-近似（图3 说明 2 是紧的）；注意该问题本身已被证明在 P 内（Ganian & Korchemna 2026）。Theorem 4.3——对 PT_comp（分支至多 q 弧），朴素贪心仅 O(q²)，改为按"单弧分值" ω_v(D_v)=f_v(D_v)/|D_v| 最大化选择父集，并利用"分支性质"（每个最优分支至多 q 弧，故 q·ω ≥ 该分支分值）以及每个父集至多被计数 (|D_v|+1) 次，得 2q-近似（图5 说明 2k 紧）。

**5. 不可近似性**：Theorem 5.3 从 Maximum Independent Set 归约（节点 v 的唯一非零分值父集为 E_v∪{p}，分值 1；"合理多棵树"与独立集一一对应，因相邻两点的非空父集会在 u←e→v←p→u 上成环），得 PT 无多项式时间 O(n^{1−ε})-近似（除非 P=NP，Håstad），PT_≤ 无 O(k/log²k)-近似（除非 UG 为假，Austrin 等）。推论 5.4 对一般 BN 结构学习给出相同界；推论 5.5 对 PT_comp 给无 O(q/log²q)-近似。

Result: **精确算法**：PT 可在 O(3^n·|I|^{O(1)}) 内精确求解（与 Grüttemeier 等 2021a 持平）；PT_≤ 在 O((2+ε)^n·|I|^{O(1)}) 内精确求解（此前最快为 O(3^n)），在 Set Cover 猜想下该复杂度几乎最优（PT_≤ 与 PT 均不能快于 2^{(1−ε)n}）。

**近似算法**：PT_≤ 有多项式时间 (k+1)-近似（在 UG 下对数因子内最优）；PT_≤^+ 有多项式时间 2-近似（紧）；PT_comp 有多项式时间 2q-近似（在 UG 下对数因子内最优）。

**不可近似性**：PT 不存在多项式时间 O(n^{1−ε})-近似（除非 P=NP）；PT_≤ 与 PT_comp 分别不存在 O(k/log²k)、O(q/log²q)-近似（除非 UG 为假）。

Conclusion: 本文系统研究了多棵树学习的精确与近似算法：精确侧给出与最优已知结果持平的 3^n 动态规划，以及对入度有界情形的 (2+ε)^n 改进，并证明其在 SCC 下近乎最优；近似侧首次给出多棵树学习的 FPT/多项式时间近似保证（(k+1)、2、2q），并配套给出基于独立集归约的不可近似性结果，说明多棵树结构更简单并不意味着比一般 BN 更好的近似保证。作者指出的开放问题包括：PT_≤^+ 的 2-近似能否显著改进、Kundu 等（2024a）的 FPT-近似思想能否迁移到多棵树、以及无入度界时 PT 能否快于 3^n；并建议将研究推广到常数树宽的受限网络（tw=1 即多棵树）。

Abstract: Polytrees are a subclass of Bayesian networks that seek to capture the conditional dependencies between a set of $n$ variables as a directed forest and are motivated by their more efficient inference and improved interpretability. Since the problem of learning the best polytree is NP-hard, we study which restrictions make it more tractable by considering for example in-degree bounds, properties of score functions measuring the quality of a polytree, and approximation algorithms. We devise an algorithm that finds the optimal polytree in time $O((2+ε)^n)$ for arbitrarily small $ε> 0$ and any constant in-degree bound $k$, improving over the fastest previously known algorithm of time complexity $O(3^n)$. We further give polynomial-time algorithms for finding a polytree whose score is within a factor of $k$ from the optimal one for arbitrary scores and a factor of $2$ for additive ones. Many of the results are complemented by (nearly) tight lower bounds for either the time complexity or the approximation factors.

</details>


### [271] [The Parameterized Complexity of Scheduling with Precedence Delays: Shuffle Product and Directed Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03727)
*Hans L. Bodlaender,Maher Mallem*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有优先约束和延迟值的调度问题的参数化复杂性。通过证明Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth两个经典问题的XNLP完全性，建立了弹性调度问题关于图宽度和延迟值的计算下界，为这些长期悬而未决的问题提供了完整的复杂性分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决调度与优先约束问题的长期悬而未决的参数化复杂性状态，特别是Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth这两个数十年未被分类的问题。

Method: 证明了Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth两个经典问题关于目标值的XNLP完全性，然后利用这些结果推导弹性调度问题的参数化复杂性下界。通过参数化规约建立了这些问题的计算硬度。

Result: 证明了Shuffle Product和Directed Bandwidth关于目标值是XNLP完全问题；推导出具有最小/最大延迟的调度问题关于有向无环图宽度和/或最大延迟值的XNLP下界；完全分类了树结构上Directed Bandwidth问题的参数化复杂性。

Conclusion: Shuffle Product被确立为XNLP完全问题的一个非常规且有前景的补充；该研究为具有延迟约束的调度问题的参数化复杂性提供了完整的分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the parameterized complexity of several variants of scheduling with precedence constraints between jobs. Namely, we consider the single machine setting with delay values on top of the precedence constraints. Such scheduling problems are related to several decades-old problems with open parameterized complexity status, notably Shuffle Product and Directed Bandwidth. We obtain XNLP-completeness results for both problems, and derive implications to scheduling with minimum (resp. maximum) delays parameterized by the width of the directed acyclic graph giving the precedence constraints, and/or by the maximum delay value in the input. Regarding Directed Bandwidth, we also settle the case of trees by showing XNLP-completeness parameterized by the target value. Beyond these results, we believe that Shuffle Product is an unusual and promising addition to the list of XNLP-complete problems.

</details>


### [272] [On Computing Total Variation Distance Between Mixtures of Product Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03839)
*Weiming Feng,Yucheng Fu,Minji Yang,Anqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了混合乘积分布间总变差距离的近似计算问题。对一般离散域给出多项式时间随机近似算法；对布尔子立方体这一特殊情形给出精确算法，但证明精确计算问题在参数线性增长时是#P-难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个混合乘积分布之间的总变差距离近似问题，这是概率分布距离估计的基础问题，在统计学习和理论计算机科学中有重要应用。

Method: 对于一般情况，使用随机化近似算法，时间复杂度为poly((nq)^(k1+k2), 1/ε)；对于布尔子立方体特殊情况，采用确定性精确算法。

Result: 给出了两个结果：(1)一般q元域混合分布：随机算法在poly((nq)^(k1+k2), 1/ε)时间内达到(1±ε)乘法误差近似；(2)布尔子立方体混合：确定性精确算法复杂度poly(n, 2^{O(k1+k2)})，且当k1+k2=Θ(n)时精确计算是#P-难的。

Conclusion: 对于一般的q元域混合分布，提供了多项式时间随机近似算法；对于布尔子立方体混合分布，提供了精确算法但可能是指数级的，并证明了精确计算问题是#P-难的，当组件总数与维度线性增长时。

Abstract: We study the problem of approximating the total variation distance between two mixtures of product distributions over an $n$-dimensional discrete domain. Given two mixtures $\mathbb{P}$ and $\mathbb{Q}$ with $k_1$ and $k_2$ product distributions over $[q]^n$, respectively, we give a randomized algorithm that approximates $d_{\mathrm{TV}}\left({\mathbb{P}},{\mathbb{Q}}\right)$ within a multiplicative error of $(1\pm \varepsilon)$ in time $\mathrm{poly}((nq)^{k_1+k_2},1/\varepsilon)$. We also study the special case of mixtures of Boolean subcubes over $\{0,1\}^n$. For this class, we give a deterministic algorithm that exactly computes the total variation distance in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,2^{O(k_1+k_2)})$, and show that exact computation is $\#\mathsf{P}$-hard when $k_1+k_2=Θ(n)$.

</details>


### [273] [Parallel Reachability and Shortest Paths on Non-sparse Digraphs: Near-linear Work and Sub-square-root Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03892)
*Vikrant Ashvinkumar,Aaron Bernstein,Maximilian Probst Gutenberg,Thatchaphol Saranurak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出在稠密图上突破Ω(√n)深度瓶颈的并行算法，reachability和shortest path在近线性工作量下分别达到n^0.136和n^0.25+o(1)深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近线性工作量的并行reachability和shortest path算法在所有密度下都需Ω(√n)深度，存在下界瓶颈，限制了并行计算效率。

Method: 提出接近线性工作量的并行算法，在m≥n^{1+o(1)}时实现亚平方根深度，采用新的图分解或组合技术。

Result: 在m=Ω(n²)极端稠密情况下，reachability算法深度仅n^0.136，shortest path算法深度仅n^0.25+o(1)。

Conclusion: 首次在稠密图上突破了o(√n)深度瓶颈，reachability可达n^0.136深度，shortest path可达n^0.25+o(1)深度。

Abstract: We present parallel algorithms for computing single-source reachability and shortest paths on directed $n$-vertex $m$-edge graphs using near-linear $\tilde{O}(m)$ work and $o(\sqrt{n})$ depth whenever $m\ge n^{1+o(1)}$. At the extreme of $m=Ω(n^{2})$, our reachability and shortest path algorithms have depth only $n^{0.136}$ and $n^{0.25+o(1)}$, respectively. The state-of-the-art parallel algorithms with near-linear work for both problems require $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ depth in all density regimes.

</details>


### [274] [Optimal Hardness of Online Algorithms for Large Common Induced Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03893)
*David Gamarnik,Miklós Z. Rácz,Gabe Schoenbach*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文研究两个独立Erdős-Rényi随机图$G_1, G_2 \sim \mathbb{G}(n,1/2)$的最大公共导出子图问题。证明贪心在线算法可找到大小为$(2-o(1))\log_2 n$的公共导出子图，并证明任何在线算法都无法以恒定概率找到大小至少为$(2+\varepsilon)\log_2 n$的公共导出子图，揭示了该问题存在计算-优化gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来Chatterjee和Diaconis证明两个独立随机图的最大公共导出子图大小以高概率为$(4-o(1))\log_2 n$，但该最优解难以通过在线算法高效找到。研究旨在探究是否存在计算-优化间隙(computation-to-optimization gap)，即最优解大小与在线算法可达到的大小之间是否存在显著差距。

Method: 1. 设计并分析简单贪心在线算法；2. 利用解空间的重叠间隙性质(OGP/overlap gap property)证明下界；3. 运用Gamarnik等人发展的插值论证方法建立OGP与在线算法局限性的联系。

Result: 1. 简单贪心在线算法可找到大小为$(2-o(1))\log_2 n$的公共导出子图；2. 证明了任何在线算法都无法以恒定概率找到大小至少为$(2+\varepsilon)\log_2 n$的解；3. 建立了问题解空间的(多重)重叠间隙性质。

Conclusion: 该问题存在显著的计算-优化间隙：最优解大小为$(4-o(1))\log_2 n$，而在线算法最多只能达到约$2\log_2 n$，间隙因子约为2。这一结果为利用OGP理论分析组合优化问题的计算复杂性提供了新的证据。

Abstract: We study the problem of efficiently finding large common induced subgraphs of two independent Erdős--Rényi random graphs $G_1, G_2 \sim \mathbb{G}(n,1/2)$. Recently, Chatterjee and Diaconis showed that the largest common induced subgraph of $G_1$ and $G_2$ has size $(4-o(1))\log_2 n$ with high probability. We first show that a simple greedy online algorithm finds a common induced subgraph of $G_1$ and $G_2$ of size $(2-o(1)) \log_2 n$ with high probability. Our main result shows that no online algorithm can find a common induced subgraph of $G_1$ and $G_2$ of size at least $(2+\varepsilon) \log_2 n$ with probability bounded away from $0$ as $n \to \infty$. Together, these results provide evidence that this problem exhibits a computation-to-optimization gap. To prove the impossibility result, we show that the solution space of the problem exhibits a version of the (multi) overlap gap property (OGP), and utilize an interpolation argument recently developed by Gamarnik, Kizildağ, and Warnke that connects OGP and online algorithms.

</details>


### [275] [An $\widetilde{O} (n^{3/7})$ Round Parallel Algorithm for Matroid Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03979)
*Sanjeev Khanna,Aaron Putterman,Junkai Song*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究 matroid 找基问题的并行轮复杂度，通过新的结构分析框架将最优轮数从 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ 改进至 $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$，缩小了存在近四十年的上下界差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近四十年悬而未决的难题：K UW证明 $O(\sqrt{n})$ 上界与 $\widetilde Ω(n^{1/3})$ 下界之间存在巨大差距，KPS 于2025年首次打破僵局达到 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$，但仍需进一步改进以缩小差距。

Method: 开发了新的结构和算法框架，将分析视角从单个元素转移到理解依赖关系如何在多个元素间同时传播。

Result: 给出 $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$ 轮的新算法，比 KPS 的 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ 有显著提升（因 $3/7 < 7/15$，即 $n^{0.429} < n^{0.467}$）。

Conclusion: 提出新算法将找基轮数减少至 $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$，首次超越 KPS 的 $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ 结果；关键创新在于从单一元素分析转向多元素依赖关系的整体框架。

Abstract: We study the parallel (adaptive) complexity of the classic problem of finding a basis in an $n$-element matroid, given access via an \emph{independence oracle}. In this model, the algorithm may submit polynomially many independence queries in each round, and the central question is: how many rounds are necessary and sufficient to find a basis?
  Karp, Upfal, and Wigderson (FOCS~1985, JCSS~1988; hereafter KUW) initiated this study, showing that $O(\sqrt{n})$ adaptive rounds suffice for any matroid, and that $\widetildeΩ(n^{1/3})$ rounds are necessary even for partition matroids. This left a substantial gap that persisted for nearly four decades, until Khanna, Putterman, and Song (FOCS~2025; hereafter KPS) achieved $\widetilde O(n^{7/15})$ rounds, the first improvement since~KUW.
  In this work, we make another conceptual advance beyond KPS, giving a new algorithm that finds a matroid basis in $\widetilde O(n^{3/7})$ rounds. We develop a structural and algorithmic framework that brings a new lens to the analysis of random circuits, moving from reasoning about individual elements to understanding how dependencies span multiple elements simultaneously.

</details>
